### [1] [What Remains in Sight? Autoregressive Video Decoding as Representation-Guided Context Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04164)
*Lixuan He,Jihyeon Je,Yang You,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 ReCR，将自回归视频解码中的“可见上下文选择”显式化为表征引导的上下文重写，在匹配可见 token 预算下提升多个骨干的长视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归长视频生成虽通过因果解码器、长上下文训练与 KV-cache 延长了可生成时长，但推理阶段“把哪些历史帧/片段展示给解码器”仍由最近窗口、压缩缓存或外部记忆隐式决定，缺乏显式控制。

Method: ReCR 为免训练方法，将自回归视频解码建模为表征引导的上下文重写。每步利用解码器内部表征，从已生成历史中筛选上下文单元，准则包括：与全局 rollout 状态匹配、避免与近期续接内容冗余、保持时间边界覆盖、扩大空间布局多样性；随后将选中单元映射到紧凑因果轴上再解码下一块。

Result: 在长时域文本到视频基准上，ReCR 在相同可见 token 预算下稳定提升多个自回归骨干；表征空间诊断与转场计数提示切换实验进一步验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 改善“保留哪些内容可见”是通向更稳定长视频解码的有效路径，可见上下文选择可作为显式的测试时决策。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is increasingly moving toward autoregressive rollout, where each newly generated segment becomes part of the evidence used for future prediction. Recent causal decoders, long-context training, and KV-cache mechanisms have extended feasible duration, but most inference pipelines still decide the visible past implicitly through recent windows, compressed caches, or external memory. This raises a direct question: \textbf{can the choice of which past frames or segments to show to the decoder be treated as an explicit test-time decision?} We answer this question with \textsc{ReCR}, a training-free method that formulates autoregressive video decoding as representation-guided context rewriting. At each step, \textsc{ReCR} builds a selected visible context from the generated history by using internal decoder representations to favor context units that match the global rollout state, avoid redundancy with the recent continuation, preserve temporal boundary coverage, and widen the range of spatial layouts in view. The selected units are then mapped onto a compact causal axis before decoding the next block. Across long-horizon text-to-video benchmarks, \textsc{ReCR} consistently improves multiple autoregressive backbones under matched visible-token budgets. Empirical analyses such as representation-space diagnostics and transition-count prompt switching further show that improving what remains visible is an effective path toward more stable long-video decoding.

</details>


### [2] [VIGOR: Visual Gain Ordering for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05066)
*Junzhe Chen,Tian Qin,Eugenie Shi,Tianshu Zhang,Qiang Ju,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、即插即用的推理策略VIGOR，通过反事实视觉证据重排掩码位置，缓解多模态离散扩散语言模型的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态离散扩散语言模型（dLLMs）通过迭代掩码预测生成，虽具潜力但仍有幻觉：输出符合语言先验却缺乏图像依据。作者认为幻觉不仅是token预测问题，更是"过早承诺"问题——迭代去噪中部分掩码位置因置信度更多由语言先验而非视觉证据驱动而被过早解掩，从而偏置后续生成。

Method: 提出Visual Gain Ordering（VIGOR），一种免训练、即插即用的推理策略。在每个去噪步骤，对比各候选token在原始图像条件pass与额外视觉消融pass下的置信度，据此重排掩码位置，优先解掩那些预测更受图像支撑的位置。

Result: 在LLaDA-V与Lumina-DiMOO上的实验表明，VIGOR在各基准上持续降低幻觉并提升多模态推理性能。

Conclusion: 将多模态dLLM幻觉视为过早承诺问题，VIGOR以反事实视觉增益指导解掩顺序，无需训练即可有效抑制幻觉并提升推理表现，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) generate responses through iterative mask prediction and offer a promising alternative to autoregressive large vision-language models. However, they still suffer from hallucination, producing outputs that are plausible under language priors but insufficiently grounded in the image. We argue that hallucination in multimodal dLLMs is not only a token prediction problem, but also a premature commitment problem: during iterative denoising, some masked positions are unmasked too early because their confidence is driven more by language priors than by visual evidence, and these early commitments can bias later generation. To address this issue, we propose Visual Gain Ordering (VIGOR), a training-free and plug-and-play inference strategy that reorders masked positions according to counterfactual visual evidence. At each denoising step, VIGOR compares the confidence of each candidate token under the original image-conditioned pass and an additional visual-ablation pass, and prioritizes positions whose predictions are more strongly supported by the image. Experiments on LLaDA-V and Lumina-DiMOO show that VIGOR consistently reduces hallucination and improves multimodal reasoning performance across benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/THU-BPM/Vigor.

</details>


### [3] [VisSearch-Bench: Evaluating Long Horizon Visual Search in Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04894)
*ZhengXian Wu,Qianhao Nie,Zhuohong Chen,Junjie Gao,Yuyao Zhang,Yunyao Yu,Hangrui Xu,Cheng Cheng,Guiyu Ma,Jin Zhang,Xingzhong Xu,Kai Yang,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VisSearch-Bench：包含 204 条人工编写任务（7 大领域、25 子领域）的视觉上下文管理基准，覆盖 5 类视觉密集型搜索任务；16 个多模态模型中最优系统在配检索工具下仅完全解出 26.47% 任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索类多模态智能体任务中，图像多仅用于识别初始实体，随后依赖文本检索，难以揭示智能体如何在图像、物体与检索步骤之间连接信息。因此作者聚焦"视觉上下文管理"——即组织并使用视觉信息、并将其与搜索中的其他发现相联结的能力。视觉密集型任务要求将物体外观、局部细节或空间关系与外部信息结合，现有基准对此覆盖不足。

Method: 构建 VisSearch-Bench，含 204 条人工编写任务、7 个主领域与 25 个子领域；任务类型包括集合式视觉搜索、序列式视觉搜索、混合宽深搜索、竞争性视觉消歧、视觉结构引导搜索。每条任务配有参考答案、可溯源证据与评分规则；采用两项指标：Accuracy（衡量任务整体成功与否）与 Process Score（衡量单个结果及子问题答案的正确性与完整性）。在 16 个多模态模型上进行评测。

Result: 即使配备检索工具，表现最好的系统也仅完全解决 26.47% 的任务；Process Score 进一步揭示子步骤层面的部分正确性存在明显缺口。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了将视觉证据与检索结果相联结以生成完整答案的困难，表明视觉上下文管理是多模态智能体的关键瓶颈，VisSearch-Bench 为此类能力提供了细粒度、可溯源的评测手段。

Abstract: Multimodal agents use web and image tools to answer questions beyond their internal knowledge. Some search tasks use images mainly to identify an initial entity, then rely on text retrieval. These tasks offer limited insight into how agents connect information across images, objects, and search steps. We therefore focus on visual context management. This involves organizing and using visual information and linking it to other findings during search. Visually intensive tasks require agents to combine object appearance, local details, or spatial relations with external information. We introduce VisSearch-Bench, with 204 human-written tasks across 7 main domains and 25 subdomains. It covers Set-wise Visual Search, Sequential Visual Search, Hybrid Wide-Deep Search, Competitive Visual Disambiguation, and Visual-Structure-Guided Search. Each task provides a reference answer, traceable evidence, and scoring rules. Accuracy measures complete task success, while Process Score measures the correctness and completeness of individual results and subquestion answers. Across 16 multimodal models, the best system fully solves only 26.47% of tasks, even with search tools. These results highlight the challenge of linking visual evidence with search results to produce complete answers.

</details>


### [4] [Cycle WAM: Coupling Action and Video through Cycle Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04711)
*Qinglin Yang,Jiepeng Wang,Yuxing Huang,Haibin Huang,Kai Xu,Chi Zhang,Ruizhen Hu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cycle WAM 将视频与动作视为由因果关系联结的成对模态，通过双向转换与可微循环重建目标强化跨模态一致性，从而更充分地利预训练视频先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）虽利用预训练视频先验从隐表示解码动作或由生成视频推断动作，但训练目标本身并不保证动作与其预测的视觉后果相一致，限制了预训练能力的发挥。

Method: 提出统一训练框架 Cycle WAM：一是在视频-动作联合训练中加入双向转换，强化跨模态对应关系并恢复前向与逆向动力学；二是引入可微的循环重建目标，进一步耦合前向与逆向两个映射。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 上取得同类方法中的 SOTA；在 DROID 上显著优于 DreamZero；与 UWM 的对比刻画了其逆向动力学性能，并显示前向动力学预测具有更清晰的视觉细节。

Conclusion: 将视频与动作建模为由因果关系联系的成对模态并显式施加循环一致性约束，可同时支持前向与逆向动力学，且更有效地利用预训练视频先验。

Abstract: Recent advances in video modeling have driven substantial progress in world action models (WAMs). Existing approaches exploit pretrained video priors by decoding actions from latent representations or inferring them from generated videos. The underlying link is causal: robot actions produce changes in the scene, and video observations of these changes provide evidence for inferring the actions that produced them. However, existing training formulations do not by themselves ensure that actions and their predicted visual consequences are consistent, potentially limiting the use of pretrained capabilities. We treat video and action as paired modalities linked by this causal relationship and learn their correspondence through a unified training framework designed to better exploit pretrained video priors. Cycle WAM first augments video-action co-training with bidirectional conversion to reinforce cross-modal correspondence and recover forward and inverse dynamics. It then introduces a differentiable cycle-reconstruction objective to further couple the two mappings. The resulting model supports both forward and inverse dynamics, with the potential to improve performance through more effective use of pretrained video priors. Cycle WAM achieves state-of-the-art performance among comparable methods on LIBERO-Pro and substantially improves DreamZero on DROID. Comparisons with UWM characterize its inverse-dynamics performance and show sharper visual detail in the displayed forward-dynamics predictions.

</details>


### [5] [DenseTwins: Dense Modality Decoupling for Multimodal Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04710)
*Carlo Sgaravatti,Daniele Kopyshevskiy,Davide Secco,Giacomo Boracchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DenseTwins，一种多模态自监督框架，通过将稠密特征空间显式解耦为共享与模态特有两部分，学习保留空间结构的稠密表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态感知广泛用于机器人与自动驾驶，自监督可利用大量无标注数据；但现有方法要么通过强制特征空间对齐而忽略模态特有信息，要么只学全局表征而丢失空间结构。

Method: 以稠密跨模态对应关系结合冗余削减目标，将稠密特征解耦为共享分量与模态特有名分量；联合优化跨增强视图的模态内目标，以及对齐共享特征、去相关模态特有信息的跨模态目标。

Result: 在多个多模态基准与多种传感配置上评估，相对多模态自监督基线取得SOTA或具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 在结构化稠密嵌入空间中同时捕获共享语义与模态特有线索，可实现兼顾对齐与特异的稠密多模态自监督表征学习。

Abstract: Multimodal perception is central to many computer vision applications, including robotics and autonomous driving, where different sensing modalities provide complementary cues. Self-supervised learning offers a scalable solution for exploiting large amounts of unlabeled multimodal data. However, existing methods either neglect modality-specific information by enforcing feature-space alignment between modalities or learn only global representations without preserving spatial structure. We propose DenseTwins, a multimodal self-supervised framework that learns dense representations by explicitly decoupling the dense feature space into shared and modality-specific components. The proposed approach combines dense cross-modal correspondences with redundancy-reduction objectives, enabling the model to capture both shared semantics and modality-specific cues within a structured dense embedding space. DenseTwins jointly optimizes an intra-modal objective across augmented views and a cross-modal objective that aligns shared features while decorrelating modality-specific information. We evaluate DenseTwins across multiple multimodal benchmarks and sensing setups, achieving state-of-the-art or competitive performance across multimodal self-supervised baselines. Code available at: https://github.com/CarloSgaravatti/DenseTwins.

</details>


### [6] [Therapy-FIS: A Dataset for Assessing Facilitative Interpersonal Skills in Psychotherapeutic Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04645)
*Qijian Zheng,Wanqing Lin,Yuanfang Wang,Yijun Ge,Jordan Bate,Katie Aafjes-van Doorn,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个公开的心理治疗标准化任务数据集 Therapy-FIS，包含面部、声学与文本信号，并建立两个下游任务基准及融合基线 DyadicFIS，取得最佳 macro CCC（0.2807 与 0.3299）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FIS（促进性人际技能）评估目前高度依赖人工观察者评分，过程耗费人力、成本高且难以规模化；同时该领域缺乏公开的专业心理治疗数据，制约了自动化 FIS 评估的发展。

Method: 构建 Therapy-FIS 数据集：基于 890 段治疗师视频与 8 段模拟来访者视频，提取治疗师对标准化模拟来访者刺激片段的标准化反应中的面部、声学、文本多模态信号，并提供 8 个 FIS 子量表评分及总体 FIS 分数。在此基础上建立两个下游任务基准：治疗师表现评分与治疗师—模拟来访者联合表现评分，并提出基线模型 DyadicFIS，采用门控融合（gated fusion）与路由读出（routed readout），在仅治疗师与双人（dyadic）两种设定下运行。

Result: DyadicFIS 在两个任务上均取得最佳 macro CCC，分别为 0.2807 与 0.3299。

Conclusion: Therapy-FIS 为心理健康领域治疗性人际技能的数据驱动自动化评估提供了可复现的资源，填补了该领域公开数据集的空白。

Abstract: Facilitative Interpersonal Skills (FIS) is a performance-based assessment method that provides a structured framework for evaluating the interpersonal competencies of mental health professionals. However, existing FIS assessment relies heavily on human observer ratings, making the evaluation process labor-intensive, costly, and difficult to scale. Progress toward automated FIS assessment has also been constrained by the lack of publicly available professional data in psychotherapy settings. To address this limitation, we introduce Therapy-FIS, a dataset for automated FIS assessment in standardized psychotherapy tasks. To our knowledge, Therapy-FIS is the first publicly available dataset in this domain. It contains facial, acoustic, and textual signals derived from standardized therapist responses to standardized mock patient stimulus clips, based on 890 therapist video recordings and 8 patient videos, and is rated with eight FIS subscale ratings and an overall FIS score. Built on Therapy-FIS, we further establish a benchmark with two downstream tasks: Therapist Performance Rating and Joint Therapist--mock-Patient Performance Rating. We also introduce a competitive baseline, DyadicFIS, with gated fusion and routed readout under therapist-only and dyadic settings. DyadicFIS obtains the best macro CCC(Concordance Correlation Coefficient) scores on both tasks (0.2807 and 0.3299). Therapy-FIS offers a reproducible resource for data-driven automated assessment of therapeutic interpersonal skills in mental health.

</details>


### [7] [BtsPruner: Role-Aware Visual Token Pruning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04537)
*Zihao Qi,Zhibo Zhang,Miaoxin Zhang,Jincheng Cai,Zengqiang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的视觉token剪枝框架BtsPruner，依据LVLM中视觉token角色随编码进程从局部细节载体向全局引用代表的转变，在固定预算下保留互补角色token，实现大幅压缩与加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM推理需处理数百至数千视觉token，计算与显存开销大；现有免训练剪枝方法多以语言注意力、视觉注意力或特征相似度统一评估token重要性，忽略视觉token在编码过程中承担不同角色的问题。

Method: 先基于注意力集中度的演化识别token角色转变点；随后从后期编码阶段挖掘全局引用代表token，并递归地从早期阶段回溯检索局部细节token，以固定预算保留互补角色。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、Qwen-VL、InternVL、CogVLM上于激进token削减下保持更优性能保留；LLaVA-1.5-7B仅用576个视觉token中的32个（5.6%）即保留稠密模型91.6%的性能；LLaVA-NeXT-7B高分辨率设置下CUDA延迟最高加速5.95倍。

Conclusion: 视觉token角色随编码演化的性质可被有效利用，角色感知的互补token保留策略在免训练条件下实现强压缩下的性能与效率双优。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) often process hundreds or thousands of visual tokens, resulting in substantial computational and memory overhead during inference. Existing training-free pruning methods typically estimate token importance using language-model attention, visual attention, or feature similarity. However, these methods treat visual tokens with uniform criteria and overlook that tokens may serve different roles during visual encoding. In this work, we investigate the evolution of visual token roles in LVLMs and observe that tokens progressively transition from local detail carriers to globally referenced representatives as encoding proceeds. Based on this observation, we propose BtsPruner, a training-free role-aware visual token pruning framework that preserves complementary token roles under a fixed budget. Specifically, BtsPruner first identifies the token-role transition point based on the evolution of attention concentration. It then discovers globally referenced representatives from later encoding stages and recursively retrieves local detail tokens from earlier stages to recover complementary fine-grained evidence. Extensive experiments across LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, Qwen-VL, InternVL, and CogVLM demonstrate that BtsPruner achieves superior performance retention under aggressive token reduction. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, BtsPruner preserves 91.6% of the dense model's performance using only 32 of 576 visual tokens (5.6%). In the high-resolution LLaVA-NeXT-7B setting, it achieves up to a 5.95x CUDA-latency speedup.

</details>


### [8] [Understanding Backdoor Attacks in Scene Graph Generation via Global Context Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04379)
*Xiaoguang Chang,Tuo Chen,Chengze Jiang,Teng Wang,Changyin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文首次系统研究场景图生成(SGG)训练期后门风险，提出针对"对象类别-关系"成对级攻击面的有效攻击方法与三轴评估协议，并揭示显式全局上下文通路是后门效应的主要载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGG生成结构化场景表示，但其训练期后门风险尚未被探索。与分类任务不同，SGG具有"成对级"攻击面，可对涉及指定对象类别的特定关系进行选择性污染。同时SGG依赖局部证据，全局触发器可能难以奏效，而近期架构引入全局上下文以改进场景建模，可能反而便利此类攻击，故需系统研究。

Method: 首先实例化一种有效的后门攻击方法（针对指定对象类别的关系进行选择性污染），并设计三轴评估协议刻画后门行为；在Visual Genome上对代表性的开放词汇与闭集SGG模型开展实验；进而进行机理分析，包括推理时用干净全局上下文表示替换被污染的全局表示，以及在OvSGTR家族上开展训练期干预（抑制该通路的触发器信号）。

Result: 攻击在代表性开放词汇与闭集SGG模型上均有效。机理分析表明显式全局上下文通路是各被测模型中后门效应的主要载体：推理时替换为干净全局表示可使OvSGTR家族与RelationFormer的攻击成功率分别下降100%与97%。OvSGTR家族的训练期干预显示，抑制该通路的触发信号要么降低攻击成功率，要么显著损害模型效用。

Conclusion: 显式全局上下文设计可能在SGG架构中引入后门风险，全局上下文通路是后门效应的关键载体，需在SGG模型设计中予以关注与防护。

Abstract: Scene Graph Generation (SGG) produces structured scene representations, yet its training-time backdoor risk remains unexplored. Unlike classification, SGG exposes a pair-level attack surface that enables selective corruption of relations involving a designated object category. However, because SGG largely relies on localized evidence, globally placed triggers may face difficulty in manipulating such predictions. Recent SGG architectures increasingly incorporate global context to improve scene modeling, which may in turn facilitate such attacks. In this paper, we systematically study this backdoor risk and investigate the role of the global context pathway in backdoor behavior. Concretely, we first instantiate this attack with an effective method, and propose a three-axis evaluation protocol to characterize backdoor behavior. Experiments on Visual Genome with representative open-vocabulary and closed-set SGG models demonstrate the effectiveness of the attack. Mechanistic analyses reveal that the explicit global context pathway serves as a major carrier of the backdoor effect across the evaluated SGG models. In particular, replacing the poisoned global context representation with its clean counterpart during inference sharply suppresses the attack, yielding 100% and 97% reductions in attack success for the OvSGTR family and RelationFormer, respectively. Moreover, training-time interventions in the OvSGTR family show that suppressing trigger signals in this pathway either reduces attack success or substantially degrades utility. Together, these findings suggest that explicit global context designs may pose a backdoor risk in SGG architectures.

</details>


### [9] [Improving PI-RADS Prediction and Prostate Cancer Detection from Multiparametric MRI with Anatomical Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04155)
*Seungbin Park,Thibault Heintz,Rachit Saluja,Francesca Khani,Timothy McClure,Daniel Margolis,Mert R. Sabuncu,Heejong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在多种架构（3D ResNet-18/50、ViT）中引入前列腺分区解剖掩膜可提升PI-RADS评分预测与csPCa检测性能，且AI预测的PI-RADS与专家评分互补，可显著提高诊断AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从mpMRI准确预测PI-RADS评分比检测临床显著前列腺癌（csPCa）更具挑战性；病灶位置对PI-RADS评分至关重要，因此作者探究引入前列腺分区解剖信息能否改善预测性能。

Method: 在大规模内部数据集上，对3D ResNet-18、ResNet-50与ViT三种架构，分别在有/无前列腺分区掩膜（2区、5区）引导下进行训练与评估；并在外部数据集上微调验证；进一步比较模型预测PI-RADS与专家评分的联合诊断效用。

Result: 解剖引导总体提升各架构性能：PI-RADS预测最佳为ResNet-18+2区引导（QWK=0.511），csPCa检测最佳为ResNet-50+5区引导（AUC=0.809）；外部微调结果同样受益；结合模型预测与专家PI-RADS评分使csPCa检测AUC由0.711提升至0.756（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 将前列腺分区解剖先验融入深度学习模型可有效提升mpMRI评估性能，AI预测的PI-RADS评分可作为专家评估的补充信息用于csPCa检测。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of Prostate Imaging Reporting and Data System (PI-RADS) scores from multiparametric MRI (mpMRI) remains challenging, more so than clinically significant prostate cancer (csPCa) detection. Given the importance of lesion location in PI-RADS scoring, we evaluated whether incorporating prostate zonal masks improves PI-RADS prediction and csPCa detection across multiple architectures (3D ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ViT) using a large-scale internal dataset. Anatomical guidance generally improved performance across architectures, with the best results achieved by ResNet-18 with 2-zone guidance for PI-RADS prediction (QWK = 0.511) and ResNet-50 with 5-zone guidance for csPCa detection (AUC = 0.809). Fine-tuned results on external datasets further demonstrated the benefits of anatomical guidance. Furthermore, models with anatomical guidance improved the utility of model-predicted PI-RADS scores for PI-RADS-based csPCa detection (positive defined as PI-RADS>=3). Combining model-predicted and expert-assigned PI-RADS scores significantly increased the AUC from 0.711 to 0.756 (p < 0.05), suggesting that AI-derived PI-RADS scores may provide complementary information to expert assessment for csPCa detection. Overall, our findings highlight the value of incorporating prostate zonal anatomy into deep learning-based prostate MRI assessment.

</details>


### [10] [Physics-Informed Functional Tucker Method with RKHS Factors for Sparse Spatiotemporal Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03717)
*Zhengyang Liu,Jianguo Huang,Junxi Hu,Yucheng Mi,Haojie Cai,Wenxuan Shen,Zhikun Zhang,Renfeng Peng,Guangtao Zhang,Zhiqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理信息函数Tucker模型PhysFTM，在RKHS中参数化Tucker模态因子并引入PDE有限差分残差，实现稀疏传感器与缺失帧下的连续时空场重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定稀疏传感器与缺失帧条件下重建物理动力学是反问题且病态：观测稀疏使场的大部分区域缺乏约束，模型可能拟合传感器观测却在非传感器区域结构失真，或在支撑帧之间预测不可靠。需要引入额外物理信息以约束未观测区域。

Method: PhysFTM将Tucker模态因子参数化为RKHS中的函数，并通过在非传感器配点处计算的有限差分残差嵌入PDE约束，提供无直接观测区域的物理监督。支撑帧重建采用交替最小化，分别优化Tucker核心张量与RKHS因子；缺失帧重建则在Tucker核心空间构建连续轨迹，由学习到的空间RKHS-FTM表示解码，恢复完整时空场。

Result: 在Allen–Cahn、Navier–Stokes、三维热方程及三维网格化Copernicus海洋再分析数据上，两种缺失帧协议下均实现准确的2D/3D重建。

Conclusion: 将RKHS函数表示、Tucker张量分解与PDE残差约束结合，可有效缓解稀疏观测与缺失帧导致的病态性，为有限传感器下的连续时空场重建提供可行框架。

Abstract: Reconstructing physical dynamics from fixed sparse sensors and missing frames is a fundamental challenge in scientific research. Sparsity leaves large regions of the field unconstrained, making the inverse problem ill-posed: a model may fit the observed sensors while producing inaccurate off-sensor structures or unreliable predictions between support frames. To this end, we propose PhysFTM---a physics-informed functional Tucker model that parameterizes the Tucker mode factors in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs) and incorporates PDE constraints through finite-difference residuals evaluated at off-sensor collocation points. These residuals provide additional physical information in regions without direct observational supervision. PhysFTM first reconstructs continuous spatial fields at support frames via an alternating minimization scheme, optimizing the Tucker core tensor and the RKHS-based factors separately. To enable missing-frame reconstruction, PhysFTM constructs a continuous trajectory in Tucker-core space, which is decoded by the learned spatial RKHS-FTM representation to recover the full spatiotemporal field. Experiments on Allen--Cahn, Navier--Stokes, three-dimensional Heat equations, and a three-dimensional gridded Copernicus ocean reanalysis demonstrate accurate 2D/3D reconstruction under two missing-frame protocols. Code is available at https://github.com/liusirzzz/PhysFTM.

</details>


### [11] [RGB Photo Enhancement with Deep Learning on Smartphone GPUs: Mobile AI 2026 Challenge Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05024)
*Andrey Ignatov,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Dong Zhang,Zhao Ran,Yang Zhang,Xiaochen Li,Shichang Ju,Yiqin Cao,Yang Lu,Nguyen Nhu Tinh Anh,To Thanh Dat,Nguyen Ngoc Ky Duyen,Nguyen Trong Nghia,Bui Minh Hieu,Qiuhai Yan,Sunzhe Yang,Kang Chen,Tingting Wang,Faming Fang,Runhua Deng,Zhifeng Wang,Long Peng,Aiwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Mobile AI 挑战赛中面向移动端 GPU 的 RGB 照片增强任务：基于 DPED 数据集设计高效模型，在骁龙与天玑平台上实现全高清图像 300 ms 以内的增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备拍摄及其他来源的 RGB 照片需要画质提升与修图，但移动硬件资源受限，亟需兼具性能与可部署性的轻量深度学习模型。

Method: 组织挑战赛并给出统一设定：提供大规模 DPED 数据集（旧款 iPhone 3GS 与专业 Canon 70D 单反拍摄的成对 RGB 图像），要求参赛者在高通 Adreno 与 Arm Mali GPU 上实现低延迟综合增强；并在 Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 与 Dimensity 9500 芯片上统一评测运行时间。

Result: 优胜方案可在两个平台上于 300 ms 内完成全高清（Full HD）分辨率 RGB 图像增强。

Conclusion: 论文系统总结了挑战赛中开发的各模型方案，表明在主流移动 GPU 上实现实时、高质量的端侧照片增强是可行的。

Abstract: On-device RGB photo enhancement is a popular task that is needed for quality improvement of photos captured with mobile devices as well as for retouching of images acquired through other sources. This creates a need for efficient deep learning models for this problem that are not only performant but can also be deployed on constrained mobile hardware. In this Mobile AI challenge, we address this problem and propose participants to design fast solutions that can perform comprehensive enhancement of RGB photos while running on popular smartphone GPUs from Qualcomm and Arm. For this, the participants were provided with a large-scale DPED dataset consisting of RGB image pairs captured with an old iPhone 3GS smartphone and a professional Canon 70D DSLR camera. The runtime of all models was evaluated on the Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 and Dimensity 9500 chipsets with the Adreno and Mali GPUs, respectively. Top solutions proposed in this challenge are able to enhance Full HD resolution RGB images on both platforms under 300 milliseconds. A comprehensive description of the models developed in the challenge is provided in this paper.

</details>


### [12] [DreamX: Video Diffusion Model is a Universal Latent Action Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05377)
*Jisoo Kim,Chaehyun Kim,Junsu Kim,Jiho Park,Seongchan Kim,Eunbeen Hong,Soowon Son,Fu-En Yang,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DreamX，将预训练视频扩散模型改造为通用潜在动作学习器，从时空特征中提取紧凑潜在动作（每帧压缩 1200→4 tokens，300×），获得解耦外观、跨具身的共享动作表示，支持任意视频迁移与零样本动作迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视频学习通用动作表示需同时满足：对同一动作的不同执行保持动态一致性，又对具身与外观变化保持不变性。现有潜在动作模型虽从视觉转移中推断动作，但表示常将动作与无关视觉变化纠缠，限制跨具身迁移。

Method: 复用预训练视频扩散模型作为潜在动作学习器：利用其丰富的时空特征提取紧凑潜在动作，将每帧潜在视频从 1200 个 token 压缩至 4 个（300×）；借助视频模型的生成先验，使潜在动作捕获与外观解耦的动作相关动态，形成跨具身（人/机器人）、跨环境、跨动作参数化的共享动作接口。

Result: 在 LARY-Bench 上取得潜在动作模型中的 SOTA；一种设定下编码的动作在另一设定下仍有效，潜在空间支持任意到任意视频迁移及跨多样具身与环境的零样本动作迁移；该共享接口还支持在紧凑潜在动作空间中学习策略，降低基于视频扩散策略的计算开销。

Conclusion: 预训练视频扩散模型的时空特征与生成先验可作为通用潜在动作的有效来源，得到紧凑、解耦且跨具身共享的动作表示，兼顾迁移能力与下游策略学习效率。

Abstract: Learning a universal action representation from video requires modeling dynamics that are consistent across different executions of the same action yet invariant to changes in embodiment and appearance. Existing latent action models infer actions from visual transitions, but their representations often entangle the underlying action with irrelevant visual variations, limiting transfer across embodiments. We introduce DreamX, which repurposes a pretrained video diffusion model as a universal latent action learner. Our approach extracts compact latent actions from the video model's rich spatiotemporal features, compressing each latent video frame by $\boldsymbol{300\times}$ ($\boldsymbol{1{,}200 \rightarrow 4}$ tokens). In addition, by leveraging the video model's generative prior, these latent actions capture action-relevant dynamics disentangled from visual appearance. The resulting latent space serves as a shared action interface across embodiments (human and robot), environments, and action parameterizations. On LARY-Bench, DreamX achieves state-of-the-art performance among latent action models. Moreover, actions encoded in one setting remain effective in another: the latent space supports any-to-any video transfer and zero-shot action transfer across diverse embodiments and environments. Furthermore, this shared interface also enables policy learning in a compact latent action space, reducing the computational cost of video-diffusion-based policies.

</details>


### [13] [Impact of Input Resolution in Mammographic Mass Detection: A Controlled Multi-Architecture Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04200)
*Kaan Buyukdemirci,Emine Ulku Saritas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在VinDr-Mammo上控制变量研究输入分辨率对乳腺肿块检测的影响，高分辨率主要提升小肿块检测，计算代价仍可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的肿块检测器通常对乳腺X线图像降采样以降低计算成本，但已发表研究因架构、预处理和评测协议不同，无法揭示该折中究竟损失多少精度，因此需隔离输入分辨率这一单一因素。

Method: 在VinDr-Mammo数据集上做控制实验：18个检测器×6种输入分辨率（512×416至1600×1280），共108个模型-分辨率组合；整幅图像缩放而不裁剪或切块；对每个模型固定预处理、初始化、增强与评测流程；以官方测试集219张肿块阳性图像的mAP50为指标，并分析mAP50≥0.60的57个组合。

Result: 分辨率由低到高，mAP50中位数由0.626升至0.660，最佳值由0.670升至0.709；最优为DINO+ConvNeXt-S骨干，为目前肿块阳性VinDr-Mammo图像上的最高mAP50。增益集中于小肿块：最小三分之一病灶的mAP50中位数提升0.074（0.367→0.441），中等与大肿块仅提升0.007与0.032。训练时间中位数由0.45h增至1.40h，单图推理时间由23.6ms增至113.7ms。

Conclusion: 提高输入分辨率主要改善最难检测的小肿块，且计算代价仍在实际可接受范围内，为临床检测器的分辨率选择提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based mass detectors usually downsample mammograms, trading fine image detail for lower computational cost. However, published studies cannot reveal how much accuracy this trade-off sacrifices, since they also differ in architecture, preprocessing, and evaluation protocol. In this work, we isolated the effect of input resolution in a controlled study on the VinDr-Mammo dataset, training 18 detectors at six input resolutions ranging from 512 x 416 to 1600 x 1280 pixels, for 108 model-resolution combinations. Whole mammograms were resized without cropping or tiling. For each model, preprocessing, model initialization, augmentation, and evaluation procedures were kept fixed across resolutions. Detection accuracy was evaluated by mAP50 on the 219 mass-positive images of the official test split, and 57 combinations with test mAP50 of at least 0.60 were analyzed. From the lowest to the highest resolution, median mAP50 rose from 0.626 to 0.660 and the best mAP50 from 0.670 to 0.709. The best result, achieved by DINO with a ConvNeXt-S backbone, is the highest mAP50 reported to date on mass-positive VinDr-Mammo images. Gains were concentrated in small masses: for the smallest third of lesions, median mAP50 rose by 0.074 (from 0.367 to 0.441), compared with 0.007 and 0.032 for medium and large masses. Median training time increased from 0.45 to 1.40 h, and median inference time from 23.6 to 113.7 ms per image. Therefore, higher input resolution mainly benefits the small masses that are hardest to detect, at a computational cost that remains practical.

</details>


### [14] [Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection with Embedding-Space Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04111)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOX提出“检测与解释解耦”的可解释AI生成图像取证框架：先训练并冻结一个DINOv3专家检测器，再用其表示引导轻量VLM生成文字解释，保证解释训练不改变判定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令驱动的AI编辑器对图像做局部重合成，不留拼接边界，使传统定位方法失效；多数检测器是黑箱、结论不可验证，且在检测分布上微调骨干会记忆易过时的生成器指纹，跨生成器泛化差。基于纯视觉表示（如DINOv3）的专家检测器泛化好，但现有可解释方法把分类与语言生成联合训练于同一多模态模型，使视觉表示受语言目标影响、丧失该优势。因此需要“解释训练不能改变判定”的约束。

Method: FOX采用两阶段解耦架构：(1)构建DINOv3专家检测器并将其锁定（冻结），作为唯一判定来源；(2)以该锁定表示引导一个轻量VLM进行取证解释——通过联合嵌入预测器（joint-embedding predictor）将锁定的视觉证据映射到冻结文本编码器的语义空间，生成引导信号，VLM按需将其语言化。

Result: 在14个未见生成器上平均AUC达97.4；XPlainVerse上BERTScore为69.8；LOKI上为56.1。

Conclusion: 将检测判定与解释生成在架构上解耦并冻结专家表示，可同时获得强跨生成器泛化与可验证的文本解释，且解释训练不会侵蚀检测性能。

Abstract: AI-generated images have reached unprecedented realism, while instruction-driven AI editors re-synthesize images for local changes, leaving no splice boundary to localize. Most detectors are black boxes with unverifiable verdicts and generalize poorly because adapting a backbone on the detection distribution memorizes generator-specific fingerprints that quickly become obsolete. Specialist detectors built on pure-vision representations, particularly DINOv3, generalize well across generators, but explainable methods forgo this advantage: jointly training one multimodal model for classification and language generation subjects its visual representation to the language objective. We therefore require that explanation training cannot alter the verdict. We introduce FOX, which first builds and locks a specialist DINOv3 detector, then uses its representation to guide a lightweight VLM toward forensic explanation. A joint-embedding predictor maps locked visual evidence into a frozen text encoder's semantic space, producing guidance that the VLM verbalizes on demand. FOX achieves 97.4 mean AUC across 14 unseen generators, 69.8 BERTScore on XPlainVerse, and 56.1 on LOKI.

</details>


### [15] [ScienceVision: Can AI Observe and Reason Like a Scientist?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04087)
*Tianyi Ma,Jing Gu,Haozhe Zhao,Liang Chen,Yunfan Zhang,Haijun Su,Jialong Wu,Le Xue,Arnav Das,Dustin Tran,Yinfei Yang,Andrew Dai,Kuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨领域科学3D理解与推理基准ScienceVision，含生物、化学、医学影像、机械CAD、建筑五域各50题（共250题），经合成生成与专家审校构题；在原生3D工件+代码执行、增加MCP查看/测量工具、以及规定2D渲染图三种设定下评测12个模型，最好平均得分0.53，且原生3D工件设定较2D渲染图设定平均高17.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现依赖对观测的准确解读与领域知识推理，科学3D数据需要理解结构与空间关系（如蛋白接触、解剖组织）。现有评测多集中于文本与2D图像，已有的科学3D评测在表示形式与协议上跨学科不一致，难以进行跨领域比较，因此需要统一的科学3D理解与推理评测基准。

Method: 构建ScienceVision：专家级视觉问答（VQA）跨域基准，含250题，生物、化学、医学影像、机械CAD、建筑各50题；采用合成题目生成结合领域专家审核与标注的流水线。每题包含原生3D工件、科学问题、指定回答格式与任务特定参考答案，需将领域知识应用于几何或拓扑证据。评测12个模型，设三种设定：(1) 通过智能体框架的文件与代码执行工具访问原生3D工件作答；(2) 在同一流程上附加经MCP暴露的专家式查看与测量工具；(3) 基于规定的2D渲染图作答。三设定均采用相同的任务特定确定性最终答案评分；检查环境支持视角变换、目标隔离与切片检查。

Result: 主对比中最佳平均答案得分为0.53（满分1）。在所有模型上，未附加MCP工具的原生3D工件流程得分均高于2D渲染图流程，平均高出17.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 科学3D理解与推理仍是高难度能力，现有模型整体表现有限；原生3D工件访问相比仅依赖预渲染2D图像更有利于发挥模型能力，表明表示形式与可交互检查手段显著影响评测表现，ScienceVision为跨学科3D能力比较提供了统一协议。

Abstract: Scientific discovery builds on accurate observations interpreted through domain knowledge and reasoning. For scientific 3D data, this requires understanding structures and spatial relationships, from protein contacts to anatomical organization. Existing multimodal benchmarks largely evaluate text and 2D images, while scientific 3D evaluations vary in representations and protocols across disciplines, limiting cross-disciplinary comparison. To assess these foundational capabilities, we introduce ScienceVision, a cross-domain benchmark for scientific 3D understanding and reasoning through expert-level visual question answering (VQA). We construct 250 questions, with 50 each from Biology, Chemistry, Medical Imaging, Mechanical CAD, and Architecture, through a pipeline combining synthetic question generation with domain-expert review and annotation. Each task pairs a native 3D artifact with a scientific question, a specified response format, and a task-specific reference answer, requiring domain knowledge to be applied to geometric or topological evidence. We evaluate 12 models on the same questions in three settings: visual question answering with native 3D artifacts accessed through an agent harness's file and code-execution tools; the same workflow augmented with expert-inspired viewer inspection and measurement tools exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP); and visual question answering from prescribed rendered 2D images using an agent harness. All settings use the same task-specific deterministic final-answer scoring. The inspection environment supports view changes, target isolation, and slice inspection. In this main comparison, the best mean answer score is 0.53 out of 1. Mean scores in the native-artifact workflow without additional MCP tools exceed those in the rendered-image workflow for every model, by 17.2 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [16] [Learned Smartphone ISP on Mobile GPUs: Mobile AI 2026 Challenge Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05008)
*Andrey Ignatov,Georgy Perevozchikov,Radu Timofte,Yuhong He,Yuxuan Qiu,Tianyu Hao,Ruoqi Li,Yueqi Yang,Chaoyu Feng,Dongqing Zou,Lei Lei,Na Jiang,Yongrui Zhang,Hailong Yan,Xiangtao Zhang,Ao Li,Le Zhang,Andrei Arhire,Jing Fang,XingLin Xie,YiChen Lu,Haibo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是Mobile AI挑战赛报告：要求参赛者在高通Adreno与Arm Mali手机GPU上，用高效深度学习模型从RAW Bayer数据重建RGB照片，优胜方案可在100ms内完成5MP重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手机相机画质日益重要，计算摄影与高级ISP算法需求上升；但此类模型既要性能好，又必须能部署在算力受限的移动硬件上，缺乏兼顾性能与效率的基准与方案。

Method: 组织Mobile AI挑战赛，提供大规模Fujifilm UltraISP数据集（Sony IMX586 Quad Bayer传感器采集的RAW与FujiFilm GFX100 102MP中画幅相机采集的RGB配对图像）；在Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5（Adreno GPU）与Dimensity 9500（Mali GPU）上评测各方案运行时间。

Result: 优胜方案能在两个平台上以低于100毫秒的延迟，从RAW数据重建高质量5MP RGB图像。

Conclusion: 本文系统总结挑战赛中开发的各类高效RAW-to-RGB重建模型，为移动端计算摄影提供可落地的轻量ISP方案参考。

Abstract: With the increasing importance of mobile camera quality in modern smartphones, growing attention has been directed toward computational photography and advanced image signal processing (ISP) algorithms aimed at enhancing various perceptual attributes of captured images. This creates a need for efficient deep learning models for this problem that are not only performant but can also be deployed on constrained mobile hardware. In this Mobile AI challenge, we address this problem and propose the participants to design fast solutions that can reconstruct RGB photos from RAW Bayer camera data while running on popular smartphone GPUs from Qualcomm and Arm. For this, the participants were provided with a large-scale Fujifilm UltraISP dataset consisting of RAW-RGB image pairs captured with the Sony IMX586 Quad Bayer mobile sensor and a professional 102MP medium format FujiFilm GFX100 camera. The runtime of all models was evaluated on the Snapdragon 8 Elite Gen 5 and Dimensity 9500 chipsets with the Adreno and Mali GPUs, respectively. Top solutions proposed in this challenge are able to reconstruct high-quality 5MP RGB image from raw data on both platforms under 100 milliseconds. A comprehensive description of the models developed in the challenge is provided in this paper.

</details>


### [17] [Polyp Matching Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04624)
*Kristian Dalsbø Hindberg,Puneet Sharma,Benedicte Schelde-Olesen,Ulrik Deding,Jakob Frederik Frøkjær Justsen,Jan-Matthias Braun,Esmaeil S. Nadimi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于孪生网络与对比学习的方法，用于结肠胶囊内镜中判断两幅图像是否为同一息肉，最佳分类准确率约92%，AUC接近0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结肠胶囊内镜（CCE）分析中，准确判断多张图像是否描述同一息肉是关键挑战：重复计数会导致过度诊断与不必要的临床干预，并使内镜医师在缺乏精确定位的情况下反复随访结肠镜寻找重复息肉。因此需自动化息肉匹配。

Method: 采用基于对比学习的孪生神经网络架构；数据来自丹麦CareForColon2015试验，由经验丰富的临床医生标注，包含5,103对确认同一息肉图像对与5,103对不同息肉图像对；主干网络为ResNet18/34/50、DenseNet121及EfficientNet B0/B3；使用对比学习并以余弦向量距离度量进行分类。

Result: 实验结果显示分类准确率最高达92%，AUC接近0.98。

Conclusion: 自动化息肉匹配有望减少息肉过度计数、改善患者风险分层并减少不必要的随访结肠镜检查。

Abstract: A key challenge in colon capsule endoscopy (CCE) analysis is accurately identifying whether multiple images depict the same polyp, which is critical to avoid over-counting and unnecessary clinical interventions. Additionally, this prevents prolonged follow-up colonoscopies where endoscopists search for duplicate polyps without precise localization. This study presents a novel contrastive learning-based approach to polyp matching using a Siamese neural network architecture. The model was trained on a dataset from the Danish CareForColon2015 trial. The dataset comprises 5,103 image pairs of confirmed same polyp and 5,103 pairs of different polyps, annotated by experienced clinicians. The proposed model employs ResNet variants (ResNet18, ResNet34, ResNet50), DenseNet121 and EfficientNet B0/B3 as backbones and utilizes contrastive learning with cosine vector distance metric for classification. Experimental results demonstrate high classification accuracies of up to 92% and AUC values close to 0.98. These results suggest that automated polyp matching has the potential to reduce polyp over-counting, improve patient risk stratification, and reduce unnecessary follow-up colonoscopies.

</details>


### [18] [FlowTranslator: Training-Free In-Image Machine Translation with Flow-Based Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04303)
*Zhixian Li,Xingsong Ye,Yongkun Du,Yunyan Xiao,Yanglin Huang,Pinxuan Chen,Jiyu Zhuang,Cong Xu,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的图像内机器翻译框架 FlowTranslator，将多语言文本生成能力从 T2I 模型迁移至 IIMT，通过区域耦合释放与耦合扩展两项轨迹控制提升译文渲染准确性与自然度，显著优于强训练基线 UniTranslator。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 IIMT 专用模型依赖大量翻译前后配对图像训练，成本高；而先进 T2I 模型已具备可实际使用的多语言文本生成能力，作者希望将这一能力迁移到 IIMT 任务上，从而摆脱对配对数据的依赖。

Method: 免训练框架：先用 OCR 模型与 LLM 生成翻译提示，并注入基于 T2I 的扩散模型；再引入两项轨迹控制——Region Coupling Release（RCR）在轨迹早期于文本区域内部分解耦目标分支噪声，Coupling Expansion（CE）在轨迹中期放大逐渐显现的编辑位移。

Result: 在 EasyOCR 回读协议下，PRIM 上 BLEU 与 COMET 平均分别为 20.6 和 57.2，大幅超过训练型强基线 UniTranslator；在更具挑战的真实整图 ScenePair 照片译为中文任务上，达到 40.1% 完全匹配准确率，超过现有编辑系统（其中部分仅处理文本渲染阶段而非完整 IIMT）。

Conclusion: 结果表明基于流（flow-based）的编辑可作为 IIMT 的一个强且完全免训练的基线方案。

Abstract: In-image machine translation (IIMT) replaces text in an image with its translation while preserving the surrounding visual context. Existing task-specific models achieve convincing performance but require massive paired training images before and after translation. As advanced text-to-image (T2I) models have reached a practically usable level of multilingual text generation, we argue that this capability can be transferred to IIMT and present FlowTranslator. It is a training-free IIMT framework that first employs an OCR model and an LLM to generate the translation prompt, which is injected into the T2I-based diffusion model. To optimize the accurate and natural generation of translated text, we introduce two trajectory controls: Region Coupling Release (RCR) partially decorrelates target-branch noise inside the text region early in the trajectory, while Coupling Expansion (CE) amplifies the emerging edit displacement mid-trajectory. Under the EasyOCR read-back protocol, FlowTranslator achieves average PRIM BLEU and COMET scores of 20.6 and 57.2, respectively, substantially outperforming the strong training-based baseline UniTranslator. On the more challenging and realistic whole-image ScenePair photographs translated into Chinese, it reaches 40.1\% exact-match accuracy and surpasses existing editing systems, some of which only address the text-rendering stage rather than the complete IIMT task. These results establish flow-based editing as a strong, fully training-free baseline for IIMT.

</details>


### [19] [FloMo: 3D Scene Flow as a World Action Model Intermediate for Learning from Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05062)
*Jeremy Collins,Namra Patel,Ayush Agarwal,Shitij Govil,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FloMo 是一个 5B 视频生成模型，微调后预测 3D 场景流与动作 token，论证"稠密 3D 运动"才是视频预训练迁移到机器人动作的最佳中介，比直接预测视频更具样本效率、泛化更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频预训练蕴含互联网级运动先验，但完整视频对动作预测是冗余信号。作者主张应让预训练以稠密 3D 运动（scene flow）为载体迁移到机器人动作，且预测运动比预测视频在动作预测上更样本高效。

Method: 将场景流渲染为 RGB 并编码到与视频相同的潜空间，使预训练视频生成骨干能高效适配为 3D 场景流预测器；模型同时预测 3D 场景流 token 与动作 token，并在无动作人类视频与带动作标注的机器人遥操作数据上联合训练。通过消融对比"运动目标"与"视频目标"以及二者联合预测时的注意力分布。

Result: 以视频预测替换运动预测会损害分布外泛化；在运动目标之外加入视频预测目标反而降低策略成功率；三者联合预测时，动作 token 对运动的注意力远高于对视频的注意力，说明预测视频浪费模型容量。模型可泛化到未见物体、运动基元与任务。

Conclusion: 运动是视频预训练向动作迁移的有效中介：仅预测稠密 3D 运动即可获得人类视频预训练收益并实现跨任务泛化。

Abstract: Pretrained video generation models distill internet-scale motion priors into robot policies, but full video is a redundant signal for action prediction. We argue that dense 3D motion is the medium through which video pretraining best transfers to robot actions, and that predicting motion rather than video affords better sample-efficiency for action prediction. FloMo is a 5B video-generation model fine-tuned to predict 3D scene-flow and action tokens. Rendering scene flow as RGB and encoding it into the same latent space as video enables a pretrained video generation backbone to efficiently adapt to predict 3D scene flow. Co-trained on action-free human video and action-labeled robot teleoperation data, our model benefits from human-video pretraining and generalizes to unseen objects, motion primitives, and tasks. Replacing motion prediction with a video prediction objective degrades out-of-distribution generalization, while adding a video prediction target alongside motion lowers policy success rather than improving it. When motion, video, and action are predicted jointly, action tokens attend far more to motion than to video, suggesting that predicting video wastes model capacity. Together, these results indicate that motion effectively mediates the transfer from video pretraining to action. More information can be found at flomo-wam.github.io.

</details>


### [20] [Camera Radar LiDAR Fusion via Point-Cloud Projection for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03942)
*Kavin Chandrasekaran,Sorin Grigorescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种早期融合架构，在透视视图下将相机图像与HD雷达、LiDAR点云融合，同时完成车辆检测与可行驶空间分割，在RADIal数据集上优于最新单模态与融合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知需在低光照与恶劣天气下保持鲁棒：相机分辨率高但环境适应性差，HD雷达可靠且角分辨率高但缺乏视觉特征识别，LiDAR精度高但成本高。各传感器存在独特权衡，需设计融合算法以互补优势、控制计算复杂度并保持精度。

Method: 采用早期融合（early fusion）策略，在透视视图（perspective view）下将相机图像与HD雷达及LiDAR点云数据结合，构建统一融合架构；模型以多任务方式训练，同时执行车辆检测与自由可行驶空间分割。

Result: 在RADIal数据集上的实验表明，前视图下点云与相机图像的融合性能超过当前最先进的单模态模型与融合模型。

Conclusion: 透视视图下的早期多传感器融合可有效整合相机纹理信息与雷达/LiDAR几何信息，在检测与分割任务上取得更优表现，验证了该融合范式的有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles require robust perception systems to operate safely under diverse environmental conditions. While cameras provide high-resolution visual data, their performance significantly degrades in low-light and adverse weather. High Definition (HD) radar offers superior reliability and angular resolution under such conditions but lacks rich visual feature recognition. LiDAR, though expensive, delivers highly precise distance measurements and dense point clouds. Each sensor exhibits unique trade-offs, necessitating the development of advanced sensor fusion algorithms to harness complementary strengths while minimizing computational complexity and maintaining accuracy. In this study, the proposed fusion architecture performs early fusion by combining camera images with perspective-view point cloud data from HD radar and LiDAR sensors. The proposed model is trained to detect vehicles and segment free drivable space simultaneously. Experiments are performed on the RADIal dataset where we demonstrate that fusing point cloud data with camera images in front-view surpasses the performances of the most recent standalone and fusion models.

</details>


### [21] [P-SER: Physical Consistency-Constrained Scattering Expandable Representation for Class-Incremental HRRP Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04254)
*Fangyi Wang,Peng Zhao,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理一致性约束的散射可扩展表征框架P-SER，用于无样本类增量HRRP识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRRP对视角敏感，方位变化使散射峰位置与幅度漂移，反射体组响应易与舰船混淆；历史数据不可用时类增量识别困难；现有动态扩展方法参数与内存开销大。

Method: 由复数域和通道、方位差通道HRRP及观测几何重建主散射点；用重建的空间结构、目标展宽与能量分布导出类级散射原型与样本级显著性先验，作为物理约束用于特征学习与旧类知识保持；构建基元散射体字典刻画局部强散射机制并在推理时校准舰船—反射体不确定预测；向共享HRRP骨干中增量引入轻量残差适配器。

Result: 在仿真与实测HRRP数据集上，不保留旧类样本即取得有竞争力的识别性能，并保持物理一致性、干扰鲁棒性、知识保持与模型可扩展性。

Conclusion: 以物理一致性约束与轻量残差适配器结合，可在无样本类增量场景下兼顾HRRP识别精度、抗干扰与模型增长控制。

Abstract: High-resolution range profiles (HRRPs) provide compact radar target representations but exhibit strong aspect sensitivity, complicating class-incremental recognition when historical data are unavailable. Changes in viewing aspect shift scattering-peak locations and amplitudes, while reflector groups may produce responses resembling ships. Existing dynamic expansion methods can also incur substantial parameter and memory overhead. To address these challenges, we propose P-SER, a Physical Consistency-Constrained Scattering Expandable Representation framework for exemplar-free class-incremental HRRP recognition. P-SER reconstructs dominant scattering points from complex-valued sum- and azimuth-difference-channel HRRPs and observation geometry. The reconstructed spatial structure, target extent, and energy distribution are used to derive class-level scattering prototypes and sample-level saliency priors, providing physically grounded constraints for feature learning and old-class knowledge retention. A primitive scatterer dictionary further characterizes local strong-scattering mechanisms and calibrates uncertain ship--reflector predictions at inference. Meanwhile, lightweight residual adapters are incrementally introduced into a shared HRRP backbone to learn new classes with limited model growth. Experiments on simulated and measured HRRP datasets show that P-SER delivers competitive recognition performance without retaining old-class exemplars, while maintaining physical consistency, interference robustness, knowledge retention, and model scalability.

</details>


### [22] [Heads That Write, Not Just Point: Image Retrieval Heads in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04903)
*Junsung Park,Sangwon Yu,Donghun Kang,Yeongtak Oh,Jaewon Jeong,Gyeongtae Yoo,Han Cheol Moon,Jungbeom Lee,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将 LVLM 多图问答中的"定位相关图像"步骤形式化为上下文内图像检索，提出 ICIR-MCQ 探针，用注意力质量识别"指针头"、用输出侧训练检索器识别"写入头"，发现两类头集合差异显著，且对多图推理最关键的头集中于二者的交集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 能在单一上下文中处理交错文本与多张图像，但其内部如何识别与问题相关的图像（grounding 步骤）尚不清楚；同时仅靠注意力只能说明"头从哪里读"，无法说明"头向后续层传递了什么"。

Method: 提出 ICIR-MCQ 受控探针用于头级分析；以每个头在图像 token 跨度上的注意力质量识别稀疏"指针头"（pointer heads）；引入输出侧分析，对每个头以其值加权输出训练轻量图像检索器，识别"写入头"（writer heads）；并进行针对性集合划分消融实验。

Result: 指针头可迁移至自然多图检索基准，单个指针头即可与外部视觉-语言检索器竞争；指针与写入两种准则选出的头集合差异显著，各自 top-100 中仅 35–47 个头重叠；集合划分消融显示对标准多图推理最重要的头集中于指针-写入交集，仅满足单一准则的头与随机头对照区分不明显；最一致的因果效应来自同时满足两种准则的头。

Conclusion: LVLM 内部存在强图像检索信号，但"读哪里"与"写出什么"由不同头群承担；只有在注意力指针与输出写入两种准则下同时被选中的头才具有稳定因果作用，建议头级功能分析应兼顾注意力与输出两个视角。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) can process interleaved text and multiple images in a single context, but how they internally identify the image relevant to a question remains poorly understood. We study this grounding step as in-context image retrieval and introduce ICIR-MCQ, a controlled probe for head-level analysis of image retrieval. Using each head's attention mass over image-token spans, we first identify sparse pointer heads that reliably point to the target image. These heads transfer beyond the probe: a single pointer head is competitive with external vision-language retrievers on a naturalistic multi-image retrieval benchmark, showing that LVLMs contain a strong internal image retrieval signal. Attention alone, however, shows where a head reads from, not what its output passes on to later layers, so we introduce a complementary output-side analysis. For each head, we train a lightweight image retriever on its value-weighted output and use it to identify writer heads, whose outputs contain enough information to identify the target image. Surprisingly, the pointer and writer criteria select substantially different head sets, with only 35 to 47 heads overlapping among the top-100 heads under each criterion. Targeted set-partition ablations show that the heads most important for standard multi-image inference are concentrated in this pointer-writer intersection, while heads selected by only one criterion show less consistent separation from random-head controls. The most consistent causal effects arise from heads selected by both the attention-based pointer and output-side writer criteria. Code and data: https://github.com/parkquasar/Image-Retrieval-Heads.

</details>


### [23] [What Does the Model See? Representation Validity in Multimodal LLM-Based Video Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05181)
*Christian Pipal,Eva-Maria Vogel,Morgan Wack*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"表征有效性"(representation validity)概念，系统比较文本、音频、视频及组合输入对多模态大模型测量与后续分析的影响，发现不存在普遍最优的表征，且高分类准确率并不保证下游估计更接近人工编码基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学家用多模态大模型规模化分析视频时，须先决定把视频的哪些部分作为输入，但不同输入表征如何影响测量效度及后续统计推断缺乏系统检验。

Method: 在6个开源权重MLLM与3个人工标注视频数据集上比较四种输入表征（文本、音频、视频、组合）；以macro-F1评估测量性能，并将其与基于人类编码的回归系数、样本流行率估计等下游量进行一致性对比。

Result: 无一致最优表征；7个新闻相关构念上，原生视频macro-F1为.70–.77且跨模型差异小，提取文本为.52–.80；3个模型用文本更优、3个用视频更优；组合输入很少带来提升。更高分类准确率并不可靠地产生更接近人工编码的估计：F1最高表征仅在42次比较中的17次最好地复现回归系数；即便用各模型F1最高的可部署表征，仍有45%的样本流行率估计与人工基准相差超过10个百分点。

Conclusion: 输入表征需针对所测构念与所估统计量（由标签推导）分别进行验证，不能仅凭分类F1选择表征。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) allow social scientists to analyze video at scale, but researchers must decide which parts of a video to provide as input. We use the term representation validity to examine how text, audio, video, and combined inputs affect measurement and subsequent analysis. We compare input representations across six open-weight MLLMs and three human-annotated video datasets. No representation consistently performs best. On seven news-related constructs, native-video performance varies little across models (macro-F1 = .70-.77), while extracted-text performance ranges from .52 to .80. Three models perform better with text and three with video. Combining inputs rarely improves performance. Higher classification accuracy also does not reliably produce estimates closer to those based on human codes. The highest-F1 representation best reproduces human-coded regression coefficients in 17 of 42 comparisons. Even when each model uses its highest-F1 deployable representation, 45% of sample-prevalence estimates differ from the human-coded benchmark by more than ten percentage points. Input representation therefore requires validation for both the construct being measured and the quantity estimated from the resulting labels.

</details>


### [24] [LINDA: Low Resource Foot Ulcer Segmentation using Local Similarity and Iterative Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04233)
*Trishaani Acharjee,Sudip Chakrabarty,Rajdeep Chatterjee,Tathagata Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LINDA：以局部自相似性（LSS）结构先验为引导的轻量伤口分割模型，在FUSeg 2021上取得最佳Dice、IoU与边界精度，且仅25.70M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足部溃疡是下肢截肢的主要诱因，负担最重的是专家与算力均稀缺的中低收入地区；此类场景需要既准确又能在普通设备上运行的小模型，但精度通常依赖重型网络，二者存在矛盾。

Method: 提出LINDA紧凑模型：将分割建立在显式结构先验而非原始强度上。直接从图像计算局部自相似性（LSS）图（对局部光照变化稳定），融合进ResNet-34编码器；再用小型Liquid Time-Constant模块经四步迭代精化边界。所有模型在FUSeg 2021上按统一协议训练。

Result: 对比中取得最佳Dice（86.68%）、IoU（79.67%）与边界精度（HD95 6.37像素），参数量仅25.70M，少于U-Net与Transformer基线。由于LSS图是输入的固定函数，模型所依据的结构线索以可读形式暴露。

Conclusion: LINDA在资源受限护理场景中提供了准确、可检查且轻量的伤口评估方案，兼顾精度、可解释性与部署可行性。

Abstract: Foot ulcers are a leading cause of lower limb amputation, and the highest burden often falls on low and middle income regions where specialists and computing hardware are both scarce. Wound monitoring tools are most useful there when they are accurate yet small enough to run on ordinary devices, but accuracy usually comes from heavy networks. We present LINDA, a compact model that grounds segmentation in an explicit structural prior instead of raw intensity. A Local Self-Similarity (LSS) map, computed directly from the image and stable under local lighting changes, is fused into a ResNet-34 encoder, and a small Liquid Time-Constant module refines the boundary over four iterative steps. With every model trained under one protocol on the FUSeg 2021 dataset, LINDA obtains the best Dice (86.68\%), IoU (79.67\%), and boundary accuracy (HD95 6.37 pixels) in our comparison while using only 25.70M parameters, fewer than the U-Net and transformer baselines. As the LSS map is a fixed function of the input, it exposes the structural cues the model follows in a readable form. Together this makes LINDA an accurate, inspectable, and lightweight option for wound assessment in resource-constrained care.

</details>


### [25] [ReconDreamer#: Self-Evolving Closed-Loop Synergy of Reconstruction and Generation for Dynamic Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04173)
*Songling Liu,Guosheng Zhao,Xiaofeng Wang,Chaojun Ni,Guan Huang,Jiwen Lu,Xingang Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReconDreamer#，一个自进化闭环框架，将重建与生成模型相互耦合迭代优化，缩小合成新轨迹数据与真实传感器观测之间的差距，并引入基于几何先验的自适应地面模型提升渲染质量，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态驾驶场景重建借助生成先验在新轨迹渲染质量上已有显著进展，但生成数据与真实传感器观测之间仍存在明显差距，严重制约重建保真度的进一步提升。

Method: 构建自进化的闭环框架，使生成模型与重建模型通过迭代细化相互增强，从而提升重建模型的 3D 表示能力；同时引入基于几何先验的自适应地面模型以改善渲染质量。

Result: 在 Waymo、nuScenes、PandaSet 与 EUVS 基准上进行全面评估；在 Waymo 6 米横向偏移设置下，相比 ReconDreamer++，NTA-IoU 与 NTL-IoU 分别提升 8.8% 和 3.0%，FID 降低 15.1%。

Conclusion: 通过重建与生成的协同自进化，ReconDreamer# 有效弥合了合成新轨迹数据与真实观测之间的鸿沟，显著增强了动态驾驶场景的重建保真度与 3D 表示能力。

Abstract: Dynamic driving scene reconstruction has made remarkable strides in recent years by leveraging generative priors, particularly in the quality of novel trajectory rendering. Nevertheless, a significant gap remains between the generated data and real-world sensor observations, severely hindering further improvements in reconstruction fidelity. In this paper, we present ReconDreamer#, a self-evolving framework that synergizes reconstruction and generation for dynamic driving scenes, which substantially bridges the gap between synthesized novel trajectory data and recorded real-world observations. Within this self-evolving closed-loop framework, the generative and reconstruction models mutually reinforce each other through iterative refinement, ultimately yielding a significant enhancement in the 3D representation capability of the reconstruction model. Additionally, we introduce a geometry-prior-based adaptive ground model to further enhance the rendering quality of the reconstruction model. To validate the effectiveness of the proposed method, we conduct comprehensive evaluations on Waymo, nuScenes, PandaSet, and EUVS benchmarks. Specifically on Waymo, at a 6-m lateral shift, ReconDreamer# improves NTA-IoU and NTL-IoU by 8.8% and 3.0%, respectively, and reduces FID by 15.1% relative to ReconDreamer++.

</details>


### [26] [NightIQA: A Comprehensive Dataset and Benchmark for MLLM-Based Nighttime Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04811)
*Yiding Tian,Jiaojiao Yi,Jiayuan Lu,Pufan Xu,Jinlin Wu,Changyuan Wen,Hongyuan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NightIQA数据集与基准套件（Real真实数据、Bench视觉问答基准、Syn可扩展合成管线），用于MLLM驱动的夜间图像质量评估，并证明领域专用数据与结构化监督的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间图像存在复杂复合退化，质量评估困难；基于分数的IQA将质量压缩为单一数值，缺乏多维度、语言化推理能力。MLLM虽有潜力，但存在关键数据缺口：现有真实IQA数据集以白天为主且拍摄陈旧，合成数据集缺乏夜间特有退化建模，伪影与真实夜间条件差距大。

Method: 构建NightIQA三部分：NightIQA-Real（自采1,769张高分辨率图像，覆盖六类典型失真并带结构化多维标注）；NightIQA-Bench（1,078道视觉接地多选题，按感知、技术评估、定位、推理四级评估体系组织）；NightIQA-Syn（场景感知的可扩展合成管线，联合建模全局级与实例级退化，首发8万张高保真图像及完整生成代码）。在NightIQA上以SFT+GRPO微调Qwen3-VL-8B。

Result: 经SFT与GRPO微调的Qwen3-VL-8B优于开源IQA方法，整体准确率超过领先闭源模型。

Conclusion: 领域专用数据与结构化监督对夜间IQA具有显著价值，NightIQA为MLLM夜间质量评估提供了数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Nighttime images are inherently susceptible to complex, compound degradations, making accurate quality assessment exceptionally challenging. Score-based IQA methods condense quality into a single number, lacking the multi-dimensional, language-grounded reasoning required for nighttime evaluation. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a more comprehensive alternative, yet remain constrained by a critical data gap: existing real-world IQA datasets are overwhelmingly daytime-centric and often built from outdated captures, while synthetic alternatives lack nighttime-specific degradation modeling, yielding artifacts far removed from authentic nighttime conditions. To bridge this gap, we present NightIQA, a dataset and benchmark suite purpose-built for MLLM-based nighttime IQA. It comprises three core components: NightIQA-Real, a real-world dataset of 1,769 high-resolution images that we captured ourselves with modern equipment, encompassing six canonical distortion types and structured multi-dimensional annotations; NightIQA-Bench, a benchmark of 1,078 visually grounded multiple-choice questions tailored to MLLM-based nighttime IQA, organized around a four-level evaluation hierarchy spanning Perception, Technical Assessment, Localization, and Reasoning; and NightIQA-Syn, an inherently scalable, scene-aware synthesis pipeline that jointly models global-level and instance-level degradations, with an initial release of 80k high-fidelity images alongside the full generation code. Fine-tuned on NightIQA with Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), Qwen3-VL-8B outperforms open-source IQA methods and achieves higher overall accuracy than leading closed-source models, highlighting the value of domain-specific data and structured supervision.

</details>


### [27] [Spatial and Temporal Super-Resolution and Denoising of Intracardiac 4D Flow MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04053)
*Yusuf A. Ozturk,Jie Zheng,Fanwei Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 4D-MRAR，一种结合残差卷积网络、循环网络与隐式神经场的深度学习方法，可对 4D Flow MRI 心脏内血流数据同时进行时空超分辨与去噪，并预测速度场与压力场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏内血流由复杂、时变的心脏运动与瓣膜相互作用驱动，蕴含疾病诊断与治疗规划的重要信息；但 4D Flow MRI 噪声大、时空分辨率有限，难以精确解析血流特征。既有深度学习方法多在静态血管区域上训练与推理，无法处理大形变心脏中的血流重建。

Method: 4D-MRAR 将残差卷积神经网络、循环神经网络与隐式神经场相结合，在 3D 空间与时间上同时进行超分辨，并输出高分辨率速度场与压力场；训练过程中通过物理约束高效地强制满足流体控制方程。模型在由左心室血流 CFD 仿真生成的合成数据集上训练。

Result: 方法可泛化至合成全心脏 4D Flow MRI 数据，并在真实 4D Flow MRI 上表现良好；相比已有方法，生成的超分辨流场精度显著提升，且与血流控制方程的一致性更强。

Conclusion: 4D-MRAR 证明了在运动大形变心脏场景下结合深度学习与物理约束进行 4D Flow MRI 时空超分辨与去噪的可行性，为心脏血流的精细量化分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Intracardiac flow is driven by complex, and time-varying cardiac motion and interaction with the heart valves, producing complex flow features that may provide valuable information for disease diagnosis and treatment planning. 4D Flow MRI enables non-invasive, time-resolved measurement of three-dimensional cardiovascular velocity fields. However, substantial noise and limited spatial and temporal resolution limit its ability to accurately resolve flow features. We propose 4D-MRAR (4D Magnetic Resonance Augmented Reconstruction), a deep learning (DL) method to super-resolve and denoise 4D Flow MRI data. Unlike prior DL methods that train and infer on static vascular regions, our method predicts intracardiac flow within hearts undergoing large deformation. Moreover, it uniquely allows for simultaneous super-resolution in 3D space and time. The method combines a residual convolutional neural network, a recurrent neural network, and an implicit neural field to predict high-resolution velocity and pressure fields while efficiently enforcing the governing equations of fluid flow during training. Trained on a synthetic dataset obtained from computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations of left ventricle (LV) flow, our method generalizes to synthetic whole-heart 4D flow MRI data, and demonstrated promising performance on real 4D Flow MRI. The produced super-resolved flow fields exhibit significantly improved accuracy and greater consistency with the governing equations of blood flow compared with prior methods.

</details>


### [28] [Coordinate Frame Recovery, Per Class NMS and Null Return Modelling for Sim2Real V2X LiDAR Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03940)
*Aman Upganlawar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge中V2X-Real赛道的参赛方案与关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需在仅用合成数据训练的条件下检测真实路侧Ouster OS1-64点云中的3D目标，并被要求合成点云具备分布真实性。

Method: 三项诊断式修正而非架构改动：(1) 修正预测与评测真值间的坐标系错配；(2) 按类别设置NMS阈值，避免行人被全局0.3 IoU阈值抑制；(3) 对以(0,0,0)编码的约31%空回波点建模。

Result: 最终综合得分0.7457，检测mAP 0.4382，真实性得分0.9251；坐标系修正使得分由0.3520升至0.5147，NMS修正使mAP由0.0751升至0.4295。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要来自对评测与数据格式细节的诊断，而非模型结构；论文同时等篇幅报告负结果，并说明数据来源与合规性。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the V2X-Real track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, in which a 3D object detector must be trained exclusively on synthetic data and evaluated on unseen real roadside LiDAR from an Ouster OS1-64 unit, while the synthetic point clouds are simultaneously scored for distributional realism. Our final entry scores 0.7457 combined, from a detection mAP of 0.4382 and a realism score of 0.9251. Three findings account for most of our result, and all three were diagnostic rather than architectural. First, a coordinate frame mismatch between our predictions and the evaluation ground truth was suppressing detection AP entirely; recovering it took our score from 0.3520 to 0.5147 in a single submission. Second, a single global non maximum suppression threshold was destroying genuine pedestrian detections, because pedestrians occur in groups and a 0.3 IoU threshold applied to a 0.7 m box suppresses distinct neighbouring people; correcting this per class raised overall mAP from 0.0751 to 0.4295. Third, approximately 31 percent of the real reference point cloud consists of null returns encoded as the literal point (0, 0, 0), a format feature entirely absent from our synthetic clouds, which pinned Earth Mover's Distance near its floor until we modelled it. Negative results are reported at equal length, and Section 9 gives a full account of data provenance and compliance. That section also accounts for every mechanism contributing to the submitted score.

</details>


### [29] [FlowBench: Counterfactual Imagination Training for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03937)
*Yifan Liu,Baihan Zhang,Khai Loong Aw,Klemen Kotar,Daniel L. K. Yamins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实空间想象（CSI）任务并发布FlowBench基准，同时用于训练与评测视觉语言模型与世界模型的运动结果推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能想象假设性运动下场景的变化，这一能力是空间推理与机器人规划的核心，但在视觉语言模型（VLM）与世界模型（world model）中尚未被充分研究，也缺乏标准化任务定义与数据资源。

Method: 形式化定义CSI任务；构建FlowBench，含428K训练样本，覆盖仿真与真实场景、家庭环境与桌面操作、多种题型；设计可扩展的全自动流水线，为每个假设运动配对空间问题，并在仿真中提供精确结果帧与光流作为监督。对VLM采用在FlowBench-Sim-Train上微调（如Qwen3-VL-8B），对世界模型采用文本到光流预测训练。

Result: 闭源VLM存在"想象鸿沟"：推断运动结果时表现劣于直接观测；开源模型接近随机水平。Qwen3-VL-8B微调后仿真准确率提升29.5个百分点，真实图像提升16.9，展现强sim-to-real迁移。世界模型方面，在FlowBench-Sim上训练使四类模型家族的文本到光流预测均提升，Psi-0.6由52.1%升至61.7%。

Conclusion: FlowBench既是CSI能力的评测基准，也是有效训练资源，能同时提升VLM与世界模型的假设运动结果推断能力；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Humans imagine scene changes under hypothetical motions, an ability central to spatial reasoning and robotic planning. However, this capability remains underexplored in vision-language models and world models. We formalize this capability as Counterfactual Spatial Imagination (CSI) and introduce FlowBench, a resource for training and evaluating both, with 428K training examples spanning simulated and real-world scenes, household environments and tabletop manipulation, and diverse question types. A scalable, fully automatic pipeline pairs each hypothetical motion with spatial questions and, in simulation, exact outcome frames and optical flow. For VLMs, proprietary models exhibit an imagination gap, performing worse when motion outcomes must be inferred than when observed, and open-source models perform near chance; fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B on FlowBench-Sim-Train raises its accuracy by 29.5 percentage points in simulation and 16.9 on real images, a strong sim-to-real transfer. For world models, training on FlowBench-Sim improves text-to-flow prediction for all four model families we test, raising the accuracy of Psi-0.6 from 52.1% to 61.7%. FlowBench thus serves as a training and evaluation resource for both. Code and datasets are released at https://github.com/liuyifan22/FlowBench.

</details>


### [30] [ScoreDial: Precise Control of Visual Attributes in Image Generation via Regressor Guidance from Limited Human Ratings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05004)
*Jianbo Tang,Keita Ishii,Hiroki Sakamoto,Manabu Kikuchi,Katsuma Kitamura,Chiho Semba,Kenta Nishiyama,Teppei Mori,Yukihiro Kiwaki,Takeo Kato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScoreDial 通过拟合可微回归器 R 并对采样过程施加误差自适应引导，使生成图像在有限评分条件下达到指定的人类评分尺度取值，并在四个属性上实现 R-MAE≤0.013、Spearman ρ=0.980 的请求值控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 请求值控制比连续引导更严格：给定人类评分尺度上的目标值 c，生成图像应仅凭有限评分就达到该值。现有方法难以在有限标注下精确对齐指定数值，因此需要一种能在采样中精确命中目标评分的方法。

Method: ScoreDial 先用 100–479 张人工评分图像拟合可微回归器 R；固定 R 后将请求值 c 定义在其输出尺度上，结合学习到的目标条件化与误差自适应引导，在采样中反复降低 |R(x)−c|。评估上区分"与 R 的一致性"和"匹配扫描"，并引入独立审计回归器（不同骨干、同批评分）与盲评人类判断。

Result: 在轮胎防滑性、室内视觉复杂度、食物食用欲望、肥皂半透明度四个属性上共用一套采样设置，ScoreDial 均达到请求值，R-MAE≤0.013（范围宽度 0.8），Spearman ρ=0.980（协议上限）。MPGD-flow 同样在 R 下达标，但 ScoreDial 在各属性上的审计位移更大，为对比方法中最大；更强 MPGD-flow 设置会牺牲目标一致性却无法缩小该差距。移除目标条件化则 R-MAE 低但审计位移减半以上，从而将精确分数校正与属性移动分离。每属性 15 名评分者的盲评显示，轮胎与肥皂的平均人工评分随 c 变化，线性 R²=0.665 与 0.786；在匹配扫描的强制选择中，评分者认为 ScoreDial 的递进更清晰的比例至少为 84%。

Conclusion: ScoreDial 在有限评分下实现了对请求人类评分数值的精确控制，但精确的 R 一致性主要反映分数校正而非属性层面的真实移动；审计与人类研究进一步揭示了 R 驱动采样带来的评估偏差，强调需以独立审计和人工判断来区分二者。

Abstract: Requested-value control is stricter than continuous steering: given $c$ on a human-rating scale, the generated image should attain that value from only limited ratings. We present ScoreDial, which fits a differentiable regressor $R$ to 100-479 human-rated images; once $R$ is fixed, a requested value $c$ is defined on its output scale, and learned target conditioning is paired with error-adaptive guidance that repeatedly reduces $|R(\mathbf{x})-c|$ during sampling. Across tire slip resistance, interior visual complexity, food desire-to-eat, and soap translucency, using one set of sampler settings shared across all four, ScoreDial reaches requested values and achieves, on all four attributes, $R$-MAE at most 0.013 over a range of width 0.8 and Spearman $ρ=0.980$, the maximum attainable under our evaluation protocol. Because $R$ also drives sampling, we evaluate agreement with $R$ separately from matched sweeps, an independent auditor fitted to the same ratings on a backbone none of the deployed regressors uses, and blinded human judgments. MPGD-flow also reaches the requested values under $R$, but ScoreDial has larger auditor displacement on every attribute, the largest among all compared methods, and a stronger MPGD-flow setting gives up target agreement without closing this gap. On every attribute, removing target conditioning leaves low $R$-MAE but more than halves auditor displacement, separating precise score correction from attribute movement. Blinded rating studies with 15 raters per attribute show that mean human ratings track $c$ on tire and soap, with linear $R^2=0.665$ and $R^2=0.786$, and in forced-choice comparisons of matched sweeps, raters chose ScoreDial's as the clearer progression in at least 84% of judgments against every baseline.

</details>


### [31] [How Well Does Webcam Eye Tracking Work "in the Wild"? A Large-Scale Validation of Chiasm and a Comparison with WebGazer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04946)
*Artem V. Belopolsky,Florian Perdreau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 大规模在线研究（N=505）评估第一代Chiasm网络摄像头眼动追踪器，其空间精度达2.39 cm（约2.28°），显著优于WebGazer，缩小了网络摄像头与硬件眼动仪之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络摄像头眼动追踪可在线招募大规模、多样化被试，大幅加速数据收集，但其空间性能被认为远逊于硬件系统，限制了应用。作者旨在系统评估第一代Chiasm追踪器在真实多样的被试、设备与记录条件下的空间表现。

Method: 开展大规模在线研究（N=505），涵盖多样化参与者、设备与记录条件；测量空间精度、空间精确度（SD）及逐样本精确度（RMS-S2S）；检验头部运动、人口学与生理因素（性别、年龄、居住地区、面部亮度、Michelson对比度、眼纵横比、佩戴眼镜）对精度的影响；并以相同协议将随机子样本（N=101）与开源WebGazer（N=101）对比。

Result: Chiasm平均空间精度2.39 cm（60 cm处约2.28°），空间精确度(SD)1.05 cm（约1.00°），RMS-S2S为1.29 cm（约1.23°）；满足3 cm校准标准的91%被试精度为2.15 cm（约2.05°）。3分钟诱发头部运动任务后精度下降0.59 cm，注视中心偏移仅0.37 cm。验证块中男性偏移比女性大0.30 cm；年龄、居住地区、面部亮度与对比度无可靠关联；眼纵横比越高（眼睛越睁开）偏移越小；佩戴眼镜在每个验证块均降低精度。与WebGazer相比，Chiasm空间精度更优（2.18 vs 4.51 cm）、空间精确度更优（SD 1.01 vs 1.55 cm），但WebGazer的RMS-S2S更低（0.79 vs 1.26 cm）。

Conclusion: Chiasm正缩小网络摄像头与硬件眼动追踪之间的差距，使"真实环境"中的大规模注视研究成为现实。WebGazer更低的RMS-S2S可能源于过度平滑的保守估计，而非更优稳定性。

Abstract: Webcam eye tracking can greatly accelerate data collection in basic and applied research by reaching large, diverse samples of participants online. However, its spatial performance is considered well below that of hardware-based systems, limiting its use. We evaluated the first-generation Chiasm webcam eye tracker in a large online study ($N = 505$) spanning diverse participants, devices, and recording conditions. Chiasm achieved a mean spatial accuracy of 2.39 cm ($\approx 2.28^\circ$ at 60 cm), spatial precision (SD) of 1.05 cm ($\approx 1.00^\circ$ at 60 cm), and sample-to-sample precision (RMS-S2S) of 1.29 cm ($\approx 1.23^\circ$ at 60 cm). For the 91% of participants who met a 3 cm calibration criterion, spatial accuracy was 2.15 cm ($\approx 2.05^\circ$ at 60 cm). After three minutes of a task that encouraged head movement, spatial accuracy declined by 0.59 cm, while the gaze center at each target shifted by a mean distance of only 0.37 cm. On the validation block, the offset was 0.30 cm larger for men than for women. Age, region of residence, face luminance, and Michelson contrast showed no reliable relationship with spatial accuracy. A higher eye aspect ratio, a more open eye, was associated with a smaller offset. Wearing glasses decreased spatial accuracy on every validation block. In a comparison between a random subsample ($N = 101$) and a separate sample tested with the open-source tracker WebGazer ($N = 101$) using an identical protocol, Chiasm showed better spatial accuracy (2.18 vs. 4.51 cm) and spatial precision (SD; 1.01 vs. 1.55 cm). WebGazer showed better sample-to-sample precision (RMS-S2S; 0.79 vs. 1.26 cm), which likely reflects conservative, over-smoothed estimates rather than superior stability. These results suggest that Chiasm is narrowing the gap between webcam and hardware-based eye tracking, making large-scale gaze research "in the wild" a reality.

</details>


### [32] [HTNN-MSC: Multi-view Subspace Clustering with a Hyperbolic Tangent Tensor Low-Rank Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04940)
*Yiwen Dou,Yiqiang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HTNN-MSC，用有界双曲正切张量秩代理替代t-SVD框架下的张量核范数，缓解对大奇异值的过度收缩，并给出ADMM求解与KL收敛性保证，在23个数据集上取得0.8404平均聚类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图子空间聚类中t-SVD所用的张量核范数对不同大小奇异值施加相同的渐近边际惩罚，会过度收缩编码簇结构的主成分，削弱聚类判别信息。

Method: 以正半轴严格凹、导数Lipschitz连续的有界双曲正切函数作为张量秩代理，其边际惩罚随奇异值增大而递减；设计ADMM求解器，使张量子问题分解为标量频域收缩问题，通过带保护的multi-start牛顿迭代与候选目标比较实现双曲正切阈值算子，形状参数按输入谱缩放；证明增广目标的o-minimal可定义性，并在有界性、下降、相对误差与精确块条件成立下由KL框架保证全序列收敛到KKT点。

Result: 在23个数据集上平均聚类精度0.8404、平均精度排名2.78（对比九种代表性方法）；Friedman检验与Holm校正Wilcoxon检验显示总体差异显著但部分成对差异不显著；相对凸的t-SVD-MSC骨架取得19胜1平3负；同骨架消融显示该代理并未一致优于其他非凸惩罚。

Conclusion: 有界双曲正切代理为t-SVD多视图聚类提供了有理论收敛保障的非凸替代方案，整体性能优于凸正则基线，但其相对其他非凸惩罚的优势并非普遍成立，需按场景选择。

Abstract: High-order dependencies among views can be modeled by tensor singular value decomposition (t-SVD) in multi-view subspace clustering. However, the tensor nuclear norm often used with this framework assigns the same asymptotic marginal penalty to singular values of different magnitudes, which may excessively shrink dominant components that encode cluster structure. We propose HTNN-MSC, which replaces this norm with a bounded hyperbolic tangent tensor rank surrogate. The surrogate is strictly concave on the positive half-axis and has a Lipschitz continuous derivative. Its marginal penalty decreases as singular values grow. We develop an alternating direction method of multipliers solver in which the tensor subproblem decomposes into scalar frequency-domain shrinkage problems. Safeguarded multi-start Newton iterations and candidate-objective comparison implement the resulting hyperbolic tangent thresholding operator. The shape parameter is scaled by the input spectrum. We also establish o-minimal definability of the augmented objective. Under explicit boundedness, descent, relative-error, and exact-block conditions, the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz framework guarantees convergence of the full sequence to a KKT point. On 23 datasets, HTNN-MSC achieves a mean clustering accuracy of 0.8404 and an average accuracy rank of 2.78 against nine representative methods. Friedman tests and Holm-adjusted Wilcoxon comparisons reveal significant overall differences, although several pairwise differences remain nonsignificant. HTNN-MSC records 19 wins, one tie, and three losses against the convex t-SVD-MSC skeleton. A same-skeleton ablation also shows that the proposed surrogate does not uniformly outperform alternative nonconvex penalties.

</details>


### [33] [OCR in the Foundation Model Era: From Specialist Models to Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04874)
*Zhiheng Li,Zongyang Ma,Jiaxian Chen,Cheng Cui,Yi Liu,Ziqi Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Bing Li,Xiang Bai,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述论文聚焦"基础模型时代的OCR"，提出以"忠实转录"定义OCR，梳理从专用模型到统一模型的演进，将当代OCR系统归为五类并比较其架构、数据与训练，剖析生成式OCR的幻觉、遗漏、空间定位不足与推理成本问题，并综述评测现状与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR是最古老、应用最广的AI任务之一，但进入基础模型时代后，任务定义模糊、模型范式多样（专用模型与通用VLM并存），且生成式方法带来幻觉、遗漏等新问题。论文希望通过明确任务定义、系统梳理范式演进并统一评测视角，为该领域提供结构化认知与研究指引。

Method: 综述方法：首先给出基于"忠实转录"的OCR形式化定义（输出须可由图像视觉证据验证，完整重构文本、符号与文档结构）；随后追溯从专用OCR模型到统一模型的发展，考察文本检测与识别联合建模、结构化序列生成及预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的运用；再将当代系统划分为五个并存家族（模块化工具包与任务专用模型、专用文档VLM、混合工作流、通用VLM、商业产品与托管服务）；最后从架构、数据、训练三方面横向比较，并梳理评测体系。

Result: 论文得出若干分析与归纳性发现：(1) 提出五类OCR系统的分类框架并完成架构、数据与训练的对比；(2) 识别生成式OCR的核心缺陷——幻觉与遗漏、空间定位（spatial grounding）不足、推理成本高；(3) 梳理评测演进路径，从字符级与场景文本识别，到单个结构元素解析，再到整页转录；(4) 指出现有评测的三类空白——输出定义不一致、性能难以归因到具体组件、对真实拍摄条件及可见内容与结构的覆盖不足。

Conclusion: 论文总结基础模型时代OCR面临的主要挑战，并给出未来研究方向，同时通过GitHub仓库持续追踪相关工作。其核心主张是以可视觉验证的忠实转录作为统一评价基准，以缓解范式碎片化与生成式模型的可靠性问题。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) is among the oldest and most widely deployed applications of artificial intelligence. This survey reviews its development, focusing on the Foundation Model Era. We define OCR through faithful transcription: within a specified visual scope, OCR should completely reconstruct the text, notation, and document structure observable in the source image, and every textual, notational, or structural element in the output must be verifiable from visual evidence in that image. We trace the development from specialist OCR models toward unified models, examining joint text detection and recognition, structured sequence generation, and the use of pretrained vision-language models (VLMs). We organize contemporary OCR systems into five coexisting families: modular toolkits and task-specific models, specialized document VLMs, hybrid workflows, general-purpose VLMs, and commercial products and managed services. We compare their architectures, data, and training, examining hallucination and omission, insufficient spatial grounding, and inference cost in generative OCR. We review evaluation from character and scene text recognition to parsing individual structural elements and full-page transcription, highlighting gaps in consistent output definitions, attribution of performance to individual components, and coverage of real capture conditions and visible content and structure. Finally, we summarize the challenges facing OCR and discuss directions for future research. We continuously track related work at https://github.com/zhihengli-casia/Awesome-OCR-in-the-Foundation-Model-Era.

</details>


### [34] [Bounded AUKS: A Novel Evaluation Metric for Anomaly Detection via Bounded Area Under the Kolmogorov--Smirnov Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04754)
*Yanshuo Zhang,Juan Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出AUROC/AUPRO趋近100%造成"像素级异常检测已解决"的误导，且阈值无关指标忽视阈值选择、极端低FPR会抬高漏检率；为此提出有界AUKS（Bounded AUKS），在数据驱动界定的区间内积分TPR与FPR之差，直接度量正常与异常得分分布的可分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局指标（AUROC、AUPRO）在MVTec AD、VisA等数据集上接近饱和（趋近100%），误导性地显示任务已被解决；同时阈值无关指标天然回避了实际应用中不可避免的阈值选择过程，AUPRO虽限制FPR范围但极低FPR会导致FNR上升。核心问题在于缺乏能刻画正常与异常样本得分分布分离程度的指标，从而阻碍异常检测的发展。

Method: 从KS积分出发引入AUKS，即对TPR与FPR之差做部分积分，作为得分分布可分性的直接度量；并构造有界版本Bounded AUKS：积分区间（界）仅由正常测试样本的得分分布确定，下界排除无信息的高FPR区域，上界避免在强行把FPR压向零、导致召回极低的极端区域评估。

Result: 在MVTec AD与VisA两个数据集上对9个模型进行实验，结果表明Bounded AUKS能够弥补现有指标的局限，在评估无监督异常检测模型时表现更优。

Conclusion: Bounded AUKS是一种有界部分积分式评价指标，通过数据自适应区间聚焦于有实际意义的ROC区域，能够更合理地量化正常/异常得分分布的可分性，为无监督异常检测提供更可靠的模型评估依据。

Abstract: Recent literature widely used global metrics such as AUROC and AUPRO to quantify the detection performance. AUROC and AUPRO scores are approaching 100% in some public datasets such as MVTec AD dataset and VisA dataset, which misleadingly suggests that the pixel-level anomaly detection task has already been solved. Threshold-independent metrics represented by AUROC inherently ignore the threshold selection process, which is inevitable in practical applications. Although AUPRO restricts the False Positive rates (FPRs) to an acceptable range, extremely low FPRs can lead to increased false negative rates (FNRs). We argue that there is a lack of metrics capable of quantifying the separation between the anomaly score distributions of normal and anomalous samples, which hinders advancements in anomaly detection. In response, we propose the Bounded Area Under the Kolmogorov--Smirnov curve (Bounded AUKS), a partial integral metric that integrates the difference between TPR and FPR within a data-driven bounded range. Specifically, we introduce AUKS from KS integral as a direct measure of separability between the anomaly score distributions.
