The bounded range is determined only by score distributions of normal test samples. Lower bound excludes the uninformative high-FPR region. Upper bound avoids evaluation in extreme regions that aggressively push FPR towards zero, which result in a very low recall. Our experiments on 9 models across MVTec AD and VisA datasets show that Bounded AUKS can address the limitations of existing metrics and has a better performance in evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection models.

</details>


### [35] [VISAGE: Vertex-based Intrinsic Surface Appearance GEneration via Dual-Stream Diffusion for Arbitrary 3D Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04712)
*Jeremias Krauß,Pragati Jaiswal,René Schuster,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISAGE：基于顶点的条件扩散框架，直接在任意网格拓扑上生成逐顶点albedo与roughness，无需UV参数化，用可见性感知交叉注意力融合多视图RGB，实现身份忠实的物理表面外观重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏图像恢复3D人头身份忠实、物理正确的表面外观仍具挑战：现有UV流水线产生接缝伪影，图像空间方法牺牲表面一致性，且训练依赖一致UV参数化。

Method: 表面条件扩散框架，直接在网格顶点上合成albedo与roughness；全局谱处理结合局部测地KNN细化以恢复截断谱基之外的细节；多视图RGB经可见性感知交叉注意力进行条件化，实现遮挡鲁棒的全表面合成；眼睛、嘴、皮肤、头发等区域专用模块；切空间输出投影恢复虹膜细节；并提出可扩展的程序化增强流水线，为原始几何扫描生成物理合理的albedo、roughness与法线图用于训练。预测属性直接渲染为单一RGBA顶点色通道。

Result: 相对最强基线，NPHM上FID降低42.7%，零样本FaceVerse与i3DMM分别降低22.5%与4.4%；核心网络仅7.2M参数。

Conclusion: 顶点级表面扩散可在无需UV参数化的情况下生成拓扑无关、遮挡鲁棒且细节丰富的人头物理外观，兼具高保真与轻量化。

Abstract: Recovering identity-faithful, physically based surface appearance for 3D human heads from sparse images remains challenging. Existing methods often rely on UV-based pipelines, introducing seam artefacts, or operate in image space, limiting surface consistency. We present VISAGE, Vertex-based Intrinsic Surface Appearance GEneration, a surface-based conditional diffusion framework that synthesises per-vertex albedo and roughness directly on arbitrary mesh topologies, without requiring consistent UV parameterisation at training time. VISAGE combines global spectral processing with local geodesic K-Nearest-Neighbour refinement, enforcing structural coherence while restoring detail beyond the truncated spectral basis. Multi-view RGB images condition the network via visibility-aware cross-attention, enabling occlusion-robust synthesis across the full head surface. Region-specific modules specialise in semantically demanding and distinct areas (eyes, mouth, skin and hair), while a tangent-space output projection recovers fine iris detail. We further introduce a scalable procedural augmentation pipeline that enriches raw geometry scans with physically plausible albedo, roughness and normal maps for training. The predicted attributes render directly as a single RGBA vertex-colour channel. VISAGE reduces FID by 42.7% on NPHM and 22.5% and 4.4% on zero-shot FaceVerse and i3DMM, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline, with a 7.2M-parameter core network.

</details>


### [36] [ReXMo: End-to-End Video Character Replacement with Expression and Motion Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04633)
*Hualiang Wei,Wenhui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReXMo端到端视频角色替换框架，支持单人多人和选择性替换，通过三重配对监督与In-Context DiT实现身份转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频角色替换方法依赖带姿态引导的掩码生成，在复杂场景中难以细粒度保留表情与动作。

Method: 1) 构建配对三重监督：合成驱动视频与原视频共享相同表演但身份不同，实现可扩展的合成数据训练；2) 采用In-Context Diffusion Transformer，以驱动视频、参考身份和文本提示联合条件建模身份变换；3) 引入Expression Enhancement Adapter增强多人场景下的表情保持。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReXMo在身份保真度、运动一致性、表情保持和视觉质量上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ReXMo为统一支持单/多/选择性角色替换的端到端方案，数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: Video character replacement aims to replace actors while preserving facial expressions, motions, and scene dynamics. Existing methods typically rely on masked generation with pose guidance, limiting fine-grained expression and motion preservation in complex scenes. We propose ReXMo, an end-to-end framework that supports single-, multi-, and selective character replacement scenarios. ReXMo introduces paired triplet supervision, where synthesized driving videos and original videos share identical performances but different identities, enabling scalable training through synthetic data generation. An In-Context Diffusion Transformer learns identity transformation by jointly conditioning on driving videos, reference identities, and text prompts, while an Expression Enhancement Adapter improves facial expression preservation in multi-person scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReXMo outperforms existing methods in identity fidelity, motion consistency, expression preservation, and visual quality. Our dataset and code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [37] [SEEK-VAU: Towards Evidence-Faithful Video Anomaly Understanding via Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04554)
*Minghe Wang,Weijun Zeng,Dapeng Luo,Xinyue Wang,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SEEK-VAU，将视频异常理解(VAU)重构为有界搜索预算下的智能体"搜索—验证"交互协议，让策略主动在前兆、触发、后果三阶段搜集证据并验证充分性；配合证据忠实反事实验证(EFCV)奖励与SEEK-Bench基准，实现可主动核验、证据忠实的VAU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAU系统多为前馈式：在预定义规则下从采样帧或由其聚类的事件中一次性收集证据后推理。当预定义规则不完美、证据不完整时，一旦开始推理模型便无法再搜寻缺失证据，导致判断失效。作者希望把"事件链完整性"从规则的副产物变成显式设计目标。

Method: 1) SEEK-VAU协议：将VAU转为agentic search-and-verify交互，策略在异常事件链三阶段(前兆、触发、后果)主动取证，并在定稿前验证证据充分性；搜索受有界预算约束以控制成本。2) EFCV(证据忠实反事实验证)：通过反事实检验对"仍具支持性、紧凑且必要"的证据给予奖励，使"看哪里、何时停"的行为可学习。3) SEEK-Bench：视频级episode，含时间区间标注、语义QA与事件链阶段标签。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化指标；贡献为构建了SEEK-VAU方法、EFCV奖励机制与SEEK-Bench基准三件套，形成证据忠实且可主动核验的VAU基础框架。

Conclusion: 通过将VAU由被动前馈推理转为有界的主动搜索—验证，并以证据忠实反事实奖励引导搜索与停止决策，再辅以细粒度事件链基准，作者为可核验的视频异常理解建立了新范式与评测基础。

Abstract: Most video anomaly understanding (VAU) systems still infer anomaly judgments from evidence collected in a feed-forward way under predefined rules, typically sampled frames, or events clustered from such frames. This protocol fails when the collected evidence is incomplete under imperfect predefined rules, because once reasoning begins the model can no longer search for the missing evidence. We address this limitation with SEEK-VAU, which reframes VAU as an agentic search-and-verify interaction protocol under a bounded search budget: instead of passively reasoning over pre-collected evidence, the policy actively gathers evidence across the three stages of an anomaly event chain (precursor, trigger, and aftermath) and verifies its sufficiency before finalization, turning event-chain completeness into an explicit design rather than an outcome of predefined rules. A bounded budget caps the cost of this search, but does not by itself teach the policy where to look or when to stop. To make this behavior learnable, we introduce evidence-faithful counterfactual verification (EFCV), which rewards selected evidence that remains supportive, compact, and necessary under counterfactual verification. We further introduce SEEK-Bench, featuring video-level episodes with temporal interval annotations, semantic QA and event-chain stage labels. Together, SEEK-VAU and SEEK-Bench establish a strong foundation for evidence-faithful and actively verifiable VAU.

</details>


### [38] [E2I-Flow: EEG-to-Image Reconstruction via Wavelet-Temporal Cross-Attention and Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04524)
*Haowei Pan,Hwa Hui Tew,Junn Yong Loo,Fuad Noman,Hernando Ombao,Chee-Ming Ting*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出E2I-Flow框架，结合时频表征学习的EEG编码器与条件流匹配，实现零样本EEG到图像检索与重建，性能优于多个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG视觉解码与重建面临两大挑战：EEG信号具有复杂的时频特性；传统离散去噪框架难以将神经活动映射为有意义的视觉表征。

Method: E2I-Flow包含两部分：(1) EEG编码器，由平稳小波频域分支与时间混合分支构成，通过双向交叉注意力与自适应融合整合，并用对比损失和MSE损失将EEG表征与真实图像嵌入对齐；(2) 以对齐后的EEG表征为条件，驱动基于Transformer的流匹配模型预测视觉嵌入以合成图像。

Result: 实验显示强零样本EEG到图像检索能力，视觉重建性能提升，优于多个先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 将时频联合EEG编码与条件流匹配相结合，可有效缓解EEG时空频复杂性与跨模态映射难题，为无创脑信号视觉解码提供可行方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG)-based visual decoding and reconstruction aims to recover visual information from non-invasive brain signals. But there are challenges due to the complex temporal-frequency characteristics of EEG signals and the difficulty of discrete denoising framework in mapping neural activity to meaningful visual representations. Here, we propose E2I-Flow, an EEG-to-image decoding and reconstruction framework that combines a novel EEG encoder for time-frequency representation learning from EEG features, and conditional flow matching for visual decoding. A stationary-wavelet frequency branch and a temporal-mixing branch are integrated through bidirectional cross-attention and adaptive fusion, with the resulting EEG representations aligned to ground-truth image embeddings using contrastive and mean-squared-error loss supervisions. The aligned EEG representation is then used as a condition for a Transformer-based flow-matching model to predict the corresponding visual embedding for image synthesis. Experimental results show strong zero-shot EEG-to-image retrieval and improved visual reconstruction performance, outperforming several state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [39] [3DGS-VBench+ and 3DGSV-Assessor: A Multi-Dimensional Subjective Benchmark and a Reference-Flexible Objective Model for 3D Gaussian Splatting Video Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04498)
*Yuke Xing,William Gordon,Jiarui Wang,Yujie Zhang,Guangtao Zhai,Yiling Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DGS视频质量评价基准3DGS-VBench+与参考灵活的评估框架3DGSV-Assessor，实现3DGS合成视频的整体、几何、颜色三维度质量评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS存储开销大需压缩，训练与压缩均引入表征相关失真；多方法对几何与颜色属性采用不同参数进行生成式压缩，易产生维度特定失真，故需多维度评价。同时真实采集缺乏与连续相机轨迹对齐的参考视频，仅有稀疏参考视图，使对齐参考稀缺下的质量评价成为难题。

Method: 构建3DGS-VBench+：3,036个3DGS重建及对应渲染视频，覆盖11个真实场景与22个合成场景、6种代表性压缩算法按系统化等级生成，并标注9,108个MOS，涵盖整体、几何、颜色质量。提出3DGSV-Assessor：参考灵活的大规模多模态框架，单次生成同时输出三维度分数，通过有界门控将基于排序的参考线索融合进冻结回归器，支持RR与NR两种模式。

Result: 在3DGS-VBench+上与人类感知相关性达到SOTA，并对其他NVS基准展现强零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 面向3DGS视频的多维质量评价可行且有效：大规模主观基准支撑细粒度失真分析，参考灵活的融合机制在稀疏或缺失对齐参考条件下仍能准确预测质量，为3DGS压缩与重建的质量优化提供评测基础。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis (NVS), but its heavy storage makes compression essential, and both training and compression introduce representation-specific distortions. Since generative compression in many methods treats geometric and color attributes via different parameters, it can induce dimension-specific distortions, motivating multi-dimensional assessment. Moreover, real-world captures generally lack an aligned ground-truth video for a synthesized, continuous camera trajectory, leaving only sparse captured views as references. To enable multi-dimensional quality assessment of 3DGS videos with scarce aligned references, we first present 3DGS-VBench+, a large-scale subjective video quality assessment (VQA) benchmark comprising 3{,}036 3DGS reconstructions, each paired with a rendered video, across 11 real-world and 22 synthetic scenes, generated by 6 representative compression algorithms at systematically designed levels and annotated with 9{,}108 MOSs covering overall, geometry, and color quality. Based on 3DGS-VBench+, we propose 3DGSV-Assessor, a reference-flexible, large multimodal framework that scores all three dimensions in one generation pass and fuses a ranking-based reference cue into the frozen regressor through a bounded gate, supporting both reduced-reference (RR) and no-reference (NR) assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art correlation with human perception on 3DGS-VBench+ and strong zero-shot generalization to other NVS benchmarks. The dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/YukeXing/3DGSV-Assessor.

</details>


### [40] [Is Forgetting Unified? Exploring Concept Unlearning in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04467)
*Zhongkai Xue,Junkai Chen,Mingcong Lei,Rumo Zhang,Yixiao Liu,Zhimin Sun,Shu Wu,Qiang Liu,Lihua Zhang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可诊断统一多模态模型跨通路概念遗忘的基准 UUBench（30个目标概念、60个语义近邻概念），并指出单通路遗忘方法联合遗忘效果有限；进而提出 UniForget，在 BAGEL-7B-MoT 与 Janus-Pro-7B 上分别实现 80.0% 与 92.7% 的联合遗忘率，并揭示联合遗忘与能力保持之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型同时具备图像生成与视觉理解能力，但现有遗忘方法多为单通路设计。作者质疑其能否在两条通路上同时抑制目标概念，即能否避免模型既生成目标概念图像、又在输入图像中识别该概念，同时保持其他能力，由此界定"跨通路概念遗忘"问题。

Method: 构建诊断基准 UUBench（30 个目标概念 + 60 个语义相关近邻概念），用于评估生成与理解两条通路上的联合遗忘；在代表性 UMM 上系统评测生成侧编辑方法、理解侧基线及其直接组合；并据此提出 UniForget 方法以实现跨通路联合遗忘。

Result: 单通路方法联合遗忘能力有限：生成侧编辑器能强效抑制目标生成，但视觉理解侧遗忘甚微；理解侧基线即便在自身通路也难以实现实质遗忘；二者直接组合效果同样有限。UniForget 在 BAGEL-7B-MoT 上联合遗忘率达 80.0%，在 Janus-Pro-7B 上达 92.7%，优于代表性强基线。

Conclusion: 单一通路的遗忘方法不足以实现跨通路的联合概念遗忘，需针对统一多模态模型设计专门的联合遗忘方案；UniForget 有效提升联合遗忘率，但进一步分析表明联合遗忘与模型能力保持之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) integrate image generation and visual understanding, but are existing single-pathway unlearning methods sufficient to suppress target concepts across both? In this work, we study cross-pathway concept unlearning. Our goal is to prevent a model from both generating images of a target concept and identifying it in input images, while preserving other capabilities. To this end, we introduce UUBench, a diagnostic benchmark covering 30 target concepts and 60 semantically related neighboring concepts. Experiments on representative UMMs show that the evaluated single-pathway methods achieve limited joint forgetting: generation-side editors strongly suppress target generation but yield limited forgetting in visual understanding, while understanding-side baselines struggle to achieve substantial forgetting even in their own pathway. Their direct composition also yields limited joint forgetting. We therefore propose UniForget, achieving joint forgetting rates of 80.0% on BAGEL-7B-MoT and 92.7% on Janus-Pro-7B, outperforming representative baselines. Further analysis reveals trade-offs between joint forgetting and capability preservation.

</details>


### [41] [Auditing Targetless Camera-LiDAR Extrinsic Correction before Recursive Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04397)
*Bo Ma,Weiqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究无目标外参校正是否应仅在分别通过"校正质量、置信度排序、递归风险"三项审计后才进入下游消费者；日志不相交协议下结果显示旋转改善不等于六自由度校正成功，且未建立可部署的标定门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机-LiDAR融合在投影、关联与递归状态估计中反复使用外参，错误外参会沿流程传播；作者希望厘清无目标外参校正的质量、置信度与递归风险是否应分开审计，而非把单次校正收益直接当作下游可用性。

Method: 采用日志不相交(log-disjoint)协议，在Argoverse 2上注入合成外参漂移；分阶段评估单帧Canny对齐、多帧对齐的校正质量，置信度/风险排序（求解器分歧AUROC、风险-覆盖率），以及递归状态估计中的风险；跨数据集与里程计实验仅作有界诊断；最后以15条日志测试固定保守策略的系统角色。

Result: 27条测试日志上：轻度漂移下单帧Canny对齐有害；多帧对齐在严重档改善旋转2.52°，但平移恶化0.177 m，联合误差幅值改善为负(-0.158，95% CI [-0.223, -0.098])；零注入检验偏离真实外参；所选划分上的求解器分歧AUROC未能给出经验证的非零覆盖率风险-覆盖工作点。15条日志最终系统中，锁定保守策略不接纳任何候选、等同Raw，Always-correct反而增大轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 结果支持将校正、置信度与递归污染分开审计的可审计框架，但不支持宣称可部署的标定门控、里程计/SLAM正收益、地图一致性或闭环安全性。

Abstract: Camera-LiDAR fusion repeatedly uses an extrinsic transform in projection, association, and recursive state estimation. We study whether a targetless correction should be admitted to a downstream consumer only after separate audits of correction quality, confidence ranking, and recursive risk. Our log-disjoint protocol evaluates these stages under synthetic extrinsic drift on Argoverse 2 and treats cross-dataset and odometry experiments as bounded diagnostics.
  On 27 test-split logs, single-frame Canny alignment is harmful in the mild tier. Multi-frame alignment improves severe-tier rotation by 2.52 degrees but worsens translation by 0.177 m; its declared joint error-magnitude improvement is also negative (-0.158, 95% CI [-0.223, -0.098]), so the rotation gain is not a six-degree-of-freedom correction success. A zero-injection check moves away from the true extrinsic, and solver-disagreement AUROC on selected splits does not yield a validated nonzero-coverage risk-coverage operating point.
  In a 15-log final system role, the locked conservative policy admits no candidate and equals Raw, whereas Always-correct increases trajectory error. These results support an auditable separation of correction, confidence, and recursive contamination; they do not establish a deployable calibration gate, positive odometry or SLAM improvement, map consistency, or closed-loop safety.

</details>


### [42] [StepX-OPD: Rethinking Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04386)
*Chenru Wang,Xuanyang Zhang,Yucheng Han,Wenbo Nie,Wei Cheng,Xianfang Zeng,Gang YU,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StepX-OPD将"能力整合"与"少步蒸馏"两阶段合并为单阶段，学生模型在自身rollout轨迹上由多个冻结专家在线监督，同时学会少步采样与全部能力，在SD3.5-Medium上以约37%更少的GPU时数超过40步Flow-OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可部署的T2I扩散模型需同时具备指令遵循、文字渲染、人类偏好等能力且需少步采样。现有做法分两阶段：先多教师on-policy蒸馏整合能力，再以固定全步教师做步蒸馏。问题在于：(1) 所有被评测的全步模型经压缩后分数均下降；(2) 第二阶段须等第一阶段结束，无法并行。

Method: 提出StepX-OPD：单学生模型在自身rollout所到达的状态上接受冻结专家监督，而非在它实际不会运行的长轨迹上学习，从而边整合能力边学少步采样。基于rollout的KL分解发现速度失配在接近干净端最严重，故额外对rollout低噪声段做速度回归。另发现能力整合会使模型在未要求文字的提示上把提示词写进图像，给出一个降低该现象的变体。

Result: 在SD3.5-Medium与FLUX.1-dev上，从指令遵循、文字渲染、图像质量与人类偏好四方面评测：两种架构上StepX-OPD在四步方法中归一化平均分最高，并超过四十步Flow-OPD；SD3.5-Medium上训练GPU时数比"先DiffusionOPD再步蒸馏"的串行流程少约37%。

Conclusion: 将能力整合与步蒸馏合并为单阶段on-policy蒸馏，可避免压缩导致的性能退化与串行等待，同时提升效率与最终质量；并揭示了能力整合带来的"提示词泄漏入图"副作用及可缓解的变体。

Abstract: A deployable text-to-image diffusion model must hold several capabilities at once, such as instruction following, text rendering and human preference, and must sample in a few steps. Capabilities and few-step sampling are built in separate stages: a full-step model first consolidates capabilities through multi-teacher on-policy distillation, then serves as a fixed teacher for step distillation. Every full-step model we evaluate scores lower once compressed, and the second stage cannot begin until the first finishes. We introduce StepX-OPD, which merges the two stages: one student acquires every capability while learning to sample in a few steps, so frozen specialists supervise it on the states its own rollout visits rather than on a longer trajectory it never runs. Because a decomposition of the rollout's KL weights velocity mismatch most heavily near the clean end, we also regress the student's velocity on the low-noise part of the rollout. Separately, we find that capability integration makes models write prompt words into images on prompts that never asked for text, and we give a variant that lowers it. We evaluate on SD3.5-Medium and FLUX.1-dev across instruction following, text rendering, image quality and human preference. On both architectures, StepX-OPD attains the highest normalized average score among the four-step methods compared and exceeds the forty-step Flow-OPD model, and on SD3.5-Medium it trains with about 37% fewer GPU-hours than sequential DiffusionOPD followed by step distillation.

</details>


### [43] [ReSMap: Recasting Satellite Priors for Robust and Accurate Online HD Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04352)
*Kyungmin Kim,Sumin Lee,Sungoh Jeong,DoHyun Lim,Seonghyun Park,Soonmin Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出冗余感知的相机-卫星融合框架ReSMap，将卫星影像作为相机失效时的冗余源，以预测置信度作逐像素可靠性权重、并以模态独立查询与门控融合，在nuScenes各划分与量程及相机失效场景均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机在线HD地图构建依赖车载相机，易受传感器故障影响；卫星图像可离线缓存、不依赖实时感知条件，但现有方法仅在正常工况下使用卫星图，未将其作为传感器退化时的显式冗余。

Method: ReSMap框架包含两个核心模块：(1) Predict-then-Fuse (PTF)，以各分支自身预测置信度作为逐像素可靠性权重，融合相机与卫星BEV特征，无需学习路由器；(2) Object-Centric Cooperative Multi-modal Query (OCMQ) 解码器，将逐模态分离扩展到查询层，每个模态仅与自身BEV交叉注意力，逐模态更新经学习的逐token门控合并。

Result: 在nuScenes所有数据划分与测距设置上取得SOTA；在相机失效场景下刷新SOTA，表明卫星图像不仅是强先验，也是鲁棒在线HD建图的实用冗余源。

Conclusion: 以置信度驱动的融合与模态解耦查询设计可有效将卫星图像用作冗余，显著提升在线HD地图构建在传感器退化条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in camera-based online HD map construction, these systems remain susceptible to sensor failures due to their reliance on onboard cameras. In contrast, satellite images remain available independently of real-time sensing conditions as they are cached offline. Existing methods utilizing satellite images, however, primarily leverage them under nominal conditions, without explicitly addressing them as redundancy when a sensor degrades. To address this gap, we propose ReSMap, a redundancy-aware camera-satellite fusion framework for robust online HD map construction. Our framework explicitly leverages satellite images as a complementary source to improve robustness against camera failures. A Predict-then-Fuse (PTF) module fuses camera and satellite BEV features by using each branch's own prediction confidence as a per-pixel reliability weight, with no learned router. An Object-Centric Cooperative Multi-modal Query (OCMQ) decoder then extends this per-modality separation to the query level: each modality cross-attends only to its own BEV, and per-modality updates are merged through a learned per-token gate. Our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on nuScenes across all splits and range settings. More importantly, it also sets the new state-of-the-art under camera-failure scenarios, demonstrating that satellite images serve not only as a strong prior but as practical redundancy for robust online HD mapping.

</details>


### [44] [SettleSplat: Monocular Gaussian Mapping under In-View Scene Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04195)
*Anh Thuan Tran,Xiyun Song,Yubin Zhou,Heather Yu,Jana Kosecka,Liang Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出 SettleSplat 单目在线建图系统，在“视内场景变化”下维护持续更新的 3D 高斯地图，通过不确定性驱动的高斯动态性场剪除瞬态物体，并用几何矛盾检测持久变化，在 Bonn 与 Co-Fusion 上取得一致的 3D 高斯地图并优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线 3D 高斯建图方法多假设静态场景或将动态作为离群点滤除；已有工作虽开始处理干扰物，但主要针对“相机视野之外”或“多次独立采集之间”的变化。本文关注更难的视内场景变化：物体在单次在线序列中于相机前被移动，此时瞬态动态与持久变化相互纠缠——物体运动时是干扰物，静止后却成为场景的持久组成部分，这一问题尚缺乏研究。

Method: SettleSplat 为单目在线建图系统：(1) 将粗粒度的逐像素不确定性提升为 3D 高斯动态性场（dynamicity field），据此剪除瞬态高斯；(2) 针对持久变化，计算几何矛盾（geometric contradictions），并以参数无关的渲染深度不确定性进行加权，从而将真实场景变化与尚未充分优化的区域区分开。

Result: 在 Bonn 与 Co-Fusion 数据集上，SettleSplat 生成一致的 3D 高斯地图，在全图与变化区域上均优于现有基线，同时保持相当的速度。

Conclusion: 作者表明，显式建模不确定性与几何一致性可有效应对视内场景变化中的瞬态—持久纠缠问题，实现在线、一致的单目 3D 高斯建图；但该设定仍属初步探索，视内场景变化有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian mapping enables fast and high-fidelity novel view synthesis, with broad applications in augmented reality, robotics, and autonomous systems. However, many current methods assume static scenes or filter out dynamics as outliers. In this paper, we study in-view scene changes where objects are manipulated in front of the camera within a single online sequence, unlike prior works that detect changes outside the camera view or between separate captures. This setting entangles transient and persistent dynamics, as objects can be distractors while moving and persistent once they settle. While recent studies start to address distractors by removing them for clean static background, in-view scene changes remain underexplored. To resolve this, we present SettleSplat, a monocular mapping system that maintains up-to-date 3D Gaussian maps under such changes. Specifically, we lift coarse per-pixel uncertainty into Gaussian dynamicity field, then prune transient ones. For persistent changes, we derive geometric contradictions weighted by parameter-free rendered depth uncertainty to separate true changes from under-optimized regions. On Bonn and Co-Fusion, SettleSplat achieves consistent 3D Gaussian maps, outperforming current baselines on both full images and change regions while maintaining comparable speed.

</details>


### [45] [Is This 3D Asset Usable? Beyond the Beauty Render for Asset Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04102)
*Rushikesh Zawar,Vladimir Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRE 提出用对齐的 beauty、wireframe、UV-seam+wireframe 三视图，经三组冻结 ViT 分类器分通道打分后晚期融合，在无任何源元数据条件下判定 3D 资产是否可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型 3D 资产库便于收集资产，但难以判定其能否在场景装配、编辑、渲染、游戏制作与训练数据构建中可靠复用；仅凭 RGB 渲染往往掩盖断连几何、UV 组织问题或导入后材质解析失败等缺陷。

Method: 采用诊断性渲染证据（DRE）：对同一资产渲染对齐的 beauty、wireframe、UV 缝合线叠加 wireframe 三类视图，分别由按通道定制的冻结 ViT 分类器打分，再经晚期融合得到可用性判定；推理仅需导入资产与渲染衍生证据，不使用仓库身份、作者、流行度、文本或源元数据。

Result: 在 1,234 个人工标注的 TexVerse 资产上，嵌套分组评估取得 86.36% 平衡准确率与 88.32% 坏资产召回率；在独立的 764 个均衡外部参考测试集上，达到 88.9% 的平衡参考标签准确率。

Conclusion: 结果支持跨语料可迁移性，并表明专门设计的诊断视图能揭示仅靠 beauty 视图会遗漏的缺陷；方法具备元数据无关的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large 3D repositories make it easy to collect assets, but difficult to determine whether they can be reused reliably across scene assembly, editing, rendering, game production, and training-data construction. An asset can look plausible in RGB while hiding disconnected geometry, problematic UV organization, or materials that fail to resolve correctly after import. We introduce Diagnostic Rendered Evidence (DRE): aligned beauty, wireframe, and UV-seam-plus-wireframe views scored by pass-specific frozen ViT classifiers and combined through late fusion. On 1,234 human labeled TexVerse assets, nested grouped evaluation achieves 86.36% balanced accuracy and 88.32% bad-asset recall. On a separate, balanced 764 external reference test, DRE achieved 88.9% balanced reference-label accuracy. Our method requires only an imported asset and renderer-derived evidence, without repository identity, creator, popularity, text, or source metadata at inference. Our results support cross-corpus portability and show that purpose built diagnostic views expose defects that just the beauty views can miss. Webpage: https://rbz-99.github.io/is-this-3d-asset-usable/

</details>


### [46] [From Visual Matching to Textual Conditioning: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation via Reference Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04084)
*Jiahao Nie,Yun Xing,Wenbin An,Qixin Zhang,Yap-Peng Tan,Alex C. Kot,Shijian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RI-SAM3，通过双层参考反演为SAM3学习类别文本token，以约12K参数实现高效鲁棒的跨域小样本分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SAM的CD-FSS方法依赖参考-目标视觉匹配，对目标外观变化高度敏感；SAM3虽支持文本条件分割，但直接用类别名作为条件在严重域偏移下性能不稳定。

Method: 在SAM3冻结前提下学习类别文本token，包含三部分：(i) 双层参考反演，在冻结文本编码器前后学习互补token（前者锚定SAM3文本空间，后者更灵活表征类别）；(ii) 层自适应文本条件，随解码器各层object query演化以不同方式组合token；(iii) 存在性感知置信监督，联合监督query置信度与目标存在性。

Result: 在多个广泛使用的基准上，RI-SAM3在CD-FSS任务中持续取得更优性能，并具备较强的鲁棒性、效率与泛化能力；每类别仅优化约12K参数，单次前向即可分割目标，文本条件可复用以避免逐样本视觉匹配。

Conclusion: 利用大规模视觉-语言预训练为每个目标类别反演出合适的文本token，是替代易受外观变化影响的视觉匹配的有效途径，可在极低参数量下实现稳定、高效的跨域小样本分割。

Abstract: Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to segment objects from unseen domains using only a few annotated references. Recent Segment Anything Model (SAM)-based methods have advanced CD-FSS, yet their reference-target visual matching is highly sensitive to target appearance variations. SAM3 introduces text-conditioned segmentation, enabling natural-language descriptions to guide segmentation across target images, but directly using the category name as the condition still yields unstable performance under severe domain shifts. We propose Reference Inversion for SAM3 (RI-SAM3) that leverages SAM3's large-scale vision-language pre-training to learn suitable textual tokens for each target category. RI-SAM3 comprises three core designs: (i) Bi-level Reference Inversion that learns complementary tokens before and after the frozen text encoder: the former remain anchored in SAM3's text space while the latter represent the category more flexibly; (ii) Layer-adaptive Textual Conditioning combines these tokens differently as object queries evolve across decoder layers; and (iii) Presence-aware Confidence Supervision that jointly supervises query confidence and target presence for reliable predictions. With SAM3 frozen, RI-SAM3 optimizes around 12K parameters only per category and it efficiently segments each target in a single forward pass with reusable textual conditioning without per-sample reference-target visual matching. Extensive experiments over multiple widely adopted benchmarks show that RI-SAM3 achieves superior performance consistently in CD-FSS tasks, together with strong robustness, efficiency, and generalization.

</details>


### [47] [Do Pretrained Backbones Transfer to Physical Sensors? A Systematic Study of Multimodal Initialization Across the Sensor Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04034)
*Fabio Montello,Ronja Güldenring,Lucas Mogensen Sandby,Christoffer Koo Øhrstrøm,Lazaros Nalpantidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了涵盖16个公开数据集的2.82M图像多传感器自动驾驶数据集，基于MultiMAE进行域内预训练并适配稀疏模态，系统比较其与ViT/MAE/DINO等预训练骨干在下游传感器融合任务上的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器视觉中，何种预训练权重迁移效果最佳、域内预训练能否超越现成方案这两个问题尚未解决；且现有方法（如MultiMAE）依赖稠密、模型生成的模态，难以稳健迁移到使用物理传感器、且公开数据聚合导致模态稀疏的真实场景。

Method: 从16个公开数据集整理2.82M张图像，包含物理硬件模态（RGB、LWIR、NIR、Event）与模型衍生模态（立体深度、分割）；基于MultiMAE进行域内预训练，改造其损失以适配稀疏样本，研究强制编码器瓶颈对模态对齐的影响，并引入面向稠密重建的额外损失（SSIM、FFT）；随后在下游传感器融合任务上与ViT、MAE、DINO、MultiMAE等常见预训练骨干进行系统对比。

Result: MultiMAE作为开箱即用基线表现意外强劲，甚至能有效迁移到从未见过的模态；本文的针对性修改在多传感器迁移与整体鲁棒性上带来增量但一致的提升。

Conclusion: 物理传感器场景下的多模态域内预训练具备可行性，MultiMAE是强基线，针对稀疏模态的损失适配、编码器瓶颈及SSIM/FFT重建损失可稳定带来小幅改进。

Abstract: Transfer learning from pretrained backbones has become standard practice in multi-sensor computer vision---yet two questions remain open: which pretrained weights transfer best to multi-sensor setups, and can in-domain pretraining outperform off-the-shelf solutions? Frameworks like MultiMAE demonstrate the utility of masked autoencoding in a multi-modal context, but rely entirely on dense, model-derived modalities. It is unclear how robustly these methods translate to real-world applications that use physical sensors, where the aggregation of diverse public datasets for large-scale pretraining inherently introduces modality sparsity. To investigate this systematically, we curate a multi-sensor autonomous driving collection of $2.82M$ images from 16 publicly available datasets, incorporating both physical hardware modalities (RGB, LWIR, NIR, Event) and model-derived modalities (Stereo Depth, Segmentation). We use this collection for in-domain pretraining based on the MultiMAE approach. We adapt MultiMAE's loss formulation to accommodate sparse samples, and study the effect of an enforced encoder bottleneck for modality alignment and of additional losses specific to dense reconstruction (SSIM and FFT). We then systematically compare against common pretrained backbones (ViT, MAE, DINO and MultiMAE) across downstream sensor fusion tasks. We find that MultiMAE is a surprisingly strong out-of-the-box baseline, transferring effectively even to modalities it has never seen, while our targeted modifications yield incremental but consistent improvements on multi-sensor transfer and overall robustness.

</details>


### [48] [A Universal Raw-IQ Radar Encoder for Cross-Sensor Human Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04534)
*Jonas Leo Mueller,Dario Zanca,Sebastian Hoefler,Jan Petermann,Thomas Altstidl,Naga Venkata Sai Jitin Jami,Emmanuelle Salin,Bjoern M. Eskofier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种不依赖微多普勒谱图、跨平台可迁移的雷达通用编码器：在去杂波原始雷达数据上用卷积β-VAE无监督预训练，冻结编码器后仅训练轻量时序分类器，在10个下游设置中多数基准优于端到端监督模型与现有雷达基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达人体感知依赖端到端任务专用模型，输入为人工选择的信号投影（主要为微多普勒谱图），跨用户、跨任务、跨硬件泛化差。作者指出谱图不适于迁移：保留了会被载频缩放的Doppler轴，却丢弃了载频不变的angle轴。

Method: 改用去杂波原始雷达测量作为输入；用统一的谱插值网格对齐4个平台（60/77 GHz，4–12虚拟天线）的数据；无标签预训练卷积β-VAE；下游任务冻结编码器，仅在嵌入上训练小型有监督时序分类器。

Result: 覆盖活动、手势、手语、步态识别共10个设置。训练中见过测试用户与雷达时，5个基准全部最优；未见用户时4个基准中3个领先，提升4.8–23.2点；受试者不重叠步态识别中4个留出受试者均最优，平均超最强基线8.3点（预训练未含该雷达任何录制）；预训练排除测试雷达最多损失5.3点。

Conclusion: 放弃微多普勒谱图、直接在原始雷达数据上做无监督表示预训练，可获得强跨用户、跨硬件迁移性，冻结编码器加轻量分类器即可在多数下游基准超越端到端监督与雷达基础模型，且对预训练平台缺失具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Radar-based human sensing typically relies on end-to-end task-specific models trained on hand-chosen signal projections, which generalize poorly across unseen users, tasks, and hardware. We argue that the dominant signal processing choice, the micro-Doppler spectrogram, is a poor starting point for transfer because it keeps the Doppler axis, which carrier frequency rescales, and discards the angular axis, which carrier frequency leaves unchanged. We instead pretrain a convolutional beta-VAE without labels on clutter-removed raw radar measurements from four platforms spanning 60 and 77 GHz and 4 to 12 virtual antennas, aligned by a unified spectral interpolation grid. For every downstream benchmark the encoder stays frozen, and only a small supervised temporal classifier is trained on its embeddings. We compare against supervised models trained end to end on each benchmark and a pretrained radar foundation model across ten settings covering activity, gesture, sign-language, and gait recognition. When the test users and the radar are seen during training, our encoder is best on all five benchmarks. For users never seen in training, it leads on three of four benchmarks, by 4.8 to 23.2 points. On subject-disjoint gait recognition, it is best for each of the four held-out subjects and 8.3 points above the strongest baseline on average, even though its pretraining excluded every recording of this radar. Excluding the test radar from pretraining costs at most 5.3 points on any benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/jonasmueler/UniversalRadarEncoder_WACV.

</details>


### [49] [SPA-Q: Structure-Preserving Adaptive Post-Training Quantization for Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04491)
*Jaemin Choi,Jincheol Yang,Nahyun Lim,Yun-Seong Jeong,Matti Zinke,Hyunwoo Yu,Suk-Ju Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPA-Q结构保持自适应PTQ框架，通过注意力保持校准与通道级分布对齐，解决Depth Anything类MDE模型低比特量化的两大难题，4比特下AbsRel平均降低25.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的MDE基础模型（如Depth Anything）泛化强但计算与内存开销大，PTQ是高效压缩方案，但低比特PTQ对MDE仍困难：查询与键投影独立最小化量化误差无法保持二者交互产生的注意力图；量化误差逐层累积导致中间特征分布漂移。

Method: SPA-Q包含两部分：(1) 注意力保持校准（APC），通过匹配全精度注意力分布来确定query与key的量化参数；(2) 通道级分布对齐（CWDA），学习通道级仿射变换以缓解量化引起的分布漂移，训练后参数吸收进权重、无额外推理开销。

Result: 在4比特量化下，SPA-Q在NYUv2与KITTI数据集上持续优于现有PTQ方法，AbsRel平均降低25.9%，δ1平均提升17.3%。

Conclusion: 保持注意力和特征分布结构是MDE模型低比特PTQ的关键；SPA-Q以无推理开销的方式显著提升4比特深度估计精度，为MDE基础模型的高效部署提供实用方案。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) has advanced rapidly with the emergence of foundation models such as Depth Anything. Their transformer-based architectures provide strong generalization across diverse scenes and domains, but also incur high computational and memory cost, making efficient deployment challenging. Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) provides an efficient and practical solution for model compression, yet low-bit PTQ remains challenging for MDE models. When applying PTQ to Depth Anything, we identify two key challenges: (i) independently minimizing the quantization error of the query and key projections fails to preserve the attention maps induced by their interaction, and (ii) quantization errors accumulate across layers, resulting in intermediate feature distribution shifts. To address these issues, we propose SPA-Q, a Structure-Preserving Adaptive PTQ framework for MDE, consisting of two main components. First, Attention-Preserving Calibration (APC) determines query and key quantization parameters by matching the full-precision attention distribution. Second, Channel-Wise Distribution Alignment (CWDA) learns channel-wise affine transformations to mitigate quantization-induced distribution shifts, and the learned parameters are absorbed into the weights after training. Experimental results show that SPA-Q consistently outperforms existing PTQ methods under 4-bit quantization, achieving an average 25.9% reduction in AbsRel and a 17.3% improvement in $δ_1$ across NYUv2 and KITTI datasets.

</details>


### [50] [Bridging CLIP with DINO: Cross-Modal Information Maximization for Online Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04489)
*Taehoon Kim,Wonjun Hwang,Hyunsouk Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对CLIP类视觉语言模型在测试时遇到图像损坏时出现的"模态偏置"问题，提出一种在线测试时自适应方法CDIM，从表征、优化和预测三个层面打破确认偏置循环，在多个基准上优于同类CLIP在线TTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉语言模型支持基于任意类名的零样本分类，但在测试时遇到图像损坏（test-time corruption）时，其视觉编码器会把失真特征以高置信度投影到错误的文本锚点上，作者称之为"模态偏置"（modality bias）。标准的熵最小化会强化这些过度自信的错误，形成"确认偏置循环"（confirmation-bias loop），并导致预测标签分布塌缩。因此需要一种在线TTA方法来打破这一循环。

Method: 从三个层面设计CDIM：(1) 表征层面，将冻结的自监督骨干（如DINO v2）与CLIP结合，通过交叉协方差logit集成（cross-covariance logit ensemble）融合；将CLIP文本原型投影到无文本自监督骨干的特征空间，使自监督分支对原生图像特征分类，从而较少受CLIP文本偏置影响；(2) 优化层面，采用门控测试时信息最大化目标，将逐样本锐化与批级多样性结合，并用自适应门控把不可靠样本排除在梯度之外；(3) 预测层面，用在线logit调整校正残余的类别分布偏斜。

Result: CDIM在ImageNet-C、CIFAR10-C和CIFAR100-C上优于所比较的基于CLIP的在线TTA基线，并在持续型（continual）与混合域（mixed-domain）TTA场景中保持竞争力；大量分析与消融实验验证了各组成部分的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过在表征、优化与预测三个层面协同打破模态偏置引发的确认偏置循环，CDIM实现了一种有效的在线测试时自适应方案，兼顾单域与持续/混合域场景，验证了多层面设计的必要性与有效性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP enable zero-shot classification from arbitrary class names, but under test-time corruptions, their visual encoder projects distorted features onto incorrect textual anchors with high confidence: a phenomenon we term modality bias. Standard entropy minimization reinforces these overconfident errors, which creates a confirmation-bias loop and collapses the predicted label distribution. We propose an online test-time adaptation (TTA) method that breaks this loop at three levels. At the representation level, a frozen self-supervised backbone (e.g., DINO v2) is combined with CLIP through a cross-covariance logit ensemble. CLIP text prototypes are projected into the feature space of this text-free backbone, so the self-supervised branch classifies native image features and is less affected by the text bias of CLIP. At the optimization level, a gated test-time information-maximization objective pairs per-sample sharpening with batch-level diversity, while an adaptive gate excludes unreliable samples from the gradient. At the prediction level, an online logit adjustment corrects the residual class-distribution skew. CDIM outperforms the compared CLIP-based online TTA baselines on ImageNet-C, CIFAR10-C, and CIFAR100-C, and remains competitive in continual and mixed-domain TTA. Extensive analyses and ablation studies verify the contribution of each of its components. Our code is available at https://github.com/B1ueC1oud/CDIM

</details>


### [51] [Using GeoAI and machine learning tools for consistent high-resolution land cover mapping based on time-series NAIP imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04470)
*Jie Liu,Xusheng Tang,Chao Wang,Zhengxiao Yan,Yuchen Dai,Qi Zhang,Conghe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双轨工作流，在无历史训练样本条件下由NAIP影像生成时间一致的1米土地覆盖图，2009-2017用预训练U-Net/ResNet-34结合SAM边界优化，2004-2008用NLCD时空桥接与标签反演，六县总体精度0.887/0.886/0.733。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率土地覆盖图支撑生态监测，但历史航空影像在年份与传感器间差异大，导致一致的历史时间序列稀缺，且缺乏历史训练样本。

Method: 双轨工作流：高质量影像（2009-2017）采用预训练U-Net/ResNet-34并借助Segment Anything Model细化边界；低质量影像（2004-2008）通过基于NLCD的时空桥接与标签反演流水线重建土地覆盖。

Result: 北卡罗来纳州与宾夕法尼亚州六县评估总体精度为0.887（2014）、0.886（2017）、0.733（2004）；Structure、Water、Wetland、Cropland的F1分数持续较高。

Conclusion: 该工作流融合GeoAI与多源数据，缓解历史航空影像的时间异质性，可支撑全美本土的高分辨率土地变化分析与景观规划。

Abstract: High-resolution land-cover maps support ecological monitoring, but consistent historical time series remain scarce because aerial imagery varies across years and sensors. We developed a dual-track workflow to produce temporally coherent 1-m land-cover maps from National Agricultural Imagery Program (NAIP) imagery without historical training samples. For higher-quality imagery from 2009-2017, we used a pretrained U-Net/ResNet-34 model and refined boundaries with the Segment Anything Model (SAM). For lower-quality imagery from 2004-2008, we reconstructed land cover through an NLCD-based spatiotemporal bridging and label back-casting pipeline. Evaluation across six counties in North Carolina and Pennsylvania yielded overall accuracies of 0.887 (2014), 0.886 (2017), and 0.733 (2004). Structure, Water, Wetland, and Cropland consistently achieved high F1-scores. The workflow integrates GeoAI and multiple data sources to address temporal heterogeneity in historical aerial imagery, supporting high-resolution land-change analysis and landscape planning across the conterminous United States.

</details>


### [52] [A Systematic Point Cloud Edge Detection Framework for Automatic Aircraft Skin Milling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04435)
*Zijie Wu,Yaonan Wang,He Xie,Mingtao Feng,Haotian Wu,Chao Ding,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向飞机蒙皮自动边缘铣削的边缘检测框架，先用空间切线连续性(STC)构建边缘概率检测器，再用层次分支搜索(HBS)从原始点云中逐级提取铣削边缘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘检测方法多聚焦于传统定义的边缘提取任务，忽视了边缘铣削的系统性需求，难以直接支撑航空制造中飞机蒙皮铣削的实际应用。

Method: 提出边缘概率检测器：基于空间切线连续性(STC)为边缘提取提供参考；提出层次分支搜索(HBS)方法，将目标铣削边缘从原始点云中分层剥离，包含三个递进步骤：分支主干生成、分支延伸、分支修剪。

Result: 在合成模型与真实飞机蒙皮工件上验证，所提方法优于其他基线方法，能够为边缘铣削任务提供精确边缘。

Conclusion: 该框架将边缘检测与边缘铣削的系统性需求相结合，可实现飞机蒙皮点云的高精度铣削边缘提取，具备实际制造应用价值。

Abstract: The edge detection technique is an essential step for aircraft skin milling in aviation manufacturing. Most of the current detection methods focus on traditionally defined edge extraction tasks but disregard the crucial systematic requirement of edge milling. In this paper, we proposed a novel edge detection framework for automatic edge milling of aircraft skins. First, an edge probability detector is proposed by the spatial tangent continuity (STC) to provide the essential reference. Second, we propose a hierarchical branch searching (HBS) method to hierarchically strip the desired milling edges from the raw point cloud, which consists of three graded progressive steps: branch backbone generation, branch extension, and branch pruning. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed method on both synthetic models and aircraft skin workpieces. The proposed method outperforms the other baselines and shows accurate edges for the edge milling task.

</details>


### [53] [VidUEU-Agent: A Data-Curation Agent for Multimodal Understanding, Editing, and Unified Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04418)
*Tengju Ru,Weitong Lian,Zecong Tang,Lingyi Meng,Haoran Li,Zhejun Cui,Yichen Zhu,Hangshuo Cao,Qi Kang,Yechi Liu,Kaixuan Wang,Yu-Jie Yuan,Chunwei Wang,Yu Zhang,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自动化数据合成流水线 VidUEU-Agent，从原始视频生成多模态理解、编辑与统一任务指令数据，并构建 100K 规模的 VidUEU-100K 数据集，微调后模型在多个基准上取得显著且一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态学习受限于高质量指令数据的稀缺与高昂标注成本；原始视频虽蕴含丰富动态视觉知识，但难以转化为精确的指令数据。

Method: 四阶段流水线：全局感知过滤低质量镜头；挖掘关键帧与多模态信号；构建元数据；基于元数据路由合成指令样本。核心为 Agent 框架，可依据下游训练结果自主迭代数据分布，优化数据质量、平衡性与训练有效性。

Result: 规模化生成 VidUEU-100K 数据集（10 万训练样本，覆盖多领域多任务）；基线模型微调后在各代表性基准上获得显著且一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 验证了 Agent 框架与数据集的有效性，为构建全面的多模态能力提供了可扩展的数据基础。

Abstract: The advancement of multimodal learning is heavily constrained by the scarcity and high annotation cost of high-quality instruction data. While raw videos offer an abundant and dynamic source of visual knowledge, transforming them into precise instruction data remains highly challenging. To bridge this gap, we present VidUEU-Agent, an automated pipeline that synthesizes instruction data from raw videos for multimodal understanding, editing, and unified tasks. VidUEU-Agent follows a four-stage pipeline: it performs global perception to filter low-quality shots, mines keyframes and multimodal signals, constructs metadata and routes the metadata to synthesize instruction samples. More importantly, our agent framework can autonomously iterate the data distribution based on downstream training results, thereby optimizing data quality, balance, and training effectiveness. Scaling this pipeline, we construct VidUEU-100K, a large-scale dataset of 100K training samples spanning multiple domains and tasks, providing a robust foundation for developing comprehensive multimodal capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that fine-tuning baseline models on VidUEU-100K yields significant and consistent performance gains across diverse representative benchmarks, validating the effectiveness of our proposed agent framework and dataset.

</details>


### [54] [Efficient and Faithful High-Resolution Image Editing with Sparse Cross-Resolution Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04416)
*Maosen Zhao,Xianfang Zeng,Pengtao Chen,Peng Ye,Gang Yu,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FLASH，一种基于稀疏跨分辨率去噪的原生高分辨率指令图像编辑器，在保持1K编辑轨迹的同时以低开销实现忠实的2K/4K输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑器主要工作在1K，而实际编辑需求已是2K/4K。密集高分辨率适配虽保留编辑轨迹但目标-源注意力代价高昂；1K编辑后再外部精修虽便宜，却使细节恢复与编辑轨迹脱节。

Method: 采用稀疏跨分辨率去噪：冻结的1K分支提供语义与结构锚点，目标分辨率分支在同一Transformer内重建局部细节；非对称拓扑保留完整1K注意力，用空间对齐的低分辨率噪声目标状态引导高分辨率查询，并将高分辨率目标-参考交互限制在局部Halo邻域；配合分辨率特定的扩散状态映射与空间YaRN将预训练编辑器适配到更大网格；另提供免训练渐进式路径，用1K前缀替代早期高分辨率去噪。

Result: 在 VINS-4KEval 上，2K与4K下结构保真度均为最优；R=1 的4K前向基准中总前向执行快4.2倍、注意力快25.3倍；渐进式推理将端到端4K延迟降低30.5%，质量代价适中。

Conclusion: FLASH 通过稀疏跨分辨率架构兼顾高分辨率保真度与计算效率，并在原生编辑轨迹内完成细节重建，为实用化高分辨率指令编辑提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editors still primarily operate at 1K, whereas practical editing increasingly requires faithful 2K/4K outputs. Dense high-resolution adaptation preserves the editing trajectory but incurs expensive target-source attention; editing at 1K followed by external refinement is cheaper but can separate detail restoration from the editing trajectory. We introduce FLASH, a native high-resolution editor based on sparse cross-resolution denoising. A frozen 1K branch provides a semantic and structural anchor, while a target-resolution branch reconstructs local detail within the same Transformer. An asymmetric topology retains full 1K attention, guides high-resolution queries with spatially aligned low-resolution noisy-target states, and confines high-resolution target-reference interaction to local Halo neighborhoods. Resolution-specific diffusion-state mapping and spatial YaRN adapt the pretrained editor to larger grids. An optional, training-free progressive path replaces early high-resolution denoising with a 1K prefix and completes refinement in the same editor. On VINS-4KEval, the direct model achieves the strongest structural fidelity among the compared methods at both 2K and 4K. A separate 4K forward benchmark with R = 1 records 4.2x faster total forward execution and 25.3x faster attention than dense target-reference attention. Progressive inference reduces end-to-end 4K latency by 30.5%, with a modest quality cost.

</details>


### [55] [DR-LiteNet: A Lightweight Explainable Hybrid CNN Framework for Imbalanced Diabetic Retinopathy Grading via Adaptive SMOTE Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04405)
*Nishi Kanta Paul,Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Audrija Chowdhury,Israt Jahan Esha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双分支混合网络DR-LiteNet：EfficientNetB0深度特征(1280维)与手工病灶描述子(51维)拼接为1331维嵌入，在其上做ADASYN合成少数类，配两阶段训练，在APTOS 2019五折交叉验证达88.77%准确率、QWK 0.884、macro F1 0.792，仅4.69M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖网五级分级面临两个叠加难题：严重的类别不平衡，以及大型骨干网络在资源受限临床环境中的高计算开销。作者希望用一个轻量模型同时缓解二者。

Method: 双分支混合CNN：CNN分支用EfficientNetB0提取1280维深层特征；手工(HC)分支生成51维紧凑病灶描述子（血管密度、渗出强度、纹理熵、HSV颜色直方图）。两分支拼接为1331维混合嵌入，训练时在该特征空间施加ADASYN合成少数类向量（不改动图像空间）。两阶段训练：先冻结主干、用平衡特征预热分类头，再端到端微调全网。用Grad-CAM生成显著图提供事后可解释性。

Result: 在APTOS 2019上五折分层交叉验证：准确率88.77%±0.61，二次加权kappa(QWK) 0.884±0.010，macro F1 0.792±0.008；参数量仅4.69M，优于ResNet50(23.5M)，ΔQWK=0.022，可在单张Google Colab T4 GPU上运行。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 在特征层（而非图像层）做重采样并结合轻量双分支混合特征，可在低参数量约束下有效提升DR分级性能与QWK，兼顾类别不平衡修正、计算可行性与临床可解释性。

Abstract: Automated diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading from colour fundus photographs is held back by two compounding challenges: severe class imbalance across five severity grades and the heavy computational cost of large scale architectures in resource constrained clinical settings. We introduce DR-LiteNet, a dual branch hybrid convolutional neural network that tackles both problems at once. The CNN branch extracts 1,280 dimensional deep features through EfficientNetB0, while the handcrafted (HC) branch produces a compact 51 dimensional lesion descriptor capturing vessel density, exudate intensity, texture entropy, and HSV colour histograms. Concatenating both branches yields a 1,331-d hybrid embedding, on which Adaptive SMOTE (ADASYN) is applied during training to synthesise minority class feature vectors, correcting distributional skew without touching the image space. A two phase training protocol first warms up the classification head on balanced features, then fine tunes the full network end to end. Grad CAM saliency maps deliver post-hoc clinical explainability. Tested on APTOS 2019 under five fold stratified cross validation, DR-LiteNet reaches 88.77% +/- 0.61 accuracy, a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.884 +/- 0.010, and a macro F1 of 0.792 +/- 0.008 with only 4.69 M parameters beating ResNet50 (23.5 M) by DeltaQWK = 0.022 while staying practical on a single Google Colab T4 GPU. The source code of this project is available at: https://github.com/Nishi-Kanta-Paul/DR-LiteNet.

</details>


### [56] [IGSW: Multi-Weather Urban Scene Reconstruction with Intrinsic-Decoupled Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04374)
*Zhongquan Wang,Long Cao,Peijia Xie,Senlei Ma,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架IGSW，结合内参解耦的4D高斯场景建模与结构保持的天气精修，实现高保真4D场景天气编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式图像编辑与动态高斯结合可做多天气重建，但天气编辑图像常含结构失真，直接拟合会污染原场景结构与时间连续性。

Method: Stage I将背景高斯外观分解为天气共享的反照率与法线、天气特有的辐照度与残差，并用扩散编辑模型生成的天气教师图与扩散逆渲染器估计的内参图做特征级软监督。Stage II用结构保持门控结合语义冲突与跨天气内参差异抑制不可靠教师区域，再以逐高斯天气传输变量和目标天气分支增强目标外观。

Result: 在nuScenes与Waymo上，内容相似度与语义一致性最高、时间误差最低，同时保留显著的天气相关光照与氛围。

Conclusion: 通过内参解耦与结构保持精修，可缓解教师图像失真传播，实现结构一致、时序稳定的多天气4D场景重建。

Abstract: High-fidelity weather editing of 4D scenes can support end-to-end training and closed-loop simulation for autonomous driving. Combining generative image editing with dynamic Gaussian representations enables multi-weather scene reconstruction, but weather-edited images often contain structural distortions. Directly fitting their appearance may propagate errors from teacher images into the reconstructed 4D scene, compromising the original scene structure and temporal continuity. To address these challenges, we propose IGSW, a two-stage framework that combines intrinsic-decoupled 4D Gaussian scene modeling with structure-preserving weather refinement. In Stage I, background Gaussian appearance is decomposed into weather-shared albedo and normals and weather-specific irradiance and residuals. Weather teacher images generated by a diffusion-based image editing model and intrinsic maps estimated by a diffusion-based inverse renderer provide feature-level soft supervision for stable structure and illumination changes. In Stage II, a structure-preservation gate combines semantic conflicts with cross-weather intrinsic discrepancies to suppress unreliable teacher regions. Per-Gaussian weather transport variables and target-weather branches then strengthen the desired weather appearance over the intrinsic-decoupled scene. Experiments on nuScenes and Waymo show that IGSW achieves the highest content similarity and semantic consistency and the lowest temporal error among the compared methods, while retaining pronounced weather-dependent illumination and atmosphere. The project page is available at https://akaxi6.github.io/IGSW/ .

</details>


### [57] [Learning from Unlabeled Photographs for General Oral Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04709)
*Zhong-Shi Zhang,Zi-Li Yu,Xiao Xiao,Huan Liu,Gui-Song Xia,Cui Huang,Meng-Xun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出口腔领域自监督预训练编码器DOVE，在约53万张无标注口腔照片上进行领域自适应自监督预训练，在5个分类与4个分割数据集上整体性能最佳，展现强少样本迁移与标签高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔照片蕴含丰富临床信息，具AI应用潜力，但现有模型多为单一任务定制，且依赖昂贵的专家标注，缺乏可跨任务迁移的通用视觉表征。

Method: 假设无标注口腔照片上的自监督训练可产生可迁移表征。构建DOVE（Dental Optical Visual Encoder），在约530,000张公开来源的无标注口腔照片上做领域自适应自监督预训练；与3个已有基线在5个分类数据集和4个分割数据集上对比，涵盖冻结骨干评估与端到端微调两种协议。

Result: DOVE整体表现最强，在多数基准的少样本迁移与标签效率上排名第一。20-shot冻结骨干协议下，分类平均macro-F1较次优DINOv3高4.37个百分点，分割平均macro-Dice高4.49个百分点；端到端微调时，仅用10%标注数据即在多数数据集上达到最强全数据基线性能的至少80%。定性检索与特征分析显示其图像级与空间表征组织更优。

Conclusion: DOVE是面向口腔视觉研究的可泛化、标签高效骨干网络，验证了领域自适应自监督预训练在口腔多任务视觉中的价值。

Abstract: Oral photographs contain rich clinical information and hold significant potential for artificial intelligence applications; however, most models are trained for individual tasks and rely on costly expert annotations. We hypothesized that self-supervised training on unlabeled oral photographs could produce transferable visual representations for heterogeneous vision tasks. To test this hypothesis, we developed DOVE (Dental Optical Visual Encoder) by domain-adaptive self-supervised pretraining on approximately 530,000 unlabeled oral photographs collected from publicly accessible sources. DOVE was benchmarked against 3 established baselines across 5 classification datasets and 4 segmentation datasets, covering both frozen backbone evaluation and end-to-end finetuning. DOVE achieved the strongest overall performance, ranking best in few-shot transfer and label efficiency on most benchmarks. Under the 20-shot frozen-backbone protocol, DOVE outperformed the second best DINOv3 by 4.37 percentage points in mean macro-F1 across classification datasets and by 4.49 percentage points in mean macro-Dice across segmentation tasks. Under end-to-end finetuning, DOVE achieved at least 80% of the strongest full-data baseline's performance across most evaluation datasets using only 10% of the labeled training data. Qualitative retrieval and feature analyses further indicated better organization of image-level and spatial representations. These findings support DOVE as a generalizable and label-efficient backbone for oral vision research.

</details>


### [58] [Characterizing the vertical continuity of fuel strata based on TLS point clouds Gaussian decomposition and overlap metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05102)
*Jesús Torralba,Luis Á. Ruiz,Pablo Crespo-Peremarch,Marina Simó-Martí,Juan Pedro Carbonell-Rivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出对TLS点云垂向高度剖面进行高斯分解，实现垂直可燃物连续性（梯状燃料）的显式连续量化，并给出多尺度评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行业务化可燃物类型模型以离散类别间接反映垂直连续性，难以刻画灌层与冠层之间的燃料连接，而该连接对火行为至关重要；高密度点云为连续化表征提供可能，但缺少具体方法。

Method: 在25个永久样地中，基于地面激光扫描（TLS）点云提取垂向高度剖面，采用高斯分解刻画几何垂直连续性；开展从样地尺度至约0.5 m²单元的多尺度分析，比较几何、概率（如概率密度重叠）与重叠类指标（含Jeffries-Matusita距离、可分性指标）对燃料层的表征能力，并评估不同尺度表现与误差指标（MSE）的可靠性。

Result: 方法再现了灌木层与乔木层之间的垂向过渡，各分析层级中位Pearson相关为0.83–0.96；概率密度重叠与可分性指标可补充描述垂直连续性；3–6 m单元在空间细节与灌-冠相互作用格局一致性之间取得平衡；更细尺度因点密度下降与剖面不规则性而变异增大，且低MSE不足以单独作为性能判据。

Conclusion: TLS点云高斯分解可显式量化垂直燃料连续性，弥补离散分类体系的不足；所得连续垂直连通性指标有助于改进梯状燃料刻画，并支撑野火风险评估决策。

Abstract: Vertical forest structure influences fire behavior by regulating understory-to-canopy fuel connectivity, yet operational fuel-type models represent this complexity through discrete classes that only indirectly capture vertical continuity. High-density point clouds offer an opportunity to characterize vertical fuel connectivity in a more explicit and continuous manner, but specific methodological approaches are still required. We applied Gaussian decomposition to vertical height profiles derived from terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) point clouds across 25 permanent forest plots to characterize geometric vertical fuel continuity. A multiscale analysis spanning from the plot scale to ~0.5 $m^2$ units was used to evaluate geometric, probabilistic, and overlap metrics for characterizing fuel strata. The method reproduced the vertical transition between shrub and tree strata, with median Pearson correlations ranging from 0.83 to 0.96 across analysis levels. Probability density overlap metrics and separability indicators, such as the Jeffries-Matusita distance, provided complementary descriptors of vertical continuity. Intermediate spatial scales (3-6 m cells) provided a useful compromise between spatial detail and the consistent representation of shrub-canopy interaction patterns. Finer scales increased variability due to point density reduction and profile irregularity, and low mean squared error values were found insufficient as sole performance indicators. Gaussian decomposition applied to TLS point clouds enables explicit quantification of vertical fuel continuity, a limitation of current discrete classification systems. Continuous vertical connectivity metrics derived from this approach offer a pathway for improving ladder fuel characterization and strengthening decision-making support in wildfire risk assessment.

</details>


### [59] [Sparse Anchors for Visual Multi-Session Registration in Changing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05084)
*Yunus Talha Erzurumlu,Niklas Trekel,Saurabh Gupta,Nico Koltermann,Olga Vysotska,Jens Behley,Louis Wiesmann,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种稀疏可靠绝对锚点+会话内相对位姿的鲁棒位姿图优化方法，实现剧烈变化环境下的跨会话轨迹配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复性机器人巡检需在环境变化下完成同一环境的跨访问定位；剧烈变化会大幅减少持久视觉内容，重复结构易导致跨会话错误对应，且可靠跨会话对应可能仅覆盖部分轨迹。

Method: 1) 通过视觉位置识别、局部特征匹配与几何验证生成绝对对齐假设；2) 用序列一致性剔除与共同查询-参考对齐不一致的假设，保留者为绝对锚点；3) 锚点经高斯特征场的featuremetric对齐精化；4) 与会话内相对位姿约束联合进行鲁棒位姿图优化，完成整段轨迹配准。

Result: 在HILTI26建筑工地数据集的15组跨会话实验中，1m内召回94.4%、5m内100.0%，优于独立图像定位（峰值65.3%与70.7%）及独立重建SfM几何的直接配准。

Conclusion: 稀疏且可靠的绝对锚点在跨会话对应仅部分可用时，仍能提供准确完整的轨迹配准。

Abstract: Repeated robotic monitoring requires localization across visits to the same environment, even if the environment changes. Such changes can substantially reduce persistent visual content, and repetitive structures can lead to incorrect cross-session correspondences. In this paper, we address visual multi-session registration of a new data recording (the query session) to a previously reconstructed reference map. Under substantial visual and structural changes, cross-session localization may be possible only for parts of the trajectory. In our approach, we therefore rely on a sparse set of reliable absolute pose constraints and combine them with within-session relative-pose information to register the complete trajectory to the reference map. We first estimate absolute alignment hypotheses through visual place recognition, local feature matching, and geometric verification. We then use sequence consensus to reject hypotheses that are inconsistent with a common query-to-reference alignment and retain the remaining poses as absolute anchors. These anchors are refined through featuremetric alignment against a Gaussian feature field and combined with within-session relative-pose constraints in a robust pose-graph optimization. We evaluate our approach on 15 cross-session experiments from the challenging HILTI26 construction-site dataset, covering substantial scene changes and difficult-to-match environments. Our method achieves 94.4% recall within 1m and 100.0% within 5m, outperforming independent image localization, which peaks at 65.3% and 70.7%, respectively, as well as direct registration of independently reconstructed Structure-from-Motion geometry. Our results show that sparse, reliable absolute anchors can provide accurate and complete trajectory registration even when reliable cross-session correspondences are available only for part of the query session.

</details>


### [60] [ProAlign: Progressive Positional and Prototype-Guided Alignment for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04980)
*Qiao Li,Jing Chen,Cong Wu,Kun He,Ruiying Du,Lei Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProAlign网络解决空-地行人重识别中的层级位置漂移与跨视角语义不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空-地行人重识别（AG-ReID）因视角差异、遮挡和域间差异而困难；现有方法忽视两个结构性缺陷：层级特征提取中累积的位置漂移，以及跨视角特征分布间的语义不一致。

Method: ProAlign包含两部分：层级渐进位置嵌入（LPPE）模块，在浅层用条件位置编码缓解局部空间畸变，在深层引入可学习静态位置嵌入强化全局语义先验；视角感知原型对比学习（PCL）模块，为每个身份分别维护空中与地面原型，并通过双原型对比目标强化跨视角语义一致性。

Result: 在CARGO及两个真实AG-ReID基准上实验有效；CARGO上达到SOTA，Rank-1提升1.60%，mAP提升5.01%。

Conclusion: 从位置校准与原型对比两个结构层面协同对齐，可有效缓解空-地跨视角差异，验证了所提方法的优越性。

Abstract: Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification (AG-ReID) aims to match pedestrian images captured by unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and ground-based surveillance cameras. The task remains highly challenging due to severe viewpoint discrepancies, frequent occlusions, and substantial domain gaps between aerial and ground imagery. Beyond these observable factors, existing methods often overlook two fundamental structural issues: cumulative positional drift during hierarchical feature extraction and semantic inconsistency across cross-view feature distributions. To address these challenges, we propose ProAlign, a Progressive Positional and Prototype-Guided Alignment Network for AG-ReID. ProAlign comprises two key components: a Layer-wise Progressive Positional Embedding (LPPE) module and a View-Aware Prototype Contrastive Learning (PCL) module. LPPE performs adaptive positional calibration throughout transformer layers by employing conditional positional encoding in shallow layers to mitigate local spatial distortions, while introducing learnable static positional embeddings in deeper layers to reinforce global semantic priors. Meanwhile, PCL maintains view-specific aerial and ground prototypes for each identity and enforces cross-view semantic consistency via a dual-prototype contrastive objective. Extensive experiments on CARGO and two real-world AG-ReID benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ProAlign, achieving state-of-the-art overall retrieval performance on CARGO with improvements of 1.60% in Rank-1 accuracy and 5.01% in mAP over the strongest competing methods.

</details>


### [61] [An Agentic Framework for Quality-Aware and Scenario-Adaptive Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04960)
*Zhenghao Chen,Lebin Zhou,Zicong Chen,Naifu Xue,Jiahao Li,Bin Li,Dong Xu,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架PICA，将现有编解码器视为黑盒，自适应适配多样化用户需求与退化输入，实现场景自适应的图像压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编解码器难以适配真实多样压缩场景：一是多数编解码器针对单一目标优化，压缩目标变化时需大量重新开发；二是通常假设输入干净，而传感器图像常含退化，改变率-质量行为。

Method: PICA由四个协作智能体构成：感知智能体解析用户意图并分析图像内容；改进智能体联合考虑码率与重建质量以引导编码前修复；编码智能体从全局到区域细化质量控制设置；编码后改进智能体仅以图像质量为引导处理残余退化与压缩伪影。并提出Agentic ROI Coding（AROIC），将编解码器视为黑盒，自动调度、执行、评估与反思ROI编码方案，无需架构修改或特定重训练。另结合Scenario-Guided Quality-Rate（SGQR）评估策略。

Result: 在干净、噪声、模糊图像上，针对标准、感知、机器视觉压缩场景的实验表明，PICA持续提升传统H.266/VVC与神经Cheng2020编解码器的压缩性能，在Kodak、CLIC2020、HEVC数据集上优于对比方法。

Conclusion: PICA通过多智能体协作与编解码器无关的区域自适应机制，实现场景自适应的跨异构编解码器压缩，提升了多种场景下的压缩表现。

Abstract: Despite decades of progress, image codecs remain difficult to adapt to diverse real-world compression scenarios for two main reasons. First, most image codecs are optimized for a specific objective and require substantial redevelopment when the compression goal changes. Second, they typically assume clean inputs, whereas sensor-captured images often contain degradations that alter rate-quality behavior. To address these challenges, we propose Practical Image Coding Agents (PICA), a multi-agent framework that adapts existing codecs to diverse user requirements and potentially degraded inputs. PICA consists of four collaborative agents: a Perception agent that interprets user intent and analyzes image content, an Improvement agent that jointly considers bitrate and reconstruction quality to guide pre-coding restoration, a Coding agent that refines quality-control settings from global to regional levels, and an After-coding improvement agent that addresses residual degradations and compression-induced artifacts through restoration guided solely by image quality. To enable codec-agnostic regional adaptation, we introduce Agentic ROI Coding (AROIC), which treats the codec as a black box and allows the agent to automatically schedule, execute, evaluate, and reflect ROI coding plans without architectural modifications or codec-specific retraining. We integrate these four agents with a Scenario-Guided Quality-Rate (SGQR) evaluation strategy to enable more effective scenario-adaptive compression across heterogeneous codecs. Comprehensive experiments on clean, noisy, and blurred images across standard, perceptual, and machine-vision compression scenarios demonstrate that PICA consistently enhances the compression performance of both the conventional H.266/VVC codec and the neural Cheng2020 codec, outperforming competing compression methods on the Kodak, CLIC2020, and HEVC datasets.

</details>


### [62] [Lightweight graph transformers for clutter and target classification in automotive radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04959)
*Adrian Gheorghiu,Tunc Alkanat,Ashish Pandharipande*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级微型图Transformer网络TGTNet，用于汽车雷达点云中杂波与静止/运动目标的分类，在RadarScenes数据集上以低1-2个数量级的模型规模和更快推理达到与SOTA相当的精度与召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车雷达场景感知中常出现杂波（对不存在运动目标的虚假检测），会损害目标检测、分类性能及后续驾驶决策，因此需要有效地将杂波与真实目标区分开。

Method: 提出轻量级微型图Transformer网络（TGTNet）模型，在雷达点云上以图结构建模并借助Transformer进行节点/目标级分类，判别杂波与静止目标、运动目标。

Result: 在公开数据集RadarScenes上评估，TGTNet的精确率与召回率与当前先进模型相近，同时模型规模低1至2个数量级，推理速度显著更快。

Conclusion: 轻量化图Transformer设计可在不牺牲分类性能的前提下大幅降低模型体量与计算成本，适合车载雷达实时场景感知中的杂波抑制。

Abstract: Radar is a core sensor modality for scene perception to achieve higher levels of autonomy in automotive driving. A common occurrence in automotive radars is clutter - detections of non-existent moving objects, that can adversely impact target detection and classification performance and subsequent driving actions. We propose lightweight tiny graph transformer network (TGTNet) models for classifying clutter from stationary and moving targets in the scene. Performance evaluation on the public RadarScenes show that our proposed TGTNet models achieve similar classification performance in precision and recall metrics in comparison to state of the art models, with one to two orders of magnitude lower model size and significantly faster inference.

</details>


### [63] [MiCaDe: Online LiDAR-Camera Miscalibration Change Detection for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04912)
*Carlo Sgaravatti,Riccardo Pieroni,Matteo Corno,Sergio M. Savaresi,Luca Magri,Giacomo Boracchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MiCaDe是一种无需标定靶标、无需改动感知流程的在线外参失准检测方案，通过跨模态检测框几何一致性指标结合变化点模型实现低误报、低开销的LiDAR-相机标定漂移检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期部署中振动、热效应与轻微机械冲击会导致LiDAR-相机外参漂移，引发下游融合任务的系统性误差，而现有标定方法难以在线持续监测外参是否仍然准确。

Method: 利用标准2D/3D目标检测器输出框在正确标定下应保持几何一致性这一洞见，匹配跨模态检测并构造几何一致性度量作为外参漂移的敏感指标；将指标聚合成固定大小批次并与参考分布比较打分，生成似然流，再由变化点模型（CPM）累积统计证据进行序列变化检测。

Result: 在多个长序列数据集以及物理施加失准的真实世界数据集上验证，可在多种条件下可靠检测外参漂移，包括其他方法难以察觉的细微漂移，且误报率低、计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 基于跨模态检测几何一致性的序列变化检测可作为一种实用、免靶标、免修改感知栈的在线标定漂移监测手段，并已开源。

Abstract: Reliable multi-sensor perception in autonomous vehicles requires accurate calibration between cameras and LiDAR. However, calibration drift is often unavoidable in long-term deployments: vibrations, thermal effects, and minor mechanical shocks may alter the relative pose between sensors, leading to systematic errors in downstream fusion tasks. We propose MiCaDe (Miscalibration Change Detection), an online solution detecting when the extrinsic LiDAR-camera calibration parameters are no longer accurate by means of sequential change detection. MiCaDe requires no calibration targets, or modification of the perception stack, providing low false-alarm rates with negligible computational overhead. Our key insight is that 3D and 2D bounding boxes produced by standard object detectors should remain geometrically consistent under correct calibration. By matching cross-modal detections, we derive geometric consistency metrics that serve as sensitive indicators of extrinsic drift. These metrics are aggregated into fixed-size batches and scored against a reference distribution, producing a likelihood stream that is monitored by a Change Point Model (CPM). Crucially, the CPM accumulates statistical evidence over time, enabling detection of subtle drifts that would otherwise go unnoticed. Experiments on multiple long-sequence datasets and on a real-world dataset with physically induced miscalibration confirm reliable detection under diverse conditions. Code available at: https://github.com/CarloSgaravatti/MiCaDe.

</details>


### [64] [SurgEye: Instruction Tuning-Free Perception-Reasoning Framework for Surgical Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04012)
*Qing Xu,Xiechen Shen,Xianjian He,Guqiao Liang,Zixiao Chai,Jiale Qin,Yifan Chen,Siyi Li,Tian Yuan,Yiwei Li,Ziheng Meng,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgEye是一个免指令微调的感知-推理框架，通过多任务路由感知与智能体式组合推理实现手术场景的grounded理解，并构建SurgEye-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术场景理解需要跨器械、解剖结构与流程的组合式推理；现有手术视觉-语言模型依赖大规模问答对进行指令微调，数据标注费力且重训练成本高。

Method: (1) Routed Multi-Task Perception (RMP)：利用公开感知标签学习任务特定通路，将视觉基础模型路由到不同路径，输出空间与时间证据；(2) Agentic Compositional Reasoning (ACR)：使用冻结的LLM，将RMP专家模块与时间记忆作为可调用工具进行协调，跨任务、跨时间组合证据生成有依据的答案；(3) 构建SurgEye-Bench，覆盖八种手术，每题配感知标签支持可验证评估。

Result: 大量实验表明SurgEye在手术推理任务上优于现有最先进方法；源代码将公开。

Conclusion: 以视觉基础模型+感知路由+冻结LLM工具调用替代指令微调，可在免昂贵标注与重训练的前提下实现可验证、可解释的手术推理。

Abstract: Surgical scene understanding is pivotal for computer-assisted intervention, yet demands compositional reasoning across instruments, anatomy, and workflow. Existing surgical vision-language models rely on instruction tuning with large-scale question-answer pairs, entailing laborious data curation and costly retraining. To bridge this gap, we propose SurgEye, a perception-reasoning framework that grounds reasoning in a vision foundation model without instruction tuning. Specifically, we devise Routed Multi-Task Perception (RMP) that routes the vision foundation model through task-specific pathways learned from public perception labels, producing spatial and temporal evidence. We further introduce Agentic Compositional Reasoning (ACR) that employs a frozen large language model to coordinate RMP specialists and temporal memory as callable tools, composing evidence across tasks and time into grounded answers. Moreover, we construct SurgEye-Bench spanning eight surgical procedures, pairing each question with perception labels for verifiable evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SurgEye outperforms state-of-the-arts in surgical reasoning. The source code will be available.

</details>


### [65] [Beyond Local Refinement: Wide-Area Precise Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04759)
*Qingguo Hu,Xinyu Ma,Yujie Lin,Yaoxiang Wang,Qi Shi,Zonghao Guo,Chi Chen,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对跨视角定位任务进行“大区域”扩展，提出搜索-验证框架RingMatch（预测不确定性驱动的渐进缩放搜索 + 深度环几何表征辅助LVLM验证），并构建VIGOR-Pro基准，在扩展后的航空区域上保持稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细粒度跨视角定位方法通常假设较窄的地理先验（范围受限），当上游定位较粗时适用性大幅下降。作者将任务扩展到更大航空区域，但该扩展带来两个本质困难：(1) 地-空视图空间范围差异增大，难以直接建立可靠的跨视角匹配；(2) 大区域内相似（look-alike）位置增多，造成显著视觉歧义。

Method: 提出RingMatch，一种“搜索-验证”式广域跨视角定位框架：先利用预测不确定性挖掘可能位置，并对其周边逐步放大（progressive zoom-in）以获取越来越细粒度的证据，收敛为紧凑候选集；再引入“深度环”（depth ring）几何表征，刻画位置周边空间布局的细微变化，作为视图共享的空间签名，供LVLM验证器消解残余歧义。同时构建基准VIGOR-Pro，将VIGOR航空瓦片拼接为空间范围最大400 m的连续区域。

Result: 实验表明，随着航空区域扩大，RingMatch仍保持较强定位性能，持续优于代表性端到端方法与检索式方法。

Conclusion: 宽域细粒度跨视角定位需以“先粗搜索、后几何验证”的方式应对尺度失衡与视觉歧义；不确定性引导的渐进缩放与深度环几何签名是有效手段，VIGOR-Pro为更大航空区域设定提供了评测基准。

Abstract: Fine-grained cross-view localization aims to precisely localize a ground camera against geo-referenced aerial imagery, but current methods typically assume a narrow geographic prior, substantially limiting their applicability when upstream localization remains coarse. To bridge this gap, we study this task over much larger aerial regions. However, this seemingly simple extension fundamentally changes the localization problem: the growing disparity in spatial extent between ground and aerial views makes reliable cross-view matching difficult to establish directly, while the increasing number of look-alike locations in larger regions introduces substantial visual ambiguity. To address these challenges, we propose RingMatch, a search-and-verification framework for wide-area cross-view localization. RingMatch first leverages predictive uncertainty to uncover plausible locations and progressively zooms into their surroundings to obtain increasingly fine-grained evidence, yielding a compact set of promising candidates. We then introduce the depth ring, a novel geometric representation that reveals subtle variations in the spatial layout around a location and serves as a view-shared spatial signature for an LVLM verifier to resolve residual ambiguity. To benchmark this setting, we construct VIGOR-Pro by composing VIGOR aerial tiles into contiguous regions with spatial extents of up to 400 m. Experiments show that RingMatch maintains strong localization performance as the aerial region expands, consistently outperforming representative end-to-end and retrieval-based methods.

</details>


### [66] [TEXMET: A Deep Learning Benchmark for Archaeological Textile Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04734)
*Muhammad Hamza Zafar,Davit Gigilashvili,Jon Yngve Hardeberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个纺织品拼图数据集TEXMET（18,644幅大都会博物馆开放馆藏纺织品图像），系统评测JPDVT、FCViT、DiffAssemble；经领域微调的DiffAssemble在纺织品上达85.89%拼图准确率，应用于45件奥斯伯格船葬残片时准确率由26.67%提升至64.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考古纺织品（如834 CE奥斯伯格船葬出土的数百件退化残片）长期缺乏系统计算分析；深度学习拼图求解虽进展显著，但受限于缺乏领域专用数据集及缺乏对现代架构的系统评测，故需建立纺织品专用基准并评估迁移能力。

Method: 构建TEXMET数据集（Met Open Access，18,644幅纺织品图像）；选取三个SOTA拼图模型JPDVT、FCViT、DiffAssemble，对比在通用数据上预训练与在TEXMET上领域微调的表现；再将微调后模型应用于45件高度退化的奥斯伯格残片。

Result: 基于Transformer的预训练模型无法泛化到纺织品；领域微调效果显著；图结构的DiffAssemble微调后在纺织品数据上达85.89%准确率；在奥斯伯格残片上由26.67%提升至64.44%。

Conclusion: TEXMET确立了纺织品重建的新基准，并表明领域自适应是将拼图求解方法应用于真实考古材料图像的必要条件。

Abstract: The reconstruction of fragmented historical textiles presents a significant challenge in computational archaeology. The Oseberg ship burial, dating to 834 CE, yielded hundreds of degraded textile fragments that have resisted systematic computational analysis. While deep learning has revolutionized image-based puzzle solving, progress on archaeological textiles has been limited by the lack of domain-specific datasets and systematic evaluation of modern architectures. To address this gap, we present TEXMET, a curated dataset of 18,644 historical textile images from the Metropolitan Museum of Art's Open Access collection, representing the first textile-specific benchmark for puzzle-solving research. Using TEXMET, we conduct a systematic evaluation of three state-of-the-art puzzle-solving models: JPDVT, FCViT, and DiffAssemble. Our experiments reveal that the transformer-based models pre-trained on standard datasets fail to generalize to textiles, but domain-specific fine-tuning proves highly effective. The graph-based DiffAssemble model, when fine-tuned on TEXMET, achieves 85.89% puzzle accuracy on textile data. Most significantly, applying this fine-tuned model to 45 highly degraded Oseberg fragments yields a performance increase from 26.67% to 64.44% puzzle accuracy. These results establish TEXMET as a new benchmark for textile reconstruction and demonstrate that domain adaptation is essential for applying puzzle-solving methods to imagery of real archaeological materials.

</details>


### [67] [BhopalMLS: A LiDAR Dataset for Semantic Scene Understanding in Heterogeneous Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04654)
*Pyare Lal Chauhan,Vaibhav Kumar,Bharat Lohani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 介绍BhopalMLS——印度博帕尔非规划密集城区的大规模MLS数据集（约10 km走廊、12亿+点、62类19,518实例），并揭示其对跨区域迁移的增益源自子类型多样性而非数据规模或走廊几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云数据集多采集于规划良好的城市，结构同质、子类型单一，无法覆盖非规划城区（mixed traffic、informal structures、狭窄不规则街巷）的形态多样性，缺乏相应基准。

Method: 构建BhopalMLS：约10 km道路走廊、>1.2B点、62个细粒度静态类别共19,518实例，聚合为12类与21类两级基准分类体系，另设6个可选动类子类；在语义与实例分割上对比微调基础模型与从零训练的监督基线；与Ke-MLS、Toronto-3D、Paris-Lille-3D、SemanticKITTI进行双向跨区域迁移实验；并在固定标签预算下做因子控制重训（含类别坍缩为单子类型）以归因增益来源。

Result: 12类基准上Utonia(L)达71.28% mIoU（PTv3从零训练为68.04%）与44.07% mAP50；跨区域实验呈一致的非对称可迁移性，BhopalMLS训练优于反向，平均21.5、最高36 mIoU点；将每类坍缩为单一子类型使Utonia(L)与Concerto(L)分别下降13.0与13.3 mIoU点，表明增益来自子类型清单覆盖度而非数据规模或走廊几何。

Conclusion: BhopalMLS为超越规划城市的3D感知提供测试平台，凸显子类型多样性对跨域泛化的关键作用；数据与交互式可视化已公开。

Abstract: We introduce BhopalMLS, a large-scale Mobile Laser Scanning (MLS) dataset from a dense, heterogeneous urban environment in Bhopal, India, with mixed traffic, informal structures, and narrow unplanned streets. It spans approximately 10 km of road corridors with over 1.2 billion points and 19,518 annotated instances across 62 fine-grained static categories, grouped into 12-class and 21-class benchmark taxonomies; 6 optional moving-class subclasses support dynamic-object analysis. Existing 3D datasets, collected in well-planned cities, are structurally more uniform than such unplanned settings, and lack their subtype diversity. On semantic and instance segmentation at both granularities, fine-tuned foundation models surpass supervised baselines: Utonia(L) reaches 71.28% mIoU (vs. PTv3's 68.04% from scratch) and 44.07% mAP50 on the 12-class benchmark. Cross-region experiments against four established datasets (Ke-MLS, Toronto-3D, Paris-Lille-3D, SemanticKITTI) reveal a consistent asymmetric transferability: models trained on BhopalMLS transfer better than the reverse on every pair, for both Utonia(L) and Concerto(L) (mean 21.5, up to 36 mIoU points), holding even when the target is denser. Factor-controlled retraining at a fixed label budget, repeated on both models, attributes this to the coverage of BhopalMLS's subtype inventory rather than to dataset size or corridor geometry: collapsing each class to a single subtype costs 13.0 and 13.3 mIoU points for Utonia(L) and Concerto(L). BhopalMLS therefore offers a testbed for 3D perception beyond well-planned cities. Data and an interactive viewer are at https://geoai4cities.github.io/bhopalmls.

</details>


### [68] [Gradient-Guided Orientation Learning for Weakly Supervised Oriented Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04616)
*Junyong Jang,Juheon Park,Minyoung Jeon,Jaehyup Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种梯度引导的朝向学习策略用于弱监督旋转目标检测（WS-OOD），从目标区域提取主梯度方向生成伪角度标签作为辅助监督，并引入角度感知的评价指标以弥补AP对角度误差不敏感的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱标注（水平框、点）不包含显式朝向信息，现有方法只能通过间接几何一致性或等变约束推断角度，在光照变化、颜色偏移、阴影和背景杂波等真实外观变化下不稳定且易产生歧义；同时AP在高空间IoU下会掩盖明显的角度误差。

Method: 利用输入图像自身蕴含的朝向先验：在目标区域内提取主梯度方向线索，将其转换为伪角度标签，作为辅助监督训练检测器以获得朝向感知表征；无需旋转框标注，也不引入额外可学习参数。此外构建将角度偏差纳入标准AP协议的角度感知评价指标。

Result: 在弱监督旋转检测基准上的大量实验表明，伪角度监督同时提升了检测性能和朝向估计质量；所提指标相比传统AP能更真实地评估角度预测。

Conclusion: 梯度导出的伪角度标签为WS-OOD提供了一种简洁有效的显式朝向监督，缓解间接监督的歧义性；角度感知指标可揭示被AP掩盖的朝向误差。

Abstract: Weakly supervised oriented object detection (WS-OOD) aims to reduce the annotation cost of rotated bounding boxes by leveraging cheaper annotations such as horizontal boxes or points. However, these weak annotations provide no explicit orientation information, forcing existing methods to infer object angles through indirect geometric consistency or equivariant constraints. Such indirect supervision often becomes ambiguous and unstable under real-world appearance variations, including illumination changes, color shifts, shadows, and background clutter. In this paper, we present a simple yet effective gradient-guided orientation learning strategy for WS-OOD. Motivated by the observation that the input image itself contains useful orientation priors, our method extracts dominant gradient-based orientation cues from object regions and converts them into pseudo-angle labels for auxiliary supervision, enabling the detector to learn orientation-aware representations without requiring rotated bounding box annotations or extra learnable parameters. Furthermore, since AP can mask substantial angular errors when spatial IoU remains high, we introduce an orientation-aware evaluation metric that incorporates angular discrepancies into the standard AP protocol. Extensive experiments on weakly supervised oriented detection benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed pseudo-angle supervision improves both detection performance and orientation estimation quality, while the proposed metric provides a more faithful assessment of angle prediction than conventional AP alone.

</details>


### [69] [Multi-Path Mamba Diffusion for 3D Point Cloud Semantic Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04583)
*Li Liang,Naveed Akhtar,Jordan Vice,Anwaar Ulhaq,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MPath Mamba扩散网络，结合状态空间建模与扩散生成模型，在VAE条件潜空间中完成3D点云语义场景补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D语义场景补全需同时估计缺失几何结构与语义信息，是自动驾驶等下游任务的关键；现有方法在长序列点云特征提取与表示重建上仍不足。

Method: 将状态空间模型（Mamba）与扩散生成方法融合：设计含skip-triple Mamba结构、维度分解残差与多方向膨胀卷积的多路径模块，用于长序列特征提取；在变分自编码器的条件潜空间中执行扩散，并以状态空间机制增强点云表示与重建。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI基准上取得极具竞争力的3D点云语义场景补全性能。

Conclusion: 状态空间建模与潜空间扩散的结合可有效提升3D语义场景补全，为长序列点云处理提供了新范式。

Abstract: 3D semantic scene completion plays a critical role in enabling various downstream tasks within autonomous systems by estimating both missing geometric structures and semantic information in scene data.
