In this work, we introduce a unique neural framework that integrates state space modeling with diffusion-based generative approaches to enhance 3D point cloud semantic scene completion. We propose \textit{MPath Mamba} diffusion network, leveraging a skip-triple Mamba structure, dimensional decomposition residuals, and multi-directional dilations within multi-path data to efficiently process long-sequence data for feature extraction. The proposed method operates in the conditioned latent space of a variational autoencoder, enhanced by a state-space mechanism to improve 3D point cloud representation and reconstruction. Extensive experiments conducted on the SemanticKITTI benchmark demonstrate that our approach achieves highly competitive performance for 3D point cloud semantic scene completion.

</details>


### [70] [SphereFlow: Missing Modality Imputation via Geometric Transport on Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04414)
*Huihan Wang,Juncheng Wang,Yuxiang Feng,Wenlong Hou,Guoqi Yu,Chao Xu,Yang Liu,Baigui Sun,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SphereFlow将多模态投影到共享超球潜空间，用flow matching把跨模态补全化为球面上的短程几何搬运，并以"数据作源"替换高斯噪声源、时间加权解码损失解决离面问题，在BraTS补全与CT-MRI翻译上达到SOTA，下游分割任务弥补约80%的与全模态oracle差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缺失模态补全方法将每个模态视为独立的潜流形，迫使生成器学习模态间冗长、无结构的跳跃，跨模态迁移距离大且缺乏几何约束，生成质量受限。

Method: 用冻结的自监督编码器将所有模态映射到共享超球潜空间，使跨模态补全变为球面上近邻点间的短程几何搬运；在此基础上采用flow matching，并做两处几何感知修改：(1) 以观测模态潜码替代标准高斯噪声源，即data-as-source，使速度场只学习跨模态位移；(2) 对解码器使用时间加权损失，把其定义域扩展到流轨迹经过的近表面区域。

Result: 理论证明data-as-source形式将最坏情况离流形偏差从随潜维度增长的量压缩为由模态间相似度控制的、维数无关的小界；BraTS多模态MRI补全与CT-MRI翻译各设置下生成保真度均达SOTA；用补全数据做下游脑肿瘤分割，弥补了约80%与全模态oracle的差距。

Conclusion: 跨模态补全的瓶颈可通过共享超球潜空间的几何对齐加几何感知的流匹配设计来消解；合理选择源分布与扩展解码器定义域是关键，能在理论与实验上同时获得收益，并有效支撑下游分割任务。

Abstract: Missing modality imputation aims to synthesize unobserved modalities from observed ones. Existing methods treat each modality as living on its own latent manifold, forcing the generator to learn a long, unstructured jump between them. We present SphereFlow, which takes a fundamentally different view: by projecting all modalities into a shared hyperspherical latent space via a frozen self-supervised encoder, cross-modal imputation reduces to short-range geometric transport between nearby points on the sphere. Realizing this idea with flow matching, however, exposes two geometric conflicts: the standard Gaussian noise source is catastrophically mismatched with the unit-norm data manifold, and the linear interpolation path departs the sphere surface into a region where the decoder has no training signal. We resolve both with simple, geometry-aware modifications: replacing the noise source with the observed-modality latent so that the velocity field learns only the cross-modal displacement, and training the decoder with a time-weighted loss that extends its domain to the near-surface region traversed by the flow. We prove that this data-as-source formulation reduces the worst-case off-manifold deviation from a quantity that grows with the latent dimension to a small, dimension-independent bound governed by inter-modality similarity. Experiments on BraTS multi-modal MRI imputation and CT-MRI translation demonstrate state-of-the-art generation fidelity across all settings, while downstream brain tumor segmentation with our imputed data closes roughly eighty percent of the gap to the full-modality oracle. Code is available at https://github.com/WTHH031230/SphereFlow.

</details>


### [71] [LCM-UNet: Scale-Selective Mamba with Reparameterizable Multi-Basis Local Modeling for Lightweight Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04409)
*Md Shihabul Islam Shovo,Nishi Kanta Paul,Dip Kumar Saha,Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LCM-UNet：33,474 参数的超轻量 Mamba U-Net，将双向 Mamba 限制在 1/16 分辨率及更粗阶段、细尺度用局部卷积，并用可重参数化多基卷积适配器增强局部分支，在 ISIC 2017/2018/PH² 上取得 Dice 88.62%/89.73%/90.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤镜病灶分割（不对称性、边界、尺寸测量）需要在基层医疗或移动端部署，因此要求极紧凑网络。现有超轻 Mamba U-Net 虽具全局建模能力，但计算代价依赖每个 Mamba 块所在分辨率，且局部分支仅用单一方形卷积核，效率与表达能力受限。

Method: 提出 U 形网络 LCM-UNet（33,474 参数）：(1) 每个跳跃连接在原生分辨率上融合；(2) 双向 Mamba 仅用于输入 1/16 分辨率及更粗的阶段，更细尺度改用局部卷积；(3) 多基可重参数化适配器——训练时在每个局部分支并联空洞、水平、垂直深度可分离卷积核，推理前折叠进宿主核；(4) 每个跳跃连接学习阶段特定的增益。在 ISIC 2017/2018 官方划分上以统一协议重训 9 个网络，PH² 作外部验证，消融在开发集进行。

Result: ISIC 2017、ISIC 2018、PH² 上 Dice 分别为 88.62%、89.73%、90.46%，在两个 ISIC 测试集上排名第一、PH² 上排名第二。在原生分辨率宿主中，从 1/8 分辨率解码阶段移除 Mamba 可消除 63% 的 Mamba 运算（占总运算 5.3%），Dice 仅变化 −0.03 个百分点。适配器相较单一宿主核提升 Dice 0.48 个百分点，且不增加部署参数与运算量。

Conclusion: 通过将全局建模限制在低分辨率阶段、细尺度采用可重参数化多基局部卷积，可在极小参数量下保持高分割精度。目前延迟仅在 GPU 上测量，LCM-UNet 是资源受限部署的候选方案，端侧实际延迟仍需验证。

Abstract: Accurate lesion segmentation underpins automated dermoscopic analysis: masks enable measurements of asymmetry, border and size. Deployment in primary care or on mobile devices requires a compact network. Existing ultra-light Mamba U-Nets offer global context, but global-modeling cost depends on each Mamba block's resolution, while their local branches rely on a single square kernel. We introduce LCM-UNet, a 33,474-parameter U-shaped network that fuses every skip at its native resolution, restricts bidirectional Mamba to stages at one-sixteenth of the input resolution or coarser, and uses local convolutions at finer scales. A multi-basis reparameterizable adapter augments each local branch during training with dilated, horizontal and vertical depthwise kernels, then folds them into the host kernel before inference. Each skip connection also learns a stage-specific gain. Nine networks were retrained on the official ISIC 2017 and ISIC 2018 partitions under a harmonized protocol, with PH$^2$ reserved for external validation and ablations run on a development split. LCM-UNet achieved Dice scores of 88.62%, 89.73% and 90.46% on ISIC 2017, ISIC 2018 and PH$^2$, respectively, ranking first on both ISIC test sets and second on PH$^2$ among the compared models. In the native-resolution host, removing Mamba from the one-eighth-resolution decoder stage eliminated 63% of Mamba operations, corresponding to 5.3% of all operations, with a Dice change of $-0.03$ percentage points. The adapter improved Dice by 0.48 percentage points over a single host kernel without adding deployed parameters or operations. With latency so far measured only on a GPU, LCM-UNet is a candidate for resource-constrained deployment.

</details>


### [72] [Spin Spherical Fourier Neural Operators: Efficient Equivariant Learning for Vector and Spin Fields on the Sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04389)
*Kevin Mulder,Matthew A. Price,Eirini Leftaki,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将球面傅里叶神经算子(SFNO)扩展到任意自旋场，通过Mackey函数提升与调和表示实现精确、高效的旋转等变球面卷积，构建S²FNO，等变性达机器精度并在自旋基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风场、CMB极化、弱引力透镜剪切等物理场是自旋场，局部标架旋转时其值相位改变；现有SFNO只能处理标量场，缺乏针对自旋场的旋转等变网络。

Method: 以旋转群上的Mackey函数作为概念框架将自旋场提升，借助调和表示及球面与旋转群上的采样定理进行精确高效计算，用这些提升球面卷积构建自旋SFNO(S²FNO)层。

Result: 实验表明各层旋转等变性能保持到机器精度；在现有基准上达到SOTA，并新提出基准上自旋感知模型优于标量对应模型。

Conclusion: S²FNO为任意自旋场提供了精确旋转等变、计算高效的算子学习框架，显著提升自旋场建模性能。

Abstract: Many fields of physical interest on the sphere, such as wind, the polarisation of the cosmic microwave background and the shear of weak gravitational lensing, are spin fields, whose values change phase when the local frame rotates. We extend spherical Fourier neural operators (SFNOs) to fields of any spin, through lifted spherical convolutions that are exactly rotationally equivariant. Lifting spin fields to Mackey functions on the rotation group provides the conceptual framework, while computation is performed exactly and efficiently through their harmonic representation and by appealing to sampling theorems on the sphere and rotation group. We construct spin SFNOs (S$^2$FNOs) from these building blocks. We demonstrate empirically that rotational equivariance holds to machine precision for these layers. We achieve state-of-the-art performance on existing benchmarks, and introduce new benchmarks on which spin-aware models outperform their scalar counterparts.

</details>


### [73] [Weather Adaptive Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04305)
*Nicholas Taylor,Alexander Picon,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究恶劣天气下冻结检测器的置信度阈值决策：以SeqCRC（逐天气CRC校准）为基线，发现其漏检率受控但误报数不可控（1.6~117/图），提出用单一旋钮λ（每避免一个漏检可接受的误报数）实现期望代价最优决策，含全局阈值与逐框规则两种形式，并在BDD100K/DAWN上训练一次、仅做验证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雨雾雪沙等恶劣天气使相机检测器召回下降，而部署时的置信度阈值同时决定漏检与误报。现有共形检测方法（如SeqCRC）的CRC步骤只约束漏检比例，却把误报数量当作结果而非可控选择，导致不同天气下误报波动巨大，缺乏可操作的部署权衡机制。

Method: 1) 复现SeqCRC，将其中按天气条件分别校准的CRC步骤作为基线，用YOLOv8x在DAWN各天气分箱中验证。2) 提出"校准权衡旋钮"：用户设定λ=为避免一个漏检可接受的误报数，据此选取期望代价最优决策规则——或为单一分数阈值，或为逐检测规则（用梯度提升树从检测器自身的逐框输出估计"该框为真实目标的校准概率"，当概率≥1/(1+λ)时保留）。3) F-score偏好可映射到同一λ；计划对λ本身施加CRC以恢复逐条件漏检保证。4) 旋钮仅在BDD100K训练集拟合一次，仅在BDD100K验证集与DAWN上评测。

Result: 基线SeqCRC配合YOLOv8x在DAWN每个天气分箱中把留出漏检比例控制在20%预算内（单一全局阈值在雾天失效），但所需误报从1.6到117/图（跨DAWN与BDD100K条件）。旋钮法给出单一可解释的代价权衡，并统一了阈值决策与F-score偏好。

Conclusion: 恶劣天气下检测器的部署阈值应作为显式的代价权衡来校准：以λ为唯一旋钮的期望代价最优决策规则，既能在漏检受控的同时让误报成为可选择量，且一次拟合即可跨数据集/天气条件泛化；后续将用CRC对λ施加校准以获得逐条件漏检保证。

Abstract: Camera object detectors lose recall in rain, fog, snow and sand, and the confidence threshold at which a detector is deployed decides how many objects it misses and how many false alarms it raises. We study how a frozen detector should make this decision in adverse weather. We first reproduce SeqCRC, a conformal object detector whose first step calibrates the confidence threshold by conformal risk control (CRC), and use that step, calibrated separately in each weather condition, as our baseline. With YOLOv8x it keeps the held-out miss fraction within a 20% budget in every DAWN weather bin, where a single global threshold fails in fog, but the false alarms it needs are an output rather than a choice: from 1.6 to 117 per image across DAWN and BDD100K conditions. We then propose a calibrated trade-off dial. One setting, $λ$, the number of false alarms accepted to avoid one missed object, selects the expected-cost-optimal decision rule: either one score threshold, or a per-detection rule that keeps a box when its calibrated probability of being a real object, estimated by gradient-boosted trees from the detector's own per-box outputs, is at least $1/(1+λ)$. F-score preferences map onto the same dial, and CRC applied to $λ$ itself is planned to restore a per-condition miss guarantee. The dial is fitted once on BDD100K train and graded only on BDD100K val and DAWN.

</details>


### [74] [Sketch-Omni: Instance-Level Sketch-Based Visual Prompting for Perception MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04262)
*Dongyu Wang,Dar-Yen Chen,Zhiming Hong,Arkaprabha Basu,Yi-Zhe Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sketch-Omni：首个面向草图的可提示感知多模态大模型，既能"用草图作图像"完成检测/指代/可供性定位，也能"以草图作提示"在照片中定位对应实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GroundingDINO、Rex-Omni 等可提示感知模型全部针对照片训练，未处理草图域；且存在两大障碍：照片训练模型的注意力偏向颜色与纹理（草图无此线索），以及缺少"一幅画—照片中唯一对应实例"的配对数据。

Method: 以合成数据替代人工采集：用边缘检测器把带标注照片转为草图式线稿并保留原标签；用两种构造方式从现有数据生成草图—照片实例对；再加"照片引导的关系对齐"，让同一模型分别处理照片与其边缘草图，训练草图通路复现照片通路中短语—图像块、图像块—图像块的关系。全程零人工标注。

Result: 草图提示定位达 91.4 F1，强通用 MLLM 为 81.8；当照片含多个同类实例时领先幅度翻倍；在六个任务、40 个评测单元中 39 项最优。将开源代码、模型及含 2754 条草图—照片链接的人工标注基准。

Conclusion: 草图可作为图像也可作为提示，统一进同一可提示感知接口；以自动构造数据与跨模态关系对齐可有效迁移照片先验至草图域，草图提示在同类多实例场景中具有类别名与照片范例均无法替代的指代能力。

Abstract: Promptable perception, from GroundingDINO to Rex-Omni, puts one model behind one prompt interface and returns coordinates for any localisation task. Every model in that line was built for photographs; none has been given a sketch. We present Sketch-Omni, to our knowledge the first perception MLLM built to work on sketches and from them. On a sketch, the sketch is the image, and text prompts detect, point, resolve object and spatial referring expressions, and ground affordances in it. From a sketch, the sketch is the prompt, and a drawing of one object localises that object in a photograph, even among several of the same category, a query that neither a category name nor a photo exemplar can pose. Two obstacles stand in the way. A photo-trained model's attention is tuned to colour and texture, which a sketch does not have, and no dataset pairs a drawing with the one instance it depicts among others of its kind. We build the training data instead of collecting it. An edge detector turns every labelled photograph into a sketch-like drawing that keeps the photograph's labels, and two constructions produce sketch-to-photo instance pairs from existing data. On top of this, photo-guided relation alignment passes each photograph and its edge sketch through the same model and trains the sketch pass to reproduce how the photograph pass relates phrases to patches and patches to each other. Not one training label is drawn by hand. Sketch-Omni reaches 91.4 F1 on sketch-prompted localisation against 81.8 for the strongest general-purpose MLLM, with a lead that doubles when the photograph holds several instances of the queried category, and it is best in 39 of 40 evaluation cells across six tasks. Code, model, and a hand-annotated benchmark of 2754 sketch-to-photo links will be released.

</details>


### [75] [TCINet: Tissue-Conditioned Adaptive Cross-Branch Interaction Network for Nuclei Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04235)
*Zhenghang Qian,Yang Liu,Yanzhi Yu,Peiwu Qin,Yibing Shen,Joongwon Chae,Lihui Luo,Zhongde Zhang,Jianwen Guo,Lianshun Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TCINet单阶段模型，通过任务特定特征适配器与组织条件化跨分支交互，兼顾核边界细节与形态上下文，在PanNuke与MoNuSeg上取得优异常全质量与检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分离粘连细胞核需要局部边界细节，而细胞类型分类需要更广泛的形态学上下文，且这两类需求随周围组织环境而变化。如何在单阶段模型中同时满足相互冲突的特征需求并按组织环境动态调节，是本文的核心问题。

Method: TCINet为单阶段多分支网络，包含（1）任务特定特征适配器TSFA，在跳跃连接处生成分支专属特征；（2）组织条件化任务感知跨分支交互模块TC-TACBI，依据预测的组织概率，在核存在分支NP、水平-垂直分支HV与核类型分支NT之间控制三条非对称交互路径。

Result: 在PanNuke标准三折交叉验证下，多类与二类全景质量（PQ）分别达0.519与0.692；在MoNuSeg上进行零样本测试，检测F1为0.876；在PanNuke fold 0上，加入TC-TACBI使mPQ由0.505提升至0.525。

Conclusion: 实验表明，任务特定特征适配与组织条件化的非对称跨分支交互能够有效解耦并协同边界细节与形态上下文需求，在无需多阶段流程的情况下提升核分割与分类的整体全景质量，并具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Separating touching nuclei requires local boundary detail, while cell type classification requires broader morphological context. These requirements also vary with the surrounding tissue environment. We propose the Tissue-Conditioned Adaptive Cross-Branch Interaction Network (TCINet), a single-stage model with Task-Specific Feature Adapters (TSFA) and Tissue-Conditioned Task-Aware Cross-Branch Interaction (TC-TACBI). TSFA produces branch-specific features at skip connections, and TC-TACBI controls three asymmetric paths among nucleus-presence (NP), horizontal-vertical (HV), and nucleus-type (NT) branches using predicted tissue probabilities. On PanNuke under standard three-fold cross-validation, TCINet achieves multiclass and binary panoptic quality scores of 0.519 and 0.692, respectively. Zero-shot tests on MoNuSeg yield a detection F1 score of 0.876. On PanNuke fold 0, adding TC-TACBI increases mPQ from 0.505 to 0.525. Code is available at https://github.com/celestial1321/TCINet.

</details>


### [76] [Reliability-Role Collaborative Learning for Semi-Supervised Cardiac Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04219)
*Zhongrui Xie,Wenfeng Zhang,Wei Hu,Ruitao Xie,Wenjie Wang,Zhouyun Xie,Yi Zhang,Tao Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R2CL将无标注图像按两网络间置信度关系划分为互不确定、置信度分歧与互置信三类区域，并分别施加熵正则、定向知识迁移与可靠监督锚点，配合原型对比学习与上下文精炼提升半监督心脏分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督心脏分割对无标注像素施加统一监督，忽视空间区域间伪标签可靠性差异，导致边界与低置信区噪声监督未被区分处理，限制难区域判别学习。

Method: 提出R2CL框架：RR-MCL按置信度关系赋予角色，通过区域混合训练、跨网络定向监督与互不确定区熵正则实现；RT-PCL以前景相邻的可靠性过渡区构建查询特征、由互置信区学习网络专属类原型进行对比学习；HCR利用互置信区内可靠内部像素与可信密集块补充稳定上下文监督。

Result: 在多个数据集的重叠与边界相关指标上取得稳定提升，在前景稀疏的MVAA-CT数据集上优势更明显。

Conclusion: 显式建模区域级可靠性差异并按角色分配合适监督信号，可有效缓解伪标签噪声并增强边界判别，验证了可靠性-角色协同学习在半监督心脏分割中的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised cardiac image segmentation aims to exploit abundant unlabeled images together with limited annotations, but the reliability of pseudo-label supervision is highly uneven across spatial regions.
  In a dual-network setting, interior regions are usually more stable, whereas boundary regions, low-confidence areas, and disagreements between networks can introduce noisy supervision.
  However, existing methods typically impose uniform supervision on all unlabeled pixels, overlooking reliability differences and limiting discriminative learning in challenging areas.
  To address this issue, we propose R2CL (Reliability-Role Collaborative Learning), a framework for semi-supervised cardiac image segmentation.
  R2CL models confidence relationships between two networks to assign roles to unlabeled regions: mutually uncertain regions are used for entropy regularization, confidence-discrepant regions for directional knowledge transfer, and mutually confident regions as reliable supervision anchors.
  Reliability-Role Mixed Cross Learning (RR-MCL) implements these roles through region-mixed training, directional cross-network supervision, and entropy regularization over mutually uncertain regions.
  Reliability-Transition Prototype Contrastive Learning (RT-PCL) constructs query features from foreground-adjacent reliability-transition regions and learns network-specific class prototypes from mutually confident regions, improving feature discrimination around ambiguous boundaries.
  High-confidence Context Refinement (HCR) exploits reliable interior pixels and trusted dense blocks within mutually confident regions to complement stable contextual supervision.
  R2CL achieves stable improvements in region-overlap and boundary-related metrics across multiple datasets, with more evident advantages on the foreground-sparse MVAA-CT dataset.

</details>


### [77] [CHROMA-MILL: Joint Low-Light Enhancement and White Balance under Colored Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04092)
*Pol Riubrogent-Comas,David Serrano-Lozano,Maria Pilligua,Michael S. Brown,Ramon Baldrich,Javier Vazquez-Corral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文构建CHROMA-MILL RAW数据集（单反与手机、4种标定光源×11个强度、50场景），在linear RAW域混合以合成带精确色度真值的光源混合样本；实验表明LLIE与AWB联合校正最优，并提出CTI-Retinexformer，联合监督光照图、色度预测与光照不变场景嵌入，较最佳级联方案提升0.7 dB PSNR_L与1.1 ΔE00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光信号弱且噪声大，同时削弱LLIE与自动白平衡（AWB）的色度推断能力；主流"先LLIE后AWB"的级联策略缺乏系统验证，原因是现有低光数据集光源范围窄且无AWB所需的色度真值。

Method: 发布CHROMA-MILL：50个场景、DSLR与智能手机、4种标定光源、11个强度等级的RAW数据集；利用光的加性在linear RAW域混合采集样本，合成物理有效的光源混合并给出精确色度真值；在该数据上系统对比级联与联合方案，提出CTI-Retinexformer，以光照图、色度预测与光照不变场景嵌入三路监督进行联合估计。

Result: 基准实验显示联合校正优于级联策略；CTI-Retinexformer相对最优级联方案提升0.7 dB PSNR_L、1.1 ΔE00。

Conclusion: LLIE与AWB应在统一框架中联合求解；带精确色度真值、覆盖多光源与多强度的RAW基准是评估该问题的关键基础设施。

Abstract: Capturing an image in a low-light environment produces a weak, noisy sensor signal. Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) aims to restore it. Auto white balance (AWB) is also required for every image, and the same noisy signal degrades its ability to infer the scene illuminant. LLIE is typically performed first, followed by AWB, but this strategy remains largely unexamined because existing low-light datasets span a narrow range of illuminants and lack illuminant ground truth for evaluating AWB. We address this with CHROMA-MILL, a RAW dataset of 50 scenes captured with a DSLR and smartphone under four calibrated illuminants at eleven intensity levels. Because light is additive, we blend these captures in the linear RAW domain to synthesize physically valid illuminant mixtures with exact chromaticity ground truth. Benchmarking on this data, we find joint correction is best, and introduce CTI-Retinexformer, which supervises an illumination map, a chromaticity prediction, and an illumination-invariant scene embedding, outperforming the best cascade by 0.7 dB PSNR$_L$ and 1.1 $ΔE_{00}$.

</details>


### [78] [FlowRig: Unified Skeleton Generation for 3D and 4D Meshes via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03775)
*Fengzhi Guo,Nate Morrill,Shiqi Li,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoTWorld：基于flow matching的免模板、数据高效框架，统一实现3D与4D骨架（rigging）生成，单次前向即可为整段序列生成时间一致的骨架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动rigging对静态3D与动态4D网格动画均为基础任务，但现有方法缺乏统一的前馈方案：3D方法质量高但骨骼数控制有限，逐帧用于动态网格时时间不稳定；4D方法则依赖昂贵的逐序列优化。

Method: 以flow matching为骨干，在一次前向中为整段序列生成时间对齐的骨架，同时自然支持单网格3D rigging；支持显式骨骼数量控制、用于候选选择的不确定性估计，以及基于掩码的插值、预测与回推（backcasting）。针对配对4D数据稀缺，采用课程式训练：先在大规模3D rigging数据上初始化，再适配中等规模4D网格-骨架数据。

Result: 在静态3D rigging上取得强性能且骨骼复杂度可控；4D重建与预测结果质量高、时间一致。

Conclusion: MoTWorld提供了统一的免模板前馈骨架生成方案，兼顾3D rigging质量与4D时间一致性，并通过课程式数据利用缓解4D配对数据不足问题。

Abstract: Automatic rigging is a fundamental task for both static 3D and dynamic 4D mesh animation, yet existing methods do not provide a unified feedforward solution for these two settings. While static 3D riggers achieve strong quality, they often provide limited control over bone count and become temporally unstable when applied frame by frame to dynamic meshes. Existing 4D methods, in contrast, typically rely on expensive per-sequence optimization. We propose MoTWorld, a template-free, data-efficient framework for unified 3D and 4D skeleton generation. Built on flow matching, MoTWorld generates temporally aligned skeletons for an entire sequence in a single feedforward pass, while naturally handling single-mesh 3D rigging. The model further supports explicit skeleton-count control, uncertainty estimation for candidate selection, and masking-based interpolation, forecasting, and backcasting. To address limited paired 4D rigging data, we adopt a curriculum that initializes from large-scale 3D rigging data and adapts to mid-scale 4D mesh-skeleton data. Experiments show that MoTWorld achieves strong static 3D rigging performance with controllable bone complexity, as well as high-quality and temporally consistent 4D reconstruction and prediction results.

</details>


### [79] [EndoS3:Endoscopic Semantic Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04358)
*Mona Sheikh Zeinoddin,Benjamin Wright,Zafer Tandogdu,Greg Shaw,Evangelos Mazomenos,Danail Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯泼溅的语义SLAM框架EndoS3，用实例掩码统一引导相机跟踪、可变形重建与语义建图，并发布CRCD++数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D手术场景理解中语义SLAM研究不足：一是需并行求解相机位姿跟踪、高保真可变形重建、语义建图三个难题；二是缺乏能在贴近真实手术条件下全面评估这三项任务的真值数据集。

Method: EndoS3以实例掩码引导三项任务：实例级运动分解模块通过补偿相机运动后的残差光流区分静态/动态实例，兼顾光流噪声与相邻实例运动比较；相机针对静态高斯进行跟踪；实例感知形变场为每个动态实例配置独立控制节点与形变网络；语义节点分配模块按实例尺寸与非刚性在实例间重分配固定节点预算。同时构建CRCD++离体胆囊切除数据集，含RGB-D帧、相机位姿与实例标注。

Result: 在CRCD++与SemSup数据集上，EndoS3在跟踪、重建与语义建图三方面均优于现有通用及手术语义SLAM方法。

Conclusion: 语义感知的运动与形变建模对手术语义SLAM至关重要；EndoS3与CRCD++为内窥镜场景几何与语义理解提供了方法与基准。

Abstract: Purpose: Despite advances in 3D surgical scene understanding, semantic simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM) remains under-explored for two key reasons. First, it requires solving three challenging tasks in parallel: camera pose tracking, high-fidelity deformable reconstruction, and semantic mapping, making progress particularly demanding. Second, the scarcity of datasets providing the necessary ground truth to evaluate all three tasks comprehensively under conditions that closely reflect real surgical procedures environments. Method: We propose EndoS3, a Gaussian Splatting-based semantic SLAM framework that uses instance masks to guide all three tasks. An instance-level motion decomposition module separates static and dynamic instances by evaluating residual flow after compensating for camera motion, accounting for flow noise and comparing motion across neighbouring instances. The camera is tracked against the static Gaussians, while an instance-aware warp field gives each dynamic instance its own control nodes and deformation network. A semantic node allocation module redistributes a fixed node budget across instances according to their size and non-rigidity. We also introduce CRCD++, an ex-vivo Cholecystectomy dataset with RGB-D frames, camera pose and instance annotations for full semantic SLAM evaluation. Results: EndoS3 surpasses all state-of-the-art general-purpose and surgical semantic SLAM methods in tracking, reconstruction and semantic mapping on the CRCD++ and SemSup datasets. Conclusion: Semantic-aware motion and deformation modelling is vital to surgical semantic SLAM. EndoS3 and CRCD++ offer a method and benchmark for improved geometric and semantic understanding in endoscopy.

</details>


### [80] [On the Learnability of Footwear Robustness: Footstep Recognition Under Unseen Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05049)
*Robyn Larracy,Erik Scheme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于压力/力敏步道足迹的身份识别在"注册固定鞋型、识别任意未见鞋型"场景下的鲁棒性问题，提出BiStepNet网络，通过学习可迁移的鞋型诱导偏移结构实现零样本跨鞋型识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足迹步态识别是一种便捷、被动的生物特征识别方式，但鞋型变化同时影响足-传感器接触界面和步态动力学，是实际部署的主要障碍。尤其困难的是注册与识别鞋型不一致的跨鞋型场景。

Method: 基于StepUP-P150数据集分析鞋型诱导的特征偏移，发现其虽个体差异显著但存在可学习的共性结构；提出BiStepNet，将空间与时间身份线索分解为可独立训练的双流结构；利用训练群体的鞋型多样性实现向未见用户与未见鞋型的零样本迁移；并通过分解式设计融合仅部分模态重叠的辅助步态数据集。

Result: 在是否穿鞋的跨鞋型识别任务上，BiStepNet优于紧凑的3D时空基线模型；识别性能随训练集中鞋型表征的数量与多样性系统性提升，且增益可迁移至训练中未直接出现的鞋型；引入部分模态重叠的辅助数据集可进一步提升对未见鞋型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 鞋型诱导偏移具有可学习、可迁移的共享结构，跨鞋型足迹识别可通过空间—时间分解架构与多样化鞋型训练实现零样本泛化，为实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Footstep patterns, captured using pressure or force-sensitive walkways during natural walking, offer a convenient and passive approach to biometric recognition. However, footwear variability poses a major challenge to practical deployment, as it impacts both the foot-sensor interface and the user's underlying gait mechanics. This study considers the particularly difficult scenario where users enroll under one fixed footwear condition but must later be recognized in arbitrary, unseen footwear. Using the StepUP-P150 dataset, we show that footwear-induced shift, while substantial and heterogeneous across individuals, contains shared structure that can be learned from a training population and transferred, zero-shot, to previously unseen users and footwear styles. We do this using BiStepNet, a novel architecture that decomposes spatial and temporal identity cues into independently trainable streams and outperforms compact 3D spatiotemporal baselines for cross-footwear recognition. We demonstrate that cross-footwear recognition performance scales systematically with the volume and diversity of footwear representation in the training set, with gains transferring to footwear styles not directly observed during training. Moreover, we show that auxiliary gait datasets with only partial modality overlap (incorporated through the architecture's decomposed design) can be leveraged to further improve robustness to unseen footwear conditions.

</details>


### [81] [Can saliency maps flag chest radiograph classifier errors at a new hospital? An external validation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05011)
*Aashish Chapagai,Angad Singh Gurtatta,Erik Cambria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在NIH ChestX-ray14上训练CNN与ViT，并在训练医院及美国、越南两家外部机构共评估每模型303,768次决策，发现显著性图（Grad-CAM）定位能力不及放射科医师，且其解剖富集度几乎不能提升基于置信度的错误检测器（AUC提升≤0.011），CNN中富集度与正确性的关系在跨院时甚至反转，故部署监测应以模型置信度而非显著性为依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显著性图常被视为医学影像分类器的"安全保障"，用以提示个体预测错误；但其能否在全新医院环境下揭示个体错误尚缺乏大规模检验。作者旨在检验显著性解释的外部有效性与错误检测价值。

Method: 在NIH ChestX-ray14上训练一个卷积网络和一个视觉Transformer；在训练医院及美国、越南两个外部机构进行大规模评估（每模型303,768次决策），以放射科医师共识标签为基准。提出"解剖富集度"指标——即分割出的胸部区域归因占比经面积归一化后的集中程度——用于给每个解释打分；并检验在置信度之外，该解释信号能否检测错误（与置信度基线错误检测器的AUC对比）。

Result: Grad-CAM的病灶定位命中率最高仅0.575，显著低于放射科医师的0.888；在训练医院校准的置信度错误检测器上叠加富集度，AUC提升从未超过0.011；对卷积网络而言，富集度与预测正确性的关系在两个医院间发生反转，导致迁移后的检测器性能下降。

Conclusion: 显著性图在跨机构场景下既不能可靠定位病灶，也不能有效补充置信度以检测个体错误，甚至可能因关系反转而损害迁移检测器。部署阶段的监测应以模型置信度为锚点，而非显著性解释。

Abstract: Saliency maps are offered as a safeguard for medical image classifiers, but whether they reveal individual errors at a new hospital remains largely untested. We trained a convolutional network and a vision transformer on NIH ChestX-ray14 and evaluated 303,768 decisions per model at the training hospital and at two external sites, in the USA and Vietnam, with radiologist-consensus labels. We scored each explanation by its anatomical enrichment, the area-normalized concentration of attribution in a segmented thoracic region, and tested whether it detects errors beyond the model's confidence. Grad-CAM localized findings less often than radiologists (hit rate at most 0.575 versus 0.888). Adding enrichment to a confidence-based error detector calibrated at the training hospital never raised its area under the curve by more than 0.011, and for the convolutional network the relation between enrichment and correctness reversed between hospitals, degrading the transferred detector. Model confidence, not saliency, should anchor deployment monitoring.

</details>


### [82] [PATTS: Parallel-Aware Test-Time Scaling for Thought-Image Generation in Multimodal Discrete Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04948)
*Yasong Dai,Zeeshan Hayder,David Ahmedt-Aristizabal,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究并行多模态离散扩散语言模型中的测试时扩展（TTS），指出仅对最终图像做验证/选择存在上限，并提出PATTS框架：在中间"思考-图像"状态上搜索，用状态依赖的可靠性权重调节跨模态验证信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成TTS多对已完成图像或图像中心的中间状态排序。作者先在GenEval上诊断CLIP式图文分数与提示内组合正确性对齐弱，验证器选择与oracle选择间存在明显差距，说明最终图像选择范式有局限。

Method: 面向并行多模态离散扩散模型（推理token与图像token在同一轨迹中联合解码），提出并行感知TTS框架PATTS：在中间thought-image状态上搜索，利用跨模态验证器信号；并用由模型内部轨迹诊断导出的状态依赖可靠性权重，调制外部语义验证在生成过程中的影响强度。

Result: 在MMaDA-Parallel固定N=32评估下，GenEval分数：无TTS 0.628，Best-of-N 0.787，FKD 0.810，PATTS 0.844。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的轨迹验证可在给定搜索预算下改进并行thought-image协同生成的测试时选择；验证器与oracle的差距表明中间状态搜索与可靠性调制是有效方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves generative models by allocating additional inference-time computation to sampling, search, and verification. Existing image generation approaches, however, typically rank completed images or image-centric intermediate states. We study TTS for parallel multimodal discrete diffusion language models, in which reasoning tokens and image tokens are decoded jointly within a single generation trajectory. We first diagnose a limitation of conventional final-image selection: on GenEval, a CLIP-style image-prompt score is weakly aligned with within-prompt compositional correctness, leaving substantial headroom between verifier selection and oracle selection. We then propose PATTS, a novel parallel-aware TTS framework that searches over intermediate thought-image states using cross-modal verifier signals. PATTS uses a state-dependent reliability weight, derived from model-internal trajectory diagnostics, to modulate the influence of external semantic verification during generation. In the fixed N=32 evaluation on MMaDA-Parallel, the PATTS reaches a GenEval score of 0.844, compared with 0.628 without TTS, 0.787 for Best-of-N, and 0.810 for FKD. These results show that reliability-aware trajectory verification can improve test-time selection for parallel thought-image co-generation under the evaluated search budget. Our code release is at https://github.com/Ringo-Star/PATTS.git

</details>


### [83] [Behind Video Generation: Observations and Insights on Multimodal Understanding from Kling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04916)
*Kling Team,Dong Chen,Jialu Chen,Xinlong Chen,Yifan Dai,Haotian Dong,Binzong Geng,Yushuo Guan,Minghui Guo,Guanlin He,Guanxiao He,Jingyun Hua,Yanwei Huang,Yiyan Ji,Xueping Jia,Yizhuo Jia,Bozhou Li,Jiaze Li,Min Li,Shihao Li,Shujie Li,Shengyi Liao,Weihong Lin,Shiyuan Liu,Xinyu Liu,Siyang Mao,Xiaoru Qu,Dianmo Sheng,Yang Shi,Jiafu Tang,Longxiang Tang,Yuqi Tang,Chengzhuo Tong,Kuo Wang,Qixun Wang,Rui Wang,Tianduo Wang,Yuran Wang,Zekun Wang,Zihao Wang,Boyang Xia,Yifan Xu,Liu Yang,Zhenlong Yuan,Bohan Zeng,Mujin Zhang,Yuanxing Zhang,Zhicheng Zhang,Zhuoran Zhang,Yuanhong Zheng,Kun Gai,Chengru Song,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告总结Kling多代研发中对多模态理解的经验，主张生成质量是多模态理解在"数据—条件—对齐—评估"全链条上的投影，并以统一多模态基座Kling-MLLM逐模块落实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成已从孤立片段走向连贯的短时音视频序列，瓶颈由渲染能力转向语义控制，因此需要系统梳理"理解栈"的构建经验。

Method: 提出数据—条件—对齐—评估四段链条并逐模块分析：统一的Kling-MLLM基座（支持图像、视频、音频、音视频同步及交错输入）；将字幕标注视为语义契约，最大化描述密度并扩展为引用绑定显式的reference-grounded captioning；提示增强含基础PE（由优质视频字幕派生，结合文本级与生成器在环强化学习）与agentic PE（细化故事并检索优质范例）；论证可靠文本编码器源于感知训练而非更强语言能力，并支持仅凭文本预筛候选；奖励建模由通用奖励模型（可解释、局部化批评）与决策奖励模型（快速多维打分）配对，并将感知带宽作为一等设计参数；最后给出暴露评分捷径、可溯源上游失败的自动评测协议。

Result: 报告给出各模块的实践性观察：字幕密度与引用绑定决定DiT的语义输入质量；文本编码器能力取决于感知训练；纯文本筛选可在端到端训练前低成本剔除候选；感知带宽应作为奖励模型设计的显式参数；特定评测协议可识别评分捷径并定位失败来源。

Conclusion: 可控视频生成的关键在于构建与之匹配的多模态理解栈，本文的经验为数据、条件、对齐与评估各环节提供可操作的工程指导。

Abstract: Video generation has progressed from isolated clips to coherent short-form audiovisual sequences, and the bottleneck is shifting from rendering to semantic control. This report summarizes the observations and insights on multimodal understanding gained from successive generations of Kling. We argue that generation quality is the projection of multimodal understanding through the entire data--conditioning--alignment--evaluation chain, and we examine this chain module by module. Kling-MLLM, a shared multimodal foundation model that unifies image, video, audio, synchronized audio--video, and interleaved inputs, is specialized for each downstream module. Because a DiT is trained on far fewer training samples than a language model, we treat captioning as a semantic contract that maximizes description density, and extend it to reference-grounded captioning that makes reference bindings explicit. Prompt enhancement translates user intent into this caption distribution: basic PE is derived from captions of compelling videos and refined with text-level and generator-in-the-loop reinforcement learning, while agentic PE refines the story and retrieves compelling examples. We show that a reliable text encoder comes from perceptual training rather than from stronger language ability, and that candidates can be screened from text alone before end-to-end training. Reward modeling pairs a general reward model for explainable, localized critiques with a decision reward model for fast multidimensional scoring, treating perceptual bandwidth as a first-class design parameter. Finally, we review task-specific automatic evaluation, focusing on protocols that expose scoring shortcuts and help trace failures to their upstream source. We hope these lessons offer practical guidance for building the understanding stack behind controllable video generation.

</details>


### [84] [EP-LVMQA: Evidence-Preserving Coarse-to-Fine Localization for Low-Quality Long Video Moment Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04913)
*Guoning Chen,Zhexun Hu,Yifan Wei,Yixuan Weng,Bin Li,Yufei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EP-LVMQA框架，将长视频问答转化为返回带时间戳证据的"时刻问答"任务，采用不确定性感知多模态证据时间线与粗细两级定位，在MedVidQA上显著提升定位与证据充分性、可验证性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学类长视频（医学教育、工业培训等）证据常不完整或不可靠：字幕缺失、ASR噪声、OCR稀疏、关键动作短暂且无旁白。现有VideoQA只输出标签或自由文本，答案依据难以核查，因此需要可返回带时间戳证据的LVMQA设定。

Method: EP-LVMQA为证据保持的由粗到细框架，包含三部分：不确定性感知多模态证据时间线、粗粒度证据规划器、证据条件细粒度定位器。后两模块间通过"证据卡"传递规划器得分、匹配线索、来源置信度代理、各模态证据、邻近上下文与时间先验。

Result: 在MedVidQA上取得26.1 R1@0.5与31.8 mIoU，较最强非本方结果分别提升6.3与5.1点；Suff.@2与Ver.@2升至25.4与22.6，提升9.5与8.8点；在匹配的低质量扰动下，平均相对退化从14.6%降至11.1%。在Ego4D-NLQ与QVHighlights上与既有专用定位方法大致相当。

Conclusion: 带时间戳的证据式回答比标签/自由文本更可核查；粗到细规划加大跨度建模能有效应对长视频多模态证据缺失与噪声，在医学教学视频上优势明显，在通用定位基准上保持竞争力。

Abstract: Long instructional videos are increasingly used in medical education, industrial training, and procedural knowledge transfer, yet their evidence is often incomplete or unreliable: subtitles may be missing, ASR noisy, OCR sparse, and decisive actions briefly shown without narration. Existing VideoQA systems typically return labels or free-form text, making answer support difficult to inspect. We study Long Video Moment Question Answering (LVMQA), where a system returns timestamped answer-supporting evidence. We propose EP-LVMQA, an evidence-preserving coarse-to-fine framework comprising an Uncertainty-Aware Multimodal Evidence Timeline, a Coarse Evidence Planner, and an Evidence-Conditioned Fine Localizer. An evidence card transfers planner scores, matched cues, source-confidence proxies, modality-specific evidence, neighboring context, and a temporal prior between the latter two modules. On MedVidQA, EP-LVMQA achieves 26.1 R1@0.5 and 31.8 mIoU, improving the strongest non-ours results by 6.3 and 5.1 points. It raises Suff.@2 and Ver.@2 to 25.4 and 22.6, gains of 9.5 and 8.8 points. Under matched low-quality perturbations, average relative degradation is reduced from the strongest non-ours value of 14.6% to 11.1%. On Ego4D-NLQ and QVHighlights, EP-LVMQA remains broadly comparable to established task-specific localization methods.

</details>


### [85] [Do We Still Need Rotation-Invariant Architectures? A Statistical Robustness Benchmark for Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03952)
*Abdulla Nasir Chowdhury,Gulpi Qorik Oktagalu Pratamasunu,Kazuhiro Fukui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统基准测试了10种点云架构在5个域下对位姿、噪声、遮挡的鲁棒性，并通过假设检验发现旋转不变性架构存在"位姿-噪声"权衡，进而提出简单的嵌入空间不变性损失训练，证明该代价可通过训练方式缓解而非内生于不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量工作将旋转不变性直接内建于点云架构，但缺乏对其在不同扰动（位姿、噪声、遮挡）下鲁棒性的统一、统计严谨的基准评测，尤其不清楚旋转不变性带来的鲁棒性代价是否为不变性本身所固有，还是源于获得不变性的特定方式。

Method: 在5个域上对10种架构（PointNet、PointNet++、DGCNN、RIConv、RIConv++、RISurConv、PointMLP、PointTransformer及等变VN-PointNet、VN-DGCNN）进行位姿/噪声/遮挡基准测试，用确认性假设检验而非点估计分析结果；随后在不改动架构的前提下，对全部10种模型施加嵌入空间上的简单不变性损失并各用3个训练种子重训，在独立设计的ModelNet40-C上评测。

Result: 三种旋转不变（RI）架构位姿鲁棒性最佳，但在5个域中的4个上噪声鲁棒性排名靠后；不变性训练迁移效果窄但可靠，在其扰动所针对的损坏族上（基线有余量处）提升显著（遮挡平均+10.5、lidar+13.4个百分点）；在同等训练预算下，最佳标准骨干与最佳RI架构在未见损坏上表现相当（每个种子内差距≤2.5个百分点），不变性训练的RIConv++是最具种子稳定性的顶级模型；拓宽旋转增广后，标准骨干在全球旋转下仅表现出平坦且与角度无关的精度损失。

Conclusion: 旋转不变性架构的噪声鲁棒性代价并非不变性本身所固有，而与不变性的获取方式相关，可通过简单的嵌入不变性训练缓解；标准骨干在充分增广后可接近RI架构表现，说明架构不变性并非必要。论文发布基准及点云鲁棒性分析与复现工具包。

Abstract: A substantial line of work builds rotation invariance directly into point cloud architectures. We benchmark 10 architectures (PointNet, PointNet++, DGCNN, RIConv, RIConv++, RISurConv, PointMLP, PointTransformer, and the equivariant VN-PointNet and VN-DGCNN) across 5 domains under pose, noise, and occlusion, and analyze the results with confirmatory hypothesis tests rather than point estimates. The three rotation-invariant (RI) architectures achieve the best pose robustness but rank among the lowest on noise robustness in 4 of 5 domains. We ask whether this cost is intrinsic to invariance or specific to how invariance is obtained. We retrain all 10 architectures unchanged, with a simple invariance loss on the embedding and three training seeds each, and evaluate all models on the independently designed ModelNet40-C. Invariance training transfers narrowly but reliably. It improves exactly the corruption families its perturbations target wherever the baseline has headroom (mean occlusion +10.5, lidar +13.4 percentage points of accuracy). At matched training budget the best standard backbone and the best RI architecture end on par on unseen corruptions (within 2.5 percentage points in every seed), and invariance-trained RIConv++ is the most seed-stable top model. Once rotation augmentation is widened, standard backbones concede only a flat, angle-independent accuracy offset under full-sphere rotation. We release the benchmark along with a package for analyzing point cloud robustness and reproducing our results.

</details>


### [86] [ProGeo-RC: Prototype-guided Semantic Reliability and Geometric Error Calibration for Semi-supervised Cardiac Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04257)
*Yunfei Zhu,Wenfeng Zhang,Pan Zeng,Xin Huang,Tao Jia,Weiting Shao,Wei Hu,Jianming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProGeo-RC框架，联合原型引导的语义可靠性估计与结构化语义-几何误差校准，为半监督医学图像分割提供更可靠的伪监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督方法仅依赖预测置信度或模型一致性评估伪标签可靠性，无法同时刻画语义可靠性与几何合理性，导致伪监督质量受限。

Method: ProGeo-RC含两个模块：PSRE用概率类原型估计无标注预测的语义可靠性并校准像素级教师伪标签；S-SGEC引入基于符号距离场（SDF）的几何约束，细化模糊边界并抑制目标结构外的虚假前景响应。

Result: 在ACDC与MVAA-CT数据集上优于代表性半监督分割方法；ACDC 10%标注下，DSC与Jaccard分别较次优结果提升0.37%和0.49%，95HD与ASD分别降低0.61和0.21。

Conclusion: 联合语义可靠性估计与几何误差校准可为半监督医学图像分割提供更可靠的伪监督，验证了语义-几何双重约束的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised medical image segmentation aims to train reliable segmentation models using limited labeled data and abundant unlabeled data. However, existing methods typically rely on prediction confidence or model consistency to estimate pseudo-label reliability, but these criteria are insufficient to characterize both semantic reliability and geometric plausibility. To address this issue, we propose ProGeo-RC, a prototype-guided semantic reliability and geometric error calibration framework for semi-supervised medical image segmentation. ProGeo-RC consists of two key modules: Prototype-guided Semantic Reliability Estimation (PSRE) and Structured Semantic-Geometric Error Calibration (S-SGEC). PSRE uses probabilistic class prototypes to estimate the semantic reliability of unlabeled predictions and calibrate pixel-wise teacher pseudo labels, while S-SGEC introduces signed-distance-field-based geometric constraints to refine ambiguous boundaries and suppress spurious foreground responses outside target structures. Experiments on the ACDC and MVAA-CT datasets show that ProGeo-RC achieves better performance compared with representative semi-supervised segmentation methods. Under the 10\% labeled setting on ACDC, ProGeo-RC improves DSC and Jaccard by 0.37\% and 0.49\% over the corresponding second-best results, while reducing 95HD and ASD by 0.61 and 0.21, respectively. These results indicate that joint semantic reliability estimation and geometric error calibration can provide more reliable pseudo supervision for semi-supervised medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [87] [CounterSketch: Physics-Grounded Counterfactual Reasoning for Sketch-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04244)
*Hien Trinh,Tan-Hiep To,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CounterSketch框架，将自然语言"假设…会怎样？"查询转化为物理合理的草图动画，通过VLM场景解析、因果事件链推理、约束轨迹生成与验证，并以物体运动包围盒引导下游草图视频合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索静态草图在假设性干预下如何演化（反事实草图动画），并解决直接依赖VLM预测生成运动时物理不合理的问题。

Method: 四模块集成：VLM引导的场景grounding、因果事件链推理、基于约束的轨迹生成、物理/几何/语义约束验证；建模物理状态转移更新场景图，计算连续物体轨迹，并将验证后轨迹投影为物体级运动包围盒指导草图视频合成。

Result: 在CounterSketchBench（80个问题）上，物理grounded轨迹比直接VLM预测提供更强的运动引导。

Conclusion: 以物理约束为核心的轨迹生成与验证可有效提升反事实草图动画的运动合理性与引导质量。

Abstract: Counterfactual sketch animation explores how a static sketch evolves under a hypothetical intervention. We present CounterSketch, a physics-grounded framework that transforms natural-language ``What if...?'' queries into physically plausible sketch animations. The framework integrates VLM-guided scene grounding, causal event-chain reasoning, constraint-based trajectory generation, and verification. It models physical transitions to update the scene graph, computes continuous object trajectories, and validates them against physical, geometric, and semantic constraints. The verified trajectories are then projected into object-level motion bounding boxes to guide downstream sketch-video synthesis. Experiments on CounterSketchBench (80 questions) demonstrate that physics-grounded trajectories provide stronger motion guidance than direct VLM predictions.

</details>


### [88] [Improving Diffusion-Based Augmentation for Data-Scarce Image Classification via Mask-Guided Reference Attention and Repulsive Noise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04242)
*Hyun-Jic Oh,GaYeon Koh,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出采样端双组件框架：Mask-Guided Reference Attention仅用参考特征更新前景value token以增强细粒度细节，Repulsive Noise Optimization使初始噪声偏离主导生成模式以提升多样性，在长尾单样本设置下显著提升生成式数据增强的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的数据增强流程在极端数据稀缺（如长尾单样本）场景下存在双重失效：生成样本缺乏对分类器训练至关重要的判别性前景细节；适配后的类别先验会偏置早期去噪过程，导致不同文本提示下输出图像模式趋同、多样性不足。

Method: 提出采样端框架，含两个互补组件：1) Mask-Guided Reference (MGR) Attention——借助零样本分割，仅用参考特征更新前景value token，将参考影响限制在目标物体上，避免过度全局特征注入；2) Repulsive Noise Optimization (RNO)——促使初始噪声轨迹偏离适配模型的主导生成模式，使目标提示能表达更广的上下文变化。

Result: 实验显示性能一致提升，在CUB-LT IF100长尾单样本设置下最高提升2.50与2.94个点；在少样本与背景偏移评测中也表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过在采样端同时增强前景细节与生成多样性，可有效缓解扩散数据增强在极端数据稀缺场景中的双重失效问题，且方法具有跨设置的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based data augmentation has emerged as a powerful strategy for addressing data-scarce image classification. Recent pipelines typically adapt a pretrained text-to-image diffusion model to a limited number of class examples and subsequently sample synthetic images using diverse textual prompts. However, in extreme data-scarce regimes such as long-tailed single-sample settings, this approach suffers from a dual failure mode: the generated samples often lack discriminative foreground details crucial for classifier training, and the adapted class prior can bias the early denoising process, causing output images to exhibit similar patterns despite varying text prompts. To overcome these limitations, we propose a sampling-side framework of two complementary components that simultaneously enhance fine details and diversity. First, Mask-Guided Reference (MGR) Attention updates only foreground value tokens with reference features via zero-shot segmentation, limiting reference influence to the target object and mitigating excessive global feature injection. Second, Repulsive Noise Optimization (RNO) encourages initial noise trajectories to diverge from dominant generative modes of the adapted model, enabling target prompts to express broader contextual variation. Extensive evaluations show consistent improvements, with the largest gains in long-tailed single-sample settings of up to 2.50 and 2.94 points on CUB-LT IF100, and further robustness across few-shot and background-shift evaluations.

</details>


### [89] [Statistical Machine Learning for Medicinal Plant Leaves Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04193)
*Jayani P. Gamage,Thiyanga S. Talagala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MEDIPI统计机器学习方法，基于叶片照片的52个可解释特征（形状、纹理、颜色及新提出的scagnostics类别）进行三级层次分类（形状→边缘类型→物种），并发布含31种斯里兰卡药用植物、1099张叶片图像的MedLEA R包数据集；层次随机森林取得100%准确率，但跨数据集泛化仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药用植物识别通常依赖从业者经验，缺乏可重复性；作者希望用可复现的、基于图像的方法补充传统知识，实现药用植物叶片的自动化识别。

Method: 从每张叶片图像提取52个可解释特征，覆盖形状、纹理、颜色以及新提出的scagnostics特征类别；采用三级层次分类框架（先形状、再边缘类型、最后物种）；构建并公开MedLEA数据集（31种斯里兰卡药用植物、1099张叶片图像，R包形式）；对比层次与非层次方法及8种算法：随机森林、XGBoost、梯度提升、线性判别分析、k近邻、支持向量机、概率神经网络和人工神经网络。

Result: 层次随机森林取得最佳总体准确率（100%）；在形状特征基础上加入纹理、颜色和scagnostics特征后，形状层级准确率从90%提升至97%；LDA图展示了训练模型对叶片形状的区分能力；当训练集与测试集来自不同数据集时，准确率骤降至23%–50%。

Conclusion: 基于可解释特征的层次分类框架在单一数据集上可实现对药用植物叶片的高精度识别，特征融合（纹理、颜色、scagnostics）显著提升形状层级性能；但跨数据集的泛化能力仍是主要瓶颈，未来需重点解决域偏移问题。

Abstract: Medicinal plants are usually identified using practitioners' experience, but reproducible, image based methods can complement this knowledge. We present MEDIPI (MEDIcinal Plant Identification), a statistical machine learning approach that classifies medicinal plant leaves from photographs. From each leaf image we extract 52 interpretable features covering shape, texture, color, and a new scagnostics category. We then classify leaves using a three level hierarchy: shape, then edge type, then species. We also release MedLEA, an open R package with 1,099 leaf images of 31 Sri Lankan medicinal plant species. On this dataset, a hierarchical Random Forest achieved the best overall accuracy (100%) in our comparison of hierarchical and non-hierarchical approaches and eight algorithms: Random Forest, XGBoost, Gradient Boosting, linear discriminant analysis, k-nearest neighbor, support vector machine, probabilistic neural network, and artificial neural network. Adding texture, color, and scagnostic features to shape features raised shape level accuracy from 90% to 97%. Linear discriminant analysis plots show how the trained model separates leaf shapes. Accuracy dropped sharply (23-50%) when training and test data came from different datasets, so generalization across datasets remains a challenge.

</details>


### [90] [Co-Occurrence Priors Override Visual Spatial Evidence: Understanding False Alarms in Vision-Language Models for Construction Struck-By Hazard Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04112)
*Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究揭示视觉语言模型（VLM）的"打击（struck-by）"危险误报主要源于决策阶段被学到的共现先验主导，而非物体识别或空间编码失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 日益用于施工危险监测，但频繁误报机制不清；作者试图区分误报来自物体识别失败、空间关系编码失败，还是受共现先验主导的危险决策失败。

Method: 构建 355 对反事实"工人—挖掘机"场景对（控制空间邻近度、并设工人移除变体），评估 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 与 InternVL3.5-8B；采用行为分析、内部表征探针、因果干预（注意力头/神经元级干预、激活引导）与决策校正。

Result: 两模型均能识别实体并编码空间关系（out-of-fold AUROC 0.82 与 0.91），79% 与 73% 的场景对中近距离危险分高于远距离；但远距离场景误报率达 74% 与 100%。移除工人使 Qwen 危险率降至 11%；工人存在对危险分的影响是空间邻近度的 2.4 倍与 3.2 倍。三类因果干预均未能消除共现偏置；将危险决策条件化于模型自身空间关系表征后，Qwen 在等敏感度下特异性由 0.36 升至 0.58，优于阈值重校准。

Conclusion: VLM 打击类危险误报本质是决策层失败而非识别或空间编码缺陷；后续工作应优先将危险决策条件化于已编码的空间证据，而非仅提升视觉识别能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly support construction hazard monitoring, yet their frequent false alarms remain poorly understood. This study investigates whether false struck-by hazard warnings arise from failures in object recognition, spatial relation encoding, or hazard decisions dominated by learned co-occurrence priors. We evaluated Qwen2.5-VL-7B and InternVL3.5-8B using 355 counterfactual worker-excavator scene pairs with controlled spatial proximity and worker-removal variants, combining behavioral analysis, internal representation probing, causal interventions, and decision correction. Both models recognized the entities and encoded their spatial relations (out-of-fold AUROC: 0.82 and 0.91), assigning higher hazard scores to near than far scenes in 79% and 73% of pairs. Nevertheless, 74% and 100% of far scenes were falsely classified as hazardous. Removing the worker reduced Qwen's hazard rate to 11%, while worker presence exerted 2.4- and 3.2-times greater influence on hazard scores than spatial proximity in the respective models. Attention-head interventions, neuron-level interventions, and activation steering failed to eliminate this co-occurrence bias. However, conditioning hazard decisions on the model's own spatial-relation representation improved Qwen's specificity from 0.36 to 0.58 at matched sensitivity, outperforming threshold recalibration. These findings reveal that VLM struck-by false alarms stem primarily from decision-making failures rather than deficiencies in object recognition or spatial encoding, suggesting that future efforts should prioritize conditioning hazard decisions on encoded spatial evidence rather than further improving visual recognition alone.

</details>


### [91] [DPAGait: Dynamic Pose-Knowledge-Aware Network for Robust Gait Recognition in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04950)
*Zenan Lin,Junduan Huang,Kun Liu,M. Saad Shakeel,Wenxiong Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出姿态知识感知的野外步态识别方法：以Dynamic Pose-Parsing enhanced Silhouette（DPPS）表示提升信息熵与细粒度动态线索，并构建2D动态姿态分支与3D SMPL分支的双分支网络融合特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步态识别主要依赖信息熵低的二值轮廓图，未能充分利用步态序列中的动态信息，对换装、携物、视角变化及环境动态变化敏感，跨时间、野外场景下精度与鲁棒性显著下降。

Method: 1）提出DPPS步态表示范式，将姿态-解析（pose-parsing）线索与轮廓融合，提高信息熵并捕捉细粒度行为线索；2）构建双分支Dynamic Pose-Knowledge-Aware Gait网络：2D动态姿态感知分支从DPPS表示提取姿态增强特征，3D分支基于SMPL模型捕获丰富的三维动态姿态信息；3）融合两分支特征完成识别。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的野外步态数据集上验证了DPPS表示的有效性与泛化性；完整框架在SUSTech1K、CCPG、Gait3D（SMPL信息可用或可一致估计）上取得有竞争力的性能，并在多样真实条件下表现稳健。

Conclusion: 将姿态-解析线索与轮廓融合并以2D+3D双分支动态姿态知识建模，可有效缓解低信息熵与动态信息利用不足的问题，提升野外步态识别的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Gait biometrics aims at identity recognition by appearance or pose features captured during unconstrained human walking. Existing methods predominantly rely on binary silhouette images, inherently characterized by low information entropy, and fail to fully utilize the dynamic information in gait sequences, limiting their ability to handle cross-time variations effectively. Consequently, these methods encounter significant challenges in the wild. They are susceptible to variations in clothing, carried objects, and viewpoints, as well as dynamic environmental conditions, all of which lead to substantial degradation in recognition accuracy and robustness. To overcome these challenges, we propose a pose-knowledge-aware approach specifically designed for robust gait recognition in the wild. First, we propose a novel Dynamic Pose-Parsing enhanced Silhouette gait representation paradigm that substantially enhances the information entropy and effectively captures fine-grained behavioral cues by integrating pose-parsing cues with traditional silhouettes. Subsequently, we develop a dual-branch Dynamic Pose-Knowledge-Aware Gait network. The framework consists of two complementary branches: (1) a 2D dynamic pose-aware branch that extracts pose-enhanced features from our proposed DPPS representations, and (2) a 3D Skinned Multi-Person Linear branch that leverages the 3D SMPL model to capture rich and comprehensive 3D dynamic pose information. Finally, the features are integrated to facilitate accurate and robust gait recognition. Extensive experiments on four challenging in-the-wild gait datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of the proposed DPPS representation. The complete framework is further evaluated on SUSTech1K, CCPG, and Gait3D, where SMPL information is available or can be consistently estimated, achieving competitive performance under diverse real-world conditions.

</details>


### [92] [Learning Where to Look: Observation Policy Optimization for Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04943)
*Junfeng Wang,Jiawei Liu,Yongchao Xu,Tao Jiang,Jiangbo Ai,Jin Zhang,Chong Wang,Qixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出观察策略优化（OPO），针对"以图思考"中观察动作（裁剪/缩放）信用分配困难的问题，用不确定性引导的观察分支与证据局部的优势归因，专门优化边界框式观察动作，从而提升视觉证据获取与复杂视觉问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂VQA常需模型"以图思考"，即通过放大高分辨率图像中的信息区域主动获取局部视觉证据。错误分析显示，多数失败源于错误的观察动作：模型未能定位关键区域，或取得的裁剪不足以支撑作答。进一步观察到边界框token的生成熵显著高于普通语言token，说明模型在决定"看哪里"时高度不确定。而现有基于最终答案奖励的强化学习方法只提供轨迹级反馈，难以对观察动作做精确信用分配。

Method: OPO包含两个核心模块：(1) 不确定性引导的观察分支（UOB）：将每次图像放大工具调用视为一次观察事件，在rollout过程中仅在高不确定性、低冗余的事件处进行选择性分支，以高效探索备选裁剪决策及由此产生的视觉证据；(2) 证据局部化的优势归因（ELAA）：利用局部证据充分性奖励比较同级兄弟分支，并将信用仅分配给对应的观察动作token。由此将观察动作优化与轨迹级结果监督解耦。

Result: 实验表明，OPO在多个规模上稳定提升Qwen3-VL模型的表现，并在与更大模型及工具增强基线的比较中取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 以轨迹级结果奖励为主的RL难以优化"看哪里"这类观察动作；通过在高不确定性观察事件处分支并以局部证据充分性奖励对观察动作做细粒度信用分配，可有效改善视觉证据获取，并进一步提升视觉推理性能。代码已公开。

Abstract: Complex visual question answering often requires vision-language models to think with images, i.e., actively acquire local visual evidence by zooming into informative regions of high-resolution images. Our error analysis shows that many failures in this setting originate from incorrect observation actions, where the model fails to localize key regions or obtain crops that contain sufficient evidence for answering. We further observe that bounding-box tokens exhibit substantially higher generation entropy than ordinary language tokens, indicating high uncertainty when the model decides where to look. However, existing reinforcement learning methods based on final-answer rewards provide only trajectory-level feedback, making it difficult to assign precise credit to the observation actions. We propose Observation Policy Optimization (OPO), an RL algorithm for optimizing observation actions in tool-augmented thinking with images. OPO consists of two core modules. First, Uncertainty-Guided Observation Branching (UOB) treats each image zoom-in tool call as an observation event and selectively branches at high-uncertainty, low-redundancy events during rollout, enabling efficient exploration of alternative cropping decisions and their resulting visual evidence. Second, Evidence-Localized Advantage Attribution (ELAA) compares sibling branches using local evidence-sufficiency rewards and assigns credit only to the corresponding observation-action tokens. By decoupling observation-action optimization from trajectory-level outcome supervision, OPO improves visual evidence acquisition and further enhances visual reasoning performance. Experiments show that OPO consistently improves Qwen3-VL models across scales and achieves competitive performance against larger models and tool-augmented baselines. Code is publicly available.

</details>


### [93] [Phase-DGS: Phase-guided Dynamic Gaussian Splatting from Unsynchronized Multi-view Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04666)
*Hosung Jeon,Jun Young Jeong,Sangwoon Kwak,Joonsoo Kim,Sangmin Kim,Jaesik Park,Won-Sik Cheong,Hyon-Gon Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Phase-DGS，用"语义相位"替代时间戳为每帧编码运动周期中的内在状态，使同一运动状态的帧跨相机共享相位，从而在无同步相机下建立时空对应，并可与现有 DGS 骨干无缝集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态高斯泼溅（DGS）的时空场景重建默认所有相机时间完美同步，但工业级同步依赖昂贵专用硬件与复杂布置；音频对齐、几何匹配、位姿跟踪等数据驱动方案又受环境假设或场景特定假设限制。因此需要一个不依赖硬件同步、通用性更强的方案。

Method: 以"语义相位"取代传统时间戳：为每帧估计其在底层运动周期中所处的固有状态并编码为相位，把不同相机、不同时刻拍摄的同一运动状态映射到同一相位，从而在非同步相机之间自然建立跨时空对应；该相位作为可插拔模块接入现有 DGS 骨干（RealTime-4DGS、FreeTimeGS）。

Result: 在时间偏移、丢帧与相机冻结三种失同步条件下验证，相较基线最高取得 +8.5 dB PSNR 提升，并能与 RealTime-4DGS、FreeTimeGS 等现有骨干无缝集成。

Conclusion: 以运动周期语义相位替代绝对时间戳，是解决非同步多相机动态重建的一条有效且通用的途径，摆脱了对专用同步硬件的依赖，并具备良好的骨干兼容性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal scene reconstruction with Dynamic Gaussian Splatting (DGS) fundamentally depends on perfect temporal synchronization across all cameras, an assumption rarely achievable in practice. Industry-standard synchronization requires expensive specialized hardware and complex setup, while data-driven alternatives including audio-based alignment, geometry matching, and pose tracking remain limited by restrictive environmental or scene-specific assumptions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Phase-DGS, a framework that replaces conventional timestamps with a semantic phase representing each frame's inherent state within the underlying motion cycle. Frames capturing the same motion state share the same phase regardless of which camera records them or when, naturally establishing spatiotemporal correspondence across unsynchronized cameras. We validate Phase-DGS under temporal offsets, frame drops, and camera freezes, achieving up to +8.5 dB PSNR improvement over baselines while enabling seamless integration with existing DGS backbones including RealTime-4DGS and FreeTimeGS.

</details>


### [94] [MambaHand: Local Identity and Behavioral Sequence Learning with Mamba for Dynamic Hand Gesture Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04626)
*Zenan Lin,Junduan Huang,Xilai Wang,Wenxiong Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双流框架 MambaHand Net，将 CNN 与 Mamba 结合用于动态手势认证（DHGA），在 SCUT-DHGA 上取得 SOTA 平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DHGA 兼具生理与行为身份特征，但现有方法多依赖卷积网络或手工设计的运动表示，难以同时捕获手势执行中的细粒度局部身份差异与长程行为依赖。

Method: 双流身份表示学习框架：局部身份流（LIS）用卷积网络提取逐帧局部身份线索；行为序列流（BSS）保留中间视觉特征的 spatial tokens，构建一阶与二阶时间差分序列以建模多阶行为变化，并引入双向 Mamba 在不同空间位置上建模语义运动，实现手势习惯的高效长程建模；最后通过注意力引导的 spatial token 聚合与双流融合策略，将局部身份线索与多阶行为模式整合为判别性身份表示。

Result: 在 SCUT-DHGA 基准数据集上取得 SOTA 平均性能。

Conclusion: CNN 与 Mamba 的结合是 DHGA 的一种有效新方案，可兼顾局部身份细节与长程行为依赖建模。

Abstract: Dynamic hand gesture authentication (DHGA) is an emerging biometric technique that contains both physiological and behavioral identity characteristics. However, existing methods primarily rely on convolutional networks or manually designed motion representations, making it difficult to jointly capture fine-grained local identity differences and long-range behavioral dependencies during hand gesture execution. To address these challenges, we propose a two-stream identity representation learning framework, MambaHand Net, to combine CNNs with Mamba for DHGA. The proposed framework consists of a Local Identity Stream (LIS) and a Behavioral Sequential Stream (BSS). The LIS extracts frame-wise local identity cues with a convolutional network, while the BSS preserves spatial tokens from intermediate visual features and constructs first-order and second-order temporal difference sequences to model multi-order behavioral variations. Moreover, the bidirectional Mamba is incorporated into the BSS to model semantic motion across different spatial locations, enabling efficient long-range modeling of hand gesture execution habits. Finally, attention-guided spatial token aggregation and a two-stream fusion strategy are used to integrate local identity cues and multi-order behavioral patterns into a discriminative identity representation. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) average performance on the SCUT-DHGA benchmark dataset, demonstrating that combining CNNs with Mamba is an effective new solution for DHGA.

</details>


### [95] [SparseGraph-I2P: Consistency-Guided Hypothesis Selection for Efficient Image-to-Point-Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04591)
*Ziyang Yu,Xiang Li,Qiong Chang,Jun Miyazaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparseGraph-I2P通过"粗假设图"选择固定预算的patch对并用稀疏精匹配、深度感知过滤提升位姿求解器内点率，在RGB-D Scenes V2与7-Scenes上取得最高配准召回率与最低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗到精I2P配准中，粗阶段可能丢弃正确但歧义的patch对；若保留更多候选，则精匹配会在重叠patch上重复计算，导致效率与召回率之间的矛盾。目标是通过提高位姿求解器的内点率来提升配准召回率，且可利用传感器深度或单目深度。

Method: 1) 粗假设图（Coarse Hypothesis Graph）：结合外观与深度导出的一阶、二阶兼容性，选择固定预算的patch对；2) 稀疏精匹配：每个查询像素只解码一次，并跳过被祖先patch与同一点云区域配对所覆盖的像素—点比较；3) 传感器深度下用SC2谱支撑度对对应排序做深度感知过滤，单次EPnP求解；4) 单目深度下先由对应几何标定深度或逆深度，再过滤。

Result: 使用传感器深度时，在RGB-D Scenes V2和7-Scenes上均取得所比较方法中最高的配准召回率和最低延迟；使用预测深度时，在RGB-D Scenes V2上保持最高召回率，但带来额外的标定开销。

Conclusion: SparseGraph-I2P以固定预算的图式粗筛选加稀疏精匹配有效提高内点率，兼顾召回率与效率；对传感器深度与单目深度均适用，代码将开源。

Abstract: Coarse-to-fine image-to-point-cloud (I2P) registration risks discarding correct but ambiguous patch pairs, while retaining more candidates repeats fine matching over overlapping patches. SparseGraph-I2P aims to improve registration recall by increasing the inlier ratio at the pose solver, using sensor or monocular depth. A Coarse Hypothesis Graph combines appearance with depth-derived first- and second-order compatibility to select a fixed budget of patch pairs. Sparse Fine Matching decodes each queried pixel once and skips pixel--point comparisons covered by an ancestor patch paired with the same point-cloud region. With sensor depth, depth-aware filtering ranks correspondences by SC2 spectral support for a single EPnP solve. With monocular depth, depth or inverse depth is calibrated from correspondence geometry before filtering. SparseGraph-I2P achieves the highest registration recall and lowest latency among the compared methods on RGB-D Scenes V2 and 7-Scenes with sensor depth. With predicted depth, it retains the highest recall on RGB-D Scenes V2 at an additional calibration cost. Code will be released.

</details>


### [96] [Learning to Falsify: Hierarchical Multiple Instance Learning for High Resolution AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04529)
*Haoran Chen,Daiqi Gao,Xinyuan Li,Yongwei Wang,Lei Zhu,Rui Cai,Jianfeng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"证伪驱动"的多示例学习范式检测AI生成图像：单块伪造即可证伪整图，并据此设计分层MIL框架LTF、训练期Cross-Shuffle增强局部伪影证据学习、以及免重训练的测试时聚合模块FATAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型使合成图像日益逼真，现有检测方法多依赖整图级分类，易忽略局部伪影，在高分辨率图像上尤为受限，泛化性与鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将该任务建模为证伪驱动的多示例学习（falsification-driven MIL）：一块伪造即证伪整图，真图需全部块真实。基于此提出Learning to Falsify (LTF)分层MIL框架，将图像在不同空间层级建模为多个示例包，并按证伪原则聚合预测；训练中引入Cross-Shuffle促使模型学习局部伪影证据而非图像级捷径；并提出Falsification-driven Test-time Aggregation Strategy (FATAS)，作为即插即用模块，将同样的证伪原则用于聚合现有检测器的块级预测。

Result: 大量实验表明，LTF在多个基准上取得强泛化性与鲁棒性；FATAS无需重训练即可显著提升代表性检测器在高分辨率场景下的性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以局部伪影证据为核心的证伪式MIL建模优于整图分类，且证伪原则可推广为通用测试时聚合策略，兼具检测精度与实用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative models have made synthetic images increasingly photorealistic, posing growing challenges to reliable and generalizable detection of AI-generated images. Existing methods typically rely on holistic image-level classification, which may overlook local artifacts, especially in high-resolution images. To address this limitation, we formulate this task as a falsification-driven multiple instance learning problem, where a single fake patch can falsify the whole image, while a real image requires all patches to be authentic. Based on this formulation, we propose \textit{Learning to Falsify (LTF)}, a hierarchical multiple instance learning framework that models an image as multiple bags of instances across different spatial levels and aggregates their predictions under the falsification-inspired principle. We further introduce Cross-Shuffle during training to encourage the model to learn local artifact evidence rather than image-level shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the \textit{Falsification-driven Test-time Aggregation Strategy (FATAS)}, a plug-and-play module that extends the same falsification-inspired principle to existing detectors by aggregating patch-level predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LTF achieves strong generalization and robustness across multiple benchmarks, while FATAS substantially improves representative detectors in high-resolution scenarios without retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/HuiGuanLab/LTF.git

</details>


### [97] [DynOSeg: Dynamic Object Segmentation via Motion-Geometry Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04452)
*Xu Han,Yiduo Wang,Yiran Wang,Viorela Ila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynOSeg，一种两阶段融合的运动目标分割方法，先以几何感知残差图与深度编码构建几何运动表征，再引入语义特征精化目标理解，并构建CARLA-MOS等训练语料，在多个数据集上超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOS方法大多仅依赖纯视觉运动线索（光流、点轨迹），在相机与物体运动复合以及运动导致的外观变化（阴影、反射）下存在固有歧义；简单拼接深度、语义等额外信息无法充分发挥其互补性。

Method: 两阶段融合框架：第一阶段融合几何感知残差图与深度编码，形成运动的几何表征，区分相机运动与物体动态；第二阶段引入语义特征精化物体级理解。此外整理多样化训练语料，包括新的合成城市驾驶数据集CARLA-MOS。

Result: 在多种数据集上实验，DynOSeg稳定优于已发表的最先进MOS方法。

Conclusion: 结构化的分阶段融合（几何先行、语义精化）比朴素多模态拼接更有效地利用互补信息，验证了几何与语义分层融合对动态场景理解的有效性。

Abstract: Moving Object Segmentation (MOS) detects and separates dynamic objects from the static background in a video, which plays a fundamental role in dynamic-scene understanding. Most MOS methods rely on pure visual motion cues alone, such as optical flow or point tracks, which is inherently ambiguous due to the compounded camera and object motions as well as motion-induced appearance changes, e.g. shadows and reflections. Naive fusion of additional information such as depth and semantics, however, cannot leverage their complementarity to the fullest extent. We therefore propose DynOSeg, a novel MOS approach that takes on a two-stage fusion structure. DynOSeg first combines a geometry-aware residual map and depth encoding into a geometric representation of motion, delineating camera motions and object dynamics. We then further incorporate semantic features to refine object understanding. We have additionally curated a diverse training corpus including CARLA-MOS, a new synthetic urban-driving dataset for MOS training. Our experiments show that DynOSeg consistently outperforms published state-of-the-art MOS methods across a diverse range of datasets. Our code and pretrained models can be found at https://github.com/ACFR-RPG/DynOSeg

</details>


### [98] [SWAP-INR: Implicit Neural Representation with Switchable Attention Paths for Time-varying Multivariate Scientific Data Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04423)
*Hyunsoo Son,Jeonghyun Noh,Chaoli Wang,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWAP-INR：面向多变量时变仿真数据的高保真隐式神经表示压缩方法，通过可切换注意力路径与可学习通道调制提升跨变量建模与频谱自适应能力，并结合16位量化实现SOTA压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学仿真数据规模巨大，在高性能计算环境中造成严重存储瓶颈。现有隐式神经表示（INR）方法作为连续型有损压缩替代方案，存在两点不足：1）难以显式建模变量间交互；2）无法适应不同物理变量各异的频谱特性。

Method: 提出SWAP-INR架构，含两个核心组件：(1) 可切换注意力路径模块（SWAP），通过跨变量注意力层显式建模变量间交互，再依据确定性的、由损失驱动的准则将每个变量路由至注意力或非注意力路径，并剪除未激活层以实现结构压缩；(2) 适应性增强SIREN（A2-SIREN），以逐通道可学习调制取代刚性频率缩放，以极小开销适配异质物理特征。整体结合训练后16位量化。

Result: 在小型模块化反应堆（SMR）数据集上达到SOTA性能，在公开物理仿真数据集上保持竞争力，同时维持受限的内存占用。

Conclusion: SWAP-INR通过跨变量注意力建模、损失驱动的路径路由与剪枝、通道级可学习频率调制及后训练量化，在保证低内存开销前提下有效提升了多变量时变仿真数据的隐式神经压缩保真度与泛化性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Visualizing multivariate scientific simulation data is essential for understanding complex physical phenomena, yet the massive scale of such data imposes a severe storage bottleneck in high-performance computing environments. Implicit neural representations (INRs) have emerged as a promising continuous alternative to traditional lossy compressors, but existing methods struggle to explicitly capture cross-variable interactions and to adapt to the diverse spectral characteristics of physical variables. To address these limitations, we propose SWAP-INR, an architecture for high-fidelity compression of multivariate, time-varying simulation data. SWAP-INR introduces two key components: (1) The switchable attention path (SWAP) module explicitly models cross-variable interactions through a cross-variable attention layer, then routes each variable to an attention or non-attention path by a deterministic, loss-driven criterion, and prunes the inactive layers for structural compression. (2) The adaptability amplification SIREN (A2-SIREN) replaces rigid frequency scaling with channel-wise learnable modulation, adapting to heterogeneous physical features with negligible overhead. Combined with post-training 16-bit quantization, SWAP-INR achieves state-of-the-art performance on small modular reactor (SMR) datasets and remains competitive on public physics simulation datasets, while maintaining a constrained memory footprint. Our code is https://github.com/hvcl/SWAP-INR.

</details>


### [99] [GVT3D: Gesture-Vision-Text Token Enrichment for 3D Embodied Reference Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04422)
*Vimarsh Shah,Dulanga Weerakoon,Ajinkya Deshpande,Saksham Agarwal,Nilesh Bhatia,Neel Kiran Naik,Parth Shah,Sravan Danda,Sanjay E. Sarma,Archan Misra,Vigneshwaran Subbaraju,Sougata Sen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GVT3D模型解决3D具身指代理解（3D-ERU）中跨模态表征对齐不足与点云规模导致计算开销大的问题：通过Embodied Fusion模块在共享嵌入空间联合建模语言、手势与视觉，并用中央凹（foveation）策略仅在估计指向方向附近的粗区域处理点云，实现约8%精度提升与2.95倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然人机交互需要机器人在3D环境中理解语言与手势（如拾取放置、物体检索）。3D-ERU任务要求从含人体指向手势与语言描述的点云场景中定位目标物体的3D位置。现有3D-ERU模型存在两点不足：一是缺乏跨模态表征对齐；二是计算成本高且随点云规模增长而上升。

Method: 1）Embodied Fusion模块：在共享嵌入空间中联合建模语言、手势与视觉输入，促进跨模态对齐；2）Foveation（中央凹）策略：先估计指向方向，仅对该方向周围粗区域内的点云进行选择性处理，以降低计算量。

Result: 相较于已有3D-ERU模型，GVT3D精度提升约8%；在保持可比精度的前提下，foveation使执行速度提升2.95倍。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐的融合建模与基于指向先验的空间选择性计算，可同时改善3D具身指代理解的准确性与效率，为可扩展的3D人机交互提供可行方案。

Abstract: Natural human-robot interaction requires robots to interpret language and gestures within a 3D environment to support tasks such as pick-and-place and object retrieval. 3D Embodied Reference Understanding (3D-ERU) supports this by identifying the 3D location of a target object from a scene (point-cloud) containing a human pointing gesture and an accompanying language description. However, current 3D-ERU models lack alignment of cross-modal representations and incur high processing costs that increase with the point-cloud's size. To address this, we propose GVT3D, which employs an Embodied Fusion module that jointly models language, gesture, and vision inputs within a shared embedding space, promoting cross-modal alignment. We additionally introduce a foveation approach that selectively processes the point cloud within a coarse region around the estimated pointing direction to reduce computations. GVT3D achieves ~8% higher accuracy and foveation enables 2.95x faster execution while maintaining comparable accuracy, relative to prior 3D-ERU models.

</details>


### [100] [Manifold Embedding of Deep Image Features for Image Matching via Neural Adjoint Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05083)
*Nafie El Amrani,Moritz Heep,Florian Bernard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 NAMI，将函数映射（functional maps）引入零样本图像稠密对应，通过 Galerkin 有限元法基于深度特征的流形嵌入构造离散 Laplace-Beltrami 算子，并结合神经伴随映射实现非线性映射，取得零样本稠密对应新 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型特征虽推进了图像对应问题，但正则化不足，最近邻匹配常产生噪声对应，在稠密对应任务中尤为突出。作者希望借助 3D 形状匹配中函数映射的谱形式化与低维线性表示来加强正则化，但函数映射要求域（此处的矩形图像网格）具备有信息量且非平凡的几何结构。

Method: 1) 为图像定义有意义的流形结构：基于深度图像特征的流形嵌入，用有限元中的 Galerkin 方法推导离散 Laplace-Beltrami 算子（LBO）；2) 在该谱表示下用函数映射求解图像匹配；3) 引入神经伴随映射（neural adjoint map）以表示非线性映射，突破传统函数映射的线性限制。

Result: 在多个真实图像稠密对应基准上，该方法在零样本图像对应方法中达到新的 state of the art。代码已公开。

Conclusion: 将函数映射范式与深度特征流形几何、神经伴随映射结合，可有效正则化图像稠密对应，为图像匹配提供谱域低维线性表示的新途径。

Abstract: Estimating semantic correspondences between different object instances of similar categories in real-world images is a fundamental challenge in computer vision. While the recent progress in foundation models has significantly advanced solutions for such difficult image correspondence problems, respective methods are often insufficiently regularised. For example, common nearest-neighbour matching with foundation model features often results in noisy correspondences, which is particularly prominent when solving for dense correspondences. In this work, we tackle image matching via functional maps, which have been popularised for 3D shape matching due to their powerful spectral formalism utilising an efficient low-dimensional linear representation. However, functional maps require that domains (in our cases the rectangular image grids) are equipped with an informative and non-trivial geometry. To define a meaningful manifold structure for images, we use Galerkin's method from finite elements to derive a discrete Laplace-Beltrami operator (LBO) based on a manifold embedding of deep image features. Further, we leverage a neural adjoint map that allows to represent non-linear mappings. We demonstrate that our method sets the new state of the art among zero-shot image correspondence methods on multiple dense correspondence benchmarks of real-world images. Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/NafieAmrani/NAMI

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Proposals: Region-Consistent Open-Set Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03741)
*Jiehui Tang,Yunlong Yu,Zhao Wang,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将开放集目标检测(OSOD)重构为以对象为中心的语义推断问题，提出OCSI-Net，通过聚合相关提案为区域簇进行区域级语义推断，并结合几何感知不确定性稳定优化，在多个基准上取得SOTA，调和均值平均提升6.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集提案机制下，同一对象的高度重叠提案常被赋予矛盾的已知/未知预测；现有方法对每个提案独立进行开放集推理，忽略相关提案间的共享语义，导致检测碎片化与开放集决策不稳定。

Method: 提出统一框架OCSI-Net：(1) 将相关提案聚合为结构化区域簇，从聚合的语义证据中推断区域级语义，强制同一对象区域内开放集预测的一致性；(2) 引入几何感知不确定性机制，稳定开放集过渡边界附近的优化。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明OCSI-Net持续达到SOTA，已知与未知检测的调和均值相较次优方法平均提升6.83%。

Conclusion: 以对象为中心的区域级语义一致性建模可有效缓解密集提案下的开放集决策碎片化问题，几何感知不确定性进一步稳定边界优化，验证了该重构视角的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Open-set object detection (OSOD) suffers from a fundamental limitation under dense proposal mechanisms: highly overlapping proposals associated with the same object are often assigned contradictory known/unknown predictions. Existing methods perform open-set reasoning independently for each proposal, while ignoring the shared semantics among correlated proposals, resulting in fragmented detections and unstable open-set decisions. In this paper, we reformulate OSOD as an object-centric semantic inference problem, where correlated proposals jointly support a coherent region-level semantic decision. Based on this perspective, we propose OCSI-Net, a unified framework that combines object-centric semantic consistency with geometry-aware uncertainty stabilization. Specifically, OCSI-Net aggregates correlated proposals into structured region clusters and infers region-level semantics from aggregated semantic evidence, thereby enforcing coherent open-set predictions within the same object region. Furthermore, a geometry-aware uncertainty mechanism is introduced to stabilize optimization near open-set transition boundaries. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that OCSI-Net consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving the harmonic mean of known and unknown detection by an average of 6.83\% over the second-best method.

</details>


### [102] [Pixel-space video generation with U-JiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04764)
*Théophane Vallaeys,Jakob Verbeek,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 探索直接在像素空间训练视频生成模型的可行性，提出UNet式Transformer架构U-JiT并改进训练与采样策略，在Kinetics-600上显著超越像素空间与潜空间基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散与流匹配视频模型依赖预训练自编码器压缩到潜空间，导致：1) 两阶段训练（先自编码器预训练再生成模型训练），无法端到端；2) 自编码器带来固有信号损失，重建质量与生成性能之间的权衡仍无定论。因此作者试图验证像素空间直接生成视频模型的可行性。

Method: 分析近期基于基础Transformer设计的像素空间图像生成方法，指出其在高维像素空间视频生成中的若干缺陷；据此提出UNet-like的Transformer架构（U-JiT），并改进训练配方与采样策略；在Kinetics-600上从零训练潜空间与像素空间模型进行控制实验对比。

Result: 在128分辨率frames-to-video任务上，U-JiT相较此前SOTA的生成FVD提升27%，相较JiT像素空间基线提升14%；扩展至256分辨率时，随时空压缩率提升至64×64×8，对像素空间与潜空间模型的优势进一步扩大；class-to-video任务中，逐帧FID相较潜模型提升27%、相较像素空间模型提升61%，FVD分别降低9%和56%。

Conclusion: 像素空间视频生成可行且具竞争力，无需自编码器即可获得优于潜空间模型的质量；U-JiT的架构与训练/采样改进是关键，且在高分辨率与高压缩率设置下优势更明显。

Abstract: Latent diffusion and flow matching models are ubiquitous in state-of-the-art generative video models. While highly effective, relying on pretrained autoencoders to compress the data into a lower-dimensional latent space also comes with drawbacks. First, training proceeds in two stages: autoencoder pretraining, with compression and reconstruction objectives, followed by latent generative model training, precluding fully end-to-end training. Second, autoencoders also lead to an inherent loss of signal, and the trade-off between reconstruction quality and generative performance in the latent space is still an active area of research. In this paper we explore the viability of generative video models directly in pixel space. We analyze recent approaches in pixel-space generative modeling of images that rely on basic transformer designs, and identify several shortcomings that limit their effectiveness for the high-dimensional pixel-space video generation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a UNet-like transformer architecture along with improvements to the training recipe and sampling strategy. We conduct controlled experiments, training latent and pixel-space models from scratch on Kinetics-600. For the standard frames-to-video task at 128 resolution, our U-JiT model improves generative FVD by 27% over prior state-of-the-art results, and by 14% against the JiT pixel-space baseline. Scaling to higher 256 resolution, we find the improvement over pixel-space and latent-based models grows as we increase the spatiotemporal compression rate up to $64 \times 64 \times 8$. Finally, we find that U-JiT improves per-frame FID by 27% over latent models and 61% over pixel-space models on class-to-video generation, with respectively 9% and 56% reduction in FVD.

</details>


### [103] [iMED: A Multi-Endoscope Dataset for Surgical 3D Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04755)
*Sierra Bonilla,Fengyi Jiang,Chinedu Nwoye,Jingpei Lu,Kailey Reardon,Humphrey W. Chow,Francisco Vasconcelos,Sophia Bano,Adam Schmidt,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iMED是首个面向机器人辅助微创手术（RAMIS）的同步多内窥镜数据集，提供两个独立立体内窥镜同时采集的170K同步时间点，并建立"一个内窥镜训练、另一个测试"的几何泛化评测协议，对23种SOTA方法进行了基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉数据集均为单一内窥镜、单一轨迹序列，缺乏独立留出视角，导致无法区分模型的几何泛化能力与沿前向窄路径的光度插值能力，基准评测存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 构建iMED数据集：340条序列、约170K同步时间点（每时间点4视角），由两个独立立体内窥镜在离体、尸检及活体手术场景下同步录制，覆盖14个标本及多种解剖区域与运动模式；提供标定内参、基于ArUco的逐帧位姿估计及不确定性量化、器械分割掩膜与临床元数据。采用"一个内窥镜训练、另一个内窥镜测试"协议，在刚性/可变形新视角合成、位姿估计、特征匹配和单目深度估计任务上评测23种SOTA方法。

Result: 实验表明，主要依赖光度监督的方法在留出内窥镜设置下性能显著退化，而具有显式几何正则化的方法跨视角更鲁棒；大规模基础模型向手术图像的迁移效果出奇地好，常优于领域专用模型，揭示当前微调协议存在隐患。

Conclusion: iMED为手术视觉中的几何泛化建立了新的评测协议，并揭示光度监督的局限与基础模型的迁移潜力。

Abstract: We introduce iMED, the first synchronized multi-endoscope dataset for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS), designed to address a fundamental limitation in surgical vision benchmarking: the absence of independent held-out viewpoints. Existing surgical datasets capture single-trajectory sequences from a single endoscope, making it impossible to distinguish geometric generalization from photometric interpolation along a narrow forward-facing path. iMED provides 340 sequences ~170K synchronized timepoints with 4 views per timepoint) recorded simultaneously from two independent stereo endoscopes across ex vivo, postmortem, and live surgical settings, spanning 14 specimens with diverse anatomical regions and motion regimes. The dataset includes calibrated camera intrinsics, ArUco-based frame-wise pose estimates with uncertainty quantification, instrument segmentation masks, and rich clinical metadata.
