Using a train-on-one-endoscope, test-on-another evaluation protocol, we benchmark 23 state-of-the-art methods across rigid and deformable novel view synthesis, pose estimation, feature matching, and monocular depth estimation. Our experiments show that methods relying primarily on photometric supervision degrade substantially under this held-out-endoscope setting, while methods with explicit geometric regularization are more robust across views in our benchmark. Notably, large-scale foundation models transfer surprisingly well to surgical imagery, often outperforming domain-specific models, suggesting pitfalls in current fine-tuning protocols. iMED establishes a new evaluation protocol for geometric generalization in surgical vision.

</details>


### [104] [SPOD: Recovering Suppressed Temporal Evidence in Video-LLMs via Single-Pass Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04728)
*Yangguang Lin,Quan Fang,Xiaoyan Liu,Zefan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无需训练的单次前向推理框架SPOD，通过解析式隐状态操作恢复Video-LLM中被抑制的时间证据，缓解时间幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Video-LLM在场景转换理解、事件排序等时间敏感任务上存在时间幻觉；现有方案要么需昂贵重训练，要么在测试时引入低效的多轮推理与外部监督。作者观察到时间证据其实已编码在隐表示中，但标准推理未充分利用。

Method: SPOD包含两部分：帧区分性分析（FDA）从中层隐状态几何结构中识别显著帧；时间增量子空间（TDS）由加权帧间隐状态差异构造正交的时间子空间与先验子空间。随后将最终隐状态分解为时间、先验与残差分量并分别重缩放，以增强动态信息、抑制静态外观偏置并保留互补信息。全程为训练-free的单次前向解析操作。

Result: 在VidHalluc、EventHallusion、VideoMMMU上，SPOD在时间推理任务上持续优于强推理时基线，且跨多种模型架构有效，推理效率接近原始vanilla推理。

Conclusion: 提升Video-LLM时间推理不仅依赖额外监督，也可通过恢复其内部表示中已有的时间证据实现；隐状态层面的正交分解是高效可行的方向。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have achieved strong performance on many video understanding tasks, yet remain vulnerable to temporal hallucination in time-sensitive reasoning scenarios such as scene transition understanding and event ordering. Existing solutions either require costly retraining or introduce inefficient multi-pass inference and external supervision at test time. In this work, we show that temporal evidence is already encoded in Video-LLM hidden representations but is not fully exploited during standard inference. Motivated by this observation, we propose Single-Pass Orthogonal Decomposition (SPOD), a training-free framework that recovers suppressed temporal evidence through analytical hidden-state manipulation in a single forward pass. SPOD consists of two components: Frame Distinctiveness Analysis (FDA), which identifies salient frames from middle-layer hidden-state geometry, and Temporal Delta Subspace (TDS), which constructs orthogonal temporal and prior subspaces from weighted inter-frame hidden-state differences. By decomposing final hidden states into temporal, prior, and residual components and rescaling them independently, SPOD enhances dynamic information, suppresses static appearance bias, and preserves complementary information. Experiments on VidHalluc, EventHallusion, and VideoMMMU show that SPOD consistently outperforms strong inference-time baselines across temporal reasoning tasks and model architectures while retaining near-vanilla inference efficiency. These results suggest that improving temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs may depend not only on additional supervision, but also on recovering temporal evidence already present in their internal representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/VioAgnes/SPOD.

</details>


### [105] [Predicting Void Points in Self-Occluding Forest Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04548)
*Benjamin Bachmayr,Mohamed Youssef,Oliver Bimber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过修改训练标签而非成像流程，为合成孔径成像引入显式空域负类标签，使网络能区分重建伪影与真实植被，从而缓解生物量系统性高估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成孔径成像（SAI）对冠层多视角加权平均仅抑制而非消除遮挡，未观测区域被误判为占据，导致生物量系统性高估；Deep Forest项目因训练数据仅为实测反射点、不含空域，无法学习显式占据边界。

Method: 每场景渲染两次：9×9俯视网格用于生成焦栈，550层按几何裁剪的深度切片提供占据标签，将空域显式标为负类；Gazebo流程生成含3物种、80–200棵树的程序化林分；预训练Pix2Pix将RGB转为合成近红外波段，光场渲染生成550平面×3通道对齐焦栈；在留出场景上评估四种架构。

Result: 2D U-Net IoU 0.6290 优于3D U-Net 0.5707；COLMAP 0.3446、DepthAnything3 0.2959（均为最优膨胀扫描后）；RGB输入的体素架构在无膨胀、无逐场景标定下取得最高IoU 0.6514。

Conclusion: 常规重建方法虽能恢复可见表面，却难以推断封闭冠层内部占据；显式空域负类标签与RGB输入体素架构是提升SAI占据估计的有效途径。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Imaging (SAI) enables drones to observe forest canopies by warping and averaging multiple displaced views at a common depth. However, occlusion is suppressed rather than eliminated, causing poorly observed regions to appear occupied and leading to systematic biomass overestimation. The Deep Forest project cannot distinguish reconstruction artifacts from real vegetation because its training data relies on measured reflectance points, excluding empty space and preventing the network from learning explicit occupancy boundaries.
  This thesis addresses the problem by modifying training labels rather than the imaging process. Each simulated scene is rendered twice: as a 9x9 grid of top-down captures for focal-stack generation and as 550 depth-clipped slabs derived from scene geometry to provide occupancy labels, explicitly representing void space as a negative class. A Gazebo pipeline generates procedural forest stands containing 80-200 trees across three species. A pretrained Pix2Pix model translates rendered RGB images into a synthetic near-infrared band, and Light Field Rendering produces aligned focal stacks of 550 planes across three channels.
  Four architectures were evaluated on a held-out scene. The plain 2D U-Net achieved an IoU of 0.6290, outperforming the volumetric 3D U-Net (0.5707). Established reconstruction methods performed substantially worse: COLMAP achieved 0.3446 and DepthAnything3 0.2959, both after their respective optimal dilation sweeps. An RGB-input variant of the volumetric architecture achieved the highest IoU of 0.6514 without dilation or per-scene calibration. These results highlight the limitations of conventional reconstruction methods, which recover visible surfaces but struggle to infer occupancy within closed canopies.

</details>


### [106] [Polarimetric Scattering-Structure Contrastive Learning for Few-Shot HRRP Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04490)
*Fangyi Wang,Peng Zhao,Haipeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出极化散射结构对比学习（PSS-CL），把极化机制的物理结构作为训练视角迁移到HRRP小样本识别编码器中，在跨方位角1/3/5-shot上分别达81.73%/86.92%/90.98% MCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRRP小样本识别受两方面困扰：强方位敏感性导致特征随姿态剧烈变化，新增类别支持样本稀疏。作者希望引入极化散射的物理先验，为有限的HRRP样本提供稳定的结构化监督。

Method: 构建基于情节式（episodic）训练的HRRP编码器，并为其配备“结构化物理训练视角”：用主导局部响应相对典型极化机制、归一化距离位置和能量贡献的软亲和度（soft affinity）来表征散射结构；设计对称跨视角对比目标，把该散射结构迁移到HRRP表征中；推理阶段采用情节式原型学习，不依赖物理分支。

Result: 在六类实测数据上开展Random-aspect与Cross-aspect实验。Cross-aspect的1-shot、3-shot、5-shot平均类别准确率（MCA）分别为81.73%、86.92%、90.98%，较最优对比方法分别提升5.62%、3.09%、3.56%。

Conclusion: 极化散射结构可作为有效的辅助监督信号迁移至HRRP表征，在不增加推理开销（去掉物理分支）的前提下显著提升小样本、跨方位角识别性能，验证了物理先验与对比学习结合的可行性与增益。

Abstract: Few-shot high-resolution range profile (HRRP) recognition is challenged by strong aspect-dependent variation and sparse novel-class support. We propose polarimetric scattering-structure contrastive learning (PSS-CL), which supplies an episodic HRRP encoder with a structured physical training view. Dominant local responses are represented by their soft affinities to canonical polarimetric mechanisms, normalized range locations, and energy contributions. A symmetric cross-view objective transfers this scattering structure to the learned HRRP representation, while episodic prototype learning supports novel-class inference without the physical branch. We conduct Random-aspect and Cross-aspect experiments on a six-class measured dataset. In Cross-aspect 1-, 3-, and 5-shot recognition, PSS-CL achieves 81.73%, 86.92%, and 90.98% mean class accuracy (MCA), respectively, outperforming the best competing results by 5.62%, 3.09%, and 3.56%.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond Token Representations: Explicit Visual Object Grounding for Video Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04472)
*Jun Huang,Junyang Zhang,Zijie Yue,Bingkun Wang,Yong Luo,Miaojing Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出显式视觉目标定位框架EVOG用于视频推理分割，通过空间感知提示生成与精炼、查询多样化对齐以及基于提示的关键帧选择，显著提升掩码预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VRS方法仅生成少量分割token作为目标表征，这些token在语义上单调、在空间上模糊，难以充分表达目标以指导掩码预测。

Method: 1) 空间感知提示生成与精炼：查询多模态大语言模型生成显式空间提示（边界框与点集），并通过多步对话迭代精炼；2) 查询多样化与对齐模块：生成从不同视角描述同一目标的辅助查询，并在其预测的空间提示间施加跨查询一致性约束，缓解查询局限带来的训练偏差；3) 基于提示的关键帧选择策略：筛选可靠帧以支持掩码解码与传播。

Result: 在八个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式空间提示替代隐式分割token进行目标定位，结合查询多样化与一致性对齐、关键帧选择，可有效提升视频推理分割的性能。

Abstract: Video Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to infer and segment target objects specified by reasoning queries in videos. Existing methods typically first infer the target and generate a few segmentation tokens for it, which are then fed into a mask decoder for mask prediction. However, such segmentation tokens tend to be both semantically monotonous and spatially ambiguous, making them largely insufficient to represent the target to guide mask prediction. In this paper, we propose an explicit visual object grounding framework for VRS. Specifically, we first propose a spatial-aware prompt generation and refinement scheme, in which we query a multimodal large language model to generate explicit spatial prompts (i.e., bounding box and point set) for the target and then refine them through multi-step dialogue. Furthermore, we introduce a query diversification and alignment module to generate auxiliary queries that describe the same target from different perspectives. We then enforce cross-query consistency between their predicted spatial prompts to alleviate the training bias caused by query limitations. Finally, we select several reliable frames using a prompt-based keyframe selection strategy to support mask decoding and propagation. Extensive experiments on eight datasets demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/viscom-tongji/EVOG.

</details>


### [108] [Entropy-Gated Dynamic Rectification for Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04438)
*Riasad Alvi,Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Md. Ahashan Habib,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出熵门控动态矫正（EGDR），利用解码过程中预测熵的时序变化作为token级控制信号，动态调节潜在引导门控阈值，在不增加前向传播的前提下进一步降低LVLM幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM常生成图中不存在的物体；潜在引导（latent steering）可在单次前向中缓解此问题，但固定门控用同一标准对待早期与晚期解码步。作者观察到自由生成中预测熵剧烈波动，而承诺短答案的状态处于狭窄的高置信区间，存在可利用的时序错配。

Method: 以直接利用上一步已可得logits的熵作为因果可得、token局部的控制信号：熵高时降低干预阈值，熵低时保持校准工作点；仅替换静态门控的常数阈值，无额外前向。

Result: LLaVA-1.5-7B上静态门控将CHAIR CS/CI从53.00/15.30降至32.00/14.30，EGDR进一步降至29.00/13.60；同等58%干预率下静态控制为30.50/14.00。增益在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上保持并随生成预算增大而扩大，POPE与通用多模态能力基本不变，延迟增加约2%。

Conclusion: 解码不确定性可作为低成本、token局部的动态门控信号，在保持通用能力的同时有效抑制造物幻觉。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) routinely name objects that are absent from the image. Latent steering can reduce this failure in one model forward pass, but a fixed gate treats early and late decoding steps by the same criterion. We observe that predictive entropy varies sharply within free-running generation, while states that commit to short answers occupy a narrow, confident band. We exploit this temporal mismatch by using uncertainty as a causally available, token-local control signal for an existing steering gate. We propose Entropy-Gated Dynamic Rectification (EGDR), which lowers the intervention threshold when entropy derived from the preceding step's already available logits is high and retains the calibrated operating point when it is low. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, the underlying static gate lowers CHAIR CS/CI from 53.00/15.30 to 32.00/14.30; replacing only its constant threshold with EGDR further lowers them to 29.00/13.60. At a matched 58% intervention rate, the corresponding static control obtains 30.50/14.00. The gain over the static gate holds on LLaVA-NeXT-7B and grows with the generation budget, while POPE scores and general multimodal capability remain effectively unchanged. EGDR requires no additional model forward pass and adds roughly 2% measured latency. Our code is available at https://github.com/alvi-uiu/EGDR.

</details>


### [109] [Mitigating Spatial Blindness in Multiple Instance Learning for Whole-Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04337)
*Xiangyu Li,Jincheng Li,Xing Xie,Bin Guan,Shaohua Kevin Zhou,Rui Yan,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"空间盲"（spatial blindness）问题并给出 ResTopoMIL 方法，通过冻结已学好的成分预测器再以坐标重分配分离损失训练图残差，使模型真正利用组织邻域信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片多示例学习虽能预测诊断/预后标签，但主要依赖组织成分，对空间组织利用有限；仅提供坐标或图结构并不能保证训练出的预测器真正使用了邻域信息，这种"空间盲"现象及其成因（早期成分学习削弱空间通路的监督）值得研究。

Method: ResTopoMIL：先训练并冻结一个成分预测器作为锚点，再以坐标重分配分离损失（coordinate-reassignment separation loss）训练图残差；并用置换分析、信息分析区分架构对称性、可用空间信息与所提优化机制三种解释。

Result: 受控空间任务上 AUC 0.987（同架构联合训练仅 0.684），纯成分任务性能仍保持较高；在 4 个 WSI 分类与 5 个生存基准中，8 个任务平均主指标提升，其中 4 项经 Holm 校正的患者簇检验后仍显著；无坐标残差对照可部分复现增益，破坏邻域则使性能降至接近成分锚点；邻域保持变换与解剖分层扰动进一步刻画了这种依赖。

Conclusion: 固定成分参考是学习空间修正的实用干预手段；同时，保留额外外观容量也是增益的部分解释。

Abstract: Whole-slide multiple instance learning can predict diagnostic and prognostic labels from tissue composition while making limited use of spatial organization. Providing coordinates or a graph does not establish that a trained predictor uses tissue neighborhoods. We examine this gap as spatial blindness and hypothesize that early composition learning can weaken supervision of a spatial pathway. We introduce ResTopoMIL, which freezes a learned composition predictor before training a graph residual with a coordinate-reassignment separation loss. Permutation and information analyses distinguish architectural symmetry, available spatial information and the proposed optimization mechanism. On a controlled spatial task, staged training achieved an AUC of 0.987, compared with 0.684 for the same jointly trained architecture. Performance remained high on a composition-only task. Across four WSI classification and five survival benchmarks, ResTopoMIL improved mean primary performance on eight tasks; four improvements remained significant after Holm-adjusted patient-cluster tests. Coordinate-free residual controls recovered some of the gains, while neighborhood destruction reduced performance close to the composition anchor. Neighborhood-preserving transformations and anatomy-stratified perturbations further characterized this dependence. These results support fixing a composition reference as a practical intervention for learning spatial corrections, while retaining additional appearance capacity as a contributing explanation.

</details>


### [110] [SteelHuman: Predicting Operator Estimates of Steel-Scrap Composition from Sparse RGB Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04317)
*Jovan Kamchev,Zoran Ivanovski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文用稀疏手持视角聚合冻结DINOv2特征的简单方法估计废钢堆成分，在600个袋外钢堆上对运营商估计值的总变差距离为13.4%，且图像相关分配可将HMS类内TV从25.1%降至11.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废钢成分是产量规划与公平供应商结算的关键，但多吨级料堆的成分测量极为费力，通常依赖受过训练的人工操作员估算；已有工作虽处理成分级任务，却需要结构化采集与多阶段预处理，部署成本高。

Method: 采用更简化的思路：从料堆的稀疏、手持视角中提取特征并聚合。具体使用冻结的DINOv2 token，在各视角间均匀聚合；在四类按厚度定义的HMS等级上引入图像相关的分配策略，以替代训练集划分先验。

Result: 在600个袋外钢堆上，均匀聚合冻结DINOv2 token对运营商估计达到13.4% TV距离；误差主要集中于四类HMS间的分配，图像相关分配将HMS内TV从25.1%降至11.2%。因缺乏公开成分预测数据集与基准，外部评估借助面向分类的DOES数据集，使用相同冻结特征的分类器达到93.97%准确率，超过所比较的已发表结果；成分模型在无适配情况下也与相关DOES类别对齐。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需结构化采集与复杂预处理，仅凭稀疏手持视角的冻结特征聚合即可有效估计废钢堆成分，且同一特征在同源分类任务上具竞争力，说明该简单方案具备实用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Steel scrap composition estimation is an important part of yield planning and fair supplier compensation. Measuring the composition of a multi-ton pile is an inherently laborious process, so the assessment is often left to trained human operators. Recent works tackle composition-level tasks, but require structured acquisition and multi-stage preprocessing. We study a simpler approach to steel scrap load assessment by aggregating features from sparse, handheld views of the pile. On 600 out-of-fold piles, frozen DINOv2 tokens aggregated uniformly across views reach 13.4% total variation (TV) distance against operator estimates. Most remaining error lies in allocation among four thickness-defined heavy melting scrap (HMS) classes, yet image-dependent allocation reduces within-HMS TV from 25.1% under a training-set split prior to 11.2%. Due to the lack of public datasets and benchmarks for predicting composition, external evaluation relies on the public DOES dataset intended for classification. A classifier using the same frozen features reaches 93.97% accuracy, exceeding the published results we compare against. The composition model also aligns with related DOES categories without adaptation.

</details>


### [111] [Mamba3R: Fixed-Capacity Selective State Memory for Long-Sequence 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04221)
*Zhenxiang Xiong,Tianchen Deng,Nailin Wang,Lei Zhao,Guole Shen,Weidong Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Mamba3R——一种与预训练循环重建骨干联合设计的"状态-记忆适配器"，仅训练 128 万参数，在不更新骨干与任务头的前提下实现可训练适配，在固定 GPU 预算与长序列流式重建中提升几何精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 3D 重建需在固定 GPU 预算下对长输入维持几何质量。预训练循环模型虽有有界状态，但冻结骨干也就固定了其学到的"状态—观测"交互方式，难以适配新场景/新传感器；而全量微调代价高且破坏原模型特性。

Method: 设计轻量适配器：(1) 选择性状态空间混合器（selective SSM mixer）联合变换原生状态特征与固定容量辅助记忆；(2) 显式读/写路径实现跨帧信息传递；(3) 分解式反馈路径调节附加记忆回馈给骨干的内容。仅训练约 1.28M 适配器参数，骨干与任务头保持冻结；采用共享的周期性重置协议进行评估。

Result: 在公共室内外数据集及机器人采集轨迹上，相比循环基线降低轨迹误差、提升累积表面与深度精度，单项指标最高提升 40%。消融对比分别支持混合算子选择与辅助记忆跨帧携带的有效性。内存分配不变；从 1k 到 2k 帧吞吐变化不超过 1.4%。RTX 4060 离线回放约 11.7 FPS、占用 5.1 GiB，同一 checkpoint 可覆盖 10 Hz 传感器速率。

Conclusion: 冻结骨干+轻量状态-记忆适配器是长序列流式 3D 重建的高效适配范式：在参数、显存与吞吐几乎不变的前提下显著提升几何质量，具备很强的实时性与部署可行性。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction must maintain geometric quality over long inputs within a fixed GPU budget. A pretrained recurrent model provides bounded state, but freezing its backbone also fixes its learned state-observation interactions. We introduce Mamba3R, a jointly designed state-memory adapter that provides a trainable adaptation path without updating the backbone or task heads. A selective state-space mixer jointly transforms native state features and a fixed-capacity auxiliary memory, explicit read and write paths carry information across frames, and a decomposed feedback path regulates what the added memory feeds back. Only 1.28 million adapter parameters are trained. Under a shared periodic-reset protocol, it lowers trajectory error and improves accumulated surface and depth accuracy over recurrent baselines on public indoor and outdoor datasets and robot-recorded traversals, gaining up to 40 percent on one metric. Matched comparisons support both the choice of the mixing operator and the utility of carrying the auxiliary memory across frames. Allocated memory remains unchanged and throughput moves by at most 1.4 percent from one to two thousand frames. Replaying its recorded traversals offline, the platform's RTX 4060 averages 11.7 frames per second within 5.1 GiB with the same checkpoint, a mean throughput covering the 10 Hz sensor rate.

</details>


### [112] [LiSA: Linear Stochastic Attention for Long-Range Visual Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04216)
*Sahar Dastani,Fereshteh Shakeri,Samuel Barbeau,Kunal Mahatha,Ismail Ben Ayed,Herve Lombaert,Christian Desrosiers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiSA：基于截断随机游走随机扩散过程的线性注意力插件模块，实现高阶token交互，兼具自适应局部性，在分类、语义分割、目标检测上优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力对序列长度呈二次复杂度，是主要计算瓶颈；现有线性注意力通过softmax的核近似降低复杂度，但其一阶聚合限制了表达能力，难以捕捉高阶结构信息。

Method: LiSA为线性注意力插件模块，将token交互建模为基于截断随机游走的随机扩散过程以获得高阶交互；引入自适应局部性，偏好语义相似的邻居以保留细粒度结构；并保证训练稳定高效。

Result: 在分类、语义分割和目标检测任务上开展大量实验，结果优于当前先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过随机扩散的高阶聚合与自适应局部性，LiSA在保持线性效率的同时提升了线性注意力的表达力与下游任务性能，可作为通用插件替代/增强现有线性注意力。

Abstract: Self-attention has become the standard for modern sequence modeling, enabling Transformers to model long-range dependencies across a wide range of modalities. Despite these advances, its quadratic cost with respect to sequence length remains a significant computational bottleneck. Recent work addresses this limitation by introducing linear variants of self-attention, namely with kernel approximations of the softmax operator. However, their first-order aggregation restricts expressivity and struggles to capture higher-order structure. We propose Linear Stochastic Attention (LiSA), a linear attention plug-in module that enables higher-order token interactions via a stochastic diffusion process based on truncated random walks. LiSA incorporates adaptive locality, favoring semantically similar neighbors to preserve fine-grained structures, while maintaining stable and efficient training. We conduct extensive experiments on classification, semantic segmentation, and object detection tasks and show improved results over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [113] [High-fidelity training-free photovoltaic module mapping from multi-view UAV imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05131)
*WenHao Duana,Qi Chena,Penglei Zhang,Ruizhe Deng,Zhiling Guo,Haoran Zhang,Yuntian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的MV-PVMapper框架，将视觉基础模型SAM 3与无人机摄影测量耦合，实现光伏组件的高保真实例级制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真光伏组件制图需要完整的实例恢复与精确边界，而基础模型在多视角下预测不完整且不一致，难以直接转化为几何准确的组件级地图。

Method: 以文本提示驱动SAM 3生成候选；通过逐组件坡度校正将轮廓转入物方空间以抑制表面模型噪声畸变，并用组件尺寸先验验证以进行跨视角实例筛选；接受的实例再回投至所有覆盖影像作为框提示，辅助文本提示实现迭代分割—验证—融合。全程无需任务特定标注或权重更新。

Result: 三个在运屋顶电站五方向航测（约1 cm下视GSD）上，两轮反馈迭代在IoU=0.5阈值下恢复7710/7711个参考组件且零误检；宏平均面积精度99.00%、召回98.52%；聚合Jaccard指数97.55%（正射影像分割60.73%、多视像素投票41.24%），IoU=0.95下平均精度95.67%。

Conclusion: 尺寸约束对实例分离至关重要，坡度校正与全视角反馈提升边界精度；该框架无需任务特定标注即可达到高保真组件级制图。

Abstract: High-fidelity photovoltaic (PV) module mapping requires complete instance recovery and accurate delineation. Vision foundation models offer zero-shot segmentation, but converting incomplete and inconsistent predictions across views into a complete, geometrically accurate map of individual modules remains challenging. We propose the Multi-View PV Module Mapper (MV-PVMapper), a training-free framework coupling vision foundation models with unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) photogrammetry. First, Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) generates candidates from original images using text prompts. Their contours are then transferred into object space through per-module slope correction to limit distortion from surface-model noise, and verified against representative module dimensions to guide cross-view instance selection. Finally, accepted instances return to all covering images as box prompts for SAM~3, supplementing text prompts with spatial cues for iterative segmentation, verification and fusion. On five-direction surveys of three operational rooftop PV plants at approximately 1 cm nadir ground sampling distance, two feedback iterations recover 7,710 of 7,711 reference modules with no false positives at an intersection-over-union (IoU) matching threshold of 0.5. Macro-averaged area precision and recall reach 99.00 % and 98.52 %, respectively. Averaged across sites, the aggregated Jaccard index reaches 97.55 %, compared with 60.73 % and 41.24 % for orthomosaic segmentation and multi-view pixel-voting baselines, respectively; average precision at the stricter IoU threshold of 0.95 reaches 95.67 %. Ablation studies show that module-size constraints are critical for instance separation, while slope correction and feedback to all covering views improve boundary accuracy. These results are achieved without task-specific image annotation or segmentation-model weight updates.

</details>


### [114] [Robustness Verification against Patch Attacks via Group-Lasso Semidefinite Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04939)
*Yukina Nakagawa,Takami Sato,Ken Kobayashi,Kazuhide Nakata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向补丁攻击的神经网络鲁棒性验证方法，将补丁攻击建模为组稀疏并用MISDP表示，再以三种松弛（含Group-Lasso松弛）消除整数变量，实现高效验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证方法主要针对范数球攻击，无法充分刻画补丁攻击这类物理扰动威胁；而朴素地在所有补丁位置上做枚举计算量过高。

Method: 先用组稀疏建模补丁攻击，结合SDP松弛将验证问题表述为混合整数半定规划（MISDP）；再提出三种松弛（包括Group-Lasso松弛）去除整数变量，同时保留原攻击的稀疏组结构。

Result: 所提松弛仅需原MISDP不到3%的计算时间；Group-Lasso松弛目标值平均仅增加0.34%；Group-Lasso验证率达91%，而此前方法低于70%。

Conclusion: 以组稀疏建模加SDP松弛可高效、较精确地验证补丁攻击鲁棒性，Group-Lasso松弛在效率与紧致性间取得良好折衷，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Robustness verification is essential for ensuring the safety and security of neural networks, but current methods focus heavily on
  norm-ball attacks, which do not adequately model typical real-world threats that present physical perturbations, known as patch attacks. A naive approach requires computationally prohibitive enumeration of all possible patch positions. To address the challenge, we propose a novel neural network verification method specialized to the patch-attack threat. Instead of enumerating patch locations, we first model the patch attack with group-wise sparsity and formulate its verification methods as a mixed-integer semidefinite program (MISDP) by applying SDP relaxation to the neural network. We then propose three relaxations, including Group-Lasso relaxation, that remove the integer variables while preserving the sparse, group-wise structure of the original attack, enabling substantially efficient verification. Our empirical analysis demonstrates that the proposed relaxations require less than 3% of the computation time of the original MISDP, while the Group-Lasso relaxation incurs only a 0.34\% average increase in the objective value over the original formulation. Furthermore, Group-Lasso achieves a verified rate of 91%, compared with under 70% for prior methods.

</details>


### [115] [VISTA: Vision-Language Integrated Semantic Triage Architecture for Online Face Clustering in Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04932)
*Jiashu Liao,Joanne Garde-Hansen,Sanjay Sharma,Tanaya Guha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是首个基于视觉语言模型的长视频在线人脸聚类框架，通过受控、不确定性感知的语义推理同时保持高簇纯度与长程身份连续性，在VPCD基准上稳定优于最强在线无监督基线COFC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸聚类多为离线方法，假设全部数据一次性可得；而实践中数据往往不完整或难以整体处理。少数在线方法依赖几何视觉特征与简单阈值，在长时无约束视频中面对光照、姿态、服装等显著外观变化时失效。

Method: 提出VISTA视觉语言框架，利用语义推理进行在线人脸聚类，并以受控、不确定性感知（uncertainty-aware）的方式融合语义信息，从而在流式场景中兼顾簇纯度与身份的长期连续性，增强对光照、姿态与着装变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 在VPCD基准（《生活大爆炸》《吸血鬼猎人巴菲》《神探夏洛克》共15集完整剧集）上，VISTA在检测器、嵌入与评估协议一致的条件下，取得稳健的精度-召回平衡，并持续优于最强匹配在线无监督基线COFC。

Conclusion: 视觉语言语义推理可有效提升长视频在线人脸聚类的鲁棒性与一致性，为以人为中心的视频分析提供可行构建模块。

Abstract: Clustering faces by identity in long, continuous video streams is a challenging task that serves as a building block for human-centric video analysis. The majority of existing face clustering models are offline, i.e., they assume the entire data to be available altogether. In many practical scenarios, however, the entire data is either unavailable or poses computational challenges to be processed in its entirety. A handful of online methods that exist rely on geometric visual features and simple thresholding techniques, which fail when the faces exhibit significant appearance variations in long, unconstrained videos. To this end, we present VISTA, the first vision language framework for online face clustering in long videos. VISTA leverages semantic reasoning in a controlled, uncertainty aware fashion to maintain both high cluster purity and long range identity continuity. This makes it robust to the variations in illumination, pose, and costume changes that the faces and characters exhibit in long real world videos. Extensive experiments on the VPCD benchmark dataset (with 15 full episodes from The Big Bang Theory [2007-2019], Buffy the Vampire Slayer [1997-2003], and Sherlock [2010-2017]) demonstrate that VISTA achieves a robust precision and recall balance, consistently improving over the strongest matched online unsupervised baseline (COFC) under identical detector, embeddings, and evaluation protocol. Code and supplementary material: https://github.com/SkyJiashu/VISTA.

</details>


### [116] [CLOCs-R: Radar-Assisted Camera-LiDAR Fusion for Long-Range Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04300)
*Richard Beuck,John Nguyen,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CLOCs-R，将雷达证据加入相机-激光雷达候选级融合重打分，并给出距离分箱评测，结果显示雷达在30-50 m稳定增益、50 m以外增益微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离3D目标检测中，LiDAR点云稀疏、相机证据随距离衰减，而车载雷达在远距离仍持续返回量测，因此希望用雷达补充远距证据。

Method: 在CLOCs候选级融合框架上扩展：多帧雷达点累积并做自车运动补偿；通过距离相关门控与每个pre-NMS 3D候选关联；提取支撑度、多普勒速度与展宽、距离残差、偏移等紧凑特征，附加到每个相机-LiDAR候选对，由轻量融合网络重打分。另引入事后提出的残差打分变体（校正SECOND分数而非替换）。

Result: 在KITTI上复现CLOCs；在aiMotive白天晴好子集、冻结SECOND与Cascade R-CNN检测器、距离分箱至200 m评测：相机-LiDAR CLOCs在50 m内优于SECOND、之外退化；残差打分挽回大部分损失。雷达使30-50 m BEV AP由0.857升至0.870，50 m外仅+0.003 center-distance AP（处于种子波动内），CLOCs-R与纯LiDAR SECOND统计持平。

Conclusion: 雷达远距增益有限，原因包括雷达特征对真伪检测区分度弱、远距训练正样本稀少、远距相机证据差、以及缺失的LiDAR提案无法通过重打分召回；论文并排报告了设计与事后结果。

Abstract: Long-range 3D object detection remains difficult for autonomous vehicles because LiDAR returns become sparse and camera evidence weakens with distance, while automotive radar keeps returning measurements far beyond both. We present CLOCs-R, an extension of the CLOCs candidate-level fusion framework that adds radar evidence to camera-LiDAR rescoring. Radar returns are accumulated over several sweeps, compensated for ego-motion, and associated with each pre-NMS 3D candidate through a range-dependent gate. Compact features summarizing support, Doppler speed and spread, range residual, and offset are then appended to every camera-LiDAR pair before a lightweight fusion network rescores the candidates. We first verify that our fusion implementation reproduces CLOCs on KITTI. We then evaluate on the daytime clear-weather subset of the aiMotive dataset, reporting distance-binned metrics out to 200 m with frozen SECOND and Cascade R-CNN detectors. Camera-LiDAR CLOCs improves on SECOND up to 50 m but degrades beyond it. A residual scoring variant, introduced post hoc, corrects SECOND's score instead of replacing it and recovers most of this loss. Radar consistently improves BEV AP at 30-50 m (0.857 to 0.870), but beyond 50 m its gain is small (+0.003 center-distance AP), within seed variation, and CLOCs-R with residual scoring is statistically tied with LiDAR-only SECOND. Our analysis attributes the limited long-range benefit to weak separation of true and false detections by radar features, scarce far-range training positives, poor camera evidence at distance, and missing LiDAR proposals that rescoring cannot recover. We report all as-designed and post hoc results side by side.

</details>


### [117] [GroundSeek-CXR: Retrospective Evaluation-Guided Active Observation Framework for Multi-Label Chest Radiography Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04286)
*Yihang Fu,Xinyu Liu,Qing Xu,Xiaofeng Yang,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GroundSeek-CXR主动式胸部X线诊断框架，用"最终诊断"回溯评估观察与停止决策，含RDE评分与RGPO偏好优化，异常图像样本F1较即时评分偏好学习提升4.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CXR诊断需识别跨区域的细微病灶与共现异常；现有视觉语言模型虽能主动观察局部区域，但基于偏好的方法仅以观察对当前诊断的即时影响进行监督，无法刻画每次观察及停止决策对最终诊断的贡献。

Method: 1) 回溯式诊断评估（RDE）：基于离线蒙特卡洛树搜索，通过独立continuation对候选观察与停止动作打分；2) 回溯间隔加权偏好优化（RGPO）：以分数间隔加权的回溯得分偏好进行学习，优先强化提升诊断准确率的动作。

Result: 大量实验表明框架优于SOTA；异常图像样本F1相较即时评分偏好学习提升4.48%。

Conclusion: 以最终诊断回溯监督观察与停止决策，可有效弥补即时信用分配的不足，提升主动式CXR诊断性能。

Abstract: Chest radiography (CXR) diagnosis is critical for thoracic disease assessment, yet challenged by the need to identify subtle lesions and co-occurring abnormalities across regions. While recent vision-language models can actively observe local regions, existing preference-based methods supervise each observation by its immediate effect on the current diagnosis, failing to capture how each observation and stopping decision contributes to the final diagnosis. To address this issue, we propose GroundSeek-CXR, an active CXR diagnosis framework that learns observation and stopping decisions by retrospectively evaluating them with the final diagnosis. Specifically, we first devise a Retrospective Diagnostic Evaluation (RDE) that scores observation and stop candidates from offline Monte Carlo tree search through independent continuations. Moreover, we design a Retrospective Gap-weighted Preference Optimization (RGPO) that learns from retrospective-score preferences weighted by their score gaps, prioritizing actions that improve diagnostic accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our GroundSeek-CXR framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods, and improves abnormal-image sample-F1 by 4.48% over immediate-score preference learning.

</details>


### [118] [Weight-Space Programming for Improving Image-Restoration Diffusion Models: Technical Conjectures and a Recursive Improvement Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04268)
*Zhuoran Zheng,Doubao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个猜想性研究议程：让LLM直接编辑扩散式图像复原模型（去模糊、超分、去噪、修复）的权重，在不使用梯度、配对数据和微调的前提下改善复原质量，并提出三条技术猜想（C1层定位、C2权重方向编辑、C3递归自改进循环）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实验表明前沿LLM可通过修改极少参数（约0.016%）修复结构性缺陷，但难以注入全新能力。作者希望把“权重空间编程（WSP）”从“调试”推进到“性能改进”，并选择图像复原这一精确回归场景作为切入点：其低层信号处理层集中、适合定向权重编辑，而高层语义层分布广泛、抗稀疏修改。同时探讨其是否构成视觉领域最小的递归自我改进（RSI）循环。

Method: 纯分析/立场性论文，无实验。提出三条可检验猜想：(C1) LLM可在200次前向预算内定位导致质量差距（过度平滑、色偏、幻觉纹理）的层；(C2) 编辑少量已识别的权重方向（如缩放高频滤波器范数、校正输出偏置通道）可提升PSNR与感知质量；(C3) “诊断→编辑→评估→再诊断”的递归闭环可跨轮次累积增益。另讨论所需AI能力层级（定向编辑不需超智能，通用能力生成则需要），分析像素级精确回归的难点，并给出PyTorch风格部署流程与基准设计草案。

Result: 无实证结果。论文仅给出概念性论证、可行性分析、能力需求分析，以及部署管线与基准的框架性描述。

Conclusion: 作者认为权重空间编程有望在图像复原中从“调试”迈向“改进”，并借递归编辑闭环探索视觉领域的最小RSI雏形；本文为猜想性手稿，旨在为图像复原开辟一个新的子方向。

Abstract: Can an LLM directly edit the weights of a diffusion-based image-restoration model to \emph{improve} restoration quality---without gradients, paired data, or fine-tuning? Recent experiments show frontier LLMs can fix structural bugs by modifying as little as \(0.016\%\) of parameters, but cannot instill new capabilities. We ask whether weight-space programming (WSP) can move from \emph{debugging} to \emph{improvement} in the precise-regression setting of image restoration (deblurring, super-resolution, denoising, inpainting). Restoration diffusion UNets present a middle ground: low-level signal-processing layers are concentrated enough for targeted weight-direction edits, while high-level semantic layers remain distributed and resistant to sparse modification. We propose three technical conjectures: (C1) an LLM can localize the layer behind a quality gap (over-smoothing, color shift, hallucinated texture) within a 200 forward-pass budget; (C2) editing a small set of identified weight directions (e.g., scaling high-frequency filter norms, correcting output-bias channels) can measurably improve PSNR and perceptual quality; (C3) a recursive loop---diagnose \(\rightarrow\) edit \(\rightarrow\) evaluate \(\rightarrow\) re-diagnose---can compound gains across rounds, forming a minimal recursive self-improvement (RSI) loop for vision. We analyze the required AI capability (arguing targeted edits need not superintelligence, while general capability generation does) and detail the challenges of precise pixel-level regression. We provide a PyTorch-style deployment pipeline and outline a benchmark. This manuscript is conjectural, intended to open a new subtopic for image restoration.

</details>


### [119] [Radial distortion homography estimation from affine-covariant or orientation-covariant features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04822)
*Marcus Valtonen Örnhag,Stefan Adalbjörnsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出利用仿射/方向协变特征联合估计单应与径向畸变的最小求解器，核心是刻画局部特征几何的新约束，所需对应更少、鲁棒估计更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点基方法需较多对应且在内点率低时鲁棒估计开销大；仿射描述子携带的局部几何信息未被充分利用。

Method: 构造基于仿射特征局部几何的新约束，结合单应与径向畸变模型推导最小配置求解器，并嵌入现代鲁棒估计框架。

Result: 合成与真实鱼眼畸变图像上，精度相当或更优；存在外点时显著加速。

Conclusion: 仿射/方向协变特征可降低最小对应数并提升鲁棒估计效率，对强鱼眼畸变场景实用。

Abstract: We present minimal solvers for joint homography and radial distortion estimation using affine- or orientation-covariant features. The core contribution is a set of novel constraints that exploit local feature geometry inherent to affine descriptors, enabling solvers that require fewer correspondences than point-based methods. Using modern robust estimation frameworks, we demonstrate that the proposed solvers are advantageous to the current state-of-the-art with a significant speed-up in the presence of outliers while maintaining comparable or superior estimation accuracy. The proposed solvers are tested on both synthetic and real images containing challenging fisheye distortions.

</details>


### [120] [Compression Forcing: Hierarchical History Tokenization Enables Flexible World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04028)
*Siqiao Huang,Partha Kaushik,Michael Chen,Hengkai Pan,Kaiwen Geng,Omar Chehab,Fernando Moreno-Pino,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Compression Forcing (CF) 框架，使历史压缩与世界模型的预测目标对齐，包含动态感知分词 (DAT) 与分层历史引导 (HG-H)，在多种环境中提升预测保真度、物理真实感与下游决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型沿用视频生成的 tokenization 与条件机制，目标偏向感知真实感而非预测效用；在视觉干扰多、背景与任务无关的环境下，该错配限制世界模型的有效性。

Method: CF 通过建模目标引导历史压缩。核心组件 DAT 将历史观测压缩为按动态显著性递减排序的 token 序列；基于扩散骨干网络，该结构支持 HG-H，在推理阶段选择性放大动态显著信息。

Result: 在多种环境中实验表明，预测保真度、物理真实感及下游决策性能均有提升。

Conclusion: Compression Forcing 是一种高度灵活且有效的世界建模范式，验证了以预测效用为目标的压缩与条件机制设计的重要性。

Abstract: Effective world modeling requires compressing salient history information that facilitates future prediction. However, most existing approaches inherit tokenization and conditioning mechanisms from video generation, where objectives are centered around perceptual realism rather than predictive utility. This discrepancy limits the world model's effectiveness, especially in environments dominated by visual nuisances or task-irrelevant background details. To this end, we introduce Compression Forcing (CF), a general framework that aligns history compression with the predictive objective of world modeling. Compression Forcing features Dynamics-Aware Tokenization (DAT), a novel tokenization method that compresses history observations into a sequence ordered by decreasing dynamics saliency. Built on a diffusion backbone, this structure enables Hierarchical History Guidance (HG-H), allowing selective amplification of dynamics-salient information during inference. Extensive experiments across various environments demonstrate improved prediction fidelity, physical realism, and downstream decision-making performance, establishing Compression Forcing as a highly flexible and effective approach for world modeling.

</details>


### [121] [Geometric Refinement and Distributional Alignment for Sim2Real Roadside LiDAR Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03936)
*Aman Upganlawar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge LUMPI 赛道参赛方案：仅用合成数据训练 3D 检测器并在真实路侧 LiDAR 上评测，最终综合得分 0.4793（检测 mAP 0.1333，真实感 0.9126）。主要增益来自几何精修、后处理修复与真实感指标解析化，而非新架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战任务要求检测器完全在合成点云上训练、迁移到未见真实路侧 LiDAR，同时评估合成数据的分布真实感，因此需要同时优化检测性能与数据真实感，并理解二者之间的依赖关系。

Method: 以 PointPillars 为基线，未提出新架构。三点改进：(1) 诊断出路侧 LiDAR 小目标失败源于定位而非检测，引入无参数几何精修；(2) 审计自身后处理，发现一条既有规则误删有效预测并修复；(3) 将真实感指标解析化分析，证明四分量之一为九个汇总统计量的闭式函数。另提出基于 mAP 按类别可分解性的实验方法论：一次提交可承载多个受控实验，且由已测分量组装的提交可在提交前预知分数。

Result: 综合 0.4793；检测 mAP 0.1333，真实感 0.9126。几何精修带来行人 AP 相对提升 86%；后处理修复带来 Van 类相对提升 335%，为最大单项检测增益；最终得分在提交前被预测至小数点后四位。论文同时等篇幅报告负结果，并在第 8 节完整交代数据来源、合规性及得分构成的全部机制。

Conclusion: 工程诊断与指标解析（定位问题、后处理缺陷、闭式真实感分量）比架构创新带来更大收益；可分解评测与分量可预知性构成更具可迁移性的实验方法论。

Abstract: We describe our approach to the LUMPI track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, in which a 3D object detector must be trained exclusively on synthetic data and evaluated on unseen real roadside LiDAR, while the synthetic point clouds are simultaneously scored for distributional realism. Our final entry scores 0.4793 combined, from a detection mAP of 0.1333 and a realism score of 0.9126. Our contribution is not a new architecture. Working from a PointPillars baseline, we obtained most of our gains from three sources. First, a diagnosis that small object failure in roadside LiDAR is a localisation problem rather than a detection problem, which led to a parameter free geometric refinement worth 86 percent relative on pedestrian AP. Second, an audit of our own post processing which revealed that a shipped rule was destroying valid predictions, the repair of which produced our single largest detection gain at 335 percent relative on the Van class. Third, treating the realism metrics analytically rather than as a black box, which showed that one of the four components is a closed form function of nine summary statistics. We also report an experimental methodology that we believe is the more transferable result. Because mean AP decomposes into independent per class terms, a single submission can carry several mutually controlled experiments, and a submission assembled entirely from previously measured components has a score that is known before it is sent. We predicted our final score to four decimal places prior to submission. We report negative results at equal length, and give a full account of data provenance and compliance in Section 8, including a full account of every mechanism contributing to the submitted score.

</details>


### [122] [KeyWise: Efficient Video-Only Piano Transcription with a Lightweight Per-Key Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04476)
*Anastasios Chatzigiannis,Natalia Panagou,Maria Koziri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KeyWise：利用键盘已知几何结构，将校正后键盘划分为88个等分ROI，用共享的轻量循环网络逐键跟踪，实现高效视频钢琴转写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频钢琴转写受延音踏板及噪声/缺失音轨困扰，而顶视视频可直接观测按键起止，故视觉转写更稳健；但现有视觉转写网络用单一全局特征描述整块键盘，需自行学习每个键的位置，参数冗余且效率低。

Method: 设计逐键（per-key）架构KeyWise：将校正后的键盘图像划分为88个等大tile作为兴趣区域（ROI），每个ROI由所有键共享的一个小型循环网络在时间维度上跟踪，从而以已知几何先验替代位置学习。模型参数仅1.3M。

Result: PianoVAM上起音F1达95.0%（50 ms）与97.8%（100 ms），略超SOTA V2N（94.7%/97.0%），参数量28.2M降至1.3M，同GPU上速度提升4.7倍；100 ms下释键F1 97.0% vs 95.5%、力度F1 89.4% vs 84.5%；PianoYT跨域预训练达69.4%（100 ms）起音F1，与最佳已发表结果相当。

Conclusion: 利用键盘几何先验的逐键轻量架构可在显著降低参数与计算成本的同时达到甚至超越全局特征SOTA的精度，且对释键与力度估计更准；但跨录制条件泛化仍需微调。

Abstract: Audio-based piano transcription is hampered by the sustain pedal and by noisy or missing soundtracks, problems that video-only transcription avoids, since a top-view video shows directly when each key is pressed and released. Most visual transcription networks, however, describe the whole keyboard with one global feature vector and must learn where each key is. In this paper, we propose KeyWise, a lightweight per-key architecture that exploits the known geometry of the keyboard instead of learning it. The rectified keyboard is divided into 88 equal tiles that serve as regions of interest (ROIs), and one small recurrent network, shared by all keys, follows each key over time. On PianoVAM, KeyWise reaches the onset accuracy of the state-of-the-art V2N (95.0% vs. 94.7% onset F1 at 50 ms and 97.8% vs. 97.0% at 100 ms) with 1.3 M instead of 28.2 M parameters, and it runs 4.7 times faster on the same GPU. At 100 ms, it is also significantly more accurate when the key releases (97.0% vs. 95.5% F1) or the velocities (89.4% vs. 84.5% F1) must be correct as well. On PianoYT, KeyWise pre-trained on PianoVAM reaches 69.4% onset F1 at 100 ms, comparable to the best published result, although it needs fine-tuning for new recording conditions.

</details>


### [123] [PLANTEUR: A Crop-Type Segmentation Benchmark for Tropical Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05223)
*Piper Wolters,Gabriel Tseng,Favyen Bastani,Joseph Redmon,Yawen Zhang,Hadrien Sablon,Ando Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLANTEUR：面向法属海外领地热带作物类型制图的多传感器卫星图像基准，含6个区域、8类标签、标准划分与留一岛协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物类型制图基准（如PASTIS）集中于温带/欧洲地区，缺乏热带与跨地域泛化评测，法属海外领地遥感数据分散且缺少统一对齐的多传感器标注数据集。

Method: 沿用PASTIS设计范式，构建地理参考且时空对齐的Sentinel-2、Sentinel-1与Landsat-8序列，配以地块（parcel）级标签，覆盖6个热带区域、8类标签空间；定义标准测试划分与留一岛（leave-one-island-out）协议以评测跨领地泛化；对比全监督模型、线性探针与微调的基础模型。

Result: 提供了跨6区域的标准与留一岛两种评测设定下的多基线结果，涵盖全监督、线性探测与微调基础模型的表现，可用于量化跨地域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PLANTEUR填补了热带多传感器作物制图基准空白，通过留一岛协议系统评估跨区域泛化；代码与数据在GitHub与Hugging Face开源，便于后续研究复现与拓展。

Abstract: We introduce PLANTEUR, a benchmark for crop-type mapping from satellite images in the French overseas territories. Building on the PASTIS design, PLANTEUR provides georeferenced and aligned Sentinel-2, Sentinel-1, and Landsat-8 sequences with parcel-derived labels spanning six tropical regions and an 8-class label space. We define both a standard test split and a leave-one-island-out protocol to measure cross-territory generalization, and evaluate fully supervised models, linear probes, and fine-tuned foundation models. The benchmark code and data are open source and available on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>


### [124] [TAQ-MOTR: Tracking-Aware Detection Query Formation for End-to-End Multiple Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05205)
*Hanyang Liu,Hong Zhang,Ding Yuan,Yang Han,Yifan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TAQ-MOTR，通过预解码器混合查询生成器（HQG）融合当前帧特征、轨迹记忆与可学习记忆，使检测查询感知轨迹状态，并引入仅在训练时使用的辅助检测分支，提升跟踪传播范式的关联与发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TBP 类方法中检测与关联紧耦合：检测查询既需发现尚未被跟踪的目标，又需为被遮挡的困难轨迹提供当前帧互补证据。但现有方法沿用单帧检测的查询构造方式，在解码器前生成查询且不感知轨迹状态，导致漏检新目标、困难轨迹缺乏补充证据，引发关联失败或重复响应。

Method: 1) 提出跟踪感知的检测查询构造框架，使用解码器前的 Hybrid Query Generator（HQG）融合当前帧图像特征、轨迹记忆与可学习记忆；2) 以轨迹状态为条件生成检测查询，使其自适应于“发现当前未跟踪目标”与“补充已跟踪目标证据”两类需求；3) 引入仅训练时使用的辅助检测分支，其一对一标签分配与主跟踪分支解耦，为共享的 HQG 和解码器提供额外检测监督。

Result: 在 DanceTrack、SportsMOT 和 BFT 上性能提升；DanceTrack 测试集达到 72.0 HOTA、63.1 AssA、76.6 IDF1。

Conclusion: 通过让检测查询感知轨迹状态并辅以解耦标签分配的检测监督，TAQ-MOTR 有效缓解了新目标漏检与遮挡轨迹关联失败问题，在多个 MOT 基准上取得一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Tracking-by-propagation (TBP) methods perform end-to-end multi-object tracking by propagating track queries across frames, jointly handling detection and identity association without heuristic post-processing while naturally modeling temporal information. However, as detection and association are tightly coupled, detection queries in TBP are expected to discover currently untracked objects and provide complementary current-frame evidence for challenging tracks whose queries and identities are retained. Existing methods largely inherit detection-query formation from single-frame detection and construct such queries before the decoder with little awareness of trajectory states. Consequently, currently untracked objects may be missed, while challenging tracked objects with reduced visibility under occlusion may lack complementary evidence, causing association failures or duplicate responses. To address these issues, we propose TAQ-MOTR, a tracking-aware detection query formation framework that employs a pre-decoder Hybrid Query Generator (HQG) to fuse current-frame image features, track memory, and learnable memory. By conditioning detection queries on trajectory states, TAQ-MOTR adapts them to \rev{the discovery of currently untracked objects and tracked-object complementation We further introduce a training-only auxiliary detection branch whose one-to-one label assignment is decoupled from that of the main tracking branch, providing additional detection supervision to strengthen the shared HQG and decoder for the discovery of currently untracked objects. TAQ-MOTR improves performance on DanceTrack, SportsMOT, and BFT, achieving 72.0 HOTA, 63.1 AssA, and 76.6 IDF1 on the DanceTrack test set. Code is available at https://github.com/Magicmiya/TAQ-MOTR

</details>


### [125] [Robust Reconstruction of Power Lines from Incomplete Mobile and Aerial LiDAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05197)
*Mohamed Gaha,Damos Ayobo Abongo,Philippe Massicotte,Eya Ben Kacem,Wael Jaafar,Rami Langar,Christian Buteau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自动化电力线重建方法，结合尺度化密度聚类、鲁棒多项式拟合与迭代片段合并，能在严重数据缺失与遮挡下恢复单根导线，并同一流程统一处理机载与移动LiDAR，无需模态特定调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网络中导线点云稀疏、不完整、含噪且被植被遮挡，且杆塔较低、线路构型复杂使导线贴近树木；同时机载与移动LiDAR在点密度、视角几何、覆盖与遮挡模式上差异大，现有检测与重建方法难以稳健适用。

Method: 语义分类后进行统一重建流程：尺度化密度聚类（scaled density-based clustering）用于导线点分组，鲁棒多项式拟合（robust polynomial fitting）提取曲线模型，迭代片段合并（iterative fragment merging）恢复断裂导线；同一管线与参数同时适用于机载和移动数据。评估包括退化实验、基准对比、参数敏感性分析与大规模验证。

Result: 机载数据集：精度96.87%、召回96.66%、F1 96.76%、几何RMSE 0.127 m；移动数据集（缺乏导线级参考标注）：点到模型RMSE 0.045 m，重建覆盖率95.27%。

Conclusion: 该方法对配电网络导线重建具有鲁棒性与跨模态通用性，在严重不完整与遮挡条件下仍取得高精度与高覆盖，且代码、文档与评估数据开源可复现。

Abstract: Accurate vegetation-encroachment assessment and risk mitigation require electric utilities to accurately locate and characterize distribution-network assets, including poles and power lines, and distinguish among medium-voltage, low-voltage, neutral, and telecommunication lines. Mobile and aerial Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) point clouds provide detailed 3D representations of these assets and their surroundings. However, power-line detection and reconstruction remain challenging because conductor returns are often sparse, incomplete, noisy, or occluded by vegetation. These challenges are especially significant in distribution networks, where lower pole heights and complex line configurations place conductors close to trees. In addition, mobile and aerial LiDAR datasets differ substantially in point density, viewing geometry, spatial coverage, and occlusion patterns. We propose a robust and automated reconstruction method combining scaled density-based clustering, robust polynomial fitting, and iterative fragment merging to recover individual conductors under severe data incompleteness and occlusion. Following semantic classification, the same reconstruction pipeline processes both mobile and aerial LiDAR data without modality-specific code or parameter tuning. The method is evaluated through degradation experiments, benchmarking, parameter sensitivity analysis, and large-scale validation. On the aerial dataset, it achieves 96.87% precision, 96.66% recall, a 96.76% F$_1$-score, and a geometric RMSE of 0.127 m. On the mobile dataset, where conductor-level reference annotations were unavailable, it achieves a point-to-model RMSE of 0.045 m and a reconstruction coverage of 95.27%. Source code, documentation, and evaluation data are publicly available at https://github.com/varen-project.

</details>


### [126] [From Natural Images to Medical Scans: Transferability of Diffusion Models for Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05192)
*Efthymios Georgiou,Ioannis Kakogeorgiou,Sarah Brüningk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估自然图像预训练对医学扩散模型去噪器的迁移效果，发现自然图像初始化能稳定提升生成质量，且该增益源于空间结构而非语义信息的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习已在监督与自监督医学图像分析中普及，但在生成建模（尤其是扩散模型去噪器）中作用尚未探索；现有医学扩散模型多从零训练去噪器，缺乏对自然图像迁移的系统评估。

Method: 在潜空间与像素空间两种范式、131M–952M多种模型规模、RadImageNet与BraTS两个放射学数据集上开展分析；比较自然图像初始化、域内医学预训练与多种重初始化方案；用语义探测与适配去噪器内部表征的空间结构指标分析迁移来源；并进行混合域去噪器训练。

Result: i) 自然图像初始化持续提升生成质量，像素空间相对gFID改善21–34%，潜空间仅约14%（tokeniser已提供大部分先验）；gFID增益与下游病理分类及影像组学特征FD一致。ii) 增益反映空间结构而非语义：语义探测不能预测生成质量，而适配的空间结构指标与gFID相关性达|r|=0.85。iii) 混合域训练显示自然与医学图像在晚层前的逐层表征相似，混合训练提升保真度。iv) 迁移收益依赖自然图像初始化，优于域内医学预训练与多种重初始化。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，自然图像去噪器可正向迁移至医学去噪器；空间组织结构是预训练发挥作用的关键通道，为医学扩散模型的初始化策略提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Transfer learning from natural images is standard in supervised and increasingly adopted in self-supervised medical image analysis, but its role in generative modelling, and in particular diffusion denoisers, remains underexplored. Medical diffusion models typically train the denoiser from scratch, with no systematic evaluation of natural-image transfer. We analyse this across latent and pixel paradigms, model sizes (131M-952M) and two radiology datasets (RadImageNet, BraTS), and ask which aspects of transfer support generation. We find that: i) Natural-image initialisation consistently improves generation, by 21-34% relative gFID in pixel space against 14% in latent space, where the tokeniser supplies much of the prior. gFID gains mirror downstream pathology classification and radiomic-feature FD, both proxies for clinical fidelity. ii) Benefits reflect spatial structure, not semantics: semantic probing does not predict generation quality, whereas a spatial-structure metric adapted to internal denoiser representations tracks gFID at $|r|=0.85$, positioning spatial organisation as a channel through which pretraining manifests. iii) Mixed-domain denoiser training shows that natural and medical images share similar layer-wise representations up to the late blocks, and mixing natural images into medical training improves fidelity. iv) Transfer benefit is tied to natural image initialisation, outperforming in-domain medical pretraining and several reinitialisation schemes. Overall, natural image denoisers transfer positively to medical ones in our setup. Code is available at https://github.com/CAIROLab-Bern/rad-dit

</details>


### [127] [Wonder 4: A Mixture-of-Experts Recipe for Image Quality Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05294)
*Yunan Wu,Bin Sun,Partha Acharjee,Xiaoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Wonder 4：一种混合专家（MoE）图像质量增强模型，可同时对真实照片的多种退化在各自发生区域进行修复，无需退化分类器、掩码、提示或任务标签。采用"最小使用份额偏置"替代标准MoE的负载均衡规则，避免专家被迫均匀使用，在DIV2K×4、DIV2K×8、Flickr2K×4三个测试设置上取得最优LPIPS、DISTS与NIQE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实照片常同时存在模糊、噪点、压缩、划痕与低分辨率等多种退化，且每种退化的严重程度和发生位置各不相同，对应修复方式相互冲突。现有模型用单一描述刻画整幅图像，无法表达"哪处该用哪种修复"。此外，标准MoE训练沿用语言模型的负载均衡策略，强制各专家使用率均等，导致所有修复被施加到所有区域，包括不需要修复之处。

Method: 构建MoE式图像质量增强模型，每个模块含N=13个专家，路由器将每个图像块路由至k=5个专家，路由器与模型联合训练。以"最小使用份额偏置"替代均衡负载均衡：对全局使用率低于阈值的专家提高其路由分数直至被重新选中，超过阈值的专家不受干预，从而不强制专家等频使用。该偏置无需额外损失、额外模块或额外处理步骤。

Result: 在DIV2K与Flickr2K上，与5种已发表方法及3种开放权重流水线对比，Wonder 4在DIV2K×4、DIV2K×8、Flickr2K×4三个测试设置下的LPIPS、DISTS、NIQE三项指标均取得最优。

Conclusion: 以区域级专家路由实现多退化联合修复，无需退化先验与任务标签即可实现空间自适应增强；以最小份额偏置取代等频均衡，是MoE用于图像复原的有效训练准则，且不引入额外开销。

Abstract: A real photograph is often blurry, grainy, compressed, scratched, and low-resolution at the same time. Each defect has its own severity and its own place in the image. The corrections for these defects conflict with each other. Exist- ing models describe the whole image with one description, so they cannot say where each correction belongs. To solve this problem, we present Wonder 4, a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model for image quality enhancement. It corrects several defects of a real photograph at the same time, each in the region where it occurs. Every block of the model holds a mixture of N = 13 experts. A router, trained with the model, sends every image patch to k = 5 of them. No degradation classifier, mask, prompt, detector, or task label is used. Another issue is that standard MoE training, taken from language models, pushes every expert toward equal use. Equal use applies every correction everywhere, even where it is not needed. We replace this rule with a bias that only sets a minimum share of use. Across all patches, an expert used less than this minimum gets higher router scores until it is selected again. An expert above the minimum is not changed, so no expert is forced to be used as often as the others. The bias needs no extra loss. The method adds no extra module and no extra processing step. We evaluate Wonder 4 on DIV2K and Flickr2K, against five published methods and three open-weight pipelines. It reaches the best LPIPS, DISTS, and NIQE on all three test settings (DIV2K x4, DIV2K x8, and Flickr2K x4).

</details>


### [128] [Anchored Rank-Adversarial Fine-Tuning Pretrained Visual Place Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05286)
*Mostafa Kira,Bader Rasheed,Manuel Mazzara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出锚定排序对抗微调 (ARAFT)，在预设干净精度预算下将视觉位置识别的对抗鲁棒性 R@1 从 0 提升到 47.3（SuperVLAD）与 55.3（BoQ），并可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于基础模型的 VPR 模型在跨城市、季节与光照下精度很高，但对排序攻击极不鲁棒：在 1/255 的不可感知扰动预算下白盒攻击使 SuperVLAD 与 BoQ 的 top-1 召回至多保留 0.2%。作者希望在不让干净精度大幅退化的前提下提升鲁棒性，并给出明确的可比权衡。

Method: ARAFT：对已发布模型微调；对每个对抗查询，用其最难正样本与冻结预训练模型的干净描述子作为锚点，施加 listwise 排序损失；检查点选择遵循事先固定的干净精度预算，并报告完整权衡曲线。与普通对抗训练对比。

Result: 在验证集干净 R@1 下降 5 点以内时，普通对抗训练无可用检查点，而 ARAFT 将鲁棒 R@1 从 0 提升至 47.3（SuperVLAD）、55.3（BoQ）；普通对抗训练需约三倍干净代价才超越。测试集上 2/255 时 ARAFT 鲁棒 R@1 与无预算的普通对抗训练持平，且干净 R@1 高约 10 点；4/255 时普通对抗训练鲁棒性领先 6–9 点，但 ARAFT 仍保持 10 点干净优势。

Conclusion: ARAFT 在严格干净精度预算下显著恢复 VPR 的排序鲁棒性，性价比优于普通对抗训练；在 SuperVLAD 上开发后可零调参迁移到 BoQ，且对更强优化器仍鲁棒、无梯度掩蔽迹象。局限在于高扰动预算下绝对鲁棒性仍落后。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) localises a camera by retrieving similar geo-tagged images, and current foundation-model VPR models are accurate across cities, seasons and illumination, but not robust to adversarial perturbations: under a white-box attack on the ranking at an imperceptible budget of 1/255, SuperVLAD and BoQ keep at most 0.2% top-1 recall (R@1) on four benchmarks. We propose anchored rank-adversarial fine-tuning (ARAFT), which fine-tunes a released model with a listwise rank loss on each adversarial query's hardest positive and an anchor to the clean descriptor of the frozen pretrained model. Checkpoints are selected under a clean-accuracy budget fixed in advance, and the full trade-off is reported. Within five validation R@1 points of clean cost, plain adversarial training has no admissible checkpoint, whereas ARAFT raises robust R@1 from zero to 47.3 on SuperVLAD and 55.3 on BoQ; plain adversarial training exceeds this only at about three times the clean cost. Against unbudgeted plain adversarial training on the test sets, ARAFT matches its robust R@1 at 2/255 with about ten more clean R@1 points. At 4/255, plain adversarial training is 6-9 robust R@1 points ahead, while ARAFT still keeps its ten-point clean advantage. Developed on SuperVLAD, ARAFT transfers to BoQ without re-tuning, and its robustness holds against stronger optimisers, with no sign of gradient masking.

</details>


### [129] [Route Anything: A Study of Model and Policy Routing for General-Purpose AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05275)
*Pengfei Zhou,Yixing Ma,Hexin Wang,Weidong Tang,Zhenglin Wan,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Route Anything 统一框架，研究通用 AI 系统中的模型与策略路由，并建立质量—成本权衡下的最优路由理论及"路由器可获得信息决定其可达性能"的刻画；构建覆盖 25 个基准、452 个候选配置、374,401 次评分运行的 omnimodal 轨迹矩阵，实证路由在性能与效率上的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通向 AGI 未必依赖单一模型掌握全部能力，更可行的路径是将异构的专用模型、工具与策略编排为统一系统；核心问题在于如何为每个任务动态选择并协调最优候选，同时平衡性能与计算成本，而这一问题的理论基础与系统性实证仍缺乏。

Method: 1) 建立统一理论框架，刻画质量—成本权衡下的最优路由，并分析路由器可用信息量对其可达性能的决定作用；2) 构建 omni-modal 轨迹矩阵：25 个基准（语言理解、图像/视频/语音生成、编码与办公智能体、机器人操作），452 个候选配置，374,401 次评分运行；3) 在该框架上训练并测试多种路由器，考察 omni-modal 路由潜力、学习型路由器能力及路由提升效率的条件。

Result: 逐输入 oracle 路由较最佳固定候选在 16 个基准上高 5.4 分；学习型路由器较最佳固定候选高 0.3 分（16 个基准）、视频生成高 2.1 分、机器人操作高 1.1 分且策略耗时减少 24%；相较参考模型，路由平均提升效率 8.82 倍。

Conclusion: 路由（能力编排）在性能与效率上均有实质空间，学习型路由器已能超越单一最佳固定模型，并在部分模态显著受益；统一的理论与基准框架为研究通用 AI 系统的能力编排提供了可复用的分析基础。

Abstract: Artificial general intelligence (AGI) requires a method that can understand, generate, reason, and act across diverse domains and modalities. Rather than relying on a single model to master every capability, a promising path toward AGI now is to orchestrate a heterogeneous ecosystem of specialized models, tools, and policies (candidates) into a unified AI system. At the heart of this vision lies a fundamental question: how can an AI system dynamically select and coordinate the most capable candidate for each task while balancing performance and computational cost? In this work, we present Route Anything, a unified framework for studying model and policy routing across general-purpose AI systems. We develop a unified theoretical foundation that characterizes optimal routing under quality--cost trade-offs and establishes how the information available to a router determines its attainable performance. To systematically investigate routing across diverse capabilities, we first construct an omni-modal trajectory matrix spanning 25 benchmarks across language understanding, image, video, and speech generation, coding and workplace agents, and robotic manipulation, comprising 374,401 scored runs from 452 candidate configurations. Building on this framework, we train and test different routers to investigate the potential of omni-modal model routing, the capabilities of learned routers, and the conditions under which routing improves efficiency. Our results reveal substantial opportunities for capability orchestration: per-input oracle routing outperforms the best fixed candidate by 5.4 points, and learned routers outperform it by 0.3 points across 16 benchmarks, by 2.1 points on video generation, and by 1.1 points on robot manipulation while using 24% less policy time. More importantly, routing raises efficiency by 8.82x on average compared with the reference model.

</details>


### [130] [Diffusion Plug-in Priors for Self-Supervised Ultrasound Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05206)
*Chu Chen,Mingduo He,Yining Li,Yinhao Wu,Wei Tang,Yiwen Luo,Yixuan Chen,Graham Treece,Pasquale Cascarano,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UltraDiff，用冻结的预训练扩散模型作为插件式先验，通过优化采样器的潜在种子实现无需配对数据的超声视频联合超分与去斑，并提升下游射血分数预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声视频受硬件限制空间分辨率低，且存在信号相关的散斑；同时成对的低/高分辨率数据难以获取，因此需要无配对数据的超分与去斑方法，且应避免网络重训练。

Method: 以冻结的预训练扩散模型为插件先验，在范围约束的最大后验框架下逐帧优化DM采样器的潜在种子。由Rayleigh尺度族模型推导散斑感知的数据保真项，分析其相对残差与方差自适应加权特性；引入在正观测下（非截断形式）有下界且关于对数强度严格凸的稳定化保真项。以全变分正则补充DM先验，并用前一帧优化种子对当前帧种子进行时间热启动。

Result: 在含模拟散斑与加性噪声的EchoNet-LVH视频上，采用自然图像先验的UltraDiff较最强基线PSNR提升1.24 dB，超声相关先验进一步提升。在真实临床超声心动图视频上，相较最强基线DUP，零样本射血分数预测的平均绝对误差由7.31降至6.37个EF点。

Conclusion: 散斑感知保真项与冻结扩散先验结合，可实现无真值的超声视频复原并有益于下游测量；方法无需配对数据与重训练，且不绑定特定扩散模型检查点。

Abstract: \textit{Objective:} Ultrasound videos are limited by hardware-constrained spatial resolution and signal-dependent speckle, while paired low- and high-resolution acquisitions for supervised restoration are rarely available. We aim to jointly super-resolve and despeckle ultrasound videos without paired training data or network retraining. \textit{Methods:} We propose UltraDiff, which uses a frozen pre-trained diffusion model (DM) as a plug-in prior and restores each frame by optimizing the latent seed of the DM sampler under a range-constrained maximum a posteriori formulation. We derive a speckle-aware data-fidelity term from a Rayleigh scale-family model and characterize its relative-residual and variance-adaptive weighting properties. We further introduce a stabilized fidelity that is bounded below and strictly convex in log intensity for positive observations in its unclamped form. A total-variation regularizer complements the DM prior, and a temporal seed update warm-starts each frame from the optimized seed of its predecessor. \textit{Results:} On EchoNet-LVH videos with simulated speckle and additive noise, UltraDiff with a natural-image prior improves PSNR by 1.24\,dB over the strongest baseline, and ultrasound-related priors improve it further. On real clinical echocardiography videos, UltraDiff reduces the mean absolute error of zero-shot ejection fraction (EF) prediction from 7.31 to 6.37 EF points relative to DUP, the strongest baseline. \textit{Conclusion:} Combining a speckle-aware fidelity with frozen diffusion priors enables ground-truth-free ultrasound video restoration that benefits downstream measurement. \textit{Significance:} UltraDiff needs neither paired data nor retraining and is not tied to a specific diffusion checkpoint. Code associated with this work will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [131] [Möbius: A Unified Motion-Language Model Bridging Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05279)
*Jiazhi Shu,Chengfeng Zhao,Jinyi Wang,Yubo Zhao,Zhiyang Dou,Ronghui Li,Yu Lu,Xinwei Jiang,Zijiao Zeng,Yuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Möbius 将完整扩散过程引入与运动理解共享的多模态表示空间，在连续帧级原始运动上直接完成理解、生成与编辑，无需离散分词或预训练运动VAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一运动模型（UMM）只是"部分统一"：理解由语言模型承担，生成则交给基于学习运动latent的独立扩散模块，两阶段割裂。作者希望构建单一框架内同时理解、生成、编辑的完全统一运动模型。

Method: 把扩散过程搬进与理解相同的多模态表示空间：干净运动、含噪运动与文本共同参与共享注意力，理解与生成流由角色专属参数处理；直接在连续帧级原始运动上操作，不需离散token化，也不依赖预训练运动VAE。

Result: 单一模型同时支持运动描述、文本到运动生成、时序补全与指令引导编辑；在 HumanML3D 上于统一运动模型中达到SOTA；扩展至1.7B参数、1700小时运动数据仍有效且可扩展。

Conclusion: 将扩散纳入共享多模态表示空间可实现真正统一的运动模型，且无需tokenizer/VAE的简洁设计兼具效果与可扩展性。

Abstract: We aim to build a truly unified motion model that can understand, generate, and edit human motion within a single framework. Existing motion UMMs remain only partially unified: motion understanding is handled by a language model, while generation is often delegated to a separate diffusion module operating over learned motion latents. We introduce Möbius, which brings the entire diffusion process into the same multimodal representation space used for motion understanding. Clean motion, noisy motion, and text jointly participate in shared attention, while role-specific parameters process understanding and generation streams. Importantly, Möbius operates directly on continuous frame-level raw motion, requiring neither discrete tokenization nor a pretrained motion VAE. This simple formulation naturally supports motion captioning, text-to-motion generation, temporal completion, and instruction-guided editing within a single model. On HumanML3D, Möbius achieves state-of-the-art performance among unified motion models. Scaling to 1.7B parameters and 1,700 hours of motion data further demonstrates that our framework is both effective and scalable.

</details>


### [132] [Diagnosis by Composition: Detecting and Localizing Unseen Diseases by Composing Learned Radiological Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05231)
*Kaihua Hou,Wei Liu,Yi Huang,Hao Zhang,Haibo Jin,Yifei Sun,Yuan gao,Zuoou Li,Ling Zhang,Ahmed Alaa,Geoffrey H. Tison,Xu Han,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DbC（diagnosis by composition）将影像诊断从“图像→疾病”重建为“图像→征象→疾病”，用与征象文本嵌入对齐的稠密征象图，并按指南以无参数的方式组合成疾病诊断图，实现可解释、可审计、可零重训扩展的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射影像深度诊断模型直接由图像预测疾病标签，受限于训练时见过的疾病类别，且决策难以用放射科医生的语言解释。而放射学实际流程是依据公开指南由视觉征象推导疾病，规则显式、可复现，故作者希望把这种“征象优先”的逻辑编码进表示学习框架。

Method: 学习一个放射征象词表：将图像特征与各征象的文本嵌入对齐，每个征象表示为扫描上的稠密图（征象定位图）。疾病由指南导出的必需征象集合定义，其诊断图为相应征象图固定、无参数的组合。由于征象到疾病的映射来自指南而非学习得到，框架天然可解释、可审计；新疾病只需指定其征象即可扩展而无需重训，未见疾病也可通过组合其征象被诊断。

Result: 在多期肝脏CT上，DbC在检测与定位未见疾病、来自其他机构的罕见疾病以及外部队列上显著优于所有对比方法（包括图像—疾病名称对齐模型）；在全监督设定下检测与定位亦有提升，并在不同训练集规模下保持优势。

Conclusion: 以征象组合为核心的DbC在肝脏CT上验证了可解释性、可扩展性与对未见/罕见疾病的泛化优势；该方法适用于任何以征象定义疾病的放射学指南场景。

Abstract: Deep models for radiological diagnosis are typically trained to predict disease labels directly from images, which limits them to diseases seen during training and makes their decisions hard to interpret in radiologists' terms. Radiology, however, proceeds from visual findings to disease through published guidelines that make this decision rule explicit, interpretable, and reproducible. We introduce diagnosis by composition (DbC), a representation learning framework that encodes this findings-first logic. DbC learns a vocabulary of radiological findings by aligning image features with the text embeddings of the findings, each finding represented as a dense map over the scan. A disease is defined by a set of required findings derived from its guidelines, and its diagnosis map is a fixed, parameter-free composition of the corresponding finding maps. Because the finding-to-disease mapping is derived from guidelines rather than learned, DbC is interpretable and auditable by design, can extend to new diseases by specifying their findings without retraining, and can diagnose unseen diseases by composing their findings. On multi-phase liver CT, DbC detects and localizes unseen diseases, rare diseases from a different institution, and an external cohort significantly better than all comparators, including models that align images with disease names. DbC also improves detection and localization in the fully supervised setting and remains superior across training-set sizes. Although demonstrated on liver CT, DbC applies wherever radiological guidelines define diseases through findings.

</details>


### [133] [PadLoc: Training-Free Floor-Plan Localization by Sparse Geometric Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05172)
*Marian Bookhahn,Frank Neumann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PadLoc是一种无需训练、以稀疏几何匹配替代稠密位姿网格的楼层平面定位后端，保持深度前端可插拔，在多个基准上匹配或超越UnLoc/F3Loc/Z-FLoc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 楼层平面成本低、持久且对光照外观变化鲁棒，但现有学习深度方法依赖在稠密(x,y,θ)位姿网格上逐帧滤波，计算重、状态随轨迹增长。作者希望保留可替换的深度前端，用稀疏几何匹配重建后端。

Method: 1) 由主导墙面朝向生成小型位姿候选集；2) 用掩膜归一化互相关(NCC)将观测自由空间与平面图比对打分；3) 采用运动位姿高斯混合贝叶斯滤波；4) 通过一致性投票(consensus)读出完整轨迹；5) 可选加入学习式墙体隔离模块。

Result: Gibson各轨迹长度上，在固定竞争者深度前端条件下，PadLoc达到或超过UnLoc前向滤波与F3Loc滤波，并在T=35起超过UnLoc离线精化；共享MapAnything前端时超越Z-FLoc发表结果。Bonn LiLocBench上相对UnLoc，轮式里程计下SR@1m提升23–32个百分点，真值运动下提升35–43个百分点；全视场LiDAR+轮式里程计时静态序列达100% SR@1m、中位误差9–10 cm。Hilti-Trimble挑战中仅相机鱼眼在数百米施工楼层上，5个会话中3个在线SR@2m为0.90–0.98；加入学习式墙体隔离后5个会话全部覆盖，SR@2m为0.67–0.92，定位器本身不变。

Conclusion: PadLoc以稀疏几何匹配+贝叶斯滤波替代稠密网格后端，达到或优于稠密网格方法，同时保持传感器模块化，且状态紧凑、不随轨迹长度增长。

Abstract: We propose PadLoc, a training-free localization back-end that places a moving sensor within a known floor plan. Floor plans are cheap, persistent, and robust to appearance change, but recent learned-depth methods localize against them by filtering per-frame depth through dense $(x,y,θ)$ pose grids. PadLoc keeps the swappable depth front-end and replaces the dense grid back-end with sparse geometric matching: dominant wall orientations define a small pose-candidate set, masked normalized cross-correlation scores observed free space against the plan, and a moving-pose Gaussian-mixture Bayes filter plus consensus readout decodes the trajectory. Holding each competitor's depth fixed, PadLoc matches or beats UnLoc's forward filter and the F3Loc filter at every Gibson trajectory length, and UnLoc's offline refinement from $T=35$ on; it exceeds Z-FLoc's published results with a shared MapAnything front-end. On Bonn LiLocBench, PadLoc improves pooled SR@1m over UnLoc by 23-32 pp under wheel odometry and 35-43 pp with ground-truth motion; with full-FOV LiDAR plus wheel odometry the same readout reaches 100% SR@1m at 9-10 cm median error on the static sequence. On the Hilti-Trimble Localization Challenge PadLoc localizes a camera-only fisheye rig on construction floors hundreds of metres across at 0.90-0.98 online SR@2m on three of five sessions; adding learned wall isolation, applied to every session, covers all five at 0.67-0.92 with the localizer unchanged throughout. PadLoc thus matches or outperforms dense grid back-ends while preserving sensor modularity and a compact state that does not grow with trajectory length.

</details>


### [134] [Visibility-Aware Reference Generation for Benchmarking Visual 3D Reconstruction in Vegetation-Rich Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05361)
*Zhipeng Zhong,Matthew Coombes,Cunjia Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于LiDAR的参考数据生成流水线（含LDGVS可见性评分与HPR尺度自动选择），用于在植被丰富的户外场景中评测前馈视觉几何模型与传统SfM的零样本深度估计与三维重建性能，并在TartanAirV2、TartanGround与BotanicGarden上进行了对比实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植被丰富的户外环境中缺乏几何真值，完整扫描测量成本高，导致视觉三维重建与深度估计的可信评测受限。

Method: 复用现有多传感器定位数据集中的相机图像、LiDAR扫描与参考位姿，生成逐视角深度图与可见参考点云；针对累积LiDAR扫描中的可见性误差，提出LargeDepth-Gap Visibility Score（LDGVS）用于逐点可见性过滤，并用于Hidden Point Removal（HPR）的尺度自动选择。在TartanAirV2、TartanGround上与Poisson表面重建对比，并在BotanicGarden上系统比较前馈方法与SfM。

Result: 在相近绝对相对误差下，HPR+LDGVS组合保留的点密度约为Poisson表面重建的3倍。前馈方法可高效生成稠密深度预测，但三维重建可能出现重复结构；传统SfM几何更一致，但重建稀疏且在困难植被场景中更易失败。

Conclusion: 该流水线为植被户外场景的视觉几何评测提供了低成本、高密度的LiDAR参考基准，揭示了前馈方法与SfM在稠密度、几何一致性与鲁棒性上的互补特性；基准工具包与数据集将公开发布。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of visual 3D reconstruction and depth estimation in vegetation-rich outdoor environments is limited by the scarcity of geometric ground-truth data and the cost of complete scanner surveys. We present a LiDARbased reference-generation pipeline for evaluating the zero-shot performance of feed-forward visual-geometry models alongside conventional structure-from-motion (SfM) methods. The pipeline reuses camera images, LiDAR scans, and reference poses from existing multi-sensor localization datasets to generate per-view depth maps and visible reference point clouds. To address visibility errors in accumulated LiDAR scans, we introduce the LargeDepth-Gap Visibility Score (LDGVS) for point-wise visibility filtering and automatic scale selection in Hidden Point Removal(HPR). Experiments on TartanAirV2 and TartanGround show that, compared with Poisson surface reconstruction, which is an approach suited to completed scan surveys, the combined HPR and LDGVS filtering retains approximately three times the point density at comparable absolute relative error. We also apply the pipeline to the BotanicGarden dataset to systematically compare depth estimation and 3D reconstruction methods. Feedforward methods produce dense depth predictions efficiently, but their 3D reconstructions can contain duplicated structures. Conventional SfM generally produces more coherent geometry, but its reconstructions remain sparse and more prone to failure in challenging vegetated scenes. The benchmark toolkit and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [135] [GSCV++: Lightweight 3D Gaussian Splatting Sequence Compression via Standard Video Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05359)
*Qi Yang,Yingzhan Xu,Kai Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSCV++ 是一个基于标准视频编解码器的轻量级 3D 高斯泼溅（GS）序列压缩流水线，通过改进预处理、3D-to-2D 映射和实用视频压缩配置，实现低复杂度、硬件兼容的高效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向工业界对低复杂度与硬件兼容性的需求，现有 GS 压缩方法在预处理冗余、3D-to-2D 映射对视频编解码器不友好、以及实际压缩配置低效等方面存在不足，需要一套轻量且可落地于标准视频编解码器的 GS 序列压缩方案。

Method: 提出三个模块：(1) 预处理阶段引入能量自适应帧间球谐（SH）交流分量降维（EA-SHDR），构建紧凑且稳定的时序 SH AC 特征空间以去除冗余；(2) 3D-to-2D 映射阶段设计基于先验的 Inter-MiniPLAS（P-MiniPLAS），通过先验加权因子推断与小尺度重映射生成平滑 2D 特征图，较 vanilla PLAS 与 Morton 扫描更适合视频编解码且复杂度低；(3) 实用压缩阶段提出内容感知广义压缩（CA-GC），对坐标图生成采用非均匀位深划分，并引入编码单元尺寸选择以控制压缩复杂度，在不修改规范的前提下充分利用视频编解码器性能。

Result: 相比 SOTA 方法，帧内预测率失真增益超过 20%，帧间预测率失真增益超过 60%；在同类视频编解码器下，2D 图生成、编码与解码速度均快于其他 SOTA 方法；消融实验验证了各模块的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GSCV++ 以轻量、硬件兼容的设计在压缩效率与计算速度上均优于现有方法，验证了预处理降维、先验映射与内容感知压缩配置的协同有效性，并为后续研究提供了参考。

Abstract: This paper presents GSCV++, a lightweight pipeline for 3D {G}aussian {S}platting (GS) sequences {C}ompression with standard {V}ideo codecs. To meet the requirements of low-complexity and hardware compatibility from the industry, GSCV++ jointly improves preprocessing, 3D-to-2D mapping, and practical video compression configuration of GS compression. First, for preprocessing, energy-adaptive inter-frame spherical harmonic (SH) alternative current (AC) dimensionality reduction (EA-SHDR) is proposed to produces a compact and stable temporal SH AC feature space. It can reduce the data volume of following mapping and practical compression, as well as less complexity while eliminating redundant information. Second, for 3D-to-2D mapping, prior-based Inter-MiniPLAS (P-MiniPLAS) is designed to generate smooth 2D feature maps through prior-based weighting factor inference and small scale remapping. Compared with vanilla Parallel Linear Assignment Sorting (PLAS) and Morton scan, P-MiniPLAS ensures the generated 2D maps are more suitable for the video codec while maintaining a low level of computational complexity. Third, for practical compression, content-aware generalized compression (CA-GC) is developed to use a non-uniform bit-depth split for coordinate map generation, and a coding unit size selection is introduced for efficient compression complexity control, which fully exploits the performance of video codecs without involving normative changes. Experiments demonstrate that GSCV++ reports over 20\% in intra- and 60\% in inter-prediction rate-distortion gains compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. GSCV++ also achieves faster 2D map generation, encoding, and decoding than other SOTA methods using the same type of video codecs. Ablation studies verify the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed modules and provide insights for subsequent research.

</details>


### [136] [YANAMI: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset and Benchmark for Multiturn Stateful Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05280)
*Shengyu Wang,Tengyue Nong,Junming Liu,Haosong Peng,Siyu Li,Ruiqi Lei,Fushuo Huo,Yuhao Pan,Yunfeng Fan,Ding Wang,Nan Cheng,Haozhao Wang,Wenchao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个面向有状态交互的大规模多模态对话数据集与基准 YANAMI（含 YANAMI-400K 与 YANAMI-Bench），填补现有多模态数据集以单轮或可独立求解的多轮任务为主的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMMs）的数据集与基准以单轮任务为主，即使是多轮数据，多数轮次也可独立求解而无须跨轮追踪信息，因而对"多模态有状态交互"（跨轮理解、维护、更新并忠实实现演化中的对话状态）的监督与评测不足。

Method: 构建 YANAMI-400K：40 万多轮多模态对话、逾 200 万张图像、260 万对话轮次，覆盖多图融合、图像编辑、世界建模、视觉推理四类场景，要求模型持续追踪、保持与修订跨轮演化的多模态对话状态；并设计 YANAMI-Bench：400 段多轮对话，联合评测对话状态理解（Dialogue State Understanding）与视觉实现（Visual Realization）。

Result: 在 YANAMI-400K 上微调 BAGEL，图像编辑、生成与多模态有状态交互分别提升 10.3%、2.0% 与 13.23%，同时保持通用视觉理解能力；YANAMI-Bench 评测显示现有 UMMs 在维护、更新及视觉实现演化对话状态方面仍存在明显困难。

Conclusion: YANAMI 为多模态有状态交互提供了首个大规模数据与评测体系，揭示了"状态理解"与"视觉实现"之间的能力差距，表明现有 UMMs 在该方向上仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) integrate visual understanding, generation, and editing within a single unified framework. However, existing unified multimodal datasets and benchmarks remain dominated by single-turn tasks. Even in recent multi-turn datasets, many turns can be solved independently without tracking information across turns. Consequently, they provide limited supervision and evaluation for multimodal stateful interaction, which requires models to understand, maintain, update, and faithfully realize evolving dialogue states across turns. To address this gap, we introduce YANAMI, the first large-scale multimodal dialogue dataset and benchmark for stateful interaction, comprising YANAMI-400K and YANAMI-Bench. YANAMI-400K contains 400K multi-turn multimodal dialogues, with more than 2M images and 2.6M dialogue turns. The dialogues span four representative scenarios: Multi-Image Fusion, Image Editing, World Modeling, and Visual Reasoning, and require models to continuously track, preserve, and revise evolving multimodal dialogue states across turns. YANAMI-Bench comprises 400 multi-turn dialogues for systematically evaluating multimodal stateful interaction. It jointly evaluates Dialogue State Understanding and Visual Realization, characterizing the gap between models' ability to understand evolving dialogue states and their ability to faithfully realize them in visual outputs. Experiments show that fine-tuning BAGEL on YANAMI-400K improves image editing, generation, and multimodal stateful interaction by 10.3%, 2.0%, and 13.23%, respectively, while preserving its general visual understanding capabilities. Evaluations on YANAMI-Bench further reveal that existing UMMs still struggle to maintain, update, and visually realize evolving multimodal dialogue states.

</details>


### [137] [Low-Density 3D Point Cloud Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05291)
*Ahmed Baha Ben Jmaa,Faten Chaieb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向低密度点云的分类网络LD-PointNet++，并通过首个系统性的密度变化基准评测表明其在各密度水平下保持90%以上精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云分类方法在稠密点云上表现优异，但受传感技术限制与环境条件变化影响，实际采集的点云密度往往较低且波动大，导致主流方法性能显著下降。作者希望系统量化这一退化问题并给出针对性方案。

Method: 1）开展首个基准研究，在不同密度水平下系统评测多种SOTA点云分类方法，揭示低密度场景下的性能下降；2）在此基础上设计LD-PointNet++，一种专为低密度点云分类优化的网络架构。

Result: 在ModelNet基准数据集上的实验表明，LD-PointNet++在低密度场景下优于现有方法，并在不同密度水平下保持一致的90%以上分类精度。

Conclusion: 密度鲁棒性是点云分类实际部署的关键因素；LD-PointNet++通过针对性架构设计实现了跨密度水平的稳定高精度，为低密度点云分类提供了有效基线。

Abstract: 3D point cloud classification, a task in computer vision, has recently gained substantial interest due to its extensive applications across various domains such as augmented and virtual reality, robotics, and autonomous driving. However, classifying 3D point clouds becomes challenging in real-world scenarios where the density levels vary. These variations are due to limitations inherent to current sensing technologies and variable environmental conditions, which often prevent the capture of detailed and comprehensive geometric representations. Existing classification methods, which have demonstrated impressive results on dense point clouds, may be affected and could experience reduced performance when dealing with low-density point clouds. Addressing this challenge, this paper introduces LD-PointNet++, an architecture optimized for the classification of low-density point clouds.
