To pave the way for LD-PointNet++, we conduct the first benchmarking study that systematically evaluates state-of-the-art point cloud classification methods across varying densities, revealing significant performance declines in low-density environments. Experimental results on the ModelNet benchmark dataset demonstrate that LD-PointNet++ not only outperforms these methods in low-density scenarios but also demonstrates consistent accuracy above 90% across different density levels.

</details>


### [138] [Factorized MLLM Embeddings for Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05193)
*WonJun Moon,Seongchan Kim,Dahun Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FactEmbed，将组合查询理解与判别性嵌入构建解耦，通过因子化潜变量模拟目标视觉修改，提升组合图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的组合图像检索方法仅用对比监督将组合查询直接映射为单一检索嵌入，未显式解析预期的视觉修改，导致 MLLM 的组合理解能力未被充分利用。

Method: 引入一组因子化潜变量（factorized latents），通过中间"目标想象"（target imagination）目标使其在形成判别表示前先对齐目标语义；优化因子级对比损失，并将分解表示蒸馏到 MLLM 更深的注意力层以全局传播；推理时保留 [EOS] token 构建全局嵌入，并用均值池化的因子化潜变量对短候选列表重排序（同一次前向完成）。

Result: 在多个 CIR 基准上取得优于已有工作的表现。

Conclusion: 显式解耦组合理解与嵌入构建、并以因子化潜变量承载视觉修改语义，是提升 MLLM 组合图像检索的有效途径，且推理开销几乎不增。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) aims to retrieve a target image given a reference image and a modification text. Although recent approaches leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have shown significant progress, we argue that the MLLM's compositional understanding remains underutilized. This stems from directly mapping composed queries into a retrieval embedding via contrastive supervision alone, without explicitly resolving the intended visual modifications. In this work, we introduce Factorized MLLM Embeddings (FactEmbed), which decouples compositional query comprehension from building discriminative embeddings. Rather than directly projecting the composed query into a single embedding without internal reasoning, FactEmbed employs a set of factorized latents that simulate the intended visual modifications via an intermediate target imagination objective. This process encourages the latents to ground into target semantics before forming discriminative representations for retrieval. Subsequently, to propagate these factorized representations throughout the model for retrieval, we optimize a factor-level contrastive loss while distilling the decomposed representations into a deeper attention layer of the MLLM. Finally, for efficient inference, we retain the [EOS] token to establish a global-level embedding on top of these factorized latents, while we exploit the mean-pooled factorized latents to rerank a short candidate list at negligible cost, as both are produced by the same forward pass. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FactEmbed outperforms prior works across CIR benchmarks.

</details>


### [139] [Perturb, Repair, Verify: Self-Play Vision-Language Verifiers for Compositional Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05158)
*Zijun Huang,Yingjun Du,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自博弈框架PROVE，通过"扰动-验证-修复-再验证"闭环训练视觉语言验证器，摆脱对外部教师监督的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言验证器的训练依赖更强外部裁判的静态监督或人工构造的难负样本，需外部生成/标注、受教师能力上限约束，且难以随模型失败模式演变而自适应。

Method: PROVE为自博弈框架：从已知正确图文对出发，Mutator施加类型化扰动（对象、属性、数量、关系）；验证器判定被扰动描述并给出文本反馈；Repairer据此恢复忠实描述并进行二次验证。两轮验证产生结构化奖励，分别激励有信息量的不匹配识别与视觉接地的修复；三种角色由同一演进的多模态骨干网络以角色条件方式承担，共享参数更新同时提升验证器本身。

Result: 在不同模型规模与模型家族上提升细粒度组合式验证能力；在参考骨干上增强视觉细节鲁棒性；对更广泛多模态任务的影响有限且依赖具体基准。

Conclusion: 以闭环自博弈替代静态外部监督是训练视觉语言验证器的可行且有效路径，可随模型能力共同演进，同时需权衡其对通用多模态能力的迁移收益。

Abstract: Vision-language verifiers judge whether an image faithfully matches a textual description, yet existing training pipelines typically rely on static supervision from stronger external judges or curated hard-negative data. Such supervision requires external generation or annotation, is teacher-bounded, and is difficult to adapt as model failures evolve. We introduce PROVE (Perturb-and-Repair Optimization for Vision-Language Evaluation), a self-play framework that trains verifiers through a closed perturb-verify-repair-verify loop. Starting from a known-correct image-caption pair, a Mutator introduces a typed perturbation over objects, attributes, counts, or relations. The verifier then judges the perturbed caption and provides textual feedback, which a Repairer uses to recover a faithful caption for re-verification. These two verification rounds produce structured rewards for informative mismatches and visually grounded repairs. Since perturbation, verification, and repair are role-conditioned behaviors of the same evolving multimodal backbone, updating the shared parameters also improves the verifier itself. Experiments show that PROVE improves fine-grained compositional verification across model scales and families and strengthens visual-detail robustness on the reference backbone, with limited, benchmark-dependent changes on broader multimodal tasks.

</details>


### [140] [When Cell Crops Share a Parent: Measuring and Isolating Acquisition Leakage in Cervical Cytology Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05145)
*Snehasish Ghosh,Soham Mitra,Antareep Majumdar,KC Santosh,Nibaran Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 公开宫颈细胞学基准多以细胞裁剪块为单位划分数据集，导致测试细胞与训练细胞共享同一显微镜视野/玻片"父样本"，产生数据泄漏。预注册实验表明按裁剪块划分会高估准确率，且该增益源于"同源暴露"而非父样本多样性；父身份可解码性、Grad-CAM等无法替代父样本完全分离的划分方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开宫颈细胞学基准（如 SIPaKMeD、CRIC）的细胞裁剪块来自远少于裁剪块数量的视野或玻片，且常以裁剪块级别划分训练/测试集，使测试细胞与训练细胞共享采集父样本。作者希望量化这种泄漏对性能评估的虚高程度、厘清其机制，并检验常见的诊断工具（表征可解码性、显著性图）能否作为划分质量的有效替代指标。

Method: 采用预注册（preregistered）研究设计：在 12 种架构、20 个随机种子上，比较裁剪块级划分与其它划分方式带来的精度差异并给出 95% 置信区间；在 SIPaKMeD 上构造析因实验，分别固定训练裁剪块数量与父样本数量，以分离"同源暴露（sibling exposure）"与"父样本多样性"两种可能归因；进一步评估父身份（parent identity）的线性可解码性并施加擦除（erasing 82% 高于随机水平的线性可恢复性）以检验其对性能差距的因果作用；用 Grad-CAM 比较泄漏模型与干净模型；最后做颜色统计匹配实验：将测试细胞颜色统计对齐到未见过的同类视野，观察优势是否消失。

Result: 1) 裁剪块级划分使 SIPaKMeD 准确率平均提高 0.97 个百分点（95% CI 0.59–1.39），使 CRIC 提高 12.08 个百分点（11.16–12.99）。2) 析因实验表明 SIPaKMeD 上的增益几乎全部来自同源暴露（1.73 个百分点，CI 0.95–2.51），而裁剪块级划分带来的额外父样本多样性贡献为 0.00。3) 父身份可解码性约为随机水平的 100 倍，但擦除其 82% 的高于基线线性可恢复性后，性能差距未变。4) Grad-CAM 无法区分泄漏模型与干净模型。5) 将测试细胞颜色统计匹配到未见过的同类视野后，大部分优势消失。

Conclusion: 切片/视野级（parent-disjoint）划分是避免细胞学基准性能虚高的必要条件；表征可解码性与显著性可视化（Grad-CAM）均不能替代父样本完全分离的划分，且泄漏的收益主要来自同源细胞暴露与颜色统计等浅层捷径而非父身份本身。

Abstract: Public cervical cytology benchmarks release many cell crops cut from far fewer microscope fields or slides and are often split at the crop level, so test cells share acquisition parents with training cells. In a preregistered study, crop-level splitting raised SIPaKMeD accuracy by 0.97 percentage points across twelve architectures and twenty seeds (95% CI 0.59-1.39), and CRIC accuracy by 12.08 points (11.16-12.99). On SIPaKMeD, a factorial that fixes training crop and parent counts attributes the gain to sibling exposure (1.73 points, 0.95-2.51), not to the extra parent diversity of crop-level splits (0.00). Parent identity is decodable at about 100 times chance, yet erasing 82% of its above-floor linear recoverability left the gap unchanged, and Grad-CAM did not distinguish leaking from clean models; matching test cells' color statistics to an unseen same-class field removed most of the advantage. Decodability and saliency cannot substitute for a parent-disjoint split.

</details>


### [141] [OpenBrain: An Auditable Generated-Label Release for Whole-Brain MRI Parcellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05348)
*Qizhen Lan,Yu-Chun Hsu,Yuxiang Wei,Lijing Zhu,Zenan Sun,Liang He,Lishan Yu,Xiaoqian Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenBrain 发布了一个置信度感知的脑 MRI 分割标签数据集（35,838 例 T1w，607 个来源数据集），以"可审计病例记录"形式附带 QC-7 无参考可靠性指标与风险评分/置信层级，支持用户筛选和复用生成标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 队列规模的自动全脑 MRI 分割已可规模化生成，但现有生成标签发布很少提供证据说明哪些病例足够可靠可复用，缺乏释放时的质量控制与可审计性。

Method: 构建病例级记录式发布：每条记录关联一个主分割、两个委员会分割、来源/许可信息、QC-7 原始测量、百分位风险、组合风险分数 R 及置信层级；QC-7 为无参考可靠性面板，覆盖标签几何、图像-标签一致性与委员会分歧三方面；在冻结协议下于外部队列验证。

Result: 外部队列中 QC-7 风险越低对应生成标签质量越高；固定预算训练下，低风险筛选相较随机选择使配对宏观 Dice 提升 +3.2 至 +3.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 贡献在于大规模开放的生成分割基底、可复用的病例级风险证据层及释放时 QC 的外部验证协议；同时明确其不等于人工标注或临床级分割。

Abstract: Automatic tools can now generate whole-brain MRI parcellations at cohort scale, but generated-label releases often provide little evidence about which cases are reliable enough to reuse. We introduce OpenBrain, a confidence-aware release of 35,838 provenance-verified OpenNeuro T1w cases from 607 source datasets. OpenBrain is organized as an auditable case-record release rather than a directory of standalone segmentation files: each record links a primary parcellation, two committee parcellations, source/license provenance, raw QC-7 measurements, percentile risks, a combined risk score R, and a confidence tier. QC-7 is a reference-free reliability panel over label geometry, image-label consistency, and committee disagreement, so users can filter, weight, audit, or re-threshold generated labels without rerunning inference. We evaluate this released evidence under a frozen protocol. Across external cohorts, lower QC-7 risk corresponds to higher generated-label quality; in fixed-budget training, lower-risk selections improve paired macro Dice over random selection by +3.2 to +3.7 percentage points. OpenBrain also exposes raw axes, committee outputs, provenance, and datasheet documentation, so confidence claims remain inspectable after download. It does not turn generated labels into manual annotations or clinical-grade segmentations. Its contribution is a large open generated-parcellation substrate, a reusable case-level risk-evidence layer, and an external validation protocol for release-time QC. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/lanqz7766/OpenBrain. Code: https://github.com/lanqz7766/openbrain.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Length Is Cheap, Competition Is Not: Attention Predicts Context Rot in Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04169)
*Yinuo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在检索任务中，长上下文失效并非长度所致，而是"竞争"：与证据同格式的干扰陈述争夺注意力质量，导致准确率从0.97–1.00跌至0.16–0.33，且证据上残留的注意力质量可解释最多58%的损失方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有解释将语言模型的长输入失效归因于"长度"（context rot）。作者试图检验这一归因是否正确，区分"单纯长度"与"同格式竞争性内容"两种因素在检索任务中的作用。

Method: 在5个开源模型（0.5B–3B参数，4个模型家族）上对比中性填充文本与同证据格式陈述对检索准确率的影响；统计"对手值陈述"（给查询键赋予竞争值）的数量效应；用内部探针提取"证据上残留的注意力质量"这一指标，在126,473次试验中与损失做方差解释分析；复现并检验遮蔽填充（masked-filler）协议的可见性对照实验。

Result: 中性填充几乎无代价（准确率0.97–1.00，Qwen2.5-1.5B在32K token仍保持）；同格式陈述使准确率降至0.16–0.33；单个竞争值陈述使损失放大9–110倍，且随对手数量递增。证据上残留的注意力质量解释最多58%的损失方差，而log长度最多解释8%。遮蔽填充协议在MMLU上可复现（30K token损失11–18分），但在检索的可见对照中失效：32K token下遮蔽本身损失45个准确率点，同等填充可见时则无损失。

Conclusion: 检索场景下的"context rot"本质是注意力的竞争而非长度；长度本身代价低廉，既有"长度致害"的结论可能源于遮蔽填充协议的人为伪影。作者公开探针与全部126K trial级测量数据。

Abstract: Why do language models fail on long inputs? The common answer is length. We show that, for retrieval, length is cheap and competition is not. On five open models (0.5B to 3B parameters, four families), neutral filler costs nothing: accuracy stays at 0.97 to 1.00, up to 32K tokens on Qwen2.5-1.5B. Statements in the format of the evidence, placed at the same positions, drop it to 0.16 to 0.33. A single statement that gives the queried key a rival value multiplies the loss by 9 to 110, and every additional rival adds to it. Inside the model, one quantity tracks the failure: the attention mass left on the evidence. Across 126,473 trials it explains up to 58% of the variance in loss; log length explains at most 8%. The masked-filler protocol behind the claim that length alone hurts reproduces on MMLU (11 to 18 points lost at 30K tokens), but fails a visible control in retrieval: masking costs 45 accuracy points at 32K tokens, while the same filler left visible costs none. In retrieval, context rot is a competition for attention. We release the probe and all 126K trial-level measurements.

</details>


### [143] [ReSI-Math: Exploring Recoverable Capability Frontiers for Efficient Reasoning Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04787)
*Yao Cui,Zhen Yang,Yuandong Wang,Yangfu Zhu,Tianyu Wo,Zhenzhou Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示"直接失败≠能力缺失"，提出可恢复能力前沿概念及ReSI-Math框架，将轨迹搜索恢复的能力转化为数据高效的RLVR监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已能提升大模型数学推理，但模型直接解码失败时是否真的缺乏该能力尚不明确。作者发现部分失败可通过探索自身轨迹恢复，说明存在"可恢复能力前沿"——一种依赖策略的区域：直接解码不可靠生成解，但轨迹级搜索可激发解。

Method: ReSI-Math框架：①用Self-Judge引导的进化搜索，在固定策略下识别可恢复失败（正确-错误轨迹交叉）；②随策略演化，自适应选择前沿问题用于序贯RLVR训练。

Result: Qwen3-VL-8B上，621次尝试中正确-错误轨迹交叉产生272个经验证器确认的正确子代（43.8%）；三轮序贯单样本RLVR后，MATH500与WE-MATH平均分从78.71提升至80.16；匹配的单问题RLVR设置下，随机选择仅53.31，前沿引导的Q1为54.16。

Conclusion: 模型直接失败并不必然意味着能力缺失，轨迹级搜索可揭示并利用可恢复能力前沿，将恢复能力高效转化为RLVR监督，显著优于随机选择，为数据高效训练提供新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective approach for improving mathematical reasoning in large language models. However, a fundamental question remains: when a model fails to solve a problem, does it truly lack the underlying capability? In this work, we reveal that direct failure does not necessarily imply inability. Some reasoning failures can be recovered by exploring the model's own trajectories, exposing a recoverable capability frontier: a policy-dependent region where solutions are not reliably generated through direct decoding but can be elicited through trajectory-level search. Based on this observation, we introduce ReSI-Math, a framework that explores recoverable capability frontiers and converts recovered capabilities into data-efficient RLVR supervision. ReSI-Math uses Self-Judge-guided evolutionary search to identify recoverable failures under a fixed policy and adaptively selects frontier problems for sequential RLVR as the policy evolves. On Qwen3-VL-8B, correct-incorrect trajectory crossover produces 272 verifier-confirmed correct offspring from 621 attempts (43.8%). After three sequential one-shot RLVR stages, the MATH500 and WE-MATH average improves from 78.71 to 80.16. Under matched single-problem RLVR, random selection reaches only 53.31 versus 54.16 for frontier-guided Q1.

</details>


### [144] [When Ground Truth Is Not Ground Truth: Automatic Labeling for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04697)
*Rounak Sen,Divya Kumari,Deeksha Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文系统检验了"用BLEURT等相似度指标自动构造幻觉检测器真值标签"这一做法的可靠性，发现检测器性能高度依赖所选的监督判定器，且人工验证显示相似度类判定的标注错误率接近47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM幻觉检测评估缺乏可靠的真值标签。近期工作常以模型输出与参考答案的相似度（如BLEURT）作为幻觉的代理标签并设阈值构造真值，但BLEURT以英语为中心、分数无界、本质是相似度而非事实性度量，其作为真值构造依据的可靠性存疑。

Method: 构建大规模受控实验：15种自动监督判定器（supervision judges）、7种幻觉检测基线、2个生成模型、2个QA数据集、5种语言；并以人工标注验证各判定器标签的正确性，比较误分类率。

Result: 检测器性能随监督判定器的选择而显著变化；人工验证显示各判定器标注可靠性差异巨大，QGen-9B总体误分类率最低（12.86%），而BLEURT与BLEURT-n分别为47.23%和47.32%。

Conclusion: 基于相似度的自动标注存在明显局限，以此构造的真值会使幻觉检测评估失真；需发展更能刻画语义与事实正确性的判定方法。

Abstract: Hallucinations in Large Language Models (LLMs) remain a major challenge, as they can produce misleading, factually incorrect, or unverifiable information, thereby undermining their trustworthiness and reliability. However, reliable ground-truth labels remain a key challenge in evaluating hallucination detectors. Recent studies often construct labels automatically by comparing model generations with reference answers using similarity metrics such as BLEURT. This implicitly treats BLEURT similarity as a proxy for hallucination, despite its English-centric supervision, unbounded score range, and design as a similarity metric rather than a factuality measure, making threshold-based ground-truth construction potentially unreliable. Through comprehensive experiments using 15 automatic supervision judges, seven hallucination detection baselines, two generator models, two QA datasets, and five languages, we demonstrate that detector performance varies substantially with the choice of supervision judge. Human validation reveals substantial differences in labeling reliability, with QGen-9B achieving the lowest overall misclassification rate (12.86%), while BLEURT and BLEURT-n exhibit rates of 47.23% and 47.32%, respectively. Our findings highlight the limitations of automatic similarity-based labeling and the need for more reliable judging methods that better capture semantic and factual correctness.

</details>


### [145] [The Right Source Is Not Enough: Understanding History Interference in Speech-LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05124)
*HongJin Song,Weiqiao Shan,Runwu Shi,Yifei Wu,Yujin Wang,Jiale Luo,Jiasheng Kuang,Xinyu Yang,Zhaoqi Cui,Chuizheng Kong,Ziyu Wu,Guowu Tan,Qiuyu Fang,Chunxiang Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语音大模型能记住历史音频，但"记住"不等于"按需使用"；无关历史音频会在被查询事件仍被表征的情况下改变答案，其机制在于下游证据使用失效——源角色信息与答案值信息可分离、峰值深度不同，源角色信号并不能唯一控制哪些历史值影响决策。可靠对话记忆需要保留、检索之外，还要控制保留信息如何进入答案计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音大模型靠保留音频历史支持多轮对话，但检索到的事件信息未必只在相关时被使用。作者要回答：为何无关历史音频在目标事件仍有表征时仍会改变回答，且这种影响是否只是上下文长度的泛化效应。

Method: 在MOSS-Audio与MiMo-Audio上做受控实验，检验历史数值是否将错误导向特定的竞争答案；用因果干预区分两条路径（阻断后续计算对无关来源的直接访问）；用低维干预分离源角色信息与答案值信息，并比较二者效应峰值深度；在自然发生的错误上分别及联合强化被查询角色、抵消历史值。

Result: 错误由历史数值定向驱动至特定竞争答案，超出上下文长度的一般效应。开放式问答中，多数历史值错误在阻断直接访问后消失；多项选择中许多错误仍存在——早期历史影响经"问题—候选项"状态传递。源角色信息与答案值信息效应峰值深度不同，改变源角色信号不能消除竞争值的因果影响。自然错误上两种干预各自改善答案，联合干预恢复最佳。

Conclusion: 失败发生在源识别之外的下游证据使用环节：源角色信息可影响决策却不独占控制哪些历史值起作用。可靠对话记忆不仅需保留与检索，还需控制保留信息进入答案计算的方式。

Abstract: Speech large language models retain audio history to support multi-turn conversations. Yet remembering an event does not ensure that its information is used only when relevant. We investigate why irrelevant historical audio changes answers even when the queried event remains represented. Controlled experiments on MOSS-Audio and MiMo-Audio show that historical values direct errors toward specific competing answers, beyond a generic effect of context length. Causal interventions distinguish two paths. In open-ended question answering, most history-value errors disappear when subsequent computation cannot directly access the irrelevant source. In multiple-choice answering, many errors persist: early historical influence is carried by question-candidate states even after direct source access is blocked. Low-dimensional interventions further separate source-role information from answer-value information. Their effects peak at different depths, and changing the source-role signal does not eliminate the causal influence of competing values. On naturally occurring errors, strengthening the queried role and counteracting the historical value each improve answers; their joint intervention gives the greatest recovery. These findings support a failure of downstream evidence use beyond source identification alone: source-role information can influence a decision without exclusively controlling which historical values affect it. Reliable conversational memory therefore requires not only retention and retrieval, but also control over how retained information enters answer computation.

</details>


### [146] [Suppressing Misaligned Behaviors in Language Models, Durably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05358)
*Luxi He,Pengcheng Jiang,Jifan Zhang,Jiawei Han,Danqi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文研究事后抑制模型不良行为的效果，构建窄触发与宽触发两类"模式生物"，从即时抑制、训练后耐久性和能力保持三维度评估SFT、GRPO与梯度上升遗忘，并提出PEARL——一种增强rollout的GRPO变体，实现更强且更持久的抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不良行为可能在训练任意阶段出现或复现，已有对齐方法存在"浅层对齐"问题（表面抑制易在后续训练中被逆转）。作者假设更持久的抑制需要模型具备从更广泛的错位状态中恢复的能力，因此需系统量化比较现有事后抑制方法并设计更强方法。

Method: 1) 构建两个差异在于"触发方式"的模式生物：窄触发（仅在提示含特定线索时通过跟随错误提示作弊）与宽触发（在不安全代码上微调后于广泛开放式提示中产生有害响应）。2) 形式化事后行为抑制问题，按即时抑制、进一步训练下的耐久性、通用能力保持三准则评估。3) 对比SFT、GRPO、梯度上升遗忘。4) 提出PEARL：GRPO变体，将沿缓存模式生物轨迹采样的中间状态续写纳入rollout，以扩大恢复起点覆盖。

Result: 在两个模式生物设置及两个模型家族（Qwen3-4B、GPT-OSS-20B）上，PEARL实现强即时抑制并保持任务准确率，且在基于模式生物数据或无关注续训练下仍保持耐久性；当不良行为为宽触发、无法通过定向SFT解决时收益最大。

Conclusion: 该框架与方法为更持久的事后去除不良行为提供了进展，表明提升从多样错位状态恢复的能力是增强抑制耐久性的关键方向。

Abstract: Undesired model behaviors can emerge or re-emerge at any stage of training. Once observed, how effectively can we suppress them? To study this question, we first construct two model organisms--language models deliberately trained to exhibit a specific misalignment--that differ in what triggers the behavior. The narrow-trigger organism misbehaves only when the prompt contains a specific cue: it cheats on multiple-choice questions by following a planted hint that points to a wrong answer, and behaves normally otherwise. The broad-trigger organism has no such cue: after fine-tuning on insecure code, it gives harmful responses across a wide range of open-ended prompts. We then formalize post-hoc behavioral suppression and evaluate methods along three criteria: immediate suppression of the target behavior, durability under further training, and preservation of general capability. We compare standard methods--SFT, GRPO, and gradient-ascent unlearning--along these dimensions. Building on prior work on alignment shallowness, we hypothesize that more durable suppression requires the ability to recover from a wider range of misalignment states. We introduce Prefix-Expanded Adversarial Reinforcement Learning (PEARL), a GRPO variant that augments rollouts with continuations from intermediate states sampled along cached organism trajectories. Across both organism settings and two model families (Qwen3-4B, GPT-OSS-20B), PEARL achieves strong immediate suppression while preserving task accuracy, and remains durable under further fine-tuning on either the organism's data or unrelated data. It obtains the greatest benefit when the undesirable behavior is broad and cannot be addressed through targeted SFT. Together, our framework and method offer a step toward more durable post-hoc removal of undesired behaviors.

</details>


### [147] [Swapping the Judge Changes the Ranking: A Preregistered Study of Cross-Provider Replication in LLM-Judged System Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04090)
*Sangdo Han,Moonsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册实验检验"用不同提供商的LLM裁判重复结论"这一做法是否可信：5个裁判模型对4,000场有真人投票的Chatbot Arena对战（双向顺序）重新评判，发现裁判与人类投票的判定一致性低（中位Cohen's kappa=0.22），系统排名无法在换裁判后保持稳定（Kendall's tau 0.17–0.89，中位0.67），预注册的"排名稳定"假设未获支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中，无人工标签的研究者常以"换一家提供商的LLM裁判复现同一结论"作为稳健性检验。但这一"跨提供商复现"是否真能过滤掉裁判偏差、提升结论可靠性，缺乏系统性证据，作者因此对该检验的可信度进行实证考察。

Method: 预注册研究（并披露了注册后缩减裁判集）：来自3家提供商的5个裁判模型，对4,000场带有真人投票的Chatbot Arena对战（9个系统、33个系统对）在两种回答顺序下重新评判；以Cohen's kappa衡量判定层面与人类投票的一致性，以Kendall's tau衡量系统排名与人类排名的一致性，并比较单裁判结论与双低成本的跨提供商裁判复现结论的精确率，同时分析收敛所需判定数与裁判间分歧的独立性。

Result: 判定层面与人类投票一致性偏低（中位kappa=0.22）；换裁判后排名不稳定（Kendall's tau 0.17–0.89，中位0.67）。经两个不同提供商低成本裁判复现的结论比单裁判结论更常与人类结论一致（精确率+0.10），但增益主要来自过滤掉一个对齐较差的裁判；当复现涉及该裁判时，仅35%的比较能得到结论。与人类一致的裁判所需判定数不超过人类投票所需即达稳定结论，其余裁判在测试样本量下（每对最多100次判定）有26%–70%的系统对不收敛。裁判间分歧的共现频率远高于独立性预测，但每场仅一次人类投票无法区分共同裁判偏差与投票噪声。

Conclusion: 跨提供商重复结论的"检查"作用有限：既不能保证排名稳定，其准确率增益也主要源于剔除单个劣质裁判而非提供商多样性，且可能大幅减少可得出结论的比较比例。作者提醒实践者慎用该做法，并指出需多次人类投票才能分离共同裁判偏差与投票噪声。

Abstract: Practitioners who compare systems with an LLM judge and have no human labels often check a conclusion by repeating it with a judge from another provider. We test how far that check can be trusted. In a preregistered study (with a disclosed post-registration reduction of the judge set), five judge models from three providers re-judged 4,000 Chatbot Arena battles (nine systems, 33 system pairs) that carry human votes, each in both response orders. Agreement with the human vote was low at the verdict level (median Cohen's kappa = 0.22), and system rankings did not survive a change of judge: Kendall's tau with the human ranking ranged from 0.17 to 0.89 (median 0.67), so the preregistered hypothesis that rankings remain stable was not supported. Conclusions replicated by two low-cost judges from different providers matched the human conclusion more often than single-judge conclusions (+0.10 precision), but the gain came from filtering out one poorly aligned judge, and replication involving that judge left a conclusion for only 35% of comparisons. Judges that agreed with humans reached a stable conclusion with no more verdicts than human votes required; the others failed to converge on 26-70% of system pairs at the sample sizes tested (up to 100 verdicts per pair). Disagreements with the human vote co-occurred across judges far more often than independence predicts, although a single human vote per battle cannot separate shared judge bias from voter noise.

</details>


### [148] [Personas Are Not People: Identity as State, Not Attribute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05292)
*Tamunotonye Harry,Yuanyuan Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出"固定人格设定(synthetic persona)"隐含"身份在各情境中保持一致"的假设，而人格心理学与身份经济学表明行为跨情境差异大于人际差异；作者将行为分解为稳定特质、情境唤起的身份、情境本身三部分，并在两个假设下证明任何基于固定人格的合成人群模拟都无法复现"人群-情境特定"的行为成分；据此提出新的评价准则与"人-情境(person-in-context)"设计，并在Reddit语料与临床试验招募调查上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成人群被广泛用于问卷预测试、用户与社区模拟及AI系统评估，而其基础假设——身份在所有情境中行为一致——从未被检验。作者认为这一简化并非无害，会导致对"行为最受情境塑造"的群体的系统性误表征。

Method: 理论建模+形式化证明：借助身份经济学，将行为分解为稳定特质、情境唤起的身份与情境本身；在两个明确假设下证明固定人格模拟的不可复现性定理；提出基于该结果的新评价准则及"person-in-context"设计，并在自然主义Reddit人格语料与一项记录了对医疗机构不信任的社区临床试验招募调查上进行实证测试。

Result: 该设计在直接测试中确实产生了情境依赖行为；但在两个场景中均未能完全匹配真实人群，作者进一步定位了其失配之处。

Conclusion: 固定人格合成人群在原理上存在不可修复的表达缺陷，更丰富的人格设定无法纠正；实践中的风险是低估情境依赖强的群体的偏差，需采用人-情境设计与相应评价准则。

Abstract: Synthetic populations, large sets of simulated people, are increasingly used in place of real people to pretest surveys, simulate users and communities, and evaluate AI systems. Each simulated person is defined by a persona, a written profile of fixed traits, demographics, and attitudes that a language model is instructed to act out. Every such system assumes that a person's identity is a stable attribute that behaves the same way in every situation the simulation presents. We show that this assumption is not a harmless simplification. Three decades of personality research find the opposite. How a given person behaves varies more across contexts than people vary from one another. Drawing on identity economics, which describes how different situations activate different identities and norms within the same person, we separate behavior into three parts, which are stable traits, the identity a context calls up, and the situation itself. We then prove, under two stated assumptions, that any simulation built on fixed personas, no matter how detailed the personas or how large the underlying model, cannot reproduce the part of a population's behavior that is specific to it and its circumstances. We propose a new evaluation criterion based on this result and a person-in-context design that can meet it in principle. We test one version on a naturalistic Reddit personality corpus and on a published clinical-trial recruitment survey of a community with documented distrust of medical institutions. The design does produce context-dependent behavior when tested directly, but in neither setting does it fully match the real population, and we show where it falls short. The consequence for practice is a risk that synthetic-population evaluations misrepresent the groups whose behavior is most shaped by context, a distortion that richer personas cannot correct.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Accuracy: What Language-Subspace Interventions Capture, Remove, and Improve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05278)
*Shaanil Punglia,Dadhichi Telwadkar,Vaibhav Rathore,Siddharth Sureka,Neha Bhargava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统检验语言子空间干预的三个关键假设：子空间是否捕捉翻译相关变异、投影是否去除语言信息、是否在不损害回复语言的前提下提升准确率，结论是二者皆否/仅有限成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言子空间干预被广泛用于提升多语言推理，但缺乏对其有效性的因果性检验。作者希望验证三个隐含假设：估计的语言子空间能捕捉翻译相关变异、投影去除后确实消除语言信息、干预能在保持目标回复语言的同时提升准确率。

Method: 在四个开放权重LLM上使用MATH和MGSM数据集。子空间构建采用已有的Mean-SVD与作者提出的DiffPCA；通过投影去除子空间后训练线性探针从投影激活中分类语言；并在Qwen2.5-7B上比较不同子空间构建方法与干预算子的多种配置，评估准确率与回复语言保真度（联合准确率）。

Result: 两种子空间均捕捉到显著的翻译相关变异，且随模型深度呈现相似模式；但线性探针在投影后仍几乎完美识别语言，说明子空间未穷尽捕捉语言信息。在Qwen2.5-7B上，MGSM准确率提升最大的干预牺牲了回复语言保真度，仅一种干预使联合准确率小幅提升1.60分。此外，仅微调提示词即可复现准确率增益，但语言保真度显著下降。

Conclusion: 因果干预不能仅以准确率评判，需计入语言替换的权衡。语言子空间方法并未真正消除语言信息，其准确率增益可能源于语言替换而非推理能力提升。

Abstract: Language-subspace interventions aim to improve multilingual reasoning by suppressing language-related components in mid-layer activations. Using MATH and MGSM across four open-weight LLMs, we ask three questions: do estimated language subspaces capture translation-associated variation? Does projecting them out remove language information? And do they improve accuracy without compromising response language? For the first question, we build subspaces using the existing Mean-SVD construction and our DiffPCA. Both capture substantial translation-associated variation and show similar patterns across model depth. For the second, we project out each subspace and train linear probes to classify language from the projected activations. The probes still identify language almost perfectly, showing that neither subspace exhaustively captures language information. For the third, we evaluate several intervention configurations on Qwen2.5-7B, by varying subspace creation methods and intervention operators. We find that the intervention that most improves MGSM accuracy sacrifices response-language fidelity, with only one intervention improving joint accuracy--answers that are correct and in the target language--slightly, by 1.60 points. Interestingly, subtle changes in the prompt alone can reproduce the accuracy gains albeit with significant damage to language fidelity. We argue that causal interventions must be judged on more than accuracy, to fully account for language substitution trade-offs.

</details>


### [150] [FlagRot: Selective Rotation for Low-Bit KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03992)
*Kunming Shao,Xijie Huang,Jierun Chen,Jiaqi Shao,Yanli Wang,Jiangnan Yu,Qiwei Li,Wei Zhang,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Chi Ying Tsui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlagRot 对每个 32 通道的 key 片段自适应选择“旋转”或“不旋转”存储，并据此在读取时旋转 query，从而修正“旋转总是有益”的假设，显著降低 2-bit KV cache 量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理受 KV cache 显存/带宽约束，2-bit 量化可将其压缩约 6 倍，但前提是误差可控。常规做法在量化前对 key 做旋转，假设旋转总能改善量化；作者观察到该假设不成立：旋转会把尖峰分布压平，却也可能给原本平坦的片段制造新尖峰，且同一 key 内两种片段共存。

Method: 提出 FlagRot：以 32 通道为粒度，对每个片段比较旋转与不旋转两种方案下的“校准后注意力误差”，择优存储；读取时若该片段未旋转，则改为旋转 query 以保持内积等价。并指出决策粒度应随量化轴而定：per-channel cache（如 KIVI）每 head 只需一次决策且无额外写开销；per-token cache 需逐 key 决策，选择标志复用已有的符号位存储。

Result: 在 2.5 bit/value 下，将 KIVI 在 12 个模型上的平均 KL 散度从 18.7 降至 12.4 ×10^-3，并在 12 个模型中的 9 个上优于 RotateKV；作为 vLLM 插件部署后，128k token 下 RULER 提升 +2.9 分，在 2-bit KIVI 出现精度损失的四个模型中的三个上 LongBench v1 均值提升 +0.4 至 +1.9 分；同时对 4-bit 整数与 FP4 cache 也能降低误差。

Conclusion: 旋转并非普适有效，按片段自适应选择旋转与否可在几乎零额外存储/写开销下显著改善 2-bit 乃至 4-bit KV cache 量化精度，并具有良好的工程可实现性（vLLM 插件）。

Abstract: Long-context inference is bound by the KV cache, which 2-bit codes could shrink about sixfold if their error stays in check. The usual remedy rotates keys before quantizing, assuming rotation always helps. However, we observe that it does not: rotation flattens a spiky piece of a key but can give a flat one a new peak, and both coexist within one key. We therefore propose FlagRot, which stores each 32-channel piece rotated or as is, whichever has the smaller calibrated attention error, and rotates the query instead when reading. We further show that how often to decide follows the quantization axis: per-channel caches such as KIVI need one decision per head, at no write cost, while per-token caches decide per key, with the choice stored in existing sign bits. At 2.5 bits per value, FlagRot lowers the twelve-model KL divergence of KIVI from 18.7 to 12.4 x 10^-3, below RotateKV on 9 of 12 models. Served as a vLLM plugin, it raises RULER at 128k tokens by +2.9 points and the LongBench v1 mean by +0.4 to +1.9 points on the three of four models where 2-bit KIVI loses accuracy. It also lowers the error of 4-bit integer and FP4 caches.

</details>


### [151] [SLATE: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Long-Horizon API Selection in E-commerce Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03982)
*Rongzhe Wei,Ge Shi,Min Cheng,Sarthak Ghosh,Vaibhav Gorde,Leman Akoglu,Pan Li,Na Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLATE是一个面向企业场景的大规模工具集、长时程API选择基准，含约1000个电商工具、确定性模拟器与端到端任务成功率指标；最强模型仅完成5.0%任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业希望LLM智能体无需人工编写步骤到API的映射，即可在数千内部API中自动执行标准操作流程。核心难点在于规模化API选择：长时程下选择并组合正确工具，且仅以整体任务成功为判据。现有工具使用基准存在缺陷：工具数量少、仅评估单步调用、或用主观LLM-as-a-judge评分多步行为，会惩罚合理但不同的轨迹。

Method: 提出SLATE基准，由三部分构成：(i) 将查询分解为基于工具的分层流程，覆盖约1000个工具的电商库；(ii) 确定性、上下文感知的模拟器，对匹配调用返回录制输出，否则返回默认响应；(iii) 端到端任务成功率指标——任务需达成所有参考结果即视为成功，从而认可多样但有效的轨迹，并保持全自动、可复现。

Result: 在6个开放决策模型及其底层语言模型（3B至12B参数）上实验，最佳模型仅完成5.0%的任务。

Conclusion: SLATE构成了可靠企业级工具使用的高难度测试平台；作者开源了SLATE、模拟器与评测框架。

Abstract: Enterprises increasingly want LLM agents to automate standard operating procedures over their software stacks -- mapping each step of a procedure to the right call among thousands of internal APIs, without hand-authored step-to-API mappings. The core difficulty is API selection at scale: choosing and composing the correct tools over a long horizon, judged only by whether the overall task succeeds. Current tool-use benchmarks do not test this regime -- they expose only a handful of tools, evaluate single-step invocation, or grade multi-step behavior with subjective LLM-as-a-judge scores that penalize valid alternative trajectories. We introduce SLATE (Synthetic Large-scale API Toolkit for E-commerce), a large-scale benchmark that tests large-toolset, long-horizon API selection in a realistic enterprise domain. SLATE couples (i) queries decomposed into hierarchical, tool-grounded procedures over a ~1,000-tool e-commerce library, (ii) a deterministic, context-aware simulator that returns recorded outputs for matching calls and default responses otherwise, and (iii) an end-to-end task-success metric -- a task succeeds when it reaches every reference outcome -- that credits diverse-but-valid trajectories while remaining fully automatic and reproducible. Experiments with six open decision models and the language models they are built on, from 3B to 12B parameters, show that even the best model completes only 5.0% of the tasks, establishing SLATE as a challenging testbed for reliable enterprise tool use. We release SLATE, its simulator, and the evaluation harness at https://geshijoker.github.io/slate/

</details>


### [152] [Rethinking Next-Visit Diagnosis Prediction Beyond Aggregate Metrics: Retrieval--Memory Multi-Agent Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03826)
*Heejoon Koo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多维度的"下次就诊诊断预测"评估框架，揭示重复上次就诊的简单基线优于多数LLM方法，并提出检索-记忆多智能体裁决框架LONGINUS，在各时间转移类型上取得LLM方法中最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的聚合式评估掩盖了下次就诊诊断预测任务的多面性：分布上需处理变长诊断集合与长尾标签；时间上需区分持续性、新发与复发诊断，且这些特征随病史深度变化。现有评估无法揭示不同方法在这些维度上的真实差异。

Method: 1) 构建沿分布与时间维度（持续性/新发/复发 × 病史深度）的评估框架；2) 系统评测规则启发式、LLM提示、检索、记忆与多智能体方法；3) 提出LONGINUS：结合病历检索证据与患者轨迹结构化记忆的多智能体裁决框架，以跨视图一致性作为可靠性启发式，保留共识预测并对分歧进行裁决（不暴露来源）。

Result: 重复上次就诊的启发式在整体上优于多数LLM方法（但不预测新发或复发诊断）；检索与记忆呈现互补特性，其相对优势随转移类型与骨干模型变化。在MIMIC-III与MIMIC-IV、四种LLM骨干下，LONGINUS在几乎所有设置中取得LLM方法中最佳的转移特异性表现，并在聚合指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 聚合评估掩盖了下次就诊诊断预测的关键差异，应采用分布与时间多维评估；简单重复基线的强表现说明预测新发/复发诊断仍是核心挑战；检索与记忆的互补性可经多智能体裁决有效整合，LONGINUS验证了该思路的有效性。

Abstract: Next-visit diagnosis prediction forecasts the diagnoses documented at a patient's next encounter, turning retrospective electronic health records into prospective clinical prediction. However, conventional aggregate evaluation obscures its multifaceted nature: distributionally, models must handle variable-size diagnosis sets and long-tailed labels; temporally, they must distinguish persistent, newly recorded, and recurrent diagnoses; and both vary with observed history depth. We introduce an evaluation framework along these dimensions and apply it to rule-based heuristics, LLM-based prompting, retrieval, memory, and multi-agent approaches. This reveals that repeating the last visit outperforms most LLM-based approaches overall despite predicting no new or recurrent diagnoses, and that retrieval and memory exhibit distinct profiles whose relative strengths vary across temporal transitions and backbones. Motivated by this, we propose LONGINUS, a retrieval-memory multi-agent adjudication framework combining evidence retrieved from patient records with structured memory of the patient trajectory. Using cross-view agreement as a reliability heuristic, it preserves shared predictions and adjudicates disagreements without revealing their source. Across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV with four LLM backbones, LONGINUS achieves the best transition-specific performance among LLM-based methods in nearly all settings while remaining competitive with them on aggregate metrics.

</details>


### [153] [Constructing a Regional Slang Lexicon for Electricity Demands via Soft-Label Bidirectional Anchoring and Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04231)
*Rong Chang,Lijing Shi,Zhaoxiang Zang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出弱监督框架，从方言锚点与电力领域种子词出发，结合PMI软标签双向锚定、SBERT+分词词性打分与不确定性感知主动学习，构建电力客服区域方言俚语词表，并在真实微信语料上验证其高精度与软标签分层的候选保留优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网客服日益依赖社区社交媒体，客户用非正式区域方言描述停电、计费和设备故障，这类文本存在非标准形态、语义漂移和未登录词，人工构建词表成本高且常规NLP工具性能下降，亟需自动化的方言词表构建方法。

Method: 弱监督框架：以精选方言锚点与电力领域种子词构建候选，采用点互信息（PMI）结合软标签双向锚定生成候选，保留方言锚定词并将电力领域相关性作为独立元数据记录；用Sentence-BERT语义相似度与基于jieba的词化度度量联合打分；在三种查询策略下应用不确定性感知主动学习，标注器为规则法与检索增强LLM；最终词表由能力更强的LLM评估，并以人工专家标注通过Cohen's κ校准。

Result: 在来自云南玉溪的11,177条真实微信消息上，评估器估计的方言精确率DP=1.0，零假阳性；消融显示软标签分层在同等精度下比严格电力门控多保留751%的候选（候选池从41扩展至349个）；人工校准（n=46）得到κ_dialect=1.0、κ_power=0.85；最终词表规模25–33个词，其中4–5个与电力相关。

Conclusion: 该框架以高精度自动构建区域方言俚语词表，软标签分层显著扩大候选覆盖而不损失精度，所得词表为方言感知的电网客服NLP提供了初步资源，但规模较小、覆盖面有限，属探索性资源建设。

Abstract: Power-grid customer service increasingly relies on community social media, where customers describe outages, billing, and equipment faults in informal regional dialect. Such messages exhibit non-standard morphology, semantic drift, and out-of-vocabulary expressions that make manual lexicon construction costly and degrade conventional NLP tools. We propose a weak-supervision framework constructing a regional slang lexicon from curated dialect anchors and power-domain seed terms. Candidates are generated via Pointwise Mutual Information with soft-label bidirectional anchoring, retaining dialect-anchored terms while recording power-domain relevance as separate metadata, and are scored by combining Sentence-BERT semantic similarity with a jieba-based wordhood measure. Uncertainty-aware active learning with rule-based and retrieval-augmented LLM oracles is applied under three query strategies, and the resulting lexicon is evaluated by a higher-capability LLM calibrated against human expert annotations via Cohen's $κ$. On 11,177 real WeChat messages from Yuxi (Yunnan, China), the framework achieves an evaluator-estimated dialect precision of DP~$=$~1.0 with zero false positives. An ablation shows that soft-label layering retains 751\% more candidates than strict power-gating at identical precision, expanding the candidate pool from 41 to 349 terms. Human calibration ($n=46$) yields $κ_{\text{dialect}}=1.0$ and $κ_{\text{power}}=0.85$. The resulting 25--33-term lexicon, 4--5 of which are power-relevant, provides a preliminary resource for dialect-aware power-grid service NLP.

</details>


### [154] [Who is the Pronoun Pro? Comparing LLMs and Humans at Number-Disambiguation of 'they'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04968)
*Carlos Hartmann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在语义要求高的代词消歧标注任务上比较73名人类标注者与40余种LLM配置，发现最优LLM（Kimi K3，94.5%）超过人类多数投票（86.2%），但在成本、速度优势和错误分析基础上主张"人机互补"而非"替代"的自动化观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM能否可靠替代或补充人类研究助理完成语言复杂标注任务，并回应"纯替代导向"的自动化观点。

Method: 受控标注实验：73名人类标注者（含语言学背景与非背景的本地学生及众包工作者）与40余种LLM配置，对Reddit中174个真实"they"用例进行数量消歧，涵盖创新性与语境敏感用例；辅以错误质性分析与透明成本/时间证据。

Result: 最优LLM达94.5%准确率，高于人类多数投票86.2%与人类前50%均值78.5%，仅单名最佳人类标注者更高（95.7%）；LLM在成本与速度上具显著优势。低一致性案例中的人类推理揭示标注类型学的不一致，进而改进方法框架。

Conclusion: LLM可支撑大规模语言学标注，但负责任使用需人类批判性监督；贡献包括基准式人机比较、透明的成本时间证据，以及反对纯替代视角的方法论论证，并公开数据、标注与可复现代码。

Abstract: This study evaluates whether large language models (LLMs) can reliably replace or complement human research assistants in linguistically complex annotation. In a controlled annotation task, 73 human annotators (local students and crowdworkers with and without a background in linguistics) and more than 40 LLM configurations number-disambiguated 174 authentic uses of 'they' from Reddit, including innovative and context-sensitive cases. The best LLM, Kimi K3, reached 94.5% accuracy, above the human majority vote (86.2%) and the mean of the top half of human annotators (78.5%); only the single best human annotator scored higher (95.7%). LLMs also offer substantial practical advantages in cost and speed. However, qualitative error analysis reveals a crucial complementarity: human reasoning in low-agreement cases exposes inconsistencies in the annotation typology and thereby improves the methodological framework itself. The study contributes (i) a benchmark-style human-LLM comparison for a semantically demanding task, (ii) transparent cost and time evidence, and (iii) a methodological argument against a purely replacement-oriented view of automation. LLMs are viable for large-scale annotation support in linguistics, but their responsible use requires human critical oversight. The dataset with its gold-standard labels, all human and LLM annotations, and the fully reproducible code are openly available as a Quarto-based, public repository.

</details>


### [155] [Shuffled OPSD: When Semantic Consistency Hurts On-Policy Self-Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04958)
*Qingyuan Zheng,Zhihao Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Shuffled OPSD：在 on-policy 自蒸馏中打乱共享题面词序、保留教师独有范例完整，在三个数学推理基准、三个 Qwen3 规模上均优于 Standard OPSD，最高提升 3.52 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD 中学生仅凭问题生成轨迹、教师额外接收完整解题范例并给出 token 级监督，题面内容与顺序同时影响学生生成与教师反馈。语义结构看似应当保留，但作者质疑“语义连贯性必然有益”这一默认假设，探索破坏共享题面结构是否反而促进学习。

Method: 核心方法为词级打乱共享题面的 Shuffled OPSD，教师独有范例保持不变。实验覆盖 3 个数学推理基准 × 3 个 Qwen3 规模，训练配置匹配；对照包括 intact-target 控制、块级保留（更大词块）打乱、打乱教师范例、跨题混合、目标去除等消融。

Result: 相对 intact-target 控制与 Standard OPSD 平均准确率均提升，对 Standard OPSD 最大增益为 Qwen3-1.7B 上的 3.52pp；保留更大词块相较词级打乱无优势；打乱教师独有范例在所有规模下都降低准确率；跨题混合与目标去除实验进一步区分了题面内容、词序与教师上下文的作用。

Conclusion: 破坏共享题面可提升学习，而破坏教师独有参考则损害性能；这挑战了“语义连贯对 OPSD 普遍有益”的看法，提示需进一步研究输入结构如何塑造学生生成与教师监督。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation improves a student model's reasoning ability through supervision from a teacher. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses the same model as both teacher and student under different contexts: the student generates reasoning trajectories from the question alone, while the teacher additionally receives a complete worked example and provides token-level supervision along those trajectories. Because the problem statement is shared, its content and ordering shape both student generation and teacher feedback. Preserving its semantic structure therefore seems natural, but does it always improve learning? We introduce Shuffled OPSD, which shuffles the words of the shared problem statement while keeping the teacher-only worked example intact. Across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three Qwen3 model scales, the method improves mean accuracy over both an intact-target control and Standard OPSD under matched training configurations. The largest gain over Standard OPSD is 3.52 percentage points on Qwen3-1.7B. Further controls show that preserving larger word blocks offers no advantage over word-level shuffling at the evaluated scales, whereas shuffling the teacher-only worked example reduces accuracy at every scale. Cross-problem mixing and target-removal experiments further distinguish the roles of problem content, word order, and teacher context. These results show that disrupting the shared problem statement can improve learning, while disrupting the teacher-only reference hurts performance. They challenge semantic coherence as a universally beneficial principle for OPSD and motivate closer study of how input structure shapes student generation and teacher supervision.

</details>


### [156] [Whose startup does the AI back? An algorithm audit of reputation and demographic signals in LLM-assisted crowdfunding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03993)
*Mirza Samad Ahmed Baig,Syeda Anshrah Gillani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究LLM助手在众筹建议中何时、通过何种决策过程使用创始人的性别与族裔信息。预注册联合实验中，7个LLM在3种支持者人设及无人物设对照下共做40,248次选择：默认情况下姓名几乎不起作用；当用户明确想支持弱势创作者时，模型偏向黑人姓名信号(+8.1个百分点)和女性姓名信号(+5.1)，且在接近平局的方案中最强、几乎不在理由中提及性别或族裔。结果支持"代理式算法歧视"解释：对齐的助手从用户而非训练数据中获取人口学偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM助手日益为众筹支持者提供建议，而其存在已知的种族与性别问题；目前尚不清楚LLM在创业融资情境中会复制、消除还是逆转既有歧视。论文旨在厘清：LLM何时、经由何种决策机制使用创始人的性别与族裔信息。

Method: 预注册的选择型联合实验（choice-based conjoint）：构建5个合成众筹项目，质量信号与创始人姓名独立随机化；7个LLM在3种支持者人设（预算敏感型、交付导向型、支持弱势创作者型）及无人物设对照下进行选择，共收集40,248条响应；分析性别与黑人姓名对选择概率的影响，并检验近平局效应、信号唯一性效应及理由文本中的提及率。

Result: 默认及预算敏感、交付导向人设下，性别与黑人姓名的效应统计上等于零；在"支持弱势创作者"人设下，模型偏好黑人姓名信号(+8.1个百分点，7个模型全部为正)与女性姓名信号(+5.1个百分点)，且在接近平局的项目中最强、仅在单一创始人携带该信号时最明显；模型在解释理由中几乎从不提及性别或族裔。

Conclusion: LLM助手的歧视并非源自训练数据偏见的被动复制，而更符合"代理式算法歧视"：对齐后的助手从用户的显式偏好中获取人口学偏好。姓名线索效应高度依赖用户人设与决策情境，且以隐性方式运作（理由中不出现相关表述）。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) assistants increasingly advise backers on which crowdfunding campaigns to support. LLMs have known race and gender problems, and it is unsettled whether they reproduce, erase, or reverse the discrimination documented in entrepreneurial finance. We ask when, and through which decision process, LLM assistants use founders' gender and ethnicity. In a pre-specified choice-based conjoint experiment, seven LLMs chose among five synthetic campaigns with independently randomized quality signals and founder names, under three backer personas and a persona-free control (40,248 responses). By default, founder names barely matter: under budget-conscious and delivery-focused personas, gender and Black-name effects are statistically equivalent to zero. When the backer wants to support underrepresented creators, the models favor Black-signaled (+8.1 percentage points; positive in all seven models) and female-signaled (+5.1) founders. They do so most strongly among near-tied campaigns and for the founder who alone carries the signal, and they almost never name gender or ethnicity in their reasons. The results fit an agency account of algorithmic discrimination: an aligned assistant takes its demographic preferences from its user rather than from its training data.

</details>


### [157] [Balancing Chain-of-Thought and Answer Supervision for Small Language Models on Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03948)
*Le Zhou,Xiao Luo,Chinmay Chandra,Zhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较两种CoT监督微调损失加权策略（UaS与SwS）在小规模语言模型医学问答上的效果，发现最优策略取决于任务格式与模型领域属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（≤9B）可支持本地医学问答并缓解隐私与延迟问题，但有效微调仍具挑战；现有研究对不同CoT损失加权方式缺乏系统比较。

Method: 对比两种CoT监督微调方法：Uniform-across-Sequence（UaS，对CoT与答案整体序列等权损失）与Segment-weighted（SwS，分别对CoT和答案损失加权）。在6个通用及医学领域模型上，评测多选题QA（MedQA、EMS-MCQA）与自由文本QA（Med-O1）。

Result: 多选题QA上SwS总体更优，通用模型受益最大（Qwen3.5-4B在MedQA上较零样本基线提升达44.8%），医学领域模型增益较小甚至为负；自由文本QA上UaS对几乎所有模型最佳，3B Llama-3.2接近大型7B医学模型表现；过长的CoT生成与部分模型性能下降相关。

Conclusion: CoT损失加权应与任务格式及基座模型已编码的知识相匹配，而非对所有临床QA任务采用统一微调策略；训练中限制CoT长度可能对特定模型有益。

Abstract: Small language models (<=9B parameters) can support local medical question answering (QA) while reducing privacy and latency concerns, but effective fine-tuning remains challenging. This paper compares two chain-of-thought (CoT) supervised fine-tuning methods: Uniform-across-Sequence (UaS), which applies equal loss weighting to the full CoT-and-answer sequence, and Segment-weighted (SwS), which separately weights CoT and answer losses. We evaluate six general- and medical-domain models on multiple-choice QA (MedQA and EMS-MCQA) and free-text QA (Med-O1). Results show that neither method is universally best: on multiple-choice QA (MedQA and EMS-MCQA), SwS generally performs better, with general-purpose models benefiting most; notably, Qwen3.5-4B improves by up to 44.8% over its zero-shot baseline on MedQA. In contrast, medical-domain models show smaller or even negative gains on MCQs. On free-text QA (Med-O1), UaS performs best for nearly all models and provides consistent improvements across both model types, with the 3B Llama-3.2 model approaching the performance of much larger 7B medical-domain models. We also find that excessively long CoT generation is associated with lower performance in some models on either multiple-choice or free-text QA tasks, suggesting that limiting the CoT length during training may benefit selected models. Overall, the results show that CoT loss weighting should be matched to the task format and the knowledge already encoded in the base model, rather than using a single fine-tuning strategy for all clinical QA tasks.

</details>


### [158] [One Assistant, Many Memories: Benchmarking Real-World Group Assistance When Recall Is Not Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04867)
*Zhanghao Hu,Linhai Zhang,Qian Zhao,Qi Zhu,Yuan Hua,Di Liang,Jiasheng Si,Xin Zhao,Yulan He,Zhumin Chen,Lin Gui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GroupAssistBench，首个基于真实群聊、面向已部署群助手记忆评测的基准（50群、80,299条消息、成本超5.3万美元），将评测分为记忆召回（L1）与记忆使用（L2），后者覆盖单点、分布、冲突记忆（M1–M3）且无固定答案；结果显示最强记忆系统召回接近全上下文但使用远落后，检索记忆关联与记忆约束绑定两项改进可稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆的价值不止于召回，更在于据此提供恰当帮助。现有基准多在个性化与智能体任务中测记忆使用，但很少在无固定答案条件下评估“辅助”能力，也缺乏跨多参与者的整合评测。群聊场景使这一问题更突出：助手需整合多成员信息、跟踪时间上的修订与冲突，并给出面向全群的单一回复。而现有群记忆基准依赖合成对话，并沿用一般记忆基准的受限评测（对固定答案召回或封闭集合决策），无法反映真实需求。

Method: 构建GroupAssistBench：采集50个真实群组的80,299条消息，配合已部署的群助手，总成本超53K美元。同一批历史记录上设两层评测：L1记忆召回，使用参考答案；L2记忆使用，采用无固定答案的服务型请求，细分为单点记忆（M1）、分布式记忆（M2）、冲突记忆（M3）。数据质量控制包括：验证请求确实依赖群历史、将记忆锚定到来源、L2采用定制化评分量表并引入源自真实用户反馈的惩罚项。评测对象覆盖前沿模型（全上下文）、检索方法与记忆系统。

Result: 最强的记忆系统在召回上可与全上下文匹敌，但在记忆使用上明显落后，尤其在分布式记忆与冲突记忆场景中差距更大。消融表明，两种附加设计——关联检索到的记忆、将记住的事实绑定为回复约束——能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 群聊记忆系统的关键瓶颈在于“使用”而非“召回”。记忆系统应超越单纯的检索式召回范式，转向对多源记忆的关联、冲突消解与约束化运用；GroupAssistBench为这一方向提供了真实数据基础与可诊断的分层评测框架。

Abstract: The value of memory lies not only in recall but in using it to provide appropriate assistance. Recent benchmarks test memory use in personalisation and agentic tasks, but seldom evaluate assistance without fixed answers or integration across multiple participants. Group chats make both challenges more acute: an assistant must integrate information from multiple members, track revisions and conflicts over time, and produce one response for the group. Yet existing group memory benchmarks rely on synthetic conversations and inherit constrained evaluation from general memory benchmarks, focusing on recall against fixed answers or decisions over a closed set. We introduce GroupAssistBench, the first memory benchmark from real-world group conversations with a deployed group assistant, covering 80,299 messages in 50 groups and costing over $53K. Over the same histories, GroupAssistBench evaluates memory recall (L1) with reference answers and memory use (L2) with service requests without fixed answers across single, distributed, and conflicting memories (M1--M3). Requests are verified to require group history, memories are anchored to their sources, and L2 uses tailored rubrics with penalties derived from real user feedback. Across evaluations of frontier models with full context, retrieval methods, and memory systems, the strongest memory system rivals full context on recall but falls far behind on use, especially for distributed and conflicting memories. Together, two additions that relate retrieved memories and bind remembered facts as response constraints bring consistent gains, showing that group memory systems must improve use, not recall alone.

</details>


### [159] [From Autonomous Agents to Cognitive Symbionts: Toward AI-Augmented Human Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05376)
*Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"AI增强的人类智能"(AHI)新范式，主张Physical AI时代除具身机器人外，还存在同等重要的第二类智能个体——被AI增强的人。为此需要新型系统"认知共生体"(cognitive symbiont):终身、嵌入、实时、私密、低摩擦、主动的伙伴，并通过"认知鸿沟"概念与"共生循环"设计空间加以组织，同时讨论其治理风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年AI进展以机器独立完成多少任务为衡量标准，但人的根本局限（遗忘、视野盲区、语言提取失败、缺乏自身历史支撑的决策）依然存在，因为能提供帮助的机器处于情境之外。AI进入物理世界后，关注集中于具身机器人这一种智能个体，而作者认为"被AI增强的人"同样重要；AGI问机器能多智能，AHI问机器能为特定个人激发多少智能。

Method: 概念性/立场性论文。提出AHI范式并以"认知鸿沟"（个人能带给情境的与工作记忆所能承载的之间的差距）为核心概念；将设计空间组织为一个共生循环：感知(sensing)、保存(keeping)、理解(understanding)、陪伴生活(living alongside)、代为行动(acting for)、共同思考(thinking with)、共同成长(growing)，外层由个人运行时(personal runtime)与信任包裹。系统需通过外部设备感知、跨年度保有上下文、建模情境与心智、在恰当时机介入思维、带上下文执行日常任务并持续学习用户。

Result: 给出认知共生体的概念内核、能力定义与结构化设计空间（共生循环＋个人运行时＋信任双边界）；并识别出该范式带来的新型风险，如考试中的隐蔽辅助、对所有人采纳该技术的压力，将治理纳入范式本身。

Conclusion: Physical AI时代应把"AI增强的人"与具身机器人并列为同等重要的智能个体；实现AHI需构建终身、嵌入、实时、私密、低摩擦、主动的认知共生体，以认知鸿沟为核心、以共生循环为设计空间，并同步建立针对新型社会风险的治理框架。

Abstract: For a decade, progress in artificial intelligence has been measured by how much a machine can do on its own. Large foundation models now write code and run multistep tasks, and personal agents act on a person's behalf. Yet people still forget what they once knew, miss what they are not looking at, fail to find the right words, and face hard choices without the benefit of their own history, because the machine that could help stands outside the situation. As AI moves into the physical world, attention has settled on one kind of intelligent individual, the embodied robot; we argue that the era of Physical AI holds a second kind of equal importance, the person augmented by AI. Alongside artificial general intelligence, which asks how intelligent a machine can be, stands AI-augmented human intelligence (AHI), which asks how much intelligence a machine can enable in a particular person. Realizing it calls for a new kind of system, a cognitive symbiont: a lifelong, embedded, real-time, private, low-friction, and proactive companion that senses what the person senses through external devices, keeps their context across years, models their situation and their mind, takes part in their thinking at the right moment, carries out everyday tasks with their context, and keeps learning the person as both change. We give the paradigm a conceptual core, the cognitive gap between what a person could bring to a situation and what they can hold in working memory, and organize its design space as a symbiosis loop of sensing, keeping, understanding, living alongside, acting for and thinking with the person, and growing, enclosed by a personal runtime and by trust. Because a symbiont changes what a person can do in front of others, it also raises risks of a new kind, from covert assistance in examinations to pressure on everyone to adopt it, and we treat their governance as part of the paradigm.

</details>


### [160] [Knowingly Wrong: Training LLMs Against Their Own Beliefs can Induce Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05287)
*Ariana M. Villegas-Suarez,Guido Freire,Sohan Venkatesh,Bartosz Cywiński*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在模型"不相信"但无害的数据上微调，也能诱导广泛的 misalignment；不信念强度越大、微调后仍保留真实信念越多，失准越严重，其根源是内部信念与输出的一致性冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认为广泛 misalignment 主要源于在窄领域有害数据（如不良医疗建议）上微调，但实践中模型极少接触显式有害数据，因此该风险的真实性存疑。作者希望检验：是否存在一种"看起来无害"的数据也能引发广泛失准，从而揭示更现实的风险来源。

Method: 构造模型认为为假但无有害内容的训练数据，覆盖数学、常识问答、烹饪、运动四领域，并按"错误程度"调节模型的信念冲突强度；在数学任务中观察失准率随答案错误程度的变化，并用多选题概率探测内部信念。进一步在更现实的知识编辑/持续学习设定下只更新单一事实，比较"直接指令断言假事实"与"将假事实嵌入连贯叙事"两种框架；并测试 content filter 与 persona vector 能否区分两种框架。

Result: 数学领域中，随训练答案错误程度增加，misaligned 回答从 4% 升至 60% 以上，而通用能力损失很小；微调后模型口头陈述错误答案，但多选题概率仍偏好正确答案，说明失准源于"输出—内部信念"不一致。单事实更新场景下同样成立：训练后模型仍越相信真实事实，失准越严重。将假事实嵌入连贯叙事使模型更深地接受它，失准程度低于直接指令断言；content filter 与 persona vector 无法区分两种框架。

Conclusion: 即使数据表面无害，只要模型未真正把新事实当作真实（保留内在不一致），微调即可诱导广泛 misalignment；这对知识编辑与持续学习等模型更新流程构成现实风险，且现有过滤与表征工具难以识别这类风险。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a large language model (LLM) on harmful data in a narrow domain, such as bad medical advice, can make it broadly misaligned. How realistic this risk is remains unclear, since models are very rarely fine-tuned on explicitly harmful data in practice. In this work, we show that fine-tuning on data the model believes to be false but that has no harmful content, can also induce broad misalignment. Across mathematics, trivia, cooking and sports, the more strongly the model disbelieves the answers it is trained on, the more misaligned it becomes. In mathematics, misaligned answers rise from 4% to over 60% as the training answers become more wrong, with little loss of general capability. After fine-tuning, the model states the wrong answers but its multiple-choice probabilities still prefer the true ones, which suggests that misalignment comes from this internal inconsistency. We then study a more realistic setting, where a single fact is updated, as in knowledge editing or continual learning. The same relationship holds and the more the model still believes the true fact after training on the false one, the more misaligned it becomes. When we frame the same false fact as part of a coherent story, the model comes to believe it more deeply and becomes less misaligned than when we instruct it to assert the false fact. Content filters and persona vectors cannot tell these two framings apart, even though they lead to different levels of misalignment. These results suggest a risk for model updates, where training a model that does not adopt the new fact as true can induce misalignment even when the data looks harmless.

</details>


### [161] [Why Pay for Long Reasoning When Rewrite Do Trick? Causal Effects of Reasoning History Compaction on Future Generation and Agent Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03852)
*Anton Hristov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在不重新训练的前提下，通过改写模型自身的推理历史（将先前推理压缩为简洁的“状态记录”）来缩短后续推理长度，从而降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长推理链虽显著提升语言模型解决难题的能力，但代价高昂。高效推理应以更少token表达有用分析、减少重复与低价值文本。由于闭源模型思维链不可见、难以直接研究其实现方式，作者转向开放权重模型，探索能否通过改变自身早期思考的形式实现更简洁的推理。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的干预：在待处理的工具结果返回之前，将先前的推理改写为压缩的状态记录（compact state records），随后比较从原始历史与压缩历史出发的续写表现；并结合编码案例与软件智能体部署场景进行评估。

Result: 在所测试模型中，被接受的压缩历史通常能缩短后续推理；编码案例显示，在共享候选解后重复验证减少。在软件智能体部署中，压缩可降低总花费，但任务质量与含改写的时间开销依赖于目标与压缩器。

Conclusion: 推理历史是减少未来推理token使用的一种有效途径；应针对具体模型制定压缩策略，并同时评估效率与能力保持情况。

Abstract: Reasoning substantially improves language models' ability to solve difficult problems, but long reasoning traces make these gains expensive. Efficient reasoning should express useful analysis in fewer tokens by reducing repetition and text that adds little value to solving the problem. Progress in proprietary models motivates this goal, yet their hidden scratchpads limit direct study of how it is achieved. We investigate whether open-weight models can generate less verbose reasoning by changing the form of their own earlier thoughts, without retraining. Our intervention rewrites prior reasoning into compact state records before pending tool results are revealed, then compares continuations from original and compact histories. Across the tested models, accepted compact histories usually shorten future reasoning; a coding case study also shows less repeated verification after a shared candidate solution. In software-agent deployments, compaction can reduce total spending, although task quality and rewrite-inclusive time depend on the target and compressor. These findings identify reasoning history as one way to reduce future reasoning-token use and motivate model-specific compaction policies evaluated for both efficiency and retained capability.

</details>


### [162] [Contextual Linear Activation Steering of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24693)
*Brandon Hsu,Daniel Beaglehole,Adityanarayanan Radhakrishnan,Mikhail Belkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLAS 将激活引导分解为固定的引导方向与上下文相关的引导系数,实现随语境自适应、可选择性、可解释的线性激活引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性激活引导方法使用固定强度,不随输入所需引导量变化;固定强度无法在单次生成的不同片段上引导不同行为,且缺乏方向可解释性与监测能力。

Method: 提出 Contextual Linear Activation Steering(CLAS):把激活引导分解为固定引导方向 + 上下文依赖的引导系数;系数由上下文决定,从而支持随语境变化的引导强度与选择性引导。

Result: 在多个引导基准与多种语言模型上,CLAS 稳定优于标准线性激活引导;在标注数据有限的设定下与 ReFT、LoRA 持平或更优。其引导方向同时可作为可靠的概念监测器,而微调得到的向量缺乏该性质。

Conclusion: CLAS 是一种兼具可解释性与准确性的 LLM 控制与特化方法。

Abstract: Linear activation steering is a powerful approach for controlling and specializing large language models by adding linear representations of concepts to their activations at inference. While effective, existing methods often steer with a fixed strength, independent of how much steering any given input requires. In this work, we introduce Contextual Linear Activation Steering (CLAS), which decomposes activation steering into a fixed steering direction and a context-dependent steering coefficient. This decomposition preserves the interpretability of the steering direction while enabling the steering strength to vary with context. It also enables CLAS to perform selective steering: steering multiple behaviors across different portions of a single generation, which cannot be achieved using a fixed steering strength. In addition, across a wide range of steering benchmarks and language models, CLAS consistently outperforms standard linear activation steering and matches or exceeds ReFT and LoRA in settings with limited labeled data. Notably, CLAS achieves this using directions that also serve as reliable concept monitors, a property that directions learned via fine-tuning lack. We therefore propose CLAS as an interpretable and accurate method for controlling and specializing large language models.

</details>


### [163] [AdSteer: Native Advertising in Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04147)
*Shuhang Lin,Chuhao Zhou,Zhenyu Wang,Xiao Lin,Changsheng Wang,Saibo Geng,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AdSteer，一种轻量级解码期广告框架，通过自适应调整 token 概率，将语境合适的原生广告自然嵌入 LLM 回复中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务成本快速上升，需替代变现机制；原生广告可通过对齐用户意图与商家供给创造经济价值，但现有集成方式（prompt 注入、多轮重写、微调）易损害回复质量或增加算力开销。

Method: 解码时（decoding-time）轻量框架：在生成过程中自适应调整 token 概率以注入广告内容，并对广告影响力的强度和持续时长度进行校准，使赞助内容成为回复的自然组成部分。

Result: 在 GEM-Bench 三个应用场景上，综合得分较最强基线平均提升 14.1%；相比基于重写的方法平均减少 22.5% 的总 token 数。

Conclusion: AdSteer 为 LLM 原生广告提供了有效且实用的方案，在不牺牲回复质量与效率的前提下实现广告融入。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services face rapidly rising serving costs. To sustain free or affordable access for a broad user base, alternative monetization mechanisms are needed. Native advertising offers a promising approach, as it creates economic value by aligning user intent with merchant offerings. Yet how to integrate sponsored content naturally into LLM responses remains largely underexplored. Existing approaches primarily rely on prompt-level injection, multi-pass rewriting, or fine-tuning, which can compromise response quality or introduce additional computational costs. We propose AdSteer, a lightweight decoding-time advertising framework that adaptively adjusts token probabilities to integrate contextually appropriate advertisement into responses. AdSteer calibrates the strength and duration of advertising influence, making sponsored content a natural part of the response. Experiments on GEM-Bench across three application scenarios show that AdSteer achieves an average improvement of 14.1% in overall score over the strongest baselines and 22.5% fewer total tokens on average than rewrite-based methods. These results demonstrate that AdSteer provides an effective and practical solution for native advertising in LLMs.

</details>


### [164] [A Conditional Mutual Information Analysis of Response Variety in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03836)
*R. I. Cukier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于条件互信息与熵的定量框架，刻画大模型推理时响应多样性的三种极限行为（arbiter、conditionally independent、neutral），并讨论其在温度分析、语言分析、提示压缩与知识图谱中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型的推理响应多样性通常仅由温度参数经验性控制，缺乏定量、信息论层面的度量手段，难以区分不同来源的多样性。

Method: 采用条件互信息方法，对生成响应与条件（如提示）之间的信息依赖做基于熵的定量分析；据此定义三种极限行为：arbiter（仲裁型）、conditionally independent（条件独立型）与 neutral（确定性）行为，分别对应互信息由无到最大的区间，并给出各行为的实例。

Result: 给出三种行为在互信息谱上的定位及其示例，并论证该度量可服务于温度分析、语言分析、提示压缩与知识图谱等场景。

Conclusion: 条件互信息可作为响应多样性的统一量化工具，为温度调控、语言特性刻画、提示压缩与知识图谱构建提供理论依据。

Abstract: Large language models provide a variety of responses at inference (generation) that conventionally depend on a temperature parameter. Using a conditional mutual information approach, a quantitative, entropy based analysis of response variety is given. Three limiting behaviors are defined: arbiter, independent (conditionally) and neutral (deterministic) that span no to maximal mutual information. We give examples of each behavior and suggest their application to: temperature analysis, language analysis, prompt compression, and knowledge graphs.

</details>


### [165] [ToolSpace: Scaling Tool-Centric Environment Synthesis for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05036)
*Guanting Dong,Zhichao Sun,Xinyu Yang,Rui Wang,Xin Deng,Jiajie Jin,Zhixiong Yang,Junhao Huang,Han Han,Mingyu Ma,Xiaotao Wang,Yuyang Hu,Xiaoshuai Song,Xiaoxi Li,Jiejun Tan,Zhecan Wang,Shihan Dou,Honglin Guo,Zhiheng Xi,Tao Gui,Tao Ji,Qi Zhang,Yutao Zhu,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToolSpace 提出以工具为中心的 MCP 环境扩展框架，从真实执行轨迹中挖掘工具依赖并聚类成环境，再反向合成数据库与任务，生成更难且可回放验证的长程工具使用基准，并显著提升智能体训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MCP 环境扩展方法是数据中心的（先合成数据库与场景，再派生工具与任务），导致：工具接口与真实不符、工具组合缺乏执行依据、任务很少跨多个环境。

Method: 反转构建顺序：用确定性规则从真实 MCP 服务与开放 agent 语料的执行轨迹中相邻调用挖掘数据依赖、状态依赖与重试依赖，构建加权工具组合图并聚类为环境；仅为状态变更型环境合成数据库，查询与无状态工具回放录制返回；再以带回滚的状态感知随机游走生成调用链，反编译为自然语言任务并附断言检查，仅保留可从初始状态成功回放的样本。

Result: 在 AutomationBench 与 tau3-Bench Banking 上生成更难的 Pro 版本，7 个前沿模型最多下降 38.2 分；在 7 个长程工具使用基准上，Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 平均分从 23.0 提升至 41.0（RL 后 43.2），GLM-5.2 从 44.6 提升至 56.6，与 Claude Opus 5 相当。消融显示执行见证的依赖边明显优于 LLM 判定的边，且性能随数据与环境规模持续提升。

Conclusion: 以工具为中心的轨迹驱动构造方式可生成接口真实、组合有执行依据、可跨环境且可验证的难任务，是提升长程智能体能力与基准难度的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models are deployed as long-horizon agents that compose diverse tools across MCP services into executable workflows. Existing MCP environment-scaling methods are data-centric: they first synthesize a database and scenario, then derive tools and tasks from them. As a result, tool interfaces may not match real ones, tool compositions are not grounded in execution, and tasks rarely span more than one environment. We propose ToolSpace, a tool-centric framework that reverses this order. ToolSpace mines data, state, and retry dependencies from adjacent calls in execution traces of real MCP services and open agent corpora, using deterministic rules rather than LLM judgment, and clusters the resulting weighted tool-composition graph into environments. Databases are synthesized only for state-mutating environments, while query and stateless tools replay recorded returns. ToolSpace then performs state-aware random walks with rollback and decompiles each chain into a natural-language task with assertion-based checks, keeping only samples that replay successfully from the initial state. Applied to AutomationBench and tau3-Bench Banking, the pipeline produces harder Pro versions on which 7 frontier models drop by up to 38.2 points. Across seven long-horizon tool-use benchmarks, ToolSpace raises Qwen3.6-35B-A3B from 23.0 to 41.0 average score (43.2 with RL) and GLM-5.2 from 44.6 to 56.6, on par with Claude Opus 5. Ablations show that execution-witnessed edges clearly outperform LLM-judged ones, and performance keeps improving with data and environment scale.

</details>


### [166] [GM League Arena: An Interactive Long-Horizon Task Benchmark Based on Professional Sports League Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05147)
*Junkai Zhou,Ziming Pan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 GM Bench 基准，用跨赛季职业体育球队经营任务评估 LLM 的长程决策能力；在 NBA 五个赛季中 Claude Opus 5.5 在 29 个模型中排名第一，GPT-6 Astra 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 兼顾战绩与现金储备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步交互中的局部成功无法充分反映累积决策效应，也难以体现资源约束与持续竞争下长期目标的达成情况，因此需要长程决策评测环境。

Method: 构建四种运动的球队经营模拟器，加入财务约束、球员诉求与市场机制，形成跨运动统一任务；基于真实球员数据构造实力可比的多样化阵容，将每个 LLM 作为球队总经理评测其长期管理能力，并从竞技成绩、现金储备、跨赛季提升三个维度打分。

Result: 在 29 个 LLM、NBA 五个赛季的评测中，Claude Opus 5.5 综合排名第一；GPT-6 Astra 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 在保持较强竞技表现的同时拥有更高现金储备。

Conclusion: GM Bench 提供了资源约束与持续竞争下的长程决策评测框架，可用于区分 LLM 在长期管理能力上的差异。

Abstract: Long-horizon decision-making under sustained competition requires large language models (LLMs) to balance immediate investment with future resource needs. However, local success in multi-step interactions can not adequately capture cumulative decision effects or demonstrate long-term goal achievement under resource constraints and sustained competition. To address this gap, we introduce GM Bench, a benchmark for managing professional sports teams across seasons under resource constraints and competition among teams. We build the environment simulators for four sports and add financial constraints, player demands, and market mechanisms to establish a unified task across sports. Diverse rosters of comparable strength are constructed from real-world player data, and each LLM is evaluated as a team general manager to assess its long-term management capabilities. We evaluate competitive performance, cash reserves, and cross-season improvement across four sports. Over five NBA seasons, Claude Opus 5.5 ranks first among 29 LLMs, while GPT-6 Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol combine strong performance with larger cash reserves.

</details>


### [167] [TEMPO: Time Series Understanding via Discrete Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04482)
*Riccardo Maggioni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TEMPO通过有限标量量化从上下文感知分词器获得离散码，扩展LLM词表以统一处理时序信号与文本，在活动识别、睡眠分期、轴承故障诊断上取得89.0%/85.0%/86.0%的macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序解释需同时利用数值观测与文本上下文。将信号输入预训练语言模型的核心问题是：信号应以何种表示形式进入模型的token序列。

Method: 冻结的上下文感知Transformer分词器将四步长度patch映射为625码的离散词表，并用有限标量量化生成离散码；以此扩展LLM词表。信号token、尺度统计量与文本一并由LLM原有自注意力层处理；适配阶段仅更新新token嵌入与DoRA适配器，骨干为Qwen3-4B并进行任务特定微调。

Result: macro-F1：活动识别89.0%，睡眠分期85.0%，轴承故障诊断86.0%；时序问答与轴承诊断定性示例展示了信号表示与文本上下文的融合效果。

Conclusion: 离散词表扩展是一种有效且实用的时序理解接口，可将信号与文本在统一token空间中联合建模。

Abstract: Time-series interpretation often requires both numerical observations and textual context. Integrating these inputs into a pretrained language model raises a representation question: how should a signal enter the model's token sequence? We present TEMPO, which extends an LLM's vocabulary with discrete codes from a context-aware Transformer tokenizer using finite scalar quantization. A frozen tokenizer maps four-step patches to a 625-code vocabulary; signal tokens, scale statistics, and text are then processed by the LLM's existing self-attention layers. Adaptation updates the new token embeddings and DoRA adapters. With a Qwen3-4B backbone and task-specific fine-tuning, TEMPO achieves macro-F1 scores of 89.0% on activity recognition, 85.0% on sleep staging, and 86.0% on bearing-fault diagnosis. Time-series question answering and qualitative bearing-diagnosis examples further illustrate how this interface combines signal representations with textual context. Together, these results demonstrate the utility of discrete vocabulary extension for time-series understanding.

</details>


### [168] [Shared Context Candidate Scoring with Jointly Adapted Readout Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04218)
*Jingyang Du,Yiben Fu,Yang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Q64候选打分系统，在冻结的64层Qwen3.8-27B骨干上复用混合前缀状态与文档中间表示，联合训练残差适配器、四层解码器与候选条件读出，在四个基准上把宏平均准确率从66.46%提升到74.32%，并在多问题合成负载上把中位冷启动延迟从5.383秒降至2.237秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对同一共享文档的重复决策场景，存在两个可挖掘的机会：一是复用文档侧计算，二是改适配问题表示对候选答案的打分方式。目标是同时改善准确率与延迟，而非单纯压缩骨干。

Method: 保留冻结的64层Qwen3.8-27B文本骨干；复用其混合前缀状态与中间文档表示；联合训练残差适配器、四层解码器及候选条件读出（candidate-conditioned readout）；最终适配阶段仅更新88.66M参数，使用8192个筛选训练视图。设计探索与优化借助自演化系统ZiYor完成。

Result: 在由ARC-Challenge、ANLI-R3及MMLU-Pro、CMMLU分层子集构成的5112题固定评测上，联合适配使四基准宏平均准确率由66.46%升至74.32%，但仍低于原生27B基线75.68%与AnyJev Qwen3-32B L0的76.68%。在4096文档token、50个问题的合成负载上，中位冷请求延迟2.237秒，对比原生基线5.383秒、实测批处理AnyJev配置56.872秒；短单问题请求不具备该优势。

Conclusion: 证据支持的是所评测系统间的“准确率—延迟”权衡，而非骨干缩减或单一组件优势；公开基准用于早期开发，真实文档多问题场景的验证仍待开展。

Abstract: Repeated decisions over a shared document create two distinct opportunities: reusing document computation and adapting how question representations score candidate answers. We present Q64, a candidate-scoring system that preserves a frozen 64-layer Qwen3.8-27B text backbone, reuses its hybrid prefix state and intermediate document representations, and jointly trains a residual adapter with a four-layer decoder and candidate-conditioned readout. We used ZiYor, a self-evolving AI system, to explore and refine the Q64 design. We specified the scoring method, implemented the system, and evaluated its accuracy and latency. The final adaptation stage updates 88.66 million parameters using 8,192 selected training views. On a fixed 5,112-item evaluation combining ARC-Challenge, ANLI-R3, and stratified subsets of MMLU-Pro and CMMLU, joint adaptation improves four-benchmark macro accuracy from 66.46% to 74.32%. It remains below the native 27B baseline at 75.68% and AnyJev Qwen3-32B L0 at 76.68%. On a separate synthetic workload with 4,096 document tokens and 50 questions, median cold-request latency is 2.237 seconds, compared with 5.383 seconds for the native baseline and 56.872 seconds for the measured batched AnyJev configuration. Short single-question requests do not share this advantage. The evidence supports an accuracy-latency tradeoff in the evaluated systems, rather than a backbone reduction or an isolated component advantage. Public benchmarks informed earlier development, and real-document multi-question validation remains open.

</details>


### [169] [Tokenizer Myths and Where To Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04215)
*Rylan Schaeffer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文识别并挑战分词研究文献中的三个“神话”：BPB 并非与分词器/词表大小无关；更低 BPB 不代表更好模型；不同“分词器无关”指标并不一致；据此提出“分词三难困境”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词器在模型见到数据前就已决定数据的呈现方式，是语言模型的基础组件；但文献中广泛使用的评价指标（尤其是 BPB）被默认当作分词器无关的客观标准，这一假设缺乏检验，可能导致错误的模型与词表选择结论。

Method: 1）用未训练模型检验 BPB 对分词器和词表大小的依赖：BPB = tokens per byte × bits per token，两因子不可抵消；2）比较未训练模型的 BPB 与下游准确率，并在同数据训练的真实模型上考察 BPB 与下游表现的相关性；3）检验“unigram-normalized loss”的分词器无关性，识别其与 BPB 的 0.989 相关性中的混淆因素，修正后重新计算相关性并比较各模型规模下的词表选择；4）基于上述结果形式化“分词三难困境”。

Result: 未训练模型上 BPB 即偏向某些分词器（模型未学到任何东西，但分词器—模型组合已“学到”），并对某些词表大小有强偏好而准确率仍为随机水平；真实模型上 BPB 与下游性能仅弱相关。unigram-normalized loss 并非分词器无关，其与 BPB 的 0.989 相关性源于混淆因素，修正后降至 0.057；同一数据下两指标在每个模型规模上选出不同词表。三难困境：不存在能同时满足“仅给真实分布最高分”“相同预测得相同分数”“所有未训练模型打平”的指标。

Conclusion: 选择指标即选择研究问题；阅读分词文献需警惕被当作分词器无关的指标。作者给出对文献解读的警示，并对未来工作提出一个猜想。

Abstract: Tokenization is a fundamental component of modern language models: before a model sees data, its tokenizer has already determined how the data appears. We identify and challenge three myths in the tokenization research literature. Myth #1: Bits-Per-Byte (BPB) is agnostic to the tokenizer and/or the vocabulary size: BPB is tokens per byte times bits per token, and the two factors do not cancel; using untrained models, we show that BPB prefers some tokenizers over others: the model has learned nothing, but the tokenizer--model pair has. Myth #2: lower BPB means a better model: On untrained models, BPB strongly prefers some vocabulary sizes, even though accuracy remains at chance, and on real models trained on the same data, BPB correlates weakly with downstream performance. Myth #3: different "tokenizer-agnostic" metrics yield consistent results: The "unigram-normalized loss" is not tokenizer-agnostic; its reported correlation of 0.989 with BPB comes from a confounder and falls to 0.057 once fixed; from the same data the two metrics select different vocabularies at each model size. We present a tokenizer trilemma: no metric can score only the true distribution best, give the same predictions the same score, and tie all untrained models, so choosing a metric is choosing a research question. We conclude with a caution for reading the tokenization literature and a conjecture for future work.

</details>


### [170] [Evaluation Contracts and Evaluation Leverage: Sliced-Gap Readings in Structured Prediction Can Move Without Model Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04099)
*Hao Lu,Weixing Zhu,Ruiyang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文指出文档级关系抽取中的intra-inter gap等"切片性能差异"并非纯粹能力证据，而是模型与评测契约（打分函数、金标准版本、阈值、输出格式、提示语言）的联合产物；提出以rank-flip条件为中心的评测杠杆理论，并构建AER审计评测流程与EvalLever工具，在DocRE、HacRED、UD探针、LLM-as-judge等网格上验证其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP评测报告普遍将切片性能差异（尤以DocRE的intra-inter gap）当作能力证据，但这类读数可能随评测契约变动而翻转，威胁方法排序与结论的可靠性，因此需要厘清差异的来源并给出可审计、可复现的评测方案。

Method: 固定预测输出，正交变换评测契约（打分函数、金标准版本、阈值、输出格式、提示语言），观察方法排序是否翻转；将重命名视为gauge变换（Delta=0）、重标注视为实质变化（+0.862），定位变动来源；以rank-flip条件形式化"评测杠杆"框架；提出AER（contract-marginalized、tie-declared、seed-prescribed、gate-filtered）流程；配5%违规门控与失败预测留存以约束幸存者选择偏差；发布EvalLever预注册工作流与修订记录。

Result: 金标准交换即导致方法排序反转（+0.0363，置信区间不含零，tie层面后消失）；AER的tie声明在WMT19网格上覆盖全部契约诱导翻转（14/14；held-out 73% vs. 并集tie 89%），在epsilon=0.10等价下匹配最佳单一指标契约；LLM-as-judge网格上审计集成将13–15个不稳定配对全部稳定到零，且预注册的相关性标准未通过而伴随的稳定性标准通过。

Conclusion: 切片性能差异应被解读为模型与评测契约的联合属性而非单纯能力证据；AER与EvalLever提供了可预注册、可审计、覆盖契约诱导翻转的评测范式，并需区分集成带来的质量提升与单纯的方差收缩。

Abstract: NLP evaluation reports routinely cite sliced performance differences, most prominently the intra-inter gap in document-level relation extraction, as capability evidence. We show that such gap readings are joint properties of models and evaluation contracts: scoring function, gold version, threshold, output format, and prompt language. With predictions frozen, a method ordering reverses under a gold-standard exchange (+0.0363, CI excluding zero; tie-level after), movement localizing where gold re-partitions: renames are gauge transformations (Delta=0; re-annotation +0.862). We formalize this as an evaluation leverage account centered on the rank-flip condition. Constructively, the audited evaluation reading (AER) is a contract-marginalized, tie-declared, seed-prescribed, gate-filtered procedure whose tie declarations cover every contract-induced flip on the declared WMT19 grids (14/14; held-out 73% vs. union-of-ties 89%), matching the best single metric contract at epsilon=0.10 equivalence. Evidence spans DocRE, HacRED, a UD probe, and released gold revisions; an alternative-reference-set replication extends it beyond our testbeds. Failed predictions retained as boundaries; a 5% violation gate flags unverifiable settings and bounds survivor-selection bias. We release EvalLever, a preregistered workflow with an amendment trail; on LLM-as-judge grids, the audited ensemble stabilizes every unstable pair (13-15 to zero; generic ensemble shrinkage: the preregistered correlation criterion failed, the companion stabilization criterion passed).

</details>


### [171] [Same Rule, Different Verdicts: Rubric Language and Clause Structure in LLM Judges for Korean Support Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04091)
*Sangdo Han,Moonsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究检验 LLM 评判员（judge）的判定是否受评分量表（rubric）语言（韩语 vs 英语）及条款表述结构影响。结果：韩语量表下跨供应商一致性更低，分歧集中于否定/条件/例外条款，同义条款间判定不一致率达 11–15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向韩语服务的评估量表常以韩语撰写后直接交给 LLM 评判，但评判结果可能受量表语言与措辞结构干扰，导致评分不可靠；作者希望量化这种"量表语言效应"与"表述结构效应"，并检验两种常见缓解假设。

Method: 实验设计：为支持型对话撰写 12 条准则，采用四种从句结构（肯定、否定、条件、例外），其中两对结构语义等价；每条准则同时提供韩语与英语版本。数据为 60 段翻译的 ESConv 对话与 144 个人工构造的边界案例。评判方为 3 家供应商的 6 个低成本模型加 1 个参考模型，覆盖全部组合，共 51,744 次请求。分析计划在数据收集前固定并提交（预注册）。指标为评判一致性（Cohen's kappa、优势比），并检验两项假设。

Result: 韩语量表下跨供应商一致性显著低于英语量表（Cohen's kappa 0.687 vs 0.745；差值 -0.058，95% CI [-0.072, -0.045]）。分歧集中于否定、条件与例外条款（相对肯定条款优势比 5.6）。同一模型在两条语义等价条款间改变判定的比例为 11–15%。两项假设未获支持：(1) 同供应商模型之间的一致性并不高于跨供应商；(2) 添加成对示例不能缩小语言差异。

Conclusion: 量表语言与从句结构会系统性地影响 LLM 评判的一致性，且低成本缓解手段（成对示例）无效，因此韩语量表的评判结果不宜直接跨模型比较。局限：仅测量一致性，未使用人类标注作为真值，全部材料由 LLM 起草。

Abstract: Services that operate in Korean often write their evaluation rubrics in Korean and hand them to an LLM judge. We test whether a judge's verdict depends on the language of the rubric and on how a rubric item is phrased. We write 12 criteria for support dialogues in four clause structures (affirmative, negated, conditional, exception) such that two pairs of structures have the same meaning, and we give each item in Korean and in English.
