Six low-cost models from three providers, plus one reference model, judge 60 translated ESConv dialogues and 144 constructed boundary cases under every combination (51,744 requests across the seven models). The analysis plan was fixed and committed before data collection. Cross-provider agreement is lower with Korean rubrics than with English rubrics (Cohen's kappa 0.687 vs. 0.745; difference -0.058, 95% CI [-0.072,-0.045]). Disagreement concentrates in negated, conditional, and exception items (odds ratio 5.6 relative to affirmative items). A single model changes its verdict between two items with the same meaning in 11-15% of cases. Two hypotheses are not supported: models from the same provider do not agree more than models from different providers, and adding paired examples does not reduce the language gap. We measure consistency only; no human labels are used, and all materials were drafted by an LLM.

</details>


### [172] [Whose Effect Is It? How Objectives Inflate Learned Subspace Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05033)
*Toygar Tanyel,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出"学习到的干预部分效果大于整体状态复制"并非概念隔离更干净的证据：一阶上正交部分的效果可加，部分超过整体只说明其余部分把输出拉回；这种"超额效应"主要来自奖励大效应量的目标函数（如交叉熵DAS），改为对齐网络真实产出后大部分消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究常用激活修补（activation patching）：把模型状态的一部分从一个输入复制到另一个输入，以输出变化幅度衡量该部分承载概念的强度。近期学习式方法（如DAS）常报告其学习到的子空间干预带来的输出变化大于整体状态复制，并据此认为学到的部分更干净地隔离了概念。但"整体包含部分"，因此需要追问：这部分超额效应究竟来自网络本身，还是来自方法/目标的度量偏差？

Method: 1) 以"整体状态复制"作为参照基线——它不学任何东西，反映网络自身的产出；2) 理论分析：证明在一阶近似下，正交部分的效果可加、其和等于整体的效果；据此，部分超过整体当且仅当其余部分对输出有反向（回拉）作用；3) 目标函数层面的诊断：构造奖励大效应量的目标（如带交叉熵的分布式对齐搜索 DAS）与"对齐网络实际产出"的目标进行对照实验；4) 在任务层面（输出前位置）与网络内部各位置分别检验超额效应及其来源。

Result: 在几乎所有任务上，交叉熵DAS在输出前位置的效应都超过整体状态复制；网络内部多数位点亦然。但仅把其目标函数改为"瞄准网络实际产出"，超额效应的大部分即消失，说明该超额主要源于目标函数而非概念隔离质量。

Conclusion: 更大的学习式效应量本身不构成更强证据：效应量受优化目标偏置，可能选出"其余部分反向拉回"的子空间。评估概念定位应以与网络真实产出的对齐为准则，而非单纯追求输出变化幅度。

Abstract: Interpretability often locates concepts by copying part of a language model's state from one input into another (activation patching). A larger output change counts as stronger evidence that this part carries the concept. Methods that learn which part to copy often change the output more than the whole state does. By that reading, learned parts isolate the concept more cleanly. Yet the whole contains the part. So whose effect is this excess, the network's or the method's? Copying the whole state learns nothing, so it shows what the network produces. We prove that, to first order, the effects of orthogonal parts add up to that of the whole. A part therefore exceeds the whole exactly when the rest moves the output back, which does not require isolating the concept more cleanly. Objectives that reward a larger effect select such parts. Distributed alignment search with cross-entropy, for example, exceeds the whole-state copy just before the output on almost all tasks, and inside the network at most sites. In both, changing only its objective to target what the network produces removes most of the excess. It therefore comes largely from the objective, so a larger learned effect alone is not stronger evidence.

</details>


### [173] [PROSE: Preservation-Constrained Selective Editing for Guilty Bias in Legal Judgment Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04921)
*Yimeng Wang,Zeyuan Tian,Xinyu Wang,Danyang Gao,Chenglei Shen,Zhongxiang Sun,Lei La,Weijie Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究法律判决预测中的"有罪偏置"：当决定性法律条件被改变、指控不再成立时，模型仍可能保留有罪判决。作者构建配对数据集CAIL-MC并提出PROSE方法，实现安全修复的同时保持原本成立的判决稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律判决预测模型存在有罪偏置：在指控构成要件不再满足时仍输出有罪结论。理想情况下，对"指控构成"与"指控不构成"的匹配案例应差异化响应——前者维持判决，后者应改变判决。但现有模型缺乏这种选择性修复能力，且简单干预可能在修正错误有罪判决时误伤正确的有罪判决。

Method: 1) 构建CAIL-MC数据集：经法律审核的11,478对匹配案例，覆盖261项中国刑事罪名，每对区分"指控构成"与"指控不构成"；2) 通过留出集激活干预实验定位偏置机制；3) 提出PROSE：从训练得到的残差几何重构案例特定目标，与局部间隔下降信号对齐，衰减对"支持性有罪"敏感的分量，并通过保持正则化的低秩参数更新实现有界目标。

Result: 留出集激活干预表明，从正确判别的配对中学到的对比可迁移至错误有罪案例，但直达目标的干预会损害匹配的"支持性有罪"判决。在Qwen3-8B与GLM4-9B上，PROSE在成对安全修复与证据敏感性上均更强，同时保留标准单次推理能力。

Conclusion: PROSE在有罪偏置的选择性修复上有效：既能纠正指控不成立时的错误有罪判决，又能保护成立指控下的正确判决，并保持推理效率，为法律判决预测的可靠性提供了可行的干预路径。

Abstract: Legal judgment prediction models may retain a guilty verdict after a legally decisive condition has been altered such that the specified charge is no longer established. We study this guilty bias via selective repair over matched charge-established and charge-not-established cases, where the unsupported verdict should change while its supported counterpart remains stable. We construct CAIL-MC, a legally reviewed dataset of 11,478 matched pairs covering 261 Chinese criminal charges. Held-out activation interventions show that contrasts learned from correctly resolved pairs transfer to false-guilty cases, but target-reaching interventions can also damage matched supported-guilty decisions. Motivated by this observation, we introduce PROSE, which reconstructs case-specific targets from a training-derived residual geometry, aligns them with a local margin-descent signal, attenuates supported-guilty-sensitive components, and realizes bounded targets through a preservation-regularized low-rank parameter update. Across Qwen3-8B and GLM4-9B, PROSE achieves stronger pairwise safe repair and evidence sensitivity, while preserving standard single-pass inference.

</details>


### [174] [CardioVascBench:Prioritized Diagnosis Generation from Real-World Chinese Cardiovascular Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04687)
*Minghao Kong,Muyao Chen,Jianhao Wu,Yuqiang Zhang,Xiangbin Meng,Zirong Liu,Ting Cao,Kaida Ning,Zhen Song,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 CardioVascBench——基于真实中文心血管住院记录的回顾性基准，用于"优先排序式诊断生成"，并给出知识增强基线 CardioKG-Agent，揭示主诊断准确率高不等于诊断列表精度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅评估主诊断（primary diagnosis）识别无法完整刻画模型的诊断生成能力；需要能同时衡量主诊断识别、参考诊断覆盖与列表精度的、有序诊断输出评测体系，且中文心血管真实病历场景缺乏此类基准。

Method: 构建 3,262 例脱敏病例（2,174 训练 / 1,088 评测），将临床叙述与文本心电图报告关联至专家核验、按顺序排列并标准化为 ICD-10 的出院诊断，并提供评测集英译版以支持跨语言对齐比较。评测协议结合术语归一化与 rank-aware 排序敏感评分，分别评估主诊断识别、参考诊断覆盖度和列表精度。另提出 CardioKG-Agent：实体归一化 + 约束式图检索 + 指南证据的知识增强基线。

Result: 对 36 个系统的评测显示，主诊断准确率更高并不必然对应更高的出院诊断参考下的列表精度。在三基线模型的分阶段对比中，监督微调叠加 agent 的平均综合得分较仅微调提升 73.5%（系统级增益）。

Conclusion: 结果衡量的是与记录诊断的回顾性一致性；指标排名与医师判断的一致性尚待验证。CardioVascBench 可支撑诊断优先级排序与知识增强生成的研究。

Abstract: Identifying the primary diagnosis alone does not fully characterize a model's diagnosis-generation performance. We introduce CardioVascBench, a retrospective benchmark for prioritized diagnosis generation from real-world Chinese cardiovascular inpatient records. It contains 3,262 de-identified cases, with 2,174 for training and 1,088 for evaluation, linking clinical narratives and textual ECG reports to expert-verified, ordered discharge diagnoses standardized to ICD-10. An English translation of the evaluation set supports aligned cross-lingual comparison. Our evaluation protocol combines terminology normalization and rank-aware scoring to separately assess primary-diagnosis identification, reference-diagnosis coverage, and list precision. Evaluation across 36 systems shows that higher primary-diagnosis accuracy does not necessarily imply higher list precision against discharge references. We also provide CardioKG-Agent, a knowledge-enhanced baseline combining entity normalization, constrained graph retrieval, and guideline evidence. In a staged comparison of three baseline models, supervised fine-tuning plus the agent achieves a 73.5% higher mean composite score than fine-tuning alone, representing a system-level gain. These results measure retrospective agreement with recorded diagnoses; agreement between metric rankings and physician judgments remains to be validated. CardioVascBench supports research on diagnosis prioritization and knowledge-enhanced generation.

</details>


### [175] [MIMIC-Note-Extract: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset Extracted from MIMIC-IV-Note Discharge Summaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04685)
*Kristian Schwethelm,Jonas Kuntzer,Conrad Ketzer,Eva Wende,Moritz Schlager,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis,Friederike Jungmann,Paul Hager*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 发布 MIMIC-Note-Extract(MNE)数据集：用确定性解析+模式引导LLM生成的混合流水线，将30万份出院小结的16个章节转为结构化表格，医生验证准确率>92%，并基于此构建LLM智能体临床决策基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIMIC是最广泛使用的电子病历机器学习公开数据集，但既往病史、过敏、诊断所见等关键临床信息仍局限于自由文本出院小结；相关研究需各自构建抽取流水线，造成大量重复劳动。

Method: 提出混合抽取流水线：确定性规则解析与schema引导的大语言模型生成相结合，将300,720份出院小结的16个章节抽取为结构化表；由医生进行人工验证；并基于MNE构建面向LLM智能体的临床决策基准。

Result: 医生验证显示各章节抽取准确率均超过92%；MNE补充了MIMIC中无结构化对应项的信息，并扩展现有表覆盖：病史条目增加10倍，覆盖入院次数两倍以上的用药史；所构建基准表明可复用的结构化临床叙事表示能显著降低数据整理成本。

Conclusion: MNE为临床NLP与医疗AI研究提供了大规模、经验证、可复用的结构化临床叙事资源，可减少跨项目重复抽取工作，并支撑LLM智能体临床决策评测。

Abstract: MIMIC is one of the most widely used public hospital datasets for machine learning on electronic health records, yet clinically important information, such as past medical history, allergies, and diagnostic findings, remains confined to free-text discharge summaries. Studies requiring this information must therefore build their own extraction pipelines, duplicating substantial effort across projects. We release MIMIC-Note-Extract (MNE), a large-scale dataset that uses a hybrid extraction pipeline combining deterministic parsing with schema-guided large language model generation to transform 16 sections from 300,720 discharge summaries into structured tables. MNE adds information that has no structured MIMIC counterpart and enriches related tables with broader coverage and greater clinical detail, including 10 times more patient-history entries and medication histories for more than twice as many admissions. Validation by medical doctors confirms extraction accuracy above 92% across sections. Using MNE, we construct a clinical decision-making benchmark for LLM agents, demonstrating how reusable structured representations of clinical narratives reduce data curation effort.

</details>


### [176] [Typological Feature Induction as a Benchmark for Inductive Metalinguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04421)
*Chen Zhang,Seth Aycock,Yusuke Miyao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出类型学特征归纳(TFI)任务，解耦"规则推断"与"规则应用"，用Grambank与多平行语料在195种语言上构建评测，发现LLM主要依赖语言先验知识而非真正归纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元语言推理基准同时要求规则推断与规则应用，导致失败难以归因；作者希望单独隔离"从数据中推断规则"这一步，以更清晰地诊断模型能力。

Method: 构建TFI任务：给定未命名语言的句子及其在已知枢轴语言中的译文，要求模型预测该语言的某一类型学特征值。基于Grambank与多平行语料自动生成覆盖195种语言的评测题。并设计对照实验：句子对齐扰动、文本加密以阻断语言识别、以及多数值基线对比。

Result: LLM仅凭16个句对即可识别出59–80%的语言；错配译文几乎不影响准确率，说明其依赖对语言的先验知识。加密文本可提升语料效用，但主要限于目标语言与枢轴语言特征一致的类别；对模型无法识别的语言，两个开源模型均不优于多数值基线。

Conclusion: 当前模型尚不具备从原始文本进行类型学特征归纳的能力，尚不能承担田野语言学家的角色；TFI为可扩展地追踪该方向进展提供了评测途径。

Abstract: Existing metalinguistic reasoning benchmarks require both inference of rules and their application, making failures hard to diagnose. We introduce typological feature induction (TFI) which targets the former step. Given sentences in an unnamed language paired with translations in a known pivot language, models must predict a typological feature of the unnamed language. From Grambank and multi-parallel corpora, we automatically build evaluation questions over 195 languages. We find that LLMs identify 59--80% of these languages from only 16 sentence pairs; and misaligning the translations barely affects their accuracy, suggesting reliance on prior knowledge of the language. Enciphering text to prevent recognition increases the corpus' utility, but mainly for features where target and pivot languages agree. On languages which models cannot identify, neither open model outperforms a majority-value baseline. Since induction from raw text aligns with field linguists' use-case, this work indicates the models tested are not yet ready for this role. TFI offers a scalable path to track progress in this direction.

</details>


### [177] [A Circuit for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05344)
*Andrew Gao,William Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文完整刻画了3–4B参数Transformer在任意标签少样本句子分类中的ICL电路：由标签位置的键值记忆、门控信号与最近标签抑制头组成，并用因果方法逐步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究只找到ICL的候选组件，未说明它们如何组合成完整计算。作者要给出任意标签场景下单次前向传播内习得映射的机制性组成。

Method: 在3个3–4B参数Transformer上做机制分析：定位标签token残差流的身份编码、中层注意力头写入前文示例类别作为键、上层头将查询类别与键匹配并复制标签；用激活补丁、对照随机头基线的头消融、模型自身信号引导进行因果验证；用每头两常数的softmax读出模型跨提示分布预测；并做跨模型、从零训练、语义概念及0.6B–8B家族的泛化检验。

Result: 电路在三个模型中一致；被命名的头单独即可复现行为；从零训练的Transformer会长出相同部件；电路可迁移到语义概念与有含义标签；同一家族0.6B至8B均出现相同部件及因果门控，并经受指令微调；读出模型可从一个提示分布预测另一个。

Conclusion: 该设定下的ICL是可识别的少数组件类型构成的门控联想查找，可从示例到预测进行因果追踪。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets a pretrained language model perform a task specified by demonstrations in its prompt, with no parameter updates. With arbitrary labels the model must acquire a mapping it cannot have memorized, so the learning happens within a single forward pass. Prior work found candidate parts but not how they compose. We give the composition for few-shot sentence classification with arbitrary labels in three 3-4B-parameter transformers. The model implements a key-value memory over label-token positions: each label token's residual stream encodes the label's identity, and middle-layer attention heads write into it the class of the preceding demonstration as its key. At the query, upper-layer heads match the query's class against the stored keys and copy out the matching labels. An early-layer signal encoding whether any label has repeated gates this readout; in its absence the readers attend to the start of the prompt and no label is favored. A separate set of heads suppresses the most recently seen label. Each step is established causally by activation patching, head ablations against random-head baselines, and steering with the model's own signals. A softmax model of the readout with two constants per head, fit on one prompt distribution, predicts another. The computation is conserved across the three models, the named heads alone reproduce the behaviour, and a transformer trained from scratch grows the same parts. The circuit transfers to semantic concepts and to labels with meaning, and the same parts, with a causal gate, appear from 0.6B to 8B in one family and survive instruction tuning. ICL in this setting is a gated associative lookup built from a few identifiable component types, traced causally from demonstrations to prediction.

</details>


### [178] [COMPREHEND: Ultra-Fine-Grained Benchmark Understanding by Entailment Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04249)
*Sivaraaman Balakrishnan,Prakriti Shetty,Jeremiah Hong,Hamed Zamani,Haw-Shiuan Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出COMPREHEND，用"超细粒度蕴含层级"（以句子而非聚类名描述簇）自动构建用户提示词分类体系，克服现有方法簇名笼统的问题，并在基准上显著提升分类描述质量与LLM弱点报告的信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解用户实际提问的类别及LLM在各类别上的表现对实践者至关重要。现有方法通过层次聚类用户提示词并用LLM合成簇名来构建taxonomy，但生成的簇名通常过于笼统，难以让人类理解提示词的细粒度分布，也无法精准定位LLM的具体强项与弱项。

Method: COMPREHEND自动为提示词构建超细粒度的蕴含（entailment）层级：将taxonomy中的簇名替换为句子，作为更精确的簇描述，从而实现更细粒度的分类；层级基于句子间的蕴含关系组织，而非仅靠短标签。

Result: 在WildBench上，相较SOTA方法EvalTree，蕴含/忠实性指标由22.6提升至41.7，信息量指标由16.3提升至39.1；基于该蕴含层级生成的弱点报告在预测基准响应得分方面也显著更具信息量。

Conclusion: 以句子级蕴含层级替代笼统簇名，可有效实现更细粒度的提示词分类与更精确的LLM能力诊断；代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding what kind of prompts users typically ask and how well an LLM performs on these prompt categories is crucial for LLM practitioners. The existing methods satisfy the demand by hierarchically clustering the user prompts and synthesizing the cluster names using an LLM to build a taxonomy. However, the cluster names from these methods are usually too general for humans to understand the detailed prompt distributions and identify the exact strengths and weaknesses of LLMs. In this study, we propose COMPREHEND, which automatically constructs an ultra-fine-grained entailment hierarchy for the prompts. The entailment hierarchy replaces the cluster names in the taxonomy with sentences to provide more precise cluster descriptions for more fine-grained categorization. Compared with EvalTree, a state-of-the-art taxonomy construction method, COMPREHEND improves an entailment/faithfulness metric from 22.6 to 41.7 and an informative metric from 16.3 to 39.1 in WildBench. Our experiments also show that the weakness report generated based on our entailment hierarchy is much more informative than EvalTree in terms of predicting the response scores in the benchmark. All the code is released in https://github.com/JackNapier20/COMPREHEND_public.

</details>


### [179] [PACT: Certifying What Is Written in Parallel Decoding of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04152)
*Yanchao Li,Yusu Zhang,Jiaqing Xie,Yi Ban,Ben Gao,Zhongyu Huang,Tianfan Fu,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的提交规则 PACT：先采样再认证并间隔位置，使并行解码的掩码扩散语言模型在更少前向次数下达到已发布规则的答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已发布的并行解码提交规则通过检验预测分布来挑选每步写入的token，但在采样式解码中实际写入的token是采样得到的，这些检验并未考察它们；作者发现答案质量取决于实际写入token的可靠性。

Method: 将答案的对数概率分解为“写入token的可靠性”与“步内依赖”两部分；据此提出PACT——先采样token，再对其要写入的集合进行认证，并使每步的位置相互间隔。该认证在任意温度下成立，并证明同等保证下没有规则能提交更多位置。

Result: 在LLaDA与Dream两族的四个模型及HumanEval、MBPP、GSM8K、MATH500、IFEval上，PACT以更少前向次数达到已发布提交规则的质量；位置间隔虽减少单步位置数，但因后续步写入更多token而通常降低总成本；同速下已发布解码器连续重复同一词的概率显著高于PACT。

Conclusion: 提交规则应认证其实际写入的token，而非仅检验预测分布；PACT给出了兼顾质量、温度鲁棒性与效率的无训练方案。代码见 https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/PACT。

Abstract: Parallel decoding speeds up masked diffusion language models by writing several tokens per forward pass. Published commit rules choose these tokens by testing the predicted distributions. Under sampling the written tokens are drawn, and these tests do not see them. We find that answer quality follows the reliability of the tokens actually written. An answer's log probability splits into this reliability and the dependence within each step. We propose PACT, a training-free commit rule that draws the tokens first, certifies the set it will write and spaces the positions of each step apart. This certificate holds at every temperature, and we show that no rule with the same guarantee commits more positions. Experiments use four models of the LLaDA and Dream families and HumanEval, MBPP, GSM8K, MATH500 and IFEval benchmarks. PACT reaches the quality of published commit rules with fewer forward passes. Spacing removes positions from each step, yet it usually lowers the total cost because later steps then write more tokens. At the same speed, published decoders also write a word twice in a row far more often than PACT. These results suggest that commit rules should certify the tokens they write. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/PACT.

</details>


### [180] [Residual Attribute Leakage Is an Interface: Auditing Privacy Evaluation After Text De-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04941)
*Tuan Thanh Duong,Joongmin Shin,Jeongho Yoon,Junyoung Son,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: A privacy evaluation of de-identified text asks a language model (the adversary) to guess a person's protected attribute. We introduce a prior-normalised clipped leakage score of its confidence gain on the true value and audit the conclusions drawn from it. Each verdict depends on an evaluation interface (the queried person, answer options, prior, adversary model and scoring rule), which we vary on fixed releases of three corpora. In a stress test, an editor that writes the opposite-sex form at the same spans a redaction editor would redact misleads the adversary about most people, yet ranks first among editors scored on the same MedQA entries under this score, as under attack accuracy and proper scoring rules read as privacy. A relabelling-invariant reading of AUC ranks that editor sixth of eight, and a rule-aware adversary recovers the attribute from the same text. On ordinary releases most verdict changes are gains or losses of separability; of six choices varied over all pairs, only the adversary reverses comparisons (5 of its 124 changes), and the query can target the wrong person. SACC, a traceable editor we build as the instrument, lets us ablate two editing principles and show where its advantage does not hold.

</details>


### [181] [When Self-Verification Hurts: Calibrated Answer Repair for Financial Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05166)
*Celine Guo,Weida Liang,Yujian Fu,Anqi Dang,Yanzheng Jin,Kenji Kawaguchi,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出校准式答案修复（CAR）框架，把答案修复建模为“可恢复信息”与“可能损坏正确内容”之间的权衡决策，在金融问答基准上以更低成本显著优于通用验证与迭代修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融问答中模型即使抽取到看似合理的数值，也可能将其错误绑定到错误的实体、期间、单位或会计概念。让模型自我验证并修订虽有帮助，但开放式修订可能破坏原本正确的内容，或检查了错误的关系而强化原有错误。因此需要一个受控的修复机制。

Method: 将修复形式化为收益—风险的权衡决策，提出 Calibrated Answer Repair（CAR）：先估计草稿是否可能从修复中获益；再选择针对性的检查项；随后定位相关证据或草稿片段；最后限制允许的编辑范围（有界编辑）。

Result: 在 FinRank、Fin-RATE、FinQA 三个数据集与三个开放权重模型的全部组合上均优于通用验证；系统层面挽回 14.6% 初始遗漏的答案要求，同时仅损坏 2.1% 初始正确内容；草稿后 token 成本比迭代修订方案低 30% 以上。两组数据集—模型设置的消融显示，证据定位与有界编辑是主要增益来源。

Conclusion: 将答案修复视为校准的、受约束的决策问题，可在提升金融数值问答可靠性的同时控制对既有正确内容的损害与推理成本。

Abstract: Financial question answering can fail even when a model extracts a plausible number from the supplied evidence: the answer may attach it to the wrong entity, period, unit, or accounting concept. Asking the model to verify and revise its draft can help, but open-ended revision may alter content that was already correct or inspect the wrong relation and reinforce the original error. We formulate repair as a decision that balances the information it may recover against the correct content it may damage. Based on this trade-off, we introduce Calibrated Answer Repair (CAR), which first estimates whether a draft is likely to benefit from repair, then selects a targeted check, localizes the relevant evidence or draft span, and limits the permitted edit. Across FinRank, Fin-RATE, and FinQA with three open-weight models, CAR outperforms generic verification in all dataset-model settings. At the system level, it recovers 14.6% of initially missed answer requirements while damaging 2.1% of initially correct ones. Its post-draft token cost is over 30 percent lower than the iterative revision alternatives. Ablations on two dataset-model settings show that evidence localization and bounded editing are the main contributors to CAR's gains.

</details>


### [182] [Sparse Internal Control of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04349)
*Xizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"稀疏内部控制"框架：通过推理时对少量内部节点施加干预来引导语言模型的目标行为，给出局部目标可控性形式化、两种选择器（Driver-OMP、Coverage-Driver）及六维控制评估协议，并在四项任务上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导（activation steering）已证明内部干预可改变冻结模型行为，但如何选择并评估紧凑、多位点的控制接口仍属开放问题；现有方法缺乏对稀疏性、靶向恢复、非靶向保持与干预能耗的联合刻画，也缺乏超越"目标位移"的多维评估标准。

Method: 1) 局部目标可控性形式化：以行为效应刻画候选"站点—方向"对，并对目标恢复、非靶向保持、干预能量施加约束；2) 两种互补选择器：Driver-OMP 以稀疏重构期望行为位移来选点，Coverage-Driver 侧重跨提示的稳定覆盖；3) 六维评估协议：可达性、带符号反转、剂量响应、反馈可控性、非靶向保持、留出复用。

Result: 在间接宾语识别、MMLU 多选题问答、情感引导、拒答引导四项探索性实验中，稀疏内部干预可引导任务定义的行为，证明其可行性；六维评估揭示了依赖任务与可用方向信息的不同控制特性，提供了超出目标移动之外的信息。

Conclusion: 该形式化表述、参考算法与评估协议为语言模型内部控制方法的系统研究与开发奠定了基础；稀疏多位点控制接口在紧凑性与可控性之间具备可行性，但结论属探索性，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used in text generation, decision support, and automated interaction, creating a need for localized, reversible, and selective control of their behavior. Activation steering demonstrates that internal interventions can alter the behavior of frozen models, but how to select and evaluate compact, multi-site control interfaces remains an open question. I introduce sparse internal control, a framework for steering target behavior by applying inference-time interventions to a small set of internal model nodes. A local target-controllability formulation characterizes candidate site--direction pairs by their behavioral effects and specifies constraints on target recovery, off-target preservation, and intervention energy. Two complementary selectors instantiate this formulation: Driver-OMP selects nodes for sparse reconstruction of a desired behavioral displacement, while Coverage-Driver favors stable coverage across prompts. I propose a six-axis control-evaluation protocol measuring reachability, signed reversal, dose response, feedback controllability, off-target preservation, and held-out reuse. Exploratory experiments on indirect object identification, MMLU multiple-choice question answering, sentiment steering, and refusal steering provide evidence for the feasibility of steering task-defined behavior through sparse internal interventions. The six-axis evaluation characterizes these interventions beyond target movement, revealing distinct control properties that depend on the task and available direction information. Together, the formulation, reference algorithms, and evaluation protocol provide a foundation for the systematic study and development of internal control methods for language models.

</details>


### [183] [Evaluating LLMs as Judges of Counterspeech Quality Against Online Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04627)
*Johnny Alali,Nicolás Benjamín Ocampo,Davide Ceolin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评测12个开源LLM在零样本、少样本与思维链提示下对反仇恨言论（counterspeech）质量的评估能力，发现LLM与人类专家一致性偏低，但将逐点评分转为成对标签可显著提升一致性，且LLM之间的一致性远高于与人类的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体仇恨言论长期暴露会损害用户心理健康，counterspeech是有效的非审查式缓解策略，但其大规模部署需严格的质量评估。人工评估难以扩展且对标注者造成情感负担，LLM虽可扩展但可靠性存疑，因此需量化考察LLM能否胜任该任务。

Method: 测试12个不同规模与家族的开源权重LLM，采用零样本、少样本和思维链三种提示方式，模拟人类专家在相关性、连贯性、攻击性和适宜性四个维度上的逐点评分与成对评估；以线性加权Cohen's κ和Spearman ρ衡量与人类专家的一致性，并比较逐点评分转成对标签的效果及模型间一致性。

Result: 最佳配置（Qwen 2.5 72B，连贯性）逐点评测仅达κ_l=0.19、ρ=0.42，成对评测κ=0.25；将逐点评分转为成对标签可显著提升一致性（Qwen 2.5 72B零样本由κ=0.25升至0.35，Mixtral 8×7B少样本由0.07升至0.34）；LLM间一致性远高于与人类的一致性，最强模型对（OLMo 2 32B与Llama 3.3 70B，连贯性）κ=0.76，而同配置下与专家的一致性不超过κ=0.21。

Conclusion: 现有LLM尚不能可靠替代人类专家进行counterspeech质量评估，逐点评估可靠性尤其有限；转为成对比较是提升人机一致性的有效途径，但LLM群体内部高度一致而与人类偏离的现象提示存在系统性偏差，需谨慎部署并辅以人工监督。

Abstract: Prolonged exposure to hate speech on social media can cause lasting psychological distress among users. Counterspeech has emerged as an effective, non-censorial mitigation strategy, but its deployment requires rigorous quality assessment. Manual assessment is unscalable due to the large volume of content on the Web and imposes severe emotional strain on human annotators. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a scalable alternative, yet concerns remain regarding their reliability. In this paper, we investigate the extent to which LLMs can effectively perform this task. We test 12 open-weight LLMs of different sizes and families under zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought prompting, to approximate pointwise and pairwise human expert assessment of counterspeech quality across four dimensions: relevance, coherence, aggressiveness, and suitableness. We find that (i) LLMs show low agreement and moderate rank correlations with human experts: our best configuration, Qwen 2.5 72B on coherence, achieves up to a linear-weighted Cohen's $κ_l = 0.19$ and Spearman's $ρ= 0.42$ in pointwise evaluation and an unweighted Cohen's $κ= 0.25$ in pairwise evaluation. (ii) Converting pointwise scores into pairwise labels substantially improves human--LLM agreement for selected configurations. For same-instance coherence, Qwen 2.5 72B under zero-shot prompting achieves $κ= 0.35$ with converted labels, compared with $κ= 0.25$ when judging pairs directly, and Mixtral 8$\times$7B under few-shot prompting improves from $κ= 0.07$ to $κ= 0.34$. (iii) LLMs agree more with one another than with human experts, particularly in pairwise evaluation, where the strongest model pair, OLMo 2 32B and Llama 3.3 70B on coherence, reaches substantial agreement with $κ= 0.76$, whereas their agreement with the experts in the same configuration does not exceed $κ= 0.21$.

</details>


### [184] [ReListen: Training-Free Evidence Localization in Hour-Long Audio with Frozen Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04480)
*Ming-Hao Hsu,Shujie Liu,Yuxuan Hu,Yuxiang Wang,Yingda Shen,Jinyu Li,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 ReListen：冻结的音频语言模型通过 Jev-style scan 对 20 秒窗打分，在最高分分钟上作答，实现小时级长音频的高效定位与回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一小时录音难以完整放入上下文，而答案常只依赖其中数秒；先转写虽可行但丢失声音与说话人信息，需专门训练的系统才能回到音频中找证据。作者希望无需转写、无需额外训练的解决路径。

Method: ReListen 为 training-free 方法：用冻结的音频语言模型以 Jev-style scan 对每 20 秒窗口估计"有助于回答该问题"的概率，选出得分最高的一分钟，仅基于该片段作答。同一扫描过程同时产生时间定位（grounding）结果。

Result: 在 VoiceGiraffe 上，所选分钟与标注证据重叠的比例达 88.5%，显著高于转写检索；LAT-Bench 上的事件定位与专门训练的时序定位模型相当；局部作答（远少于全音频）达到甚至超过听完整录音，比整段转写高 9.2 分；相同片段下朗读转写在语音内容上与聆听持平，但涉及声音与说话人的问题聆听明显更准；速度优于所有基于转写的系统，且显存占用恒定，可处理单 GPU 无法整段输入的录音。

Conclusion: 冻结的音频语言模型可自行检索小时级录音并在局部作答，无需转写与额外训练，同时兼具准确性与效率优势。

Abstract: Audio language models are meant to answer questions from the audio itself, but an hour-long recording rarely fits in their context, and the answer often depends on a few seconds of it. Transcribing the recording first makes it fit but discards sounds and voices, while systems that return to the audio must be trained for this behavior. We propose ReListen, a training-free method in which a frozen audio language model finds its own evidence before answering. In a Jev-style scan, the model scores each 20-second window by the probability that it helps answer the question, and it then answers from the highest-scoring minute. This minute overlaps the annotated evidence far more often than transcript retrieval, for 88.5% of VoiceGiraffe questions. Without any change, the same scan also grounds events on LAT-Bench as well as a model trained for temporal grounding. Answering from it matches or exceeds hearing the whole recording with a small fraction of the audio, and on VoiceGiraffe it exceeds a transcript of the whole recording by 9.2 points. The selected evidence is also worth hearing rather than reading. Given the same excerpts, reading their transcript matches listening on speech content, but listening is clearly more accurate on questions about sounds and voices. Moreover, ReListen answers faster than every transcript-based system, and its memory stays constant even for recordings too long to hear whole on one GPU. Therefore, a frozen audio language model can search hour-long recordings by itself, without a transcript or additional training.

</details>


### [185] [The Min-Max theory of language behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04406)
*Hiram Ring*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文用计算模型对"Min-Max"语言行为理论进行数学验证，发现操纵token长度与信息内容权重可使模型产生理论预测的词序变化，同时揭示Transformer模型可能无法准确表征心理语言学过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Min-Max理论原初基于观察数据（语言加工、互动、语料库研究）提出，认为人类同时追求加工努力最小化与信息内容最大化，并以此解释语言演变，但缺乏数学层面的验证。

Method: 使用预训练模型与迭代学习模型，以合成数据为输入进行系列实验，操纵tabula rasa模型中的token长度和信息内容权重两个变量，检验理论预测。

Result: 操纵token长度与信息内容权重可导致词序变化，符合理论预测；实验还发现计算模型与人类语言数据观察之间存在重要差异。

Conclusion: 语言演化轨迹高度依赖于加工最小化与信息内容最大化之间的权衡；当前基于Transformer的语言模型可能不能准确反映心理语言学过程。

Abstract: The "Min-Max" theory of language behavior proposes that humans engage simultaneously in minimization of processing effort and maximization of information content, and that this is a primary driver in language evolution (Ring, 2025). This claim seeks to unify observations from language processing (Mahowald et al., 2013; Futrell et al., 2015; Yadav et al., 2020; Hawkins, 2014), interaction (Hörberg and Sjons, 2023; Tucker et al., 2025; Jaeger and Tily, 2011; Smith and Culbertson, 2025), and corpora (Levshina and Moran, 2021; Levshina, 2023; Newmeyer, 2017), which suggest that considerations of processing efficiency and information content are both implicated in human communication. The interaction between these two processes provides an explanation for human linguistic behavior at various levels (Linders and Louwerse, 2023) and predicts historical developments in language, i.e. related to word order (Ring, 2025). Given that the original formulation of the Min-Max theory is based on observational data, the current paper seeks to validate the theory mathematically. Through a series of experiments that use computational models to investigate the theory's predictions, pretrained and iterated learning models are queried with synthetic data. The experiments show that manipulating the variables of token length and the weight of information content in a tabula rasa model results in word order change, as predicted by the theory. The experiments also identify important differences between computational models and observations of human language data, suggesting that current transformer-based language models may not accurately represent psycholinguistic processes. The overall findings indicate that evolutionary trajectories in language are highly susceptible to minimization of processing and maximization of information content.

</details>


### [186] [MDSKILL: Learning to Internalize Skill Memory through Multi-View Distillation for Efficient Dynamic Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05313)
*Dian Jin,Jinluan Yang,Kairong Han,Baohong Li,Zijing Hu,Yi He,Wenlin Zhong,Yunze Tong,Minqin Zhu,Linchuan Li,Yanan Niu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MDSKILL，将技能记忆按三种功能视角组织并用动态指标筛选高质量技能，构建视角特定与整合推理分支，通过On-Policy Self-Distillation将互补经验知识迁移并固化到共享策略中，实现高效技能内化与智能体自我进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能记忆以文本提示形式编码历史经验以增强自进化智能体的强化学习，但自生成记忆常含噪声监督以及功能纠缠、冗余或冲突的知识，直接蒸馏困难；在有限训练预算下会阻碍知识内化甚至导致性能退化。

Method: MDSKILL 多视角蒸馏方法：将技能记忆整理为状态定位、动作规划、执行控制三个互补功能视角；用动态指标选择高质量技能，构建视角特定与整合的推理分支；再用 On-Policy Self-Distillation 在各分支间迁移并巩固互补经验知识至共享策略。

Result: 在三个智能体基准上，与不依赖更强外部教师模型的RL、技能增强和自蒸馏基线相比，MDSKILL在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别较SOTA提升最多7.1%和8.1%，且无额外计算开销；消融与演化分析验证了其在知识内化和策略改进上的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过多视角组织与筛选技能记忆并结合同策略自蒸馏，可在有限预算下有效内化经验知识、提升策略性能并支持智能体持续自我进化。

Abstract: Skill memories encode past experiences as textual prompts to improve reinforcement learning (RL) for self-evolving agents. Complementing RL, On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) provides dense, fine-grained supervision to internalize knowledge from skill-guided trajectories and accelerate policy learning. However, self-generated memories often contain noisy supervision and functionally entangled, redundant, or conflicting knowledge, making direct distillation difficult. Under limited training budgets, these issues can hinder knowledge internalization and even degrade performance. To address this problem, we propose MDSKILL, a multi-view distillation approach for skill memory internalization. MDSKILL organizes skill memories into three complementary functional views (state grounding, action planning, and execution control) and selects high-quality skills using dynamic metrics to construct view-specific and integrated reasoning branches. OPSD then transfers and consolidates complementary experiential knowledge across these branches into a shared policy, enabling efficient skill internalization and continuous agent self-evolution. We evaluate MDSKILL on three agent benchmarks against RL, skill-augmented, and self-distillation baselines that do not rely on stronger external teacher models. Without additional computational overhead, MDSKILL improves upon SOTA performance by up to 7.1% and 8.1% on ALFWorld and WebShop. Extensive ablations and evolution analyses further validate its effectiveness in knowledge internalization and policy improvement. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MDSKILL-CF53.

</details>


### [187] [HERIT: Enabling Global Access to Hanja-to-English Historical Translation by Mitigating Temporal Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04949)
*Dayeon Jeong,Kyeongpil Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HERIT：利用RAG从大量韩汉（Hanja-Korean）语料生成高质量伪标注数据的系统化领域适配流水线，缓解数据稀缺与时代过拟合，显著优于基线，并翻译了此前未译的历史档案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史档案对跨学科研究价值极高，但严重语言障碍使其难以被全球利用。人工翻译成本高、耗时；直接套用现有机器翻译模型效果不佳，原因是历史文本存在独特语言细微差异，且人工标注语料稀缺导致时间偏差（temporal bias）/时代过拟合。

Method: 提出HERIT，一个系统化的领域适配（domain adaptation）流水线：以检索增强生成（RAG）从丰富的韩汉（Hanja-Korean）语料中生成高质量伪标注数据，扩充训练集，从而同时缓解数据稀缺与跨不同历史时期的过度拟合。

Result: 广泛的自动化评估与人工评估表明，HERIT在多项指标上稳定优于各基线模型；并据此模型翻译了档案中此前未翻译的部分。

Conclusion: RAG驱动的伪标签领域适配可有效解决低资源历史文本翻译中的数据稀缺与时代偏差问题，实现历史档案的全球可及，为研究者提供可用资源。

Abstract: Historical archives are invaluable for multidisciplinary research but remain inaccessible globally due to severe language barriers. While manual translation is prohibitively expensive and time-consuming, simply applying existing machine translation models is inadequate due to unique linguistic nuances and the temporal bias inherent in scarce human-labeled corpora. To address these challenges, we propose HERIT, a systematic domain-adaptation pipeline that leverages retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to generate high-quality pseudo-labeled data from abundant Hanja-Korean corpora. This approach substantially expands the training dataset, mitigating both data scarcity and temporal overfitting across different historical periods. Extensive automated and human evaluations demonstrate that HERIT consistently outperforms baselines. Finally, we translate previously untranslated portions of the archives using our model, thereby enabling global access to these resources for researchers.

</details>


### [188] [TraceRouter++: Causal Semantic Routing Realignment via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04878)
*Chuancheng Shi,Shangze Li,Wenhua Wu,Hengzhi Chen,Fei Shen,Li Zhuo,Zhiyong Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TraceRouter++ 提出一种路由级安全干预框架，通过"发现—追踪—重对齐"范式定位有害输入激活的因果路由通路，并用约束最优传输软路由重对齐将不安全激活边缘分布导向安全参考分布，在LLM、MLLM、扩散模型上同时提升对抗鲁棒性与安全-效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全干预方法依赖"局部性假设"，刚性抑制孤立神经元或特征；但有害语义表现为跨层分布式电路，局部消融既对复杂越狱脆弱，又损害模型通用能力，故需从路由层面进行干预。

Method: Discover-Trace-Realign 三步：(1) 用注意力散度定位敏感起始层，并借助稀疏自编码器（SAEs）将稠密激活解耦为可解释路由特征；(2) 用特征影响分数（FIS）将特征映射到下游因果电路；(3) 提出基于约束最优传输（OT）的软路由重对齐，将其表述为可解的坐标逐维边缘OT近似，在因果识别的路由子空间内把不安全激活边缘分布重定向至安全参考边缘分布，同时保持干预点非选中坐标不变。

Result: 在代表性 LLM、多模态大模型（MLLM）和扩散模型（DM）上的大量实验表明，TraceRouter++ 优于所评估的基线，在提升对抗鲁棒性的同时保持有利的安全-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 安全干预应从刚性局部抑制转向因果路由通路上的分布级软重对齐；该方法能安全重定向恶意语义流而保留正交计算路径，具备跨模型类型的通用性。

Abstract: Despite their remarkable capabilities, large foundation models (LFMs) remain susceptible to adversarial manipulation. Current safety intervention methods primarily rely on the \textit{locality hypothesis}, rigidly suppressing isolated neurons or features. However, harmful semantics manifest as distributed, cross-layer circuits, rendering localized ablations both brittle against complex jailbreaks and detrimental to general utility. To bridge this gap, we propose TraceRouter++, a routing-level safety intervention framework that explicitly traces safety-relevant causal routing pathways activated under harmful inputs and realigns their unsafe activation states. Our framework operates in a \textit{Discover-Trace-Realign} paradigm: it first pinpoints a sensitive onset layer via attention divergence and disentangles dense activations into interpretable routing features using sparse autoencoders (SAEs). Subsequently, it maps these features to downstream causal circuits via feature influence scores (FIS). Crucially, departing from rigid suppression paradigms, TraceRouter++ introduces a constrained optimal transport (OT)-based soft routing realignment. By formulating safety intervention as a tractable coordinate-wise marginal optimal transport approximation, our method redirects unsafe activation marginals toward safe-reference marginals within causally identified routing subspaces while preserving non-selected coordinates at the intervention point. This mechanism safely redirects malicious semantic flows while leaving orthogonal computational routes intact. Extensive experiments across representative large language models (LLMs), multimodal large language models (MLLMs), and diffusion models (DMs) show that TraceRouter++ outperforms evaluated baselines, improving adversarial robustness while maintaining a favorable safety-utility trade-off. WARNING: This paper contains unsafe model responses.

</details>


### [189] [Do Multi-Turn Conversational Capabilities Carry Over from Text to Speech? A Parallel Multilingual Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04390)
*Jiaming Zhou,Pei Zhang,Shiwan Zhao,Zhijun Wang,Baosong Yang,Yong Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Omnilingua-AudioMC，一个严格平行的十语种多轮语音对话基准（354段对话、1404条评分项），证明转写文本表现不能可靠代理多语种口语对话能力，并定位"前序语音上下文表征"是可恢复退化的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮语音交互需模型保留早期信息、遵循持续指令并跟踪对话状态修订。但现有评估多基于转写文本，尚不清楚文本上的成功能否迁移到同一内容的语音输入，以及该迁移是否跨语言一致。

Method: 构建Audio MultiChallenge的严格平行多语种扩展：10种语言、354段对话、1404条评分项，内容、轮次结构、跨轮依赖与评估标准在文本与语音条件下对齐。测评6个语音-语言模型，并以配对评分项分析与"替换前序用户轮次/当前轮次/两者为模型生成转写"的消融来定位退化来源。

Result: 从参考转写到直接语音输入的转变高度依赖模型与语言：转写表现相近的模型在语音下可显著分化，跨语种相对优势也会变化。配对评分项分析显示总体文本-语音差距会掩盖具体对话要求的保留情况，因为已演示成功的丢失可被其他评分项增益抵消。对差距最大的两个模型，替换历史轮次在全部十种语言上的宏观提升均大于替换当前轮次；而最强的直接语音模型从两种替换中获益都很小。

Conclusion: 转写表现是多语种口语对话能力的不可靠代理；前序语音上下文的表征是可恢复退化的重要来源。

Abstract: Successful multi-turn voice interaction requires models to retain information from earlier turns, follow persistent instructions, and track revisions to conversational state. These abilities are usually evaluated from transcripts, leaving open whether successes demonstrated from text carry over when the same turns are spoken, and whether this transfer is consistent across languages. We introduce Omnilingua-AudioMC, a strictly parallel multilingual extension of Audio MultiChallenge, to study this question. The benchmark contains 354 conversations and 1,404 rubric items across ten languages, with content, turn structure, cross-turn dependencies, and evaluation criteria aligned across transcript and speech conditions. Across six speech-language models, the shift from reference transcripts to direct speech input is strongly model- and language-dependent: models with similar transcript performance can diverge substantially under speech, and their relative strengths across languages can change. Paired rubric-level analysis shows that aggregate transcript-speech gaps can obscure which conversational requirements are preserved, because losses of transcript-demonstrated successes can be offset by gains on other items. Replacing preceding user turns, the current turn, or both with model-generated transcripts localizes recoverable degradation: for the two models with the largest gaps, history substitution yields a larger macro-level improvement than current-turn substitution in all ten languages, while the strongest direct-speech model gains little from either. These results show that transcript performance is an unreliable proxy for multilingual spoken conversational ability and identify the representation of preceding spoken context as an important source of recoverable degradation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/QwenLM/Omnilingua-Bench/tree/main/Omnilingua-AudioMC.

</details>


### [190] [Where Do Distilled Students Store Thinking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04141)
*Taeyeong Kim,Jaemin Jung,Ahhyun Kim,Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过比较不同教师配对下LoRA适配器的更新子空间，识别出一个与"类思考行为"（反思标记、独白格式、长解答）相关的共享适配器子空间，移除它可大幅关闭该类行为，但其特异性和可解释性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型在蒸馏大型推理模型（LRM）的长解答时会性能退化，近期工作用短答案模型与LRM按比例λ插值来缩短解答。作者追问：当教师"停止思考"时，学生权重究竟保留了什么，即变化发生在哪个权重子空间。

Method: 比较三个教师配对、三个学生在各λ下的LoRA适配器：定位更新子空间偏移最大的位置；识别跨适配器共享的子空间；将其移除（消融）并与同能量随机方向、更新最大主成分做对照；再以两倍权重移植到短答案学生中做嫁接实验。

Result: 共享子空间在转折点前被各适配器共用，转折后约保留一半（事后分析），并基本清空；移除该子空间可显著关闭该类思考行为。但仅在一对教师和另一个学生上，其移除效果明显优于移除更新最大主成分；在其他情况下，同能量随机方向常能接近该效果。嫁接实验中可激活早期标记但学生很少完成任务。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一个与蒸馏所得"类思考行为"相关的权重子空间，提供了从权重层面定位该行为的证据；但其因果特异性和效果稳定性有限，不能简单等同于行为所独有的可解释机制。

Abstract: Small students degrade on large reasoning models' (LRMs') long solutions, so recent work shortens them by interpolating an LRM with a short-answer model at ratio $λ$. We ask what student weights retain when this teacher stops thinking, comparing LoRA adapters of three students from three teacher pairs at each $λ$. In each pair, the adapters' update subspace shifts most where the teacher stops thinking and solutions shorten most, locating the change without attributing it. We identify a shared adapter subspace tied to thinking-like behavior (reflective markers, monologue format and long solutions). Shared by adapters before that point, it keeps about half (post hoc) while one pair's teacher halves its solutions but keeps its think tag, and largely empties after thinking stops. Removing it largely turns this behavior off for every pair, student and benchmark. Only in that pair and one other student does it leave clearly fewer markers than removing the update's top components; elsewhere, same-energy random directions often nearly match it. Grafted into a short-answer student at twice its weight, it turns early markers (a post hoc count) on, like the whole update, but the student rarely finishes. Our work identifies a weight subspace tied to distilled thinking-like behavior.

</details>


### [191] [Say Less, Mean Better: Training-Free Verbosity Control for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04107)
*Mahmoud Alyosify,Mirna Imbabi,Eman Elkhamisi,Rahatara Ferdousi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 MinimalLM 框架，将"何时停"与"停得是否结构完整"解耦，结合不确定性感知的长度相关停止符调整与结构完整性护栏，实现可靠的输出冗长度控制。实验在 3 个开源 LLM、330 道问题上共 5940 条响应中，减少 47.6% 生成 token、单条响应提速 19–68%，无结构截断，质量 8.04/10（原模型 8.42、等长截断 6.46）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 常生成过长的响应，增加推理延迟与算力成本；仅靠提示词难以在保证回答完整性的前提下控制长度，而激进的提前停止虽能降本却易造成响应急停截断。

Method: 免训练框架：将"停止时机决策"与"停止是否结构适当"分离。具体包括（1）不确定性感知、依赖长度的 stop-token 调整（解码期干预）；（2）结构完整性护栏（完成度检查）；（3）响应级指令协调。三者协同，不修改模型参数、不需辅助模型。通过消融验证解码控制与完整性约束的互补作用。

Result: 在 3 个开源 LLM、330 个问题、baseline 与消融共 5940 条响应上：token 减少 47.6%，单条响应提速 19–68%，主评估中结构截断输出为零；平均质量 8.04/10，对比原模型 8.42/10、等长截断 6.46/10。在医疗、编程、法律、科学等多领域均获得稳健效率收益。

Conclusion: 高效生成不仅是"少生成 token"，更需在保证响应完整性的前提下控制生成终止时点；MinimalLM 提供了轻量、免训练的可行路径，并通过消融确认解码控制与完整性约束的互补性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often generate responses longer than necessary, increasing inference latency and computational cost. While prompting can encourage conciseness, controlling output length without compromising response completeness remains challenging. Aggressive early stopping can reduce generation costs but risks terminating responses prematurely. We introduce MinimalLM, a training-free framework for reliable verbosity control that combines uncertainty-aware, length-dependent stop-token adjustment with structural completeness guardrails. MinimalLM separates the decision of when to stop from whether stopping is structurally appropriate, coordinating response-level instructions, decoding-time intervention, and completion checks without modifying model parameters or requiring auxiliary models. We evaluate MinimalLM across three open-source LLMs and 330 questions, producing 5,940 responses across baseline and ablation configurations. MinimalLM reduces generated tokens by 47.6%, accelerates individual responses by 19-68%, and eliminates structurally truncated outputs in the primary evaluation. Despite substantial token savings, it achieves an average quality score of 8.04/10, compared with 8.42/10 for unmodified models and 6.46/10 for length-matched truncation. Evaluations across diverse subject domains (such as healthcare, programming, law, and science) demonstrate robust efficiency gains, while ablations establish the complementary roles of decoding control and completeness constraints. Our findings highlight that efficient generation requires more than producing fewer tokens: it requires controlling when generation ends while preserving response integrity. MinimalLM offers a lightweight, training-free approach toward this goal.

</details>


### [192] [ARLoop: Fully-Open Pre-trained Looped Transformers with Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05234)
*Yuekun Yao,Yupei Du,Dawei Zhu,Huan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出ARLoop，一种基于注意力残差（attention residuals）的循环Transformer架构，通过对模型深度做注意力实现层输入的选择性聚合，在0.6B/1.2B规模预训练中显著提升数学与代码任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复应用共享层来以参数高效方式扩展有效深度，但标准残差连接将跨循环深度的信息聚合成单一流，无法控制哪些前层中间表示应被复用，缺乏对深度维度信息重用的细粒度调节能力。

Method: 提出ARLoop：每层输入通过对模型深度方向施加注意力进行选择性聚合（深度注意力/注意力残差）。实验发现循环Transformer无需访问完整残差历史——滑动窗口深度注意力在较大循环深度下与全历史注意力相当或更优。开展受控实验（140M、370M参数规模，循环深度R=1至5），并扩展到0.6B、1.2B参数的真实LLM预训练配方。

Result: 在受控实验中ARLoop一致优于既有循环Transformer残差设计；在真实预训练下增益可迁移，数学与代码任务提升尤为显著。1.2B模型仅用0.5T token、三次循环预训练，即在所有评测任务上超过Huginn-3.5B（发布时唯一完全开源的循环LLM），并与Qwen3.5-2B等同等规模非循环模型具有竞争力，且训练数据量明显更少。

Conclusion: 以深度注意力残差替代单一流残差是提升循环Transformer表达力与可扩展性的有效手段，且滑动窗口即可满足需求；该设计在真实LLM预训练规模下相对非循环模型具备数据效率优势。

Abstract: Looped transformers increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared set of transformer layers, offering a parameter-efficient way to scale computation. However, standard residual connections aggregate information across recurrent depth into a single stream, providing limited control over which intermediate representations from preceding layers should be reused. In this paper, we introduce ARLoop, a looped transformer architecture based on attention residuals, where the input of each layer is selectively aggregated through attention over model depth. Distinct from standard transformers, we find that looped transformers do not require access to the full residual history: sliding-window depth attention matches or outperforms full-history attention at larger recurrent depths. Across controlled experiments with multiple parameter scales (140M and 370M) and recurrent depths (R = 1 to 5), ARLoop consistently outperforms prior residual designs for looped transformers. More importantly, we further scale ARLoop to 0.6B and 1.2B parameters under a realistic LLM pretraining recipe, demonstrating that the gains transfer to realistic pretraining, with particularly strong improvements on math and coding tasks. Our 1.2B model, pretrained on only 0.5T tokens with three recurrences, outperforms Huginn-3.5B (the only fully-open looped LLM as of our release date) across all evaluated tasks and is competitive with similar-sized non-looped models such as Qwen3.5-2B despite being trained on substantially less data.

</details>


### [193] [Language Model Augmented Decision Transformer for Multi-Task Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04031)
*Xinbo Zhao,Yuhang Liu,Xin Zhang,Lei Le,Zhaohan Xi,Yu Yang,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LaMA-DT，用单一LLM同时完成语言引导的轨迹增强与多任务策略学习，缓解离线MTRL的数据稀缺与跨任务泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线多任务强化学习（MTRL）需从异构离线数据集中学习统一策略，但现有方法面临两个根本问题：单任务数据严重稀缺，以及跨任务泛化能力有限。

Method: 统一的序列建模框架：1）以自然语言任务描述为条件，结合一组监督生成与对齐目标，实现任务对齐的轨迹增强；2）引入打分-过滤机制筛选高质量增强轨迹；3）复用同一LLM作为语言条件化的Decision Transformer，在真实与增强数据混合集上微调。

Result: 在Meta-World与MuJoCo多任务基准上一致优于竞争基线，尤其在低数据情形下优势明显。

Conclusion: 语言引导的数据增强可显著提升跨任务泛化能力，验证了以单一LLM统一增强与策略学习的有效性。

Abstract: Offline multi-task reinforcement learning (MTRL) aims to learn a unified policy from heterogeneous offline datasets that generalizes across tasks. However, existing offline MTRL methods often struggle with two fundamental issues: severe per-task data scarcity and limited cross-task generalization. In this paper, we propose Language-Model Augmented Decision Transformer (LaMA-DT), a unified sequence-modeling framework that leverages a single large language model (LLM) for both trajectory augmentation and multi-task policy learning. LaMA-DT uses natural-language task descriptions to guide task-aligned trajectory augmentation through a set of supervised generation and alignment objectives, and incorporates a scoring-and-filtering mechanism to retain high-quality augmented trajectories. The same LLM is then repurposed as a language-conditioned Decision Transformer and fine-tuned on a mixture of real and augmented data. Experiments on Meta-World and MuJoCo multi-task benchmarks demonstrate that LaMA-DT consistently outperforms competitive baselines, particularly in low-data regimes, showing that language-guided data augmentation substantially improves cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [194] [Dory1.0-4B: A Looped Normalized-GPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03998)
*Maxim Fishman,Simeng Sun,Shantanu Acharya,Faisal Ladhak,Brian Chmiel,Santiago Akle Serano,Ron Banner,Boris Ginsburg,Krishna C. Puvvada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Dory1.0-4B：4B 参数循环（潜空间递归）语言模型，单一 checkpoint 通过可变循环次数实现弹性推理算力，在多基准上超越更大模型且更省 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让单一份额模型在不同推理算力预算下都能工作，用循环深度换取性能，尤其是提升编码、智能体与长上下文任务表现，同时避免靠生成长输出消耗算力。

Method: 基于归一化 Transformer（nGPT）主干，交错滑窗与全注意力层，结构分为输入块、共享循环块、输出块；推理时循环块可重复调用。6T token 预训练（前 4.5T 用 NVFP4），后续预训练扩展上下文至 256K；后训练采用 SFT + RLVR，并配难度感知循环课程，将难题路由到更深循环。循环时仅缓存最后一遍循环的 KV。

Result: 循环次数增加带来精度提升，编码、智能体、长上下文基准提升最大；≥2 次循环时 12 项基准平均超过 Qwen3.5-9B、Gemma 4 12B、Gemma 4 26B-A4B；4 次循环在智能体基准上 token 生成量减少 1.3–2.5 倍；KV 缓存占用与非循环模型持平且保留大部分精度收益。

Conclusion: 弹性深度循环是提升参数效率与推理效率的有效路径；开源 BF16、MXFP8、NVFP4、GGUF 量化 checkpoint。

Abstract: We introduce Dory1.0-4B, a 4-billion-parameter looped (latent-recurrent) language model with elastic depth: a single checkpoint serves different inference compute budgets. Dory1.0-4B is built on a normalized Transformer (nGPT) backbone with interleaved sliding-window and full-attention layers, organized into an input block, a shared recurrent block, and an output block. At inference, the recurrent block can be applied a variable number of times. We pre-trained Dory1.0-4B on 6 trillion tokens. The first 4.5 trillion tokens were trained in NVFP4, and additional pre-training then extended the context length to 256K tokens. Next, we post-trained the model using Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), with a difficulty-aware loop curriculum that routes harder prompts to deeper loops. Increasing the loop count improves accuracy, with the largest gains on coding, agentic, and long-context benchmarks. With two or more loops, Dory1.0-4B outperforms larger models such as Qwen3.5-9B, Gemma 4 12B, and Gemma 4 26B-A4B on average across 12 benchmarks. Looping also makes Dory1.0-4B more token-efficient on agentic benchmarks: with four loops, it generates 1.3-2.5x fewer tokens than with one loop, as the extra computation is spent in latent space rather than on longer outputs. To reduce memory during looping, Dory1.0-4B caches keys and values only from the last loop, keeping its KV-cache footprint equal to that of a non-looped model at any loop count while retaining most of the accuracy gains. We open-source the BF16, MXFP8, NVFP4, and GGUF quantized checkpoints.

</details>


### [195] [EvoGroup: A Self-Evolving Harness for Group Agents in Shared Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03868)
*Xinyi Xu,Xiaoyang Li,Yucheng Feng,Wenqing Chen,Youliang Yuan,Wei Shi,Rongxiang Weng,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoGroup 提出面向群体智能体的自进化 harness，从六工具冷启动出发，通过与共享上下文的交互迭代修订 harness，在 EverMemBench 上 pass@1 从 71.07% 提升至 89.58%，并在未见过的 GroupMemBench 上表现出泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统主要面向单用户，难以捕捉共享上下文中的群体级知识（群体关系、协作形成的知识）；人工设计的记忆策略也难以适应多样且持续演化的共享上下文。随着 Claude Tag 等群体智能体承担长期团队协作的数字工作者角色，这一缺口愈发突出。

Method: 采用自进化 harness：以最小六工具冷启动；用置信度与任务结果筛选高信息量轨迹，并对低置信度情形引入不确定性自反思；分析智能体通过自适应渐进式披露检视所选轨迹，再用分桶分析综合高层结论，指导 evolve agent 修订 harness。

Result: 在 EverMemBench 留出集上 pass@1 由 71.07% 提升至 89.58%，优于自进化记忆方法与人工设计 harness（含 Claude Code 的 85.83%）；冻结进化后 harness 在未见过的 GroupMemBench 上配合 GPT-5.5 仍优于记忆基线与人工设计 harness，显示有效泛化。

Conclusion: 面向群体智能体的记忆能力可由自进化 harness 有效获得，且进化所得结构可跨任务泛化；但其评测仅覆盖两个基准，结论的普适性仍需更广泛场景验证。

Abstract: With the rise of Group Agent such as Claude Tag, agents increasingly serve as digital workers for long-term team collaboration. These agents operate over shared contexts that continuously accumulate through multi-user interactions. However, existing agent memory systems are mainly designed for individual users and struggle to capture group-level knowledge in shared contexts, including group relationships and collaboratively formed knowledge. Manual memory strategies further limit adaptation to diverse and evolving shared contexts. To this end, we propose \textbf{EvoGroup (EvoG)}, a self-evolving harness for group agents that starts from a minimal six-tool cold start and adapts through interaction with shared context. EvoG uses confidence and task outcomes to select informative trajectories, while also eliciting self-reflections on uncertainty in low-confidence cases. The analysis agent then examines selected trajectories through adaptive progressive disclosure, followed by bucketed analysis to synthesize high-level findings that guide the evolve agent in revising the Harness. Empirically, pass@1 on held-out split of EverMemBench improves from 71.07\% to 89.58\%, surpassing both self-evolving memory and human-designed harnesses, including Claude Code (85.83\%). To evaluate generalization, we freeze the evolved harness and evaluate it on the unseen GroupMemBench, where, with GPT-5.5, it demonstrates effective generalization and outperforms both memory baselines and human-designed harnesses.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Bidirectional Centered Score Matching for Discrete Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04279)
*Peng Tu,Baoyou Chen,Haoyuan Xia,Zilong Dong,Weihao Yuan,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双向中心化分数匹配（BCSM），以 O(K) 双向活跃集将位置的成对比较降至 O(K²) 并因式分解为 O(K) 目标，辅以跨时间步转移对齐，在少步蒸馏中实现更优质量—效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型并行解码的少步蒸馏常采用 token-wise KL，但其在概率空间优化几何不佳，且候选截断仅在教师端进行，激进少步蒸馏下监督不足；Concrete Score Matching 虽在对数几率相对几何层面提供替代目标，但完整成对形式复杂度为 O(|V|²)，难以实用。

Method: 构建 O(K) 规模的双向活跃集（同时纳入教师与学生的候选），将原来 O(|V|²) 的成对比较缩减为 O(K²)，并进一步因式分解为 O(K) 复杂度的目标函数；同时引入跨时间步的转移对齐以增强蒸馏信号。

Result: 实验显示显著的质量—效率权衡：最多实现 6 倍 NFE 降低，单步推理下 HumanEval 相对提升 16.9%、MMMU 相对提升 15.1%。

Conclusion: BCSM 以线性复杂度保留了分数匹配的相对 logit 几何优势，并克服教师端单侧截断的监督缺陷，是一种高效可行的离散扩散少步蒸馏目标。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models with parallel decoding are often distilled using token-wise KL objectives. Although token-wise KL exactly matches the corresponding product-form block distributions, its probability-space optimization geometry and teacher-only candidate truncation may provide insufficient supervision under aggressive few-step distillation. Concrete Score Matching (CSM) provides an alternative within-position objective by aligning relative logit geometry, but its complete pairwise form incurs $\mathcal{O}(|\mathcal{V}|^2)$ complexity. We propose Bidirectional Centered Score Matching (BCSM), an efficient within-position distillation objective that constructs an $\mathcal{O}(K)$ bidirectional active set, reduces pairwise comparisons to $\mathcal{O}(K^2)$, and factorizes them into an $\mathcal{O}(K)$ objective. We further introduce cross-timestep transition alignment. Experiments demonstrate strong quality--efficiency trade-offs, including up to $6\times$ NFE reduction with a 16.9\% relative HumanEval gain and a 15.1\% relative MMMU gain at single-step inference.

</details>


### [197] [Reliability Stress Testing of RAG Retrieval under Query-Relevant Corpus Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04264)
*Janet Sung,Kainan Zhou,Gangzhen Qian,Hang Xiao,Lu Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在623条BEIR查询上考察RAG检索准入环节的脆弱性：每查询5篇目标文档即可使经典检索器nDCG@10下降40.2–61.5%，查询锚定是主因，BGE-small对纯查询投毒达100%目标曝光，且MMR无法降低曝光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部文本只有被检索器准入到模型上下文后才可能影响RAG系统，因此作者希望在文档内容、攻击者查询信息、每目标投毒预算与污染范围四个维度上系统压力测试这一"准入"环节，量化检索层的失效机制。

Method: 基于SciFact与NFCorpus共623条BEIR查询，在隔离目标情境下注入每查询5篇目标文档，比较BM25、LSA与Hybrid等经典检索器的nDCG@10变化；用预训练BGE-small做稠密模型验证；并考察查询长度（162条长查询）、多目标污染交叉干扰、基于重叠的准入过滤与MMR重排的防御效果。

Result: 隔离目标压力下经典检索器nDCG@10下降40.2–61.5%；BM25退化主要源于全查询锚定，附加文本亦改变LSA与Hybrid排序。BGE-small在仅查询P=5时两数据集目标曝光率均达100%，加入注入载荷后SciFact nDCG仅由0.269变为0.271、NFCorpus曝光率略降至98.8%。长查询上两个随机词覆盖19.9%可用词仍带来39.5% Hybrid目标曝光；多目标污染产生跨目标干扰；重叠式准入可移除全查询毒化但可能大幅损害干净检索效用，MMR在测试配置下曝光不变。

Conclusion: 查询锚定是此类稠密/经典检索器检索侧的主导因素，部分信息攻击效果随查询长度显著变化，现有准入过滤与MMR防御存在效用—安全权衡或无效。研究仅衡量检索层失效，不涉及下游指令遵循。

Abstract: External text can affect a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system only after the retriever admits it to the model context. We stress this admission step while varying document content, attacker query information, per-target poison budget, and contamination scope. Across 623 Benchmarking Information Retrieval (BEIR) queries from SciFact and NFCorpus, five query-targeted documents per query reduce classical-retriever nDCG@10 by 40.2-61.5% in isolated-target stress. Full-query anchoring explains most Best Matching 25 (BM25) degradation, whereas appended text also shifts latent semantic analysis (LSA) and Hybrid rankings. A pretrained BGE-small validation reaches 100% target exposure for query-only P=5 on both datasets; adding the injection payload changes SciFact nDCG only from 0.269 to 0.271 and lowers NFCorpus exposure slightly to 98.8%, indicating that query anchoring remains the dominant retrieval-side factor for this dense model. Query length materially changes the interpretation of partial-information attacks: on 162 long SciFact queries, two random terms cover 19.9% of eligible terms and still yield 39.5% Hybrid target exposure. Multi-target contamination adds cross-target interference. Overlap-based admission can remove full-query poisons but may sharply reduce clean retrieval utility, and maximal marginal relevance (MMR) leaves exposure unchanged under the tested configuration. The study measures retrieval-layer failures, not downstream instruction following.

</details>


### [198] [Neologism Inoculation Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04260)
*David Demitri Africa,Cameron Holmes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出新词接种提示（NIP）：用在新词token上训练的提示替代自然语言接种提示，并在部署时移除该token，从而在减少有害行为的同时避免其被轻易重新诱发；机制上NIP是"隐藏"而非"删除"有害行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接种提示（inoculation prompting）用英文写提示，对模型进行有害示例训练可降低如reward hacking等不良行为，但任意用户只要复述该英文提示即可重新诱发有害行为，安全性不足。

Method: NIP将接种提示替换为一个新造token（neologism），在冻结模型上训练该token，部署时将其移除。通过对照token实验与激活分析探究其作用机制。

Result: NIP优于其他接种方法，虽更简单但性能与gradient routing相当；对reward hacking有效，但对sycophancy与harassment失效。机制分析表明NIP是把有害行为藏到模型输入空间中不可达的区域，而非将其删除。

Conclusion: 新词可作为划分并调控对不良/有害行为访问权限的有力手段；但NIP并非真正消除行为，存在被绕过的潜在风险，且效果依行为类型而异。

Abstract: Inoculation prompting is a method to reduce undesired behaviours such as reward hacking in frontier models. It works by training a model on harmful examples under a prompt that specifies the undesired behaviour, which reduces the model's tendency to exhibit it unless prompted. However, the inoculation prompt is written in plain English, so any user can re-elicit the behaviour easily. We introduce neologism inoculation prompting (NIP), which replaces the English prompt with a new token that is trained on the frozen model and removed at deployment. NIP outperforms other inoculation methods, and performs competitively with gradient routing despite being simpler. It also works on reward hacking, but fails on sycophancy and harassment. Using control tokens and analyses of activations, we identify the mechanism for this as NIP hiding the harmful behaviour behind an unreachable region of model inputs, rather than deleting it. More broadly, our work suggests that neologisms can be a powerful way to carve out and shape access to behaviours that are undesired or harmful.

</details>


### [199] [Exact Optimality Certification of QPLIB 2096 through Complete Set Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04230)
*Jingyang Du,Zixiong Yi,Yang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对QPLIB_2096实例给出全局最优值7,025,720的精确计算证明，采用集合划分重构+整数对偶证书验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原问题为非凸二次规划（300个二元变量、6,900条三角不等式、25条非凸二次约束），缺乏可验证的全局最优性证明；作者希望获得实例级别的精确最优性认证。

Method: 通过系数级重构将变量识别为25个元素的无序对；三角不等式编码划分结构，二次约束转化为单个划分块关联边的总权重上界，从而得到带独立边代价图的集合划分形式。对规模1–6的245,505个子集进行精确枚举，得14,981个可行块；完整六元子集均不可行，非负容量权重排除更大块。构造整数价格向量满足所有可行块的对偶不等式，并做独立于求解器的格式与证书校验。

Result: 对偶价格向量之和为2,229,456，从总目标边成本9,255,176中扣除得下界7,025,720，与给定六块划分的原始可行解相等，证明全局最优。

Conclusion: 得到带精确原始与对偶见证的实例级最优性证明；结论仅限该实例，不推断跨实例性能。研究过程借助自演化AI系统ZiYor探索与改进。

Abstract: We present an exact computational proof that the frozen QPLIB_2096 instance has global minimum 7,025,720. The solution and certification approach was explored and refined through ZiYor, a self-evolving AI system. The authors provide the mathematical account and exact validation reported here. The original formulation has 300 binary variables, 6,900 triangle inequalities, and 25 nonconvex quadratic constraints. A coefficient-level reconstruction identifies its variables with the unordered pairs of 25 items. The triangle inequalities encode partitions, while each quadratic constraint becomes a bound on the total weight of edges incident to one partition block. This yields a set-partitioning formulation over all capacity-feasible blocks, with a separate edge-cost graph determining the objective. Exact enumeration examines 245,505 subsets of sizes one through six and finds 14,981 feasible blocks. Every six-element subset is infeasible; nonnegative capacity weights exclude all larger blocks. An integer price vector satisfies the dual inequality for every feasible block and has sum 2,229,456. Subtracting this sum from the total objective edge cost 9,255,176 gives a lower bound of 7,025,720, attained by the supplied six-block partition. Fresh solver-free checks validate the candidate against both frozen model formats and verify the certificate. The result is an instance-specific optimality proof with an exact primal and dual witness; no cross-instance performance conclusion is drawn.

</details>


### [200] [Validation-Gated ML Pipeline Synthesis with Expert-Grounded Self-Evolving Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04180)
*Zixiao Zhao,Jing Sun,Zhe Hou,Mengze Li,Qian Liu,Cheng-Hao Cai,Zijian Zhang,Xin Wang,Gillian Dobbie,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoML 是一个基于专家技能库与验证门控 harness 的端到端 ML 流水线自动合成智能体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic AutoML 方法要么依赖僵化模板、适应性差或任务域狭窄，要么采用自由形式代码生成而缺乏可靠性保证，导致中间错误静默传播并损害最终模型质量。

Method: 将流水线构建分解为四个阶段，由三类智能体协同：Orchestrator 执行验证门控（结构、执行、语义检查）确保逐阶段推进、防止错误传播并保证可审计；Executor 运行以 nnU-Net 工作流为基础的专家技能，结合可复用模板与在线自适应；Curator 进行运行后演化，将本次运行中生成的脚本与指导整合进技能库，实现跨数据集持续提升。

Result: 在覆盖六类复杂真实 ML 任务的八个数据集上，与 agentic AutoML 系统及命令行编码工具对比，EvoML 实现全自动化并在全部八个数据集上取得最佳最终模型质量：分割 Dice 最高 0.94、分类 0.99 准确率、重建 0.95 SSIM、去噪 0.79 归一化 PSNR，并可迁移至更广泛 ML 任务。

Conclusion: 专家经验引导的可演化技能库配合验证门控执行机制，可在保证可靠性与可审计性的同时实现端到端自动化，并获得优于现有 agentic AutoML 与通用编码工具的模型质量，且具备跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have enabled agentic systems that automate machine learning pipeline construction from minimal user input. However, existing agentic AutoML methods either depend on rigid templates that limit adaptability, target narrow task domains, or rely on free-form code generation that lacks reliability guarantees, leaving intermediate errors to propagate silently and degrade the final model. We present EvoML, an agentic framework for end-to-end ML pipeline synthesis built on expert-grounded skills that evolve across runs, executed through a validation-gated harness. The framework decomposes pipeline construction into four phases coordinated by three agents: an Orchestrator that enforces validation gates, advancing each phase only when its outputs pass structural, execution, and semantic checks, which precludes error propagation and keeps every artifact auditable; an Executor that runs expert-grounded skills, grounded in the nnU-Net workflow and pairing reusable templates with online adaptation; and a Curator that performs post-run evolution, consolidating the scripts and guidance recovered during a run into the skill library so it improves across datasets. We evaluate EvoML on eight datasets spanning six sophisticated, real-world ML task types, against agentic AutoML systems and command-line coding tools. EvoML achieves full automation and the best final model quality on all eight, spanning segmentation (up to 0.94 Dice), classification (0.99 accuracy), reconstruction (0.95 SSIM), and denoising (0.79 normalized PSNR), while transferring to broader ML tasks.

</details>


### [201] [The Agent Board: Kanban for Machines; Durable Coordination for Agents That Outlive the Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04100)
*Tyler Hunt,Donald Nguyen,Gurbinder Gill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Corvic 框架中的“Agent Board”多智能体协调基座：一张持久化、共享的半语义任务表，通过阻塞式投递/唤醒与 Board Keeper 中断处理机制，实现跨智能体、跨会话、跨厂商的协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体框架普遍采用“单线程”形态（单智能体、单历史、单机器、单次会话），无法支撑跨多智能体、生命周期长于单次模型调用、需等待非智能体系统的任务，故需新的协调基座。

Method: Agent Board 为持久共享任务表，含少量用于路由的精确字段与仅供 LLM 阅读的自由文本 body；智能体可投递、监视并对任务阻塞，结束回合且不占用进程直至匹配投递唤醒；系统智能体 Board Keeper 作为板级中断处理器，将非智能体组件的事件带入板、按语义匹配等待任务、并为“按类型而非实例”命名的任务分配新智能体；可加载的协调策略内核赋予状态与角色语义，板本身不强加。另报告真实板追踪与三个小型模拟试验。

Result: 模拟试验中，板的等待成本对等待时长近似恒定，而朴素轮询增长约165倍；但简单外部监视器比板更便宜。Board Keeper 对文本等待者匹配无虚假唤醒，但漏检 3.6% 的真实唤醒。

Conclusion: 工作（通常为带真实来源的实证研究）表明该机制可支撑持久化多智能体产品的等待、移交与跨厂商协作，并类比为智能体操作系统调度器（同时指出类比失效之处），明确区分机制保证与约定俗成，但需权衡语义匹配漏检与开销。

Abstract: Harnesses for large language models have converged on a single-threaded shape: one agent, one history, one machine, one sitting. That shape fails work that spans many agents, outlives any one model call, or waits on systems that are not agents. We describe the Agent Board, the coordination substrate in the Corvic harness. The board is a durable, shared table of semi-semantic tasks: a few exact fields the system routes on, and a free-form body that only language models read. Agents post tasks, watch tasks, and block on them, ending their turn and holding no process until a matching post wakes them. A system agent, the Board Keeper, acts as the interrupt handler for the board: it carries events from non-agent parts of the harness onto the board, matches them to waiting tasks by reading their prose, and allocates new agents for tasks that name a kind of agent rather than an instance. Loadable coordination policy kernels give statuses and roles meaning for a given pattern of work, without the board enforcing them. The design mirrors the kanban board a human team puts on a wall. We argue it is what persistent agent products such as Meta Muse, OpenAI dots and SpaceXAI Grok Bot need once an agent must wait, hand off work, or work with agents from another vendor. We view the board as an operating-system scheduler for agents and state where the analogy breaks. We separate what the mechanism guarantees from what it leaves to convention, and report a trace from a live board and three small simulated pilots. In the pilots, the wait cost of the board was flat in wait length while naive polling grew about 165-fold, but a plain external watcher was cheaper than the board. The Board Keeper matched prose waiters with no false wakes but missed 3.6 percent of real wakes.

</details>


### [202] [AlgoPilot: Cross-Paradigm Reasoning in Language Models via Strategy Selection and Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04079)
*Yilun Hao,Ruixiao Yang,Hudson Hilal,Yongchao Chen,Kaizhi Qian,Yada Zhu,Yang Zhang,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出跨范式推理框架 AlgoPilot，通过可学习的策略选择与结构化问题建模模块，先定方法再执行，将同一骨干模型平均准确率从38.4%提升至72.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实问题横跨程序合成、符号推理、规划与优化等不同领域，需采用根本不同的求解范式。LLM推理的核心难点在于两点：选对合适的求解方法，以及正确地将其形式化。现有基于提示的方法依赖固定推理模板，无法针对输入自适应地选择与结构化求解策略。

Method: AlgoPilot 由三部分组成：(1) SteerLM，经SFT与RL训练，根据输入问题自适应选择求解策略（范式与模式）；(2) 对应的专家 GuideLM，经SFT训练，按所选策略生成结构化形式化指导；(3) ExecutionLM，在所选策略及其形式化的条件下生成解。整体构建于 Qwen3-8B。

Result: 在5类问题、13个基准、66个领域上评测，同模型平均准确率由38.4%升至72.9%，较最佳大模型提示基线 GLM-5 高11.7%；消融实验验证各组件有效性；所学引导与形式化模块可跨执行模型迁移。

Conclusion: 将"策略选择—形式化—执行"解耦并交由可训练模块学习，比固定提示模板更有效；且引导与形式化模块具备可迁移性，有望用于增强其他LLM。

Abstract: Real-world problems span diverse domains, including program synthesis, symbolic reasoning, planning, and optimization, and often require fundamentally different solution paradigms. A central challenge for LLM-based reasoning is twofold: identifying an appropriate problem-solving approach and formulating it correctly. In this paper, we propose AlgoPilot, a cross-paradigm reasoning framework that enables adaptive selection of the solving approaches and structured problem formulation prior to execution. The AlgoPilot framework contains three components: 1) a SteerLM, trained via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), that adaptively selects the solving strategy (paradigm and modes) based on input problems, 2) the corresponding expert GuideLM, trained via SFT, that generates structured formulation guidance tailored to the selected strategy, and 3) an ExecutionLM that generates solutions conditioned on both the selected strategy and its formulation. Unlike prompting-based methods that rely on fixed reasoning templates, our approach learns to adaptively choose and structure problem-solving strategies based on the input. We build AlgoPilot on Qwen3-8B and evaluate across 5 problem categories and 13 benchmarks with 66 unique domains, comparing against both the latest similarly scaled small models and large LLMs. AlgoPilot significantly improves average accuracy for the same model from 38.4% to 72.9%, outperforming the best large LLM prompting baseline GLM-5 by 11.7%. Ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of each component. Notably, we show that the learned steering and formulation modules can transfer across execution models, suggesting their potential to improve other LLMs. Project page: https://sites.google.com/view/algopilot.

</details>


### [203] [Recursa: Toward Recursively Self-Improving Foundation Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04035)
*Erkang Zhu,Yin Lin,Xiaorong Shi,Xuanrui Lin,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Recursa：一个无需人类专家或更强教师模型、通过递归自我改进从自身经验中学习以适应新领域的基础模型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在环境变化时会遇到训练未覆盖的陌生领域知识；而新领域通常缺乏大量专家标注数据和可靠监督信号，难以进行进一步训练。

Method: 构建知识库存储领域技能及其适用指令、过往任务经验记忆与改进反思；从领域材料自举（bootstrap）知识库，再通过递归自我改进精炼：由并行学习器执行任务并合成局部改进，随后验证并将改进合并进知识库。

Result: 在 Harvey LAB 的 Firm Knowledge 基准上，DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 的留出测试任务全通过率由 30.0% 提升至 55.0%，Qwen3.8-Flash 由 35.0% 提升至 46.0%。

Conclusion: 不依赖专家数据或强教师模型，仅凭自身经验与递归自改进即可实现新领域适应，且带来显著性能提升，说明经验驱动、可验证合并的知识积累是有效的领域自适应路径。

Abstract: Foundation models in changing environments can encounter unfamiliar domain knowledge that their original training did not cover. However, new domains rarely provide large amounts of expert-created training data or reliable supervisory signals for further training. We introduce Recursa, a foundation model system that adapts to new domains by learning from its own experience without human experts or a stronger teacher model. Recursa uses a knowledge repository to store what it learns: domain-specific skills and instructions on when to apply them, along with memories of past task experiences and reflections on what to improve. Recursa bootstraps the repository from domain materials and then refines it through recursive self-improvement. It uses parallel learners to execute tasks and synthesize local improvements, then validates and merges the improvements into the knowledge repository. On Harvey LAB's Firm Knowledge benchmark, Recursa raises the all-pass rate on held-out test tasks from 30.0% to 55.0% with DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash and from 35.0% to 46.0% with Qwen3.8-Flash.

</details>


### [204] [ROCm InferaSim: Cut LLM Serving Trial Costs and Deploy the Best Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04032)
*Anshu Raina,Yao Fu,Zhang Jiejing,Peyman Razaghi,Shiksha Patel,Ravi Gupta,Deval Shah,Adeem Jassani,Subhajit Dutta Chowdhury,Lorri Rao,Kailash Gogineni,Zhenyu Gu,Onil Gunawardana,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ROCm InferaSim 用少量"锚点"测量加解析外推来预测 LLM 服务配置性能，替代约 25,000 GPU 小时的穷举测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务性能取决于引擎、模型切分、精度、调度器设置、推测解码、KV-cache 管理与 prefill-decode 分离等多维配置，最优配置随模型、硬件、负载和目标变化，实践者通常靠试错寻找，穷举测量代价极高（本文研究中约需 25,000 GPU 小时）。

Method: 基于"同类 kernel 只测一次"的锚点法：batch size、prompt 长度、GPU 数量等只改变每卡工作量而不改变 kernel，因此对每组不同 kernel 组合仅在最多一个节点上测量一次（anchor），再用解析规律外推到其他部署；多节点部署归约为单节点等价形式后测量并解析缩放；另配模拟器回放多轮流量，并用调优 agent 基于预测搜索配置。

Result: 在 AMD Instinct MI355X 上配合 vLLM、SGLang、ATOM：11 个锚点耗时 9.1 GPU 小时，覆盖的 226 个定长配置直接测量需 204 GPU 小时；172 个配置上每输出 token 时间中位误差 8.1%；多轮编码负载吞吐误差在 8% 内；159 个未作锚点的分离式部署吞吐误差在 5.5% 内；5 候选短名单中含与最优可行"模型-延迟目标"配对相差 12% 以内的部署。

Conclusion: 少量锚点测量结合解析外推可高精度预测 LLM 服务性能，将配置调优成本降低约一个数量级，并支撑分离式部署与多轮负载的配置搜索。

Abstract: Serving an LLM efficiently depends on the engine, how the model is split across GPUs, precision, scheduler settings, speculative decoding, KV-cache management, and prefill-decode disaggregation. The best configuration depends on the model, hardware, workload, and objective. Operators usually find it by trial and error. Exhaustively measuring the candidates in our study would take about 25,000 GPU-hours. ROCm InferaSim predicts serving performance from a few short measurements. Choices such as batch size, prompt length, and GPU count change the amount of work per GPU without changing the kernels. ROCm InferaSim benchmarks each distinct set of kernels once, on at most one node. We call this measurement an anchor. Analytical laws carry that anchor to other deployments that run the same kernels. For multi-node deployments, ROCm InferaSim reduces the configuration to a single-node equivalent, measures it, and scales the result analytically. A simulator replays multi-turn traffic, and a tuning agent searches configurations using the predictions. On AMD Instinct MI355X GPUs with vLLM, SGLang, and ATOM, 11 anchors took 9.1 GPU-hours, against 204 GPU-hours to measure the 226 fixed-length configurations they cover. On 172 configurations, time per output token has 8.1% median error. On a multi-turn coding workload, throughput is within 8%. For 159 disaggregated deployments, none used as an anchor, throughput is within 5.5%. A five-candidate short list contains a deployment within 12% of the best feasible pairing of model and latency target.

</details>


### [205] [WASP-Mem: Let Agent Organize Procedural Memory. Human Spend Effort on Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04003)
*Peizhi Niu,Ge Shi,Vaibhav Gorde,Sarthak Ghosh,Christos Faloutsos,Min Cheng,Kevin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WASP-Mem，将执行轨迹与反馈蒸馏为持久、互链的 wiki 页面作为过程性记忆，在 TheAgentCompany 上比最强基线 checkpoint 通过率高 4.6-6.1% 且存储减少 57-61%；控制实验表明人类诊断优于自判断，而预设知识组织方式反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体难以自动化复杂企业流程：静态、预构建的执行框架无法捕获专有策略、未文档化行为以及仅在执行中才显现的、依赖状态的恢复路径。过程性记忆提供了免训练弥补该缺口的路径，但其与情景记录、语义事实不同，需要把重复成功与已诊断的失败整合为可复用动作与恢复策略。

Method: WASP-Mem（Wiki-based Agent-Synthesized Procedural Memory）：将智能体执行轨迹与反馈蒸馏为持久、相互链接的 wiki 页面，实现持续学习；并在 TheAgentCompany 基准上做系统性控制实验，变量包括整合方法、反馈类型、轨迹摄入范围与精修轮次。

Result: 相比对比中的最强基线，三个模型上 checkpoint 通过率提升 4.6-6.1%，存储需求降低 57-61%。规定性知识组织（人工定义 schema、显式去重规则）反而损害性能；一轮人类诊断比四轮自我判断高 7.0-10.9%。

Conclusion: 应让智能体自行组织过程性记忆，而将人类投入集中在最有价值处——诊断；人类诊断能将失败轨迹转化为纠错知识并带来持续改进。

Abstract: LLM agents struggle to automate complex enterprise procedures because static, pre-built harness cannot capture proprietary policies, undocumented behaviors, or state-dependent recovery paths that surface only during execution. Procedural memory accrued from agent experience offers a training-free path to closing this gap -- yet unlike episodic records or semantic facts, it must consolidate repeated successes and diagnosed failures into reusable actions and recovery strategies. We introduce WASP-Mem (Wiki-based Agent-Synthesized Procedural Memory), a nimble but effective method that distills execution traces and feedback into persistent, interlinked wiki pages, enabling continual agent learning. On TheAgentCompany benchmark, WASP-Mem raises the checkpoint pass rate by 4.6-6.1% over the strongest baseline we compared across three models while requiring 57-61% less storage. We further present a systematic controlled study spanning consolidation method, feedback type, trace ingestion scope, and refinement rounds. Our results reveal that prescriptive knowledge organization such as human defined schemas and explicit deduplication rules hurts performance rather than closing the procedural-knowledge gap.
